Astroglial dysfunction in models of CDKL5 deficiency disorder.推荐
PMID 43031738 · Frontiers in cellular neuroscience · 2026 10 30
作者：Pickering C, Bakoulina A, McLeod F, Pantziarou A et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
摘要：CDKL5 deficiency disorder (CDD) is a rare developmental epileptic encephalopathy typically caused by loss of function variants in the gene encoding the X-linked serine-threonine kinase CDKL5. CDKL5 is highly expressed in the brain during development, and key neuronal functions of the kinase include cytoskeletal organisation and synaptic stability. However, at present, little is known about the function of astroglia in CDD. Given the importance of these cells in synaptic development and homeostasis, as well as dysfunction in other epileptic diseases, it was hypothesised that astrocytes may contribute to CDD pathology. Induced pluripotent stem cells harbouring a CDKL5 loss-of-function mutation (and isogenic controls) were derived from CDD patient fibroblasts and differentiated into astrocytes (iAstros). Analysis of iAstros revealed transcriptomic, proteomic and functional dysregulation in CDKL5-mutant iAstros relating to water transport and immunological function, including a diminished response to TNFα stimulation. Moreover, iAstros showed increased branching and reduced phosphorylation of the known CDKL5 target end-binding protein 2 (EB2) - indicative of disrupted cytoskeletal regulation in a manner similar to CDKL5-null neurons. Finally, we report proof-of-concept of novel in vitro models of CDD. CDKL5 was knocked down in adult and foetal human organotypic brain slices through transduction with an AAV encoding a novel CDKL5 shRNA. Slices transduced with the CDKL5 shRNA displayed a trending increase in spontaneous network activity compared to those transduced with a scrambled control, demonstrating the functionality of this model. Importantly, interrogation of these models revealed dysregulation of key astrocytic proteins congruent with the human glial stem cell model. Consequently, this study describes the generation of novel human models of CDD and initial findings of astrocytic dysfunction - paving the way for novel discovery and therapeutic intervention.
Case Report: A novel hemizygous nonsense variant in PPP1R3F gene associated with developmental delay, intellectual disability and autism spectrum disorder.浏览
PMID 43031693 · Frontiers in neuroscience · 2026 10 30
作者：Yang Q, Zhang Q, Zhang Q, Zhou X et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：病例报告
摘要：BACKGROUND: PPP1R3F deficiency syndrome is an extremely rare, recently identified X-linked recessive neurodevelopmental disorder. It is caused by hemizygous variants in the PPP1R3F gene, which encodes a glycogen-targeting subunit of the brain-specific protein phosphatase 1 (PP1). Dysfunction of PPP1R3F disrupts glycogen metabolism in astrocytes, leading to insufficient energy supply to synapses during critical periods of brain development and subsequently causing neurodevelopmental abnormalities. Only a small number of cases of PPP1R3F deficiency syndrome have been reported to date, which significantly limits our comprehensive understanding of the clinical features of the disease and the relationship between genetic variants and clinical manifestations in patients. METHODS: Whole-exome sequencing (WES) was performed on a Chinese male presenting with intellectual disability (ID), developmental delay (DD), and autism spectrum disorder (ASD). Sanger sequencing was then used to identify and determine the origins of the variant. We evaluated the pathogenicity of the identified variant following the established ACMG/AMP guidelines. Additionally, we conducted a thorough review of previously reported cases to summarize the variants and clinical features of PPP1R3F deficiency syndrome. RESULTS: We identified a novel hemizygous nonsense variant [NM_033215.5: c.907C>T, p.(Gln303*)] in the PPP1R3F gene in the proband, which was inherited from his mother. The patient we described here exhibited clinical phenotypes similar to those associated with PPP1R3F gene variants but lacked other reported multisystem manifestations, such as epilepsy, brain abnormalities, and dysmorphic features. An analysis of the 15 variants and their associated phenotypes in 15 patients from both the current and previous studies demonstrates that PPP1R3F gene variants can lead to a variety of phenotypic deficits, and a clear genotype-phenotype correlation has not yet been established. Despite the high variability in some clinical manifestations, the core phenotypic features of PPP1R3F variants include varying degrees of ID, DD, speech delay, dysmorphic facial features, and ASD. CONCLUSION: Our findings support DD, ID, ASD, and speech impairment as core features of PPP1R3F deficiency syndrome, while also highlighting the extensive clinical variability. This study further expanded the genetic and phenotypic spectrum associated with PPP1R3F deficiency syndrome.
Central nervous system involvement in relapsing polychondritis with inflammatory changes in the cerebrospinal fluid and cranial MRI abnormalities: a case series report.推荐
PMID 43031684 · Frontiers in medicine · 2026 10 30
作者：Chen J, Luo Z, Hua P, Wang B et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
摘要：OBJECTIVE: To explore the clinical features of relapsing polychondritis (RP) with central nervous system (CNS) involvement as the major manifestation. METHODS: A total of 11 RP patients who were hospitalized in the Department of Neurology, the Affiliated Brain Hospital of Nanjing Medical University from January 2020 to December 2024 were retrospectively enrolled. Their clinical manifestations, laboratory indicators, cerebrospinal fluid (CSF) changes, and cranial magnetic resonance imaging (MRI) findings were comprehensively analyzed. RESULTS: CNS involvement was the major initial manifestation in the vast majority of RP patients (90.9%, 10/11), as cognitive impairment (n = 10), headache (n = 7), psychiatric/behavioral symptoms (n = 6), epileptic seizures (n = 4), limb numbness (n = 4), limb weakness (n = 4), and limb tremor (n = 3). CSF analysis revealed an inflammatory response in the CNS, as evidenced by increased levels of neutrophils, albumin, and immunoglobulins at varying degrees. White matter degeneration (n = 7) and brain atrophy (n = 7) were the pronounced changes on cranial MRI. Brain lesions were mainly distributed in the cortex, subcortex, and infratentorium. Parenchymal or meningeal enhancement was observed on MRI scans in three RP patients with CNS involvement. Arterial spin labeling (ASL) detected three cases of hypoperfusion and two cases of hyperperfusion in the brain. All patients were treated with corticosteroids, and six were managed with additional immunotherapies. Finally, ten RP patients were improved and discharged. CONCLUSION: neurological deficits vary widely in RP patients with CNS involvement, with cognitive decline as the most common feature. Inflammatory changes in the CSF indicate a neuroinflammatory mechanism involved in the pathogenesis of RP. Distinct imaging features on cranial MRI are closely associated with the involvement into CNS in RP, offering references for distinguishing the acute inflammation stage from the chronic degeneration stage during the development of RP.
Preparedness of undergraduate health students to provide women-specific epilepsy care: knowledge gaps and stigmatizing attitudes in Saudi Arabia.推荐
PMID 43031661 · Frontiers in neurology · 2026 10 30
作者：Alzahrani F, Alharbi Y, Almuzaini Y, Abdulhakeem A et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
摘要：BACKGROUND: Women with epilepsy have distinct reproductive, clinical, and psychosocial healthcare needs that require women-specific care. This study assessed the preparedness of undergraduate health students by examining their knowledge, attitudes, and stigmatizing beliefs. METHODS: A cross-sectional study was conducted among 339 undergraduate health students at Taibah University, Saudi Arabia, using a structured self-administered questionnaire. Multiple linear regression examined the factors associated with total knowledge score, while the ≥60% knowledge benchmark was retained for descriptive and exploratory sensitivity analyses. RESULTS: The mean knowledge score was 7.37 ± 2.96 out of 16 (46.1%), and 23.6% met the ≥60% benchmark. Knowledge gaps were most evident in reproductive health, hormonal influences, fertility, antiseizure medication safety, and contraceptive counseling topics. Attitudes were generally supportive, although stigma persisted regarding marriage, parenting, and close relationships. Male gender and earlier academic year were associated with lower adjusted knowledge scores, whereas field of study, epilepsy history, formal education, and recent training were not significantly associated with knowledge. CONCLUSION: Undergraduate health students demonstrated substantial knowledge gaps in women-specific epilepsy care, despite generally supportive attitudes. Reviewing curricular coverage and addressing identified gaps in reproductive health, medication safety, contraception, pregnancy-related care, and stigma may enhance preparedness for evidence-based, patient-centered care. The threshold-based logistic sensitivity analysis should be considered exploratory because of the low events-per-parameter ratio.
Exploratory analysis of cell-free miRNA expression changes in patients with isolated first seizure and new-onset epilepsy.推荐
PMID 43031603 · Frontiers in molecular biosciences · 2026 10 30
作者：Meng RC, Lesko A, Robinson F, Baraban E et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
摘要：One in ten individuals will experience a seizure in their lives, but only 43%-61% will be diagnosed with new-onset epilepsy (NOE). Existing methods to distinguish between a first unprovoked seizure and a diagnosis of epilepsy are inadequate and often lead to unnecessary treatment with antiseizure medications. To uncover potential targets associated with NOE, we analyzed cell-free circulating miRNA expression in plasma samples obtained from 20 patients who presented with a first unprovoked seizure. Patients were monitored for 2 years following their initial seizure, and a total of 7 patients experienced a second seizure within the follow-up period. Analysis of the miRNA expression levels revealed 15 differentially expressed miRNAs between patients who experienced only a first unprovoked seizure versus those who had a second seizure. These 15 miRNAs were experimentally linked as regulators to 160 genes related to epilepsy, including genes involved in the mTOR and WNT signaling pathways. These findings highlight a number of key circulating miRNA targets that may be further developed as a biomarker signature in larger cohorts and may offer insights into factors associated with epileptogenesis.
International Multicenter Retrospective Study on UBTF-Related Neurodevelopmental Disorder.浏览
PMID 43031596 · Neurology. Genetics · 2026 11 09
作者：Balletto G, Carli S, Coppola A, Torella A et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
摘要：BACKGROUND AND OBJECTIVES: UBTF encodes the upstream binding transcription factor (UBTF), a protein essential for rRNA synthesis. Dominantly acting pathogenic variants, either de novo or inherited, have been associated with a broad clinical childhood-onset neurodegenerative/neurodevelopmental spectrum whose genotype-phenotype correlation has not been accurately defined yet. This multicenter study aims to conduct a retrospective analysis of a large cohort of patients with pathogenic variants in the UBTF gene to refine diagnostic criteria and identify clinically relevant outcome measures for future trials. METHODS: Clinical centers provided clinical, molecular, and neuroimaging data on patients carrying pathogenic or predicted pathogenic UBTF variants, consistent with the clinical phenotype. Data were collected using an human phenotype ontology-based standardized electronic case report form and included unreported cases or updated information on patients reported in the literature from January 2017 to December 2024. RESULTS: A cohort of 49 patients (23 previously unreported) was included in the study. The disease typically presented at ≤3 years (89.6%) with neuroregression in 71.4% of cases, either in a child with preexisting neurodevelopmental delay (42.9%) or after previously normal psychomotor development (28.6%). Less frequently, neurodevelopmental delay occurred in isolation (26.5%). During disease progression, patients developed behavioral abnormalities (81.6%), movement disorders (78.3%), autonomic manifestations (50.0%), and epilepsy (34.7%). At the last follow-up (mean age 13 years and 6 months), only 2 patients were deceased (4.1%). The main neuroimaging findings included white matter hyperintensity (68.1%), cerebral (66.0%) and cerebellar (51.1%) atrophy, and thinning of the corpus callosum (38.3%). However, brain MRI findings were reported as normal in 21.3% of cases. We identified 7 novel UBTF variants (c.271G>C; c.349A>T; c.495C>A; c.517G>A; c.761A>G; c.1048-2A>G; and c.1625_1626+15del). Genotype-phenotype analysis led to the distinction of 2 allelic, clinically distinct disorders: a childhood-onset neurodegenerative disease due to gain-of-function variants, characterized by the triad of neuroregression, movement, and behavioral disorders, and a neurodevelopmental disorder with a milder clinical course associated with loss-of-function/hypomorphic variants. DISCUSSION: The results from our deep-phenotyping analysis suggest the classification of UBTF-related disease into 2 main clinical syndromes impacting the natural history of the disease with a different degree of severity and complexity. This novel observation should be considered in the management and prognosis of patients and will be fundamental in the design of targeted therapies.
Epilepsy at the Airport.推荐
PMID 43031431 · European journal of neurology · 2026 Nov
作者：Russell C, Choekyi T, Peña-Ceballos J, Moloney PB et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
摘要：BACKGROUND: Limited data exists on which people, including those with epilepsy, are at increased risk of seizures during air travel and the contribution of specific travel-related triggers. METHODS: We retrospectively reviewed attendances at our Epilepsy Rapid Access Clinic (RASC) over a 19-month period to identify patients presenting with seizures related to air travel. We also included patients identified by the epilepsy team who had not attended the clinic. RESULTS: Of 242 RASC attendees, nine patients (3.7%) had seizures associated with air travel. An additional nine patients were identified through other sources. All reported events were bilateral tonic-clonic seizures. Six (33%) patients had known epilepsy, of whom five had idiopathic generalised epilepsy (IGE). Four of these six patients had well-controlled epilepsy prior to the travel-related seizure relapse. Six (33%) patients were diagnosed with epilepsy after evaluation, including two with IGE. Twelve (67%) patients presented with seizures whilst awaiting departing flights, with a notable clustering around morning departures. Sleep deprivation was reported by 15 (83%) patients as a trigger. CONCLUSION: These findings suggest that patients with IGE may be at higher risk of seizures in these circumstances. The predominance of events in departure terminals, particularly before morning flights, highlights a potentially modifiable risk factor. Targeted counselling on modifiable risk factors may reduce risk and anxiety and should be incorporated into routine clinical care to support safer air travel. Thus, patients should be routinely educated about the potential of air travel as a seizure trigger.
Calcitonin gene-related peptide (CGRP) in epilepsy: Mechanistic insights from preclinical models.推荐
PMID 43031326 · Epilepsia open · 2026 11 13
作者：Romozzi M, Pani L, Benatti C, Vollono C et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：综述
摘要：Calcitonin gene-related peptide (CGRP) is a pleiotropic neuropeptide widely expressed in the central and peripheral nervous systems, with established roles in neurovascular regulation, synaptic transmission, and neuroinflammation. While CGRP has been extensively characterized in migraine pathophysiology, its role in epilepsy remains incompletely defined and is primarily supported by experimental evidence. Preclinical studies consistently demonstrate CGRP upregulation following prolonged seizures, particularly in models of status epilepticus, and suggest that CGRP signaling may contribute to key processes implicated in epileptogenesis. These include modulation of neuronal excitability, blood-brain barrier permeability, and neuroimmune responses, such as microglial activation. At the same time, CGRP has been shown to engage neuroprotective pathways, including anti-apoptotic and anti-oxidative mechanisms, as well as barrier-stabilizing effects, supporting a complex and context-dependent role. Shared pathophysiological features between migraine and epilepsy, such as cortical hyperexcitability, neuroinflammation, and cortical spreading depolarization, provide a conceptual framework in which CGRP may act as a convergent mechanistic mediator. However, direct evidence in human epilepsy remains limited, and the translational relevance of these findings requires careful interpretation. This review provides a focused synthesis of mechanistic insights from experimental models, highlighting the dual role of CGRP in seizure-related processes and delineating current gaps in knowledge. Potential clinical implications, including the effects of CGRP-targeting therapies, are discussed in a hypothesis-generating context, with emphasis on the need for rigorous studies to clarify their relevance in epilepsy. PLAIN LANGUAGE SUMMARY: Migraine and epilepsy sometimes occur together and share changes in brain activity. This review examines CGRP, a signaling molecule involved in migraine, and its possible role in seizures. Animal studies suggest that CGRP can either increase abnormal brain activity and inflammation or protect nerve cells, depending on the conditions. Early observations in people raise the possibility that migraine medicines blocking CGRP could also affect seizures, but they do not prove a benefit. Further research is needed to determine whether these medicines can safely reduce seizures or prevent epilepsy.
Predictors of Early Postoperative Seizures Following STA-MCA Bypass in Adult Moyamoya Angiopathy.推荐
PMID 43030592 · Journal of clinical medicine · 2026 10 30
作者：Ciocca R, Aboud N, Boccardi E, Sarmiento D et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
摘要：Background: Postoperative epileptic seizures are a recognized complication after superficial temporal artery-middle cerebral artery (STA-MCA) bypass for Moyamoya angiopathy (MMA), but their determinants remain incompletely understood. We aimed to identify clinical, radiological, and perioperative factors associated with early postoperative seizures following adult STA-MCA bypass. Methods: We retrospectively analyzed 75 hemispheric revascularization procedures performed in adult MMA patients between 2005 and 2023, with each operated hemisphere treated as an independent analytical unit. The primary outcome was the occurrence of at least one in-hospital postoperative seizure; the primary exposure was cerebral hyperperfusion syndrome. Univariate and multivariable Firth penalized-likelihood logistic regression was used to account for the small sample size and rare outcome. Results: Postoperative seizures occurred in 14 of 75 procedures (18.7%). Hyperperfusion was identified in 6 procedures (8.0%) and showed the strongest univariate association with seizures (OR 10.20), followed by diabetes mellitus (OR 6.05). Right-hemispheric procedures showed a protective effect in the univariate analysis (OR 0.24). In separate multivariate adjusted models, hyperperfusion (OR 10.28) and diabetes mellitus (OR 5.70) remained associated with postoperative seizures (OR 10.28 and OR 5.70, respectively). Pre-existing epilepsy, antiepileptic treatment, and preoperative EEG abnormalities were not associated with seizure risk. Conclusions: Early postoperative seizures after direct STA-MCA bypass in adult MMA were most consistently associated with postoperative cerebral hyperperfusion in our cohort, while pre-existing epilepsy and preoperative EEG abnormalities showed no statistically significant associations. Early recognition of hyperperfusion may support risk-stratified postoperative surveillance and individualized management.
Seizure Occurrence After Solid Organ Transplantation: A Systematic Review and Meta-Analysis.推荐
PMID 43030498 · Journal of clinical medicine · 2026 10 27
作者：Kang SJ, Lee RR, Jeon H, Rhew K
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：综述
摘要：Background/Objectives: Seizures are clinically important neurological complications after solid organ transplantation (SOT), but their reported frequency varies widely. We aimed to estimate the pooled seizure occurrence proportion after SOT and summarize reported seizure-related clinical factors. Methods: PubMed, Embase, and Web of Science were searched on 28 February 2026 for prospective or retrospective observational studies published in 2000-2025 that included SOT recipients and reported post-transplant seizures. No language restrictions were applied. Risk of bias was assessed using the Joanna Briggs Institute critical appraisal checklist for prevalence studies. Study-specific proportions were logit-transformed and pooled using random-effects models. Organ- and age-specific subgroup analyses, prediction intervals, sensitivity analyses, and assessments of small-study effects were performed. Results: Thirty-four reports representing 32 independent cohorts and 8816 recipients were included. The pooled seizure occurrence proportion was 5.3% (95% CI, 3.9-7.1%), with substantial heterogeneity (I2 = 87.0%) and a 95% prediction interval of 1.1-22.8%. Pooled seizure occurrence was higher in pediatric than adult cohorts (10.7% vs. 4.4%; between-group p = 0.011), although this study-level difference should not be interpreted as evidence of an independent effect of pediatric age. Commonly reported clinical contexts included calcineurin inhibitor-related neurotoxicity, metabolic or electrolyte disturbances, posterior reversible encephalopathy syndrome, cerebrovascular or structural brain injury, infection or systemic complications, and pre-existing neurological conditions. The evidence was limited by predominantly retrospective designs and heterogeneous seizure ascertainment and follow-up. Conclusions: The pooled study-specific seizure occurrence proportion after SOT was 5.3%, but occurrence varied substantially across populations and ascertainment periods, as reflected by the wide 95% prediction interval (1.1-22.8%). This estimate should not be interpreted as a uniform individual risk across transplant populations. Post-transplant seizures should prompt evaluation for potentially reversible and treatment-relevant causes. This review received no external funding and was not registered.
Nutraceutical, Nutritional and Phytotherapeutic Interventions for Neuropsychiatric Disorders Associated with Intellectual Disability: Mechanisms and Clinical Evidence.浏览
PMID 43030172 · Nutrients · 2026 10 27
作者：Cernigliaro F, Giliberti A, Nardello R
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：综述
摘要：Intellectual disability (ID) is frequently accompanied by a range of neuropsychiatric and neurological comorbidities, including anxiety, depression, autism spectrum disorder (ASD), attention-deficit/hyperactivity disorder (ADHD), sleep disturbances, and epilepsy. Conventional pharmacological treatments often provide partial relief and may be associated with adverse effects, prompting interest in adjunctive strategies such as phytotherapy. This review examines the current evidence on selected plant-derived compounds-particularly Crocus sativus (saffron), Bacopa monnieri, Curcuma longa (curcumin), and other botanicals-in the management of disorders associated with ID. Mechanistic pathways, clinical evidence, and safety considerations are critically discussed. Although some phytotherapeutic interventions have shown preliminary evidence of potential benefit and may modulate pathways involving neuroinflammation, oxidative stress, and neurotransmitter function, direct clinical evidence in individuals with ID remains limited. Further well-designed randomized controlled trials, particularly in ID-specific populations, are needed to establish efficacy, safety, optimal dosing, and clinical applicability.
An ESP32-S3 Multimodal Wearable Sensor Platform with Embedded XGBoost for Focal-to-Bilateral Tonic-Clonic Seizure Detection and IoT Alerting.推荐
PMID 43029509 · Sensors (Basel, Switzerland) · 2026 10 23
作者：Torad MA, Thabet MH, Taha MY, Torad AAM et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
摘要：Wearable seizure-detection systems can provide objective event monitoring and rapid caregiver alerting without continuous bedside observation. This study presents a resource-constrained multimodal wearable sensor platform for focal-to-bilateral tonic-clonic seizure (FBTCS) detection using edge artificial intelligence and Internet of Things (IoT) communication. The prototype integrates an ESP32-S3 microcontroller, an analog electromyography (EMG) channel, an MPU6050 inertial sensor, MAX30102 photoplethysmography, GPS, a TFT display, local vibration/buzzer outputs, and Blynk cloud telemetry. The seizure-decision path uses synchronized EMG and acceleration acquired at 100 Hz in non-overlapping 10 s windows. EMG and acceleration are resampled for compatibility with the reference feature pipeline, 13 statistical features are extracted from each modality (26 total), and an 85-tree XGBoost model is executed locally. A probability threshold of 0.85 is combined with temporal smoothing, consecutive-window confirmation, and a 60 s refractory interval. Software verification showed maximum absolute probability differences of approximately 1.19 × 10-7 for random feature-vector tests and 1.49 × 10-8 for end-to-end recorded windows relative to the Python reference. Within the reported validation record covering 2643.69 h and 18 record-labeled FBTCS events, 12 events were detected (12/18; 67% sensitivity). Precision was approximately 26%, F1-score approximately 38%, the reported false-alarm rate was 0.31 per 24 h, and median detection latency was 61.5 s. Approximately 34 false alarms are estimated from the reported false-alarm rate and evaluation duration; this is an estimated count rather than a percentage or directly observed count. Hardware, local alarm, display, GPS, and cloud communication functions passed functional integration tests. The small event count, uncertain label provenance, low precision, and long latency mean that these data support an engineering proof-of-concept only and do not establish clinical applicability.
Temporal Patterns of Serum Neuroprotectin D1 and Resolvin D1 After Motor and Nonmotor Epileptic Seizures: A Prospective Repeated-Measures Study.推荐
PMID 43028577 · Diagnostics (Basel, Switzerland) · 2026 11 09
作者：Sengul A, Tasci I, Berilgen Gurgoze A, Albayrak S et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
摘要：Objective: Neuroinflammatory activation is well recognized in epilepsy, whereas the temporal behavior of specialized pro-resolving mediator (SPM) responses after seizures remains poorly defined. We evaluated changes in serum neuroprotectin D1 (NP-1) and resolvin D1 (RvD1) between the early postictal period and a seizure-free period of at least 1 month and compared both time points with healthy controls. Methods: This prospective controlled repeated-measures study included 48 adults with epilepsy and 50 healthy controls. Blood was obtained within 3 h after a seizure and again after at least 1 month of seizure freedom; controls provided one sample. Serum NP-1 and resolvin D1 (RvD1) were measured using commercial enzyme-linked immunosorbent assay (ELISA) kits. The prespecified primary analysis compared paired postictal and seizure-free measurements using the Wilcoxon signed-rank test. Patient-control comparisons were considered secondary/exploratory. Results: Median NP-1 increased from 50.01 ng/mL (IQR 3.58-98.62) postictally to 67.78 ng/mL (59.81-121.81) during the seizure-free period (Z = -5.333, p < 0.001, r = 0.770). RvD1 did not change significantly (326.67 [207.77-899.95] vs. 441.47 [223.67-710.81] pg/mL; p = 0.139, r = 0.215). Both mediators were lower than controls postictally (both p < 0.001). During seizure freedom, NP-1 and RvD1 remained lower than controls (p = 0.008 and p < 0.001, respectively). The absolute percentage difference from the control median was greater for RvD1 than for NP-1 during seizure freedom (60.74% vs. 42.84%; p = 0.001, r = 0.462). Conclusions: The pro-resolving response in epilepsy appears temporally heterogeneous. NP-1 showed substantial within-patient recovery, whereas Resolvin D1 remained persistently reduced. These findings are hypothesis-generating and support larger serial-sampling studies with targeted lipidomic validation rather than establishing either mediator as a diagnostic biomarker.
A TLR4-Associated Microglia-Endothelial Inflammatory Axis in Drug-Resistant Temporal Lobe Epilepsy: Integrated Transcriptomic and Genetic Evidence.推荐
PMID 43028205 · International journal of molecular sciences · 2026 11 06
作者：Liu Z, Yin J
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
摘要：Microglia-driven neuroinflammation contributes to epileptogenesis and drug resistance in temporal lobe epilepsy (TLE), but the cellular organization of the neuroinflammatory hub and its coupling to the cerebrovascular endothelium remain unresolved. We performed a retrospective secondary analysis of publicly available datasets, integrating paired hippocampal bulk transcriptomics (GSE134697), an integrated temporal-lobe single-cell/nucleus RNA sequencing (sc/snRNA-seq) atlas (9 patients with drug-resistant TLE and 4 controls), two-sample Mendelian randomization (MR) and summary-based Mendelian randomization (SMR), and independent hippocampal (n = 77) and peripheral blood (n = 141) cohorts. Five candidate neuroimmune hub genes (TGFB1, ICAM1, TLR4, TLR2 and CCL2) were prioritized using a prespecified, threshold-dependent workflow and mapped to a microglia-cerebrovascular endothelium-peripheral myeloid cell axis. sc/snRNA-seq identified an activated endothelial subpopulation, detected only in TLE samples, and expansion of a microglial compartment with disease-associated microglia (DAM)-like features; CellChat and MultiNicheNet prioritized microglia-derived SPP1, C1QA and MIF as candidate mediators of endothelial inflammatory activation via S1PR1/ITGB1, CD93 and CD74. Plasma kynurenine showed a nominally significant association with focal epilepsy in MR (inverse-variance weighted [IVW] OR = 1.96, 95% CI 1.10-3.52, p = 0.024), whereas SMR did not detect genetically predicted effects of TLR4 or TLR2 expression on focal epilepsy risk; these findings do not establish an acquired disease mechanism. A TGFB1 + TLR4 two-gene signature showed exploratory discrimination in an independent hippocampal cohort (area under the receiver operating characteristic curve [AUC] = 0.868), and blood TLR4 was selectively elevated in drug-naïve newly diagnosed patients (AUC = 0.684). Targeted gene set enrichment analysis (GSEA) and molecular docking nominated simvastatin as a repurposing candidate predicted computationally to interact with MD-2; this prediction has not been functionally validated. This multi-cohort study identifies a TLR4-associated microglia-endothelial inflammatory axis in drug-resistant TLE and generates hypotheses for prospective biomarker studies and experimental drug-repurposing research.
One Disorder, Two Brains: Challenges in Modeling Epilepsy and Associated Movement Disorders in Mice.推荐
PMID 43028160 · International journal of molecular sciences · 2026 11 05
作者：Travin DI, Anisimova PE, Bogovarova KA, Kustova AO et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：综述
摘要：Modeling epileptic syndromes co-occurring with movement disorders in mice often fails to fully recapitulate human phenotypes due to distinct gene-specific and anatomical mechanisms. This review categorizes these translational barriers into three primary groups. First, ortholog heterotopia and heterochrony drive spatiotemporal shifts in gene expression, causing orthologs to activate at disparate developmental stages or within different cell populations across species. Second, molecular redundancy and compensation obscure phenotypes through the upregulation of alternative proteins in response to target protein suppression, the activation of modifier genes, or neural network rewiring. Third, structural variations in striatal anatomy fundamentally alter corticostriatal loop functioning. Characterizing these divergent mechanisms is essential for improving the predictive validity of preclinical models and advancing targeted therapies.
Biphasic Modulation of Absence Seizures by Lisuride in GAERS: Dissecting the Role of 5-HT2A Receptors.推荐
PMID 43028060 · International journal of molecular sciences · 2026 11 01
作者：Venzi M, Crunelli V, Di Giovanni G
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
摘要：Serotonergic signalling modulates absence seizures, and activation of 5-HT2A receptors with TCB-2 suppresses spike-and-wave discharges in Genetic Absence Epilepsy Rats from Strasbourg (GAERS). We investigated the effects of lisuride, a serotonergic and dopaminergic ligand with low 5-HT2A receptor signalling efficacy, and examined the contribution of these receptors. Spontaneous seizures were recorded in freely moving male GAERS following systemic administration of lisuride (0.1 or 0.5 mg/kg), with or without pretreatment with the 5-HT2A receptor antagonist MDL11,939. Low-dose lisuride produced a modest pro-absence effect, whereas the higher dose produced a biphasic response: early seizure suppression followed by increased seizure activity. MDL11,939 did not abolish the early suppression, but the late increase observed with lisuride alone was not observed following pretreatment. These findings suggest that the early and late responses involve different mechanisms, with 5-HT2A receptors potentially contributing to the later phase. Lisuride's effects differ from those reported for the potent 5-HT2A receptor TCB-2, consistent with its broader receptor profile. Further work is needed to determine the contribution of its other targets, including dopaminergic receptors.
Familial Transmission and Phenotypic Heterogeneity in Germline GATOR1-Related Epilepsy: A Systematic Review and Meta-Analysis.推荐
PMID 43028020 · International journal of molecular sciences · 2026 10 30
作者：Li L, Bao Y, Huo L, Ji X et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：Meta分析
摘要：Incomplete penetrance and variable expressivity complicate interpretation of germline GATOR1 variants and individual-level phenotype prediction. We conducted a family-based systematic review and meta-analysis, reconstructing individual-level data from published pedigrees and reassessing variants using GATOR1-specific classification criteria. Sixty-three studies were included; 59 contributed 734 individuals heterozygous for germline pathogenic or likely pathogenic (P/LP) variants from 227 families. The pooled proportion of affected individuals was 65.7%. Recurrent variant status, variant consequence, DEPDC5 protein domain, transmitting-parent phenotype, and overall parental origin did not consistently distinguish affected from unaffected individuals, although paternal DEPDC5 transmission showed an exploratory association with higher disease expression (90.4% vs. 82.4%, p = 0.032). Among affected individuals, missense variants were associated with earlier seizure onset than loss-of-function (LoF) variants (0.67 vs. 4.50 years, p = 0.002). LoF variants in the DEPDC5 LassoA domain showed exploratory associations with cognitive impairment, MRI abnormalities, and epilepsy surgery (FDR-adjusted p ≤ 0.046). An expanded synthesis identified 14 published same-gene germline-somatic second-hit cases, of which 85.7% had focal cortical dysplasia (FCD) IIa; quantifiable somatic variant allele fractions (VAFs) for sequence-variant second hits were below 5% in all six single-region cases, whereas multiregion sampling in three cases showed spatial variation, including values below 1%. These findings suggest that disease expression and post-onset phenotype represent partly distinct aspects of clinical heterogeneity in GATOR1-related epilepsy.
Impaired Mitochondrial Translation and Bioenergetic Dysfunction Associated with the MT-RNR1 m.1014A>G Variant.推荐
PMID 43027763 · International journal of molecular sciences · 2026 10 24
作者：Marrone I, Ura B, Celsi F, Zupin L et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：病例报告
摘要：Pathogenic variants in MT-RNR1 are classically associated with aminoglycoside-induced sensorineural hearing loss through disruption of the mitochondrial 12S rRNA decoding center. Their contribution to pediatric neurological disease remains poorly defined. We identified a novel homoplasmic MT-RNR1 variant, m.1014A>G, in a patient with drug-resistant focal epilepsy beginning at 9 months of age who remained undiagnosed after trio whole-exome sequencing filtered using an extensive epilepsy gene panel. The variant was also detected in her mother. Structural modeling suggests that the variant, affecting a highly conserved region of the mitochondrial 12S rRNA, may alter the mitochondrial small ribosomal subunit assembly. To investigate its pathogenicity, we performed comprehensive functional studies in patient-derived fibroblasts. Unlike canonical MT-RNR1 variants, m.1014A>G did not confer aminoglycoside toxicity. Functional in vitro studies on patients' fibroblasts demonstrated increased 12S/16S rRNA ratios, reduced abundance of both mitoribosomal subunit proteins, decreased levels of mitochondrially encoded respiratory-chain subunits, and impaired oxidative phosphorylation. These abnormalities were accompanied by mitochondrial network fragmentation, increased mitochondria-lysosome colocalization suggestive of enhanced mitophagy and reduced mitochondrial DNA copy number. Altogether, the findings are consistent with defective mitoribosome assembly and altered mitochondrial translation, resulting in a bioenergetic defect. Our study points to a previously unrecognized candidate pathogenic mechanism associated with MT-RNR1 and may expand the phenotypic spectrum of MT-RNR1-related disorders to include maternally inherited infantile-onset epileptic encephalopathy. These findings highlight the importance of dedicated mitochondrial DNA analysis in children with unexplained early-onset epilepsy despite negative nuclear genomic testing.
Genetic Variations in Neuroinflammatory Chemokine Receptor Genes in Patients with Epilepsy: An Exploratory Study.推荐
PMID 43027722 · International journal of molecular sciences · 2026 10 23
作者：Pekkoc-Uyanik KC, Agay ER, Uzun H
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
摘要：Epilepsy is a common neurological disorder in which genetic and neuroinflammatory mechanisms may contribute to differences in seizure control. Chemokine receptor signaling plays an important role in inflammatory processes, and genetic variations in neuroinflammatory chemokine receptor genes may potentially influence the prognosis of epilepsy. This study aimed to investigate genetic variations in the neuroinflammatory chemokine receptor genes CCR5 and CXCR4 in patients with epilepsy and to explore their potential association with seizure control during treatment. Targeted next-generation sequencing (tNGS) was performed to investigate genetic variations in the CCR5 and CXCR4 genes using genomic DNA obtained from 22 patients with epilepsy receiving antiseizure medication (ASM) treatment with levetiracetam and/or valproic acid. Patients were classified into two groups according to seizure frequency to evaluate the association between genetic variation and clinical prognosis. A total of seven genetic variants were identified in the CCR5 and CXCR4 genes, including three variants in CCR5 and four variants in CXCR4. However, sufficient genotype variation for group-wise comparisons was available only for CCR5 rs1799988 and rs1800023. No significant differences were observed in the genotype or allele distributions of the CCR5 and CXCR4 gene variants between the responsive and persistent groups. The two CCR5 variants, rs1799988 and rs1800023, showed strong linkage disequilibrium (D' = 1.000, r2 = 0.913) and were included in a single haplotype block. None of the identified CCR5 haplotypes was significantly associated with seizure control. In this preliminary cohort, no statistically significant associations were detected between the analyzed genetic variations and seizure control during treatment; however, the limited sample size may have reduced the statistical power to detect potential associations. Strong linkage disequilibrium was observed between rs1799988 and rs1800023 (D' = 1.000, r2 = 0.913), consistent with previously reported patterns. Given the small sample size and the limited number of informative variants, these findings should be interpreted as preliminary and hypothesis-generating rather than as evidence for or against a genetic contribution to seizure control. Larger, independent cohorts are needed to assess the potential clinical relevance of CCR5 and CXCR4 variation in epilepsy.
Antisense Oligonucleotidesin Genetic Epilepsies and Developmental and Epileptic Encephalopathies: Molecular Mechanisms, Translational Evidence and Disease-Modifying Potential.推荐
PMID 43027021 · Biology · 2026 11 05
作者：Leo A, Roberti R, Vono PF, Rajone V et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：综述
摘要：In genetic developmental and epileptic encephalopathies, disability results from both the molecular defect and epileptic activity. Antiseizure medications modulate pathways involved in seizure initiation and propagation but generally do not correct the disease-causing molecular alterations that contribute to epileptic susceptibility and neurodevelopmental dysfunction. Antisense oligonucleotides (ASOs) modify the splicing of ribonucleic acid (RNA) transcripts, promote RNA degradation, or reactivate silenced alleles. This narrative review examines variant-guided mechanisms, translational evidence, clinical outcomes, and barriers to implementation. ASO programs targeting SCN1A, SCN2A, SCN8A, KCNT1, GNAO1, and UBE3A range from individualized treatments to phase 3 trials. Published uncontrolled zorevunersen studies report preliminary clinical signals in Dravet syndrome, whereas sponsor-reported results from the randomized, sham-controlled EMBRAVE Part A study suggest seizure reduction with elsunersen in SCN2A-related disease. Conversely, sponsor-reported phase 3 Aspire topline results indicate that apazunersen did not meet its primary cognitive or key secondary multidomain endpoint in Angelman syndrome. These findings are not equivalent in evidentiary strength. Intrathecal delivery, heterogeneous central nervous system (CNS) exposure, target-dosage sensitivity, and compound-specific toxicity influence the therapeutic window. Disease modification requires controlled longitudinal demonstration of meaningful outcomes beyond target engagement or seizure reduction, considering development, adaptive functioning, survival, and treatment burden.

摘要：BACKGROUND: Tuberous sclerosis complex (TSC) frequently causes epilepsy, but cell-type-specific molecular alterations in human lesions remain incompletely understood. Spreading Depolarization after Traumatic Brain Injury and Subdural Hematoma: A Context-Specific Framework for Recognition and Clinical Interpretation without Electrocorticography.推荐
PMID 43026402 · Neurocritical care · 2026 11 13
作者：Oda K, Grubb K, Saluja RS, Marcoux J
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：综述
摘要：Spreading depolarization (SD) is a near-complete breakdown of transmembrane ion gradients that propagates through gray matter and imposes substantial metabolic stress. Direct subdural electrocorticography (ECoG) has established SD as a common and prognostically relevant secondary injury process in a subset of patients with severe traumatic brain injury (TBI), whereas a smaller body of literature implicates SD in recurrent transient neurological symptoms after subdural hematoma (SDH), particularly chronic SDH (cSDH) and after hematoma evacuation. These populations should not, however, be treated as a single clinical syndrome. In this narrative review, we distinguish three context-specific evidence domains: (1) directly monitored SD in severe or surgical TBI, (2) SD as a plausible contributor to recurrent stereotyped focal symptoms after cSDH/SDH evacuation, and (3) imaging correlates of prior SD-associated spreading ischemia. We emphasize that routine scalp electroencephalogram (EEG) does not diagnose SD in patients with intact skull, that seizures and SD can coexist, and that cortical-predominant or laminar ischemic injury on magnetic resonance imaging (MRI) can support but cannot establish active SD. We propose a context-specific clinical approach for centers without ECoG that focuses on recognizing when SD should enter the differential, defines what conventional EEG and imaging can and cannot establish, and identifies situations in which direct monitoring may be valuable. Standard physiological optimization remains foundational in severe acute brain injury but is not an SD-specific treatment recommendation. Ketamine can suppress ECoG-confirmed SD, whereas evidence for nimodipine, memantine, topiramate, and other candidate therapies remains context dependent or investigational. Prospective validation is required before unmonitored clinical patterns are used as treatment triggers.

Role of AngioVac in the Management of Severe MRSA Tricuspid Endocarditis With Multisystem Complications.浏览
PMID 43025845 · Journal of investigative medicine high impact case reports · 2026 11 13
作者：Fox JP, Jagra AS, Touza M, Tewoldemedhin N et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：病例报告
摘要：Right-sided infective endocarditis (IE) caused by methicillin-resistant Staphylococcus aureus (MRSA) in people who inject drugs often causes septic pulmonary emboli and critical illness, and many of these patients are too unstable for cardiac surgery when they present. We report a 38-year-old woman with active intravenous drug use, untreated hepatitis C, and a seizure disorder who presented with MRSA bacteremia, a large tricuspid valve vegetation, and cavitary septic pulmonary emboli complicated by respiratory failure, shock, and empyema. Despite 33 days of antibiotics, she remained febrile with progressive pulmonary complications, and cardiothoracic surgery judged her too high risk for valve replacement. She underwent percutaneous vegetation debulking with the AngioVac system through femoral venous access, with removal of approximately 80% of the vegetation and no procedural complications. Her subsequent course included massive hemoptysis with cardiac arrest, bronchial artery embolization, video-assisted thoracoscopic decortication, tracheostomy, and gastrostomy, after which she stabilized and was discharged to a long-term acute care hospital. Approximately 3 months later, she underwent tricuspid valve replacement and survived the operation. This case shows how percutaneous aspiration can serve as a bridge to definitive valve surgery in critically ill patients with right-sided IE who are not initial surgical candidates, and it highlights the multidisciplinary care these patients need.
Serum Cytokine Concentrations in Dogs with Idiopathic and Structural Epilepsy: An Exploratory Cross-Sectional Study.推荐

摘要：Neuroimmune mechanisms may contribute to epilepsy but circulating cytokine patterns in dogs with distinct clinical classifications of epilepsy remain incompletely characterized. This exploratory cross-sectional study compared serum concentrations of seven cytokines associated with Th1, Th2, Th17, inflammatory, and regulatory immune responses among healthy dogs and dogs clinically classified as having idiopathic epilepsy (IE) or structural epilepsy (SE). Twenty-eight dogs were included: health controls (n = 11), IE (n = 10), and SE (n = 7). Establishing a functional standard for hiPSC-neural seizure liability MEA assays.推荐
PMID 43025660 · Neurotoxicology · 2026 11 13
作者：Clements M, Blinova K, Cato M, Chaudhary KW et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：综述
摘要：The increasing emphasis on New Approach Methodologies (NAMs) to improve the human relevance of safety assessments and reduce reliance on vertebrate animal testing has driven growing interest in in vitro models for neurotoxicity evaluation. The multielectrode array (MEA) assay using human induced pluripotent stem cell (hiPSC)-derived neural cultures has emerged as a promising platform for early detection of drug-induced seizure liability. However, a lack of standardized experimental practices and functional acceptance criteria has limited cross-study comparability and hindered broader adoption of the assay. Here, we define a functional framework for the hiPSC-neural seizure liability MEA assay by integrating evidence from 11 published studies with insights from 11 laboratories with extensive assay experience. This approach is informed by comparison with the analogous hiPSC-cardiomyocyte proarrhythmia MEA assay, which was standardized through the Comprehensive in vitro Proarrhythmia Assay (CiPA) initiative. We identify critical assay variables that influence spontaneous neural activity and pharmacological responsiveness and propose a practical protocol checklist to improve reproducibility and reporting transparency. Building on this analysis, we introduce a functional cell standard for hiPSC-neural models, based on defined baseline activity thresholds and response to the reference proconvulsant, picrotoxin. Key functional readouts include mean firing rate, network burst dynamics, and synchrony, which collectively reflect intrinsic network excitability and coordination. Although in vitro neural networks do not fully recapitulate in vivo brain physiology, the MEA assay detects network-level electrophysiological correlates of seizure-like activity and provides a scalable, human-relevant approach for early hazard identification. Collectively, this work establishes a pragmatic, evidence-based framework to support standardization of the hiPSC-neural seizure liability MEA assay and to facilitate its broader adoption as a NAM in safety pharmacology.

命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）

摘要：OBJECTIVE: High prevalence of onchocerciasis and epilepsy prompted a shift from annual to biannual community-directed treatment with ivermectin (CDTI). Expanded repeat RNAs drive aberrant cortical network activity in benign adult familial myoclonus epilepsy.推荐

摘要：Expansions of short nucleotide repeats cause diverse neurological disorders, yet how they perturb neuronal function remains unclear. Benign adult familial myoclonus epilepsy (BAFME) is caused by intronic pentanucleotide repeat expansions in genes including SAMD12. Here, we establish patient-derived cortical organoids and a microelectrode array platform, coupled with a frequency-decomposition-based analytical framework, to characterize BAFME-associated neuronal hyperexcitability and compare organoid electrophysiological features with those observed in patient electroencephalographic recordings. Chemical variability and emerging trends in pink cocaine in Italy: A forensic perspective on a complex drug mixture.推荐
PMID 43025270 · Forensic science international · 2026 11 11
作者：Vernich F, Mineo F, Romani L, Stefani L et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：病例报告
摘要："Pink cocaine", also known as "tusi", is an illicit drug mixture whose chemical composition is highly variable and often differs from that suggested by its street name. Although ketamine and 3,4-methylenedioxymethamphetamine (MDMA) are frequently identified, 2 C compounds are generally absent from seized samples. In this study, four seizures of "pink cocaine" recovered by the Italian Police Forces between February 2024 and July 2025 were investigated by gas chromatography coupled with a flame ionisation detector (GC-FID) and gas chromatography-mass spectrometry (GC-MS) to characterise their qualitative and quantitative composition. The classification of the seized powders as "pink cocaine" was based on information provided by the Italian Police Forces. All samples (N = 6) contained ketamine and MDMA, with concentrations ranging from 11.0% to 56.3% and from 2.8% to 27.3%, respectively. Caffeine was detected in five samples, while ephedrine and 1-(2-chloro-N-methylbenzimidoyl)cyclopentanol, a ketamine synthesis precursor, were identified in one sample. Notably, 2C-B was detected in two independent samples, whereas cocaine was not detected in any seizure. These findings confirm the marked heterogeneity of products marketed as "pink cocaine" and demonstrate that their composition may differ substantially both in the relative concentrations of the main psychoactive constituents and in the presence of additional substances. The detection of 2C-B in two seizures provides further evidence of its occurrence in "pink cocaine" formulations circulating in Europe, despite its limited detection in previous forensic investigations. Continued monitoring of these products is warranted to identify emerging compositional patterns and assess the toxicological risks associated with their unpredictable composition.
Subjective memory complaints and depressive mood in patients with epilepsy at the time of first seizure and one year later.推荐
PMID 43025183 · Epilepsy & behavior : E&B · 2026 11 13
作者：Mueller CJ, Strehlau S, Weil J, Menzler K et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
摘要：Patients with epilepsy (PWE) often report subjective memory complaints (SMC) that reportedly do not thoroughly relate to 'objective' memory measures, but to depressive symptoms. While depressive symptoms appear to be involved very early in the disease, it is relatively unclear when SMC develop over the course of the illness. We examined two patient groups, namely PWE and patients, who had a first seizure (seizure patients, SP), but did not subsequently get the diagnosis epilepsy regarding factors influencing SMC scores at the time of the first seizure as well as one year later using regression analyses. These factors include a depressive symptom score, demographic and clinical variables. Additionally, we investigate the development of SMC and depressive symptoms over the time span of the first year after first seizure in both patient groups using analyses of variance (ANOVA). Regression analyses results revealed that depressive symptoms predicted subjective memory complaints at both time points. Group, based on diagnosed illness, did not predict an additional significant amount of variance. ANOVA results regarding SMC scores revealed that there were no systematic differences between patient groups and no significant development throughout the first year after first seizure. Results on depressive symptoms showed that PWE reported overall more depressive symptoms than SP. These findings hint towards the importance of monitoring depressive symptoms in epilepsy not only in patients who have had epilepsy for a long time, but also in newly diagnosed patients. Future studies are needed to investigate the beginning of SMC in epilepsy.
From potential to practice: An International survey on technology adoption and needs in epilepsy neuropsychology.推荐
PMID 43025182 · Epilepsy & behavior : E&B · 2026 11 13
作者：Rayner G, Busch RM, Dollman A, Eyres J et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
摘要：BACKGROUND: Technological integration offers opportunities to improve neuropsychological practice in epilepsy care. Compared to other healthcare fields, neuropsychology has been relatively slow to incorporate these advancements into clinical practice. OBJECTIVES: To describe the use of technology in epilepsy neuropsychology and identify priorities for technological development. METHODS: An online survey of multiple choice and open-ended questions was developed in English and translated into Spanish, then sent to practicing neuropsychologists working in epilepsy care across all six International League Against Epilepsy world regions. The survey collected data on the settings in which neuropsychological services are provided to people with epilepsy, the technologies currently in use, the barriers to technological adoption, and desired future developments. RESULTS: 179 neuropsychologists from 39 countries participated in the survey. The majority of respondents were women (69 %), aged 30-49 (60 %), and held a PhD (60 %). Key barriers to technological adoption included concerns around tool validation, financial limitations to purchase existing tools, and a lack of culturally and linguistically relevant normative data. Priorities for future development included predictive algorithms, automating psychometric aspects of clinical practice, and better leveraging of functional neuroimaging technologies. CONCLUSIONS: This international survey highlights that while epilepsy neuropsychologists are open to adopting new technologies in their clinical practice, there are several barriers hampering uptake across the field. Survey responses highlighted that technological developments that are practical, accessible, evidence-based, and suitable for culturally and linguistically diverse populations are required to meet the needs of the epilepsy neuropsychology workforce.
Psychological interventions for functional/dissociative seizures: A systematic review.推荐
PMID 43025181 · Epilepsy & behavior : E&B · 2026 11 13
作者：Hawkes S, Koychev I, Gallacher J, Edwards M
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：综述
摘要：OBJECTIVE: Functional/dissociative seizures (FDS) have limited treatment options despite the major societal and personal impact of this condition. The efficacy of psychological interventions varies considerably and is influenced by social, economic, and personal factors. To guide future research on the condition, a systematic review of the published literature on the psychological treatments available for FDS was conducted. Current psychological interventions including diagnosis communication, psychoeducation, psychotherapy, hypnosis, and behavioural therapies have yet to show sufficient evidence of long-lasting successful treatment for FDS. The frequency and impact of FDS requires continued treatment development beyond the limited options currently available. Such treatments have been systematically reviewed for this purpose. METHODS: A comprehensive literature search was carried out using the keywords: dissociative seizures, functional seizures, NEA, non-epileptic, pseudoseizures, psychogenic seizures, psychological treatment, hysterical seizures, and pseudoepileptic. Studies evaluating psychological treatments for FDS were included. Studies including patients with comorbid seizure disorders were also included. RESULTS: A total of 55 studies (58 publications) met the inclusion criteria. A wide variety of psychological interventions for FDS have been investigated. Existing evidence suggests that several psychological interventions may be effective, with cognitive behavioural therapy demonstrating the strongest evidence base. Promising findings were also reported for diagnosis communication, psychoeducation, psychodynamic therapy, mindfulness-based therapy, behavioural therapy, and combined treatment approaches. CONCLUSION: While a range of psychological treatments may be beneficial, cognitive behavioural therapy currently possesses the strongest evidence supporting its efficacy. However, no clear evidence suggests whether a particular intervention is most appropriate for specific patient subgroups. Structured diagnosis communication and psychoeducation appear to be valuable early interventions but may require additional psychological therapy to maintain treatment gains. Large studies of high methodological quality with longer follow-up periods are required to establish the most effective forms of treatment.
Management of Prenatally Diagnosed Midline Brain Malformations: A Practice Survey of Fetal Neurologists From the United States.浏览
PMID 43025030 · Pediatric neurology · 2026 10 26
作者：Chirigos A, Agarwal S, Ostrander B, Young M et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
摘要：BACKGROUND: Supratentorial midline brain malformations (MBMs) comprise one third of fetal neurology consultations. MBMs include absent septum pellucidum, corpus callosum abnormalities, and holoprosencephaly. Outcomes include endocrine (pituitary dysfunction) and ophthalmologic (optic nerve hypoplasia) complications, epilepsy, and neurodevelopmental impairment. In 2025, management recommendations for prenatally diagnosed MBMs were published. Our study characterized care of these infants. METHODS: We distributed a REDCap survey in April 2024 to US-based pediatric neurologists regarding care of infants with prenatally diagnosed MBMs. RESULTS: Twenty surveys were completed from 15 institutions across the United States. Most (19/20, 95%) perform multidisciplinary fetal neurology consultations. MBM complexity affected delivery location (11/20, 55%) and postnatal imaging recommendations (15/20, 75%). All pursue endocrine evaluation for infants with certain MBMs, and 14/20 (70%) recommend unequivocal endocrine evaluation. Most (19/20, 95%) recommend ophthalmologic evaluation for certain infants (86% refer for holoprosencephaly, 71% for complex MBMs, and 57% for isolated absent septum pellucidum or isolated absence of the corpus callosum in female infants), and 12/20 (60%) recommend universal ophthalmology referral. Developmental monitoring through pediatric neurology clinic and early intervention (EI) were broadly recommended. While many of these recommendations align with expert consensus guidelines, notable differences include the approach to endocrinology evaluation for isolated MBMs, use of cranial ultrasound, and EI referral practices. CONCLUSIONS: Patients with prenatally identified MBMs require a multidisciplinary approach, including endocrine and ophthalmologic evaluations and developmental monitoring, to optimize neurodevelopmental outcomes and facilitate early diagnosis of complications. This survey revealed variability in management and deviations from expert consensus guidelines, highlighting current gaps in care.

Intravenous Lacosamide in Pediatric Epilepsy: Experience From a High-Complexity Center in Bogotá, Colombia.推荐
PMID 43025028 · Pediatric neurology · 2026 10 21
作者：Penagos-Vargas NE, Montoya-Ossa D, Espitia Segura OM, Farfán-Albarracín JD
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
摘要：BACKGROUND: To evaluate the efficacy and safety of intravenous lacosamide (IV LCM) in pediatric patients with epilepsy. METHODS: A retrospective descriptive cohort study was conducted in patients younger than 18 years hospitalized at HOMI Fundación Hospital Pediátrico La Misericordia (Bogotá, Colombia) between November 2018 and January 2023. Patients treated with IV LCM were included, excluding those with status epilepticus or seizure clusters (subject of a separate study and analysis). Sociodemographic variables, epilepsy characteristics, medication use, seizure control, and adverse effects were analyzed descriptively. RESULTS: A total of 140 patients were included (median age: 10.9 years; 53.6% male). Most patients had focal epilepsy (89.3%), with structural etiology being the most common cause (44.3%). A total of 68.6% of patients showed a greater than 50% reduction in seizure frequency, 30% had no change in seizure frequency, and 1.4% experienced worsening. Favorable responses were observed in focal epilepsy (71.2%) and in structural etiology (43/62; 69.4%). Adverse effects occurred in 2.9% of cases, mainly sinus bradycardia and nausea. No significant changes were observed in hemodynamic, renal, or hepatic parameters after treatment. CONCLUSIONS: This study suggests that IV LCM is an effective and safe option for the management of uncontrolled or drug-resistant epilepsy in patients who were temporarily unable to take oral medications and were already receiving the medication, as well as an add-on therapy in patients with uncontrolled epilepsy.
Safety of responsive neurostimulation in pediatric and young adult patients with drug-resistant epilepsy: a systematic review and meta-analysis.推荐
PMID 43024880 · Journal of neurosurgery. Pediatrics · 2026 11 13
作者：Perez-Chadid DA, Valdivia D, Azmi A, Wijaya JH et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
摘要：OBJECTIVE: Responsive neurostimulation (RNS) is increasingly being used for pediatric drug-resistant epilepsy (DRE), but the safety of the procedure in children remains unclear. The authors systematically reviewed published clinical outcomes in pediatric RNS, with an emphasis on complications and other adverse events. METHODS: The authors performed a PRISMA-guided systematic review and meta-analysis. PubMed, Embase, Web of Science, and the Cochrane Library were searched from inception through August 12, 2025. They included observational studies reporting outcomes of RNS in patients aged ≤ 21 years at implantation and extractable pediatric data. Two authors independently screened articles and extracted data. The primary outcome was the proportion of patients experiencing at least 1 complication. A sensitivity analysis restricted to extractable patients aged ≤ 18 years was also performed. RESULTS: Thirty-five studies met inclusion criteria, representing 358 pediatric patients who underwent RNS implantation. Most studies were case series (62.9%), followed by case reports (25.7%) and cohort studies (11.4%); all were conducted in the United States. The mean patient age at implantation was 14.8 years, and the mean follow-up was 20.3 months among studies reporting follow-up. Overall, 38 patients experienced at least 1 complication. The most frequently reported complications were infection (3.4%), transient neurological deficits (3.1%), return to the operating room (2.2%), and explantation (2.0%). Less common events included stimulation-related side effects (1.4%), CSF-related complications (1.1%), and wound dehiscence and device malfunction (0.6% each). Random-effects meta-analysis demonstrated a pooled overall complication proportion of 10.3% (95% CI 7.0%-14.9%; I2 = 0.0%). In the ≤ 18-year sensitivity analysis, 31 studies comprising 203 patients were included, with a pooled complication proportion of 8% (95% CI 4%-16%). CONCLUSIONS: Published pediatric RNS experience suggests that complications are comparable to rates reported for similar procedures and are most often transient neurological symptoms, reoperation, or infection. However, the evidence base is largely limited to small observational reports with heterogeneous outcome definitions and high risk of bias. Higher-quality multicenter prospective studies with standardized reporting and longer follow-up are needed to better define safety and effectiveness of RNS in pediatric DRE. Systematic review registration no.: CRD420261339088 (https://www.crd.york.ac.uk/prospero/).
Developmental Aspects of Language Mapping with Stereoelectroencephalography.推荐
PMID 43024735 · Journal of clinical neurophysiology : official publication of the American Electroencephalographic Society · 2026 11 13
作者：Bearden DJ, Arya R
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
摘要：This review examines developmental perspectives on the use of stereoelectroencephalography (SEEG) for delineating language areas and networks in pediatric patients with drug-resistant epilepsy, both as a clinical tool for presurgical evaluation and as a modality for gaining neuroscientific insights into speech and language development. Language development is a dynamic process that begins before birth and continues into adulthood, shaped by the maturation of brain networks. SEEG allows high-resolution study of these networks, providing a unique perspective on the organization of language systems. This review traces language acquisition from early infancy through adolescence and adulthood, highlighting the pivotal role of neural oscillations. We then summarize classical and contemporary neural models of language organization, highlighting the distributed and hierarchical networks, and emphasize the high spatial and temporal resolution of SEEG for presurgical planning that enables clinicians to optimize neurosurgical interventions while preserving essential language areas and the tracts connecting them. Our review also addresses the human brain's remarkable plasticity and capacity for reorganization, which are especially pronounced in children and vital for adaptation to and recovery from neurologic injury or surgery. We focus specifically on developmental reorganization in pediatric patients with drug-resistant epilepsy and its implications for language mapping with SEEG. The review concludes with recommendations for designing language-mapping batteries tailored to the unique needs of pediatric patients.
Ohtahara syndrome in early infancy: A case report and review of the literature.推荐
PMID 43024292 · Medicine · 2026 Nov 13
作者：Ahmad MH, Ali M, Mehmood T, Murtaza S et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：综述
摘要：RATIONALE: Ohtahara syndrome (OS), also known as early infantile epileptic encephalopathy, is a rare and severe neurologic condition characterized by early-onset intractable seizures, tonic spasms, and a characteristic suppression-burst pattern on electroencephalography. Early diagnosis and management are particularly challenging in resource-limited settings where access to genetic and metabolic testing is limited. PATIENT CONCERNS: This case report describes a 20-month-old infant with OS, illustrating diagnostic and management challenges in a resource-limited setting. The child, born at term via cesarean section due to breech presentation, reportedly had age-appropriate early development until 2 months of age, when episodes of abnormal muscular rigidity and twitching were noted. DIAGNOSIS: The diagnosis of OS was based on the clinical history and a typical suppression-burst pattern on electroencephalography, in the context of limited access to advanced diagnostics. Brain magnetic resonance imaging showed no overt structural abnormalities, and genetic testing was unavailable. INTERVENTIONS: The patient was treated with oral sodium valproate and adjunctive brivaracetam. Management challenges included pharmacoresistance, unavailability of adrenocorticotropic hormone therapy, and inability to personalize therapy due to limited genetic and metabolic testing. OUTCOMES: The patient achieved seizure freedom, although developmental delay persisted. Close monitoring was emphasized to detect possible progression to West syndrome or Lennox-Gastaut syndrome. LESSONS: Early recognition, detailed history-taking, and clinical examination were essential to diagnosing this rare condition in a resource-limited setting. Improved access to diagnostic and genetic evaluation, where feasible, may help optimize outcomes in similar cases, although this cannot be established from a single case.
Clinically Meaningful Reduction in Seizure Frequency in Patients with Tuberous Sclerosis Complex Treated with Cannabidiol.推荐
PMID 43024142 · Neurology and therapy · 2026 11 13
作者：Thiele EA, Greco T, Nortvedt C, Vyas K et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
摘要：INTRODUCTION: In antiseizure medication clinical trials, a ≥ 50% reduction in seizure frequency is a relatively standard primary efficacy endpoint, but the real-world threshold for response perceived as clinically impactful for patients with tuberous sclerosis complex (TSC) is largely unassessed. We investigated this threshold in patients with TSC from the randomized controlled trial evaluating cannabidiol (CBD; Epidyolex® [EU]/Epidiolex® [USA]; ClinicalTrials.gov ID: NCT02544763). METHODS: This post hoc analysis used receiver operating characteristics (ROC) to quantify thresholds at which monthly seizure frequency reduction could be deemed clinically important in pooled data from patients receiving 25 or 50 mg/kg/day CBD (n = 132) for 16 weeks. Reductions in seizure frequency were anchored to Caregiver Global Impression of Change (CGIC; caregiver-reported assessment tool) ratings of 'slightly improved' or better, or 'much improved' or better. Anchor-based methods and distribution-based methods were applied to determine the thresholds for a clinically important response and minimal clinically important difference, respectively. RESULTS: The best-fit ROC-derived threshold for a clinically important response associated with a CGIC of 'slightly improved' or better was a 33% reduction in seizure frequency; for 'much improved' or better, the threshold was a 52% reduction. Thresholds for the minimal clinically important difference, based on half standard deviation (standard error of the mean), were - 17.8% (- 19.5%) for a CGIC rating of 'slightly improved' or better, and - 17.2% (- 18.9%) for CGIC rating of 'much improved' or better. Spearman's correlation was 0.54 (P < 0.001). CONCLUSION: When anchored to a CGIC rating of 'slightly improved' or better, the threshold for a clinically important response in seizure reduction was 33%, suggesting that a cut-off of 50% may overlook patients who are achieving meaningful improvements in their overall condition, as perceived by their caregivers. This exploratory analysis may help physicians to ascertain the level of improvement that patients and caregivers perceive as beneficial in TSC, particularly when seizure reductions are < 50%. CLINICAL TRIAL REGISTRATION NUMBER: ClinicalTrials.gov ID: NCT02544763.
A Novel Variant of Congenital Disorder of Glycosylation Presenting with Psychomotor Retardation, Restricted Diffusion in the Brainstem and Corpus Callosal Malformation: A Case Report.推荐

作者：Ajay V, Thomas B, Kesavadas C, Sundaram S et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：病例报告
摘要：BACKGROUND: Congenital disorders of glycosylation (CDG) are a heterogeneous group of inherited metabolic disorders caused by defects in glycan biosynthesis, often resulting in multisystem involvement with prominent neurological manifestations. ALG9-related CDG (CDG type I) is a rare entity with limited cases described in the literature. CASE DESCRIPTION: We report a 2.5-year-old female child with developmental delay, infantile spasms, and late-onset generalized tonic-clonic seizures (GTCS), born to consanguineous parents. Neuroimaging revealed delayed myelination involving the anterior temporal lobes, mild cerebellar volume loss, corpus callosal thickening with loss of the isthmus and posterior slant of the splenium, and a distinctive finding of symmetric restricted diffusion along the dorsal brainstem medial lemniscal tracts. Whole-exome sequencing identified a novel homozygous missense variant in ALG9 (c. 340T>C; p.Tyr114Cys), confirming the diagnosis of ALG9-CDG. To our knowledge, corpus callosal malformations and restricted diffusion involving the medial lemniscal tracts have not been previously reported in ALG9-CDG. CONCLUSION: This case expands the radiological spectrum of ALG9-related CDG and highlights the importance of advanced neuroimaging and molecular diagnostics in children with unexplained developmental delay and epilepsy.
Identifying Pediatric and Developmental Screening Targets With Biologically Informed Molecular Representations.浏览
PMID 43023643 · Clinical pharmacology and therapeutics · 2026 11 13
作者：Acitores Cortina JM, Schut MC, Tatonetti NP
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
摘要：More than 75% of Food and Drug Administration approved drugs lack pediatric dosing, efficacy, or safety data, and roughly 40% of medications prescribed to children are used off-label. For most therapies children receive, dose, formulation, or indication has never been formally evaluated in anyone under 18. In this work, we build on our previous model, BioMADE, to identify toxicity screening targets whose relevance is specific to defined developmental stages. We analyzed 3.8 million drug-condition records from KidSIDES across 7 developmental stages and 6 system organ classes. Univariate analysis identified biologically coherent genes associated with pediatric conditions, including known genes like GABRB1 (pathogenic variants cause developmental and epileptic encephalopathy), SLC6A1 (associated with developmental encephalopathies), and GRIN2A (defining gene of epilepsy-aphasia spectrum). Signals were the strongest in early life, spanning multiple organ systems. The highest univariate model was gene CTSS, area under the receiver operating characteristic curve of 0.776; 95% confidence interval 0.62-0.90. Multivariate models surpassed 0.8 area under receiving operator curve in 13 datasets (0.81-0.91), indicating that multi-gene drug interaction profiles carry predictive signal beyond single-target associations. We further identify secondary targets that may influence pediatric development and contribute to pediatric-specific adverse drug events, many of which are not included in current safety screening panels. Our findings support the development of novel pediatric-specific safety screening panels, with a particular emphasis on respiratory and congenital disorders in early life. Prioritizing relevant drug targets for pediatric screening can potentially reduce the challenges of pediatric clinical trials and advance safer, more precise pediatric drug therapies.

摘要：BACKGROUND AND AIMS: To report a case of PIGG-related distal hereditary motor neuropathy (dHMN) associated with a novel missense variant and describe its clinical and electrodiagnostic features. METHODS: Case report with chart review. RESULTS: A 25-year-old woman with childhood-onset progressive distal motor impairment was evaluated for distal weakness without sensory loss. She had two isolated febrile seizures before 2 years of life but no developmental delay, intellectual disability, or learning difficulties. There were no other affected family members. Electrodiagnostic testing showed an axonal, distal motor-predominant neuropathy with conduction block. Brain MRI was normal.
