Clinical Outcomes of Invasive Home Mechanical Ventilation in Children With Trisomy 13 and Trisomy 18.浏览
PMID 43023591 · Pediatric pulmonology · 2026 Nov
作者：Kumar E, Green GE, Hassan F, Ramsey AM et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
摘要：INTRODUCTION: Trisomy 13 and 18 (T13/18) are rare aneuploidies traditionally associated with severe congenital cardiac, pulmonary, neurologic and other anomalies and high 1-year mortality. Mortality has been improving with greater adoption of life-sustaining interventions including cardiac surgery, tracheostomy and home mechanical ventilation (HMV) but long-term outcomes of invasive HMV in this population is not known. The objective of this study was to evaluate outcomes of invasive HMV in T13/18. We hypothesized longer times to HMV liberation among children with T13/18 (Group 1) compared to other children on invasive HMV (Group 2, trisomy 21; Group 3, tracheo-bronchomalacia without aneuploidy; Group 4, all other causes of respiratory failure without aneuploidy). METHODS: This was a retrospective, descriptive, observational study of electronic medical records. Median duration of mechanical ventilation was compared using non parametric tests. RESULTS: Among 20 patients with T13/18 initiated on invasive HMV, 5 were liberated (25%). All patients liberated also underwent cardiac surgery and gastrostomy tube placement. A greater proportion of patients in Group 2 (68%), Group 3 (66%) and Group 4 (41%) were liberated from HMV. Among patients liberated from invasive HMV in all 4 groups, there was no significant difference in the median duration, in months (IQR), of invasive HMV: Group 1, 25 (15.5-41.5); Group 2, 23 (14-31); Group 3, 21 (16.25-27.75); Group 4, 19 (13-37.5); p = 0.928. Failed liberation from HMV in patients with T13/18 was attributed to additional comorbidities including seizures and gastrointestinal disease. CONCLUSION: Although the proportion of children with T13/18 liberated from HMV was lower than other groups, the duration of HMV among those who were liberated was comparable to that of children without T13/18, when accompanied by other life-sustaining interventions.
Vorasidenib in IDH1/2-mutant low-grade gliomas: a prospective study on volumetric MRI and seizure outcome.推荐

发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
摘要：BACKGROUND: The IDH1/2 inhibitor vorasidenib demonstrated efficacy in residual or recurrent grade 2 IDH1/2-mutant gliomas after surgery. PATIENTS AND METHODS: We conducted a prospective single-centre study on 54 grade 2 IDH1/2-mutant astrocytomas and IDH1/2-mutant 1p/19q-codeleted oligodendrogliomas treated with vorasidenib. Understanding Healthcare Professionals' Perspectives on Cannabis for Medical Purposes in Canada: A Scoping Review.浏览
PMID 43023098 · Medical cannabis and cannabinoids · 2026 07 23
作者：Renard J, De Felice M, Kane K, Rosen LG
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：综述
摘要：BACKGROUND: The legalization of recreational cannabis in Canada has raised significant challenges for healthcare providers (HCPs), who are increasingly requested to navigate conversations with patients about cannabis use, effects, benefits, and risks. SUMMARY: This scoping review aimed to: (1) identify HCP knowledge gaps and barriers related to medical cannabis use; (2) explore their perceptions and practices surrounding medical cannabis use in healthcare; and (3) assess HCP education and training needs. A search of PubMed, PsycNet, CINAHL, and grey literature identified 240 studies published between October 2018 and March 2026 that focused on Canadian HCP views, practices, challenges and needs related to medical cannabis. Of 187 articles screened, 26 met inclusion criteria. HCP-endorsed uses for medical cannabis were primarily for treatment of oncological nausea pain, chronic pain, and pediatric epilepsy. Many HCPs remain hesitant to consider cannabis in their treatment plans due to limited knowledge and training, stigma, regulatory challenges, cost, and insufficient clinical evidence. Across studies, HCPs expressed a strong desire for more formal cannabis education and clearer regulatory and institutional guidance. KEY MESSAGES: HCPs in Canada remain cautious about integrating cannabis into clinical care, citing safety concerns, limited clinical evidence, and systemic barriers. Addressing these challenges through targeted education and clearer clinical guidance may help build HCP confidence and support safer, evidence-informed patient care.
Functionalization of Carbon Nanotubes with 5‑Phenylvaleryl, Pentadecanoyl, and Adipoyl Chloride: Synthesis and Spectroscopic Surface Analysis Toward Biomarker Sensing.推荐
PMID 43023011 · ACS omega · 2026 10 28
作者：Galanti A, Trabelsi A, Maynard R, Reyes M et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
摘要：Carbon nanotubes (CNTs) were covalently modified with aromatic (5-phenylvaleryl, 5PC), long-chain aliphatic (pentadecanoyl, PDC), and oxygen-rich/carboxylated (adipoyl, ACC) chemistries to create a tunable surface interface for detecting volatile and semivolatile chemical biomarkers. Following oxidative activation, 3-aminopropyltriethoxysilane (APTES) amination, and acyl-chloride coupling, successful attachment and differentiation of linker classes were evaluated using X-ray photoelectron spectroscopy (XPS), ultraviolet photoelectron spectroscopy (UPS), and C KLL Auger analysis. Distinct spectroscopic fingerprints corresponding to aromatic, aliphatic, and oxygenated functionalities were resolved despite the intrinsically carbon-rich substrate. These orthogonal signatures provide a compact quality-control strategy for CNT functionalization and establish a framework for correlating surface chemistry with predicted analyte interactions, including aromatic interactions, nonpolar adsorption, and acid-base or hydrogen-bond pairing. This surface chemistry palette is intended to enable flexible CNT-based sensor arrays for noninvasive monitoring of clinically relevant biomarkers, including ketones associated with diabetes, nitrogen-containing species linked to chronic kidney disease, and volatile compounds implicated in preictal seizure states. Sensor-level performance was not evaluated in the present study and is the subject of ongoing device work.
The Effect of Intravenous Lipid Emulsion in Tricyclic Antidepressant Poisoning: A Systematic Review.推荐
PMID 43022816 · Journal of research in pharmacy practice · 2026 10 17
作者：Sajedi A, Otroshi A, Arabizadeh N, Taheri A et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：综述
摘要：Tricyclic antidepressant (TCA) poisoning may cause severe hypotension, QRS complex prolongation, arrhythmias, seizures, and cardiac arrest. Intravenous lipid emulsion (ILE) has been proposed as an adjunctive rescue treatment, but its role remains uncertain. We conducted a Preferred Reporting Items for Systematic Reviews and Meta-Analyses-guided systematic review without meta-analysis to evaluate clinical outcomes and safety of ILE in adult patients with TCA poisoning. PubMed/MEDLINE, Embase, Scopus, Web of Science, the Cochrane Library, Google Scholar, Persian-language databases, and gray-literature sources were searched through December 2025. Methodological quality was assessed using design-specific Joanna Briggs Institute critical appraisal checklists for case reports and case series. Uncontrolled case-based evidence was synthesized narratively using descriptive case-level proportions with exact 95% confidence intervals (CIs). Twenty-four studies published between 2009 and 2023 were included: 20 case reports, three case series, and one registry-derived case series, representing 42 cases of uncontrolled case-based evidence. Survival was reported in 41 of 42 cases (97.6%). Survival was reported in all documented arrest cases receiving ILE, although concurrent resuscitative interventions preclude attribution. Hypotension resolved in 14 of 17 cases (82.4%), whereas wide-QRS and arrhythmia resolution occurred in 6 of 14 cases (42.9%) and 4 of 9 cases (44.4%), respectively. Reported adverse events included acute respiratory distress syndrome, hypertriglyceridemia with laboratory interference, and mild liver abnormalities. The evidence was limited by publication bias, lack of comparator groups, selective reporting, concurrent treatments, and absent systematic safety monitoring. Therefore, the findings do not establish the effectiveness or safety of ILE in all TCA poisoning cases. More research is needed before ILE can be supported for routine or first-line use, but ILE may be considered as rescue therapy in selected patients with refractory, life-threatening toxicity alongside standard therapies, with careful monitoring for potential harms.
Tau spread plays an etiopathogenic connection between seizures and Alzheimer's disease.推荐
PMID 43022618 · iScience · 2026 11 03
作者：Jiang Y, Wu L, Li W, Li L et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
摘要：Patients with Alzheimer's disease (AD) display heightened seizure susceptibility, in which neuronal hyperactivity during seizures correlates with exacerbated Tau pathology and cognitive impairment. This study integrated public transcriptomic datasets with machine learning approaches, suggesting Tau spread as a key biological association between seizure activity and AD. We validated activity-dependent Tau release under epileptic stimulation using primary neurons and human induced pluripotent stem cells (hiPSC)-derived neuronal models. Further validation in an AAV-based mouse Tau propagation model revealed robust bidirectional interactions: pathological Tau overexpression worsened seizure outcomes, while seizures accelerated Tau propagation across brain regions, inducing neuronal loss and gliosis. This study characterizes the activity-regulated interplay between epileptic activity and AD-associated Tau pathology, offering a potential therapeutic target for AD comorbid with epilepsy.
A case report of Wilson's disease with rare frontal lobe white matter lesions.推荐
PMID 43022588 · Frontiers in pediatrics · 2026 10 29
作者：Du X, Guo H, Ding L, Liang C et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：病例报告
摘要：Wilson's disease (WD) is an autosomal recessive disorder of copper metabolism, typically presenting with hepatic dysfunction and bilateral basal ganglia abnormalities on magnetic resonance imaging (MRI). We report an unusual pediatric case of WD in a 13-year-old boy who presented with subtle neuroregression over the course of one year and recent focal seizures. Magnetic resonance imaging revealed prominent frontal-dominant leukoencephalopathy, which initially raised suspicion for Alexander disease. Brain MRI demonstrated extensive, symmetric T2-weighted and FLAIR hyperintensities predominantly affecting the frontal white matter, with involvement extending to the basal ganglia, thalamus, and brainstem. Notable imaging findings included cystic changes within the frontal lesions and areas of restricted diffusion in the cortical and subcortical regions on diffusion-weighted imaging (DWI). The diagnosis was confirmed by the presence of Kayser-Fleischer rings, low serum ceruloplasmin levels, elevated 24-hour urinary copper excretion, and identification of a homozygous pathogenic ATP7B mutation (c.2333G > T). This case demonstrates that WD can radiologically mimic leukodystrophies, underscoring the importance of considering WD in the differential diagnosis of pediatric leukoencephalopathy-even in the absence of classic hepatic or pronounced extrapyramidal findings.
Probiotic modulation of gut microbiota in preterm infants: a narrative review of clinical evidence, mechanisms, and translational gaps.浏览
PMID 43022580 · Frontiers in microbiology · 2026 10 29
作者：Xie D, Yuan H, Zhang D
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：综述
摘要：Preterm infants are at a heightened risk for gut dysbiosis, a condition characterized by reduced microbial diversity, delayed colonization by beneficial commensals such as Bifidobacterium, and overgrowth of opportunistic pathogens. This disrupted microbial ecosystem is intricately linked to the pathogenesis of life-threatening conditions, including necrotizing enterocolitis (NEC), late-onset sepsis (LOS), and feeding intolerance, and is also implicated in long-term neurodevelopmental and respiratory outcomes. Probiotic supplementation has emerged as a prominent strategy to shift the microbiota toward a more eubiotic-like composition and mitigate these risks. This narrative review synthesizes evidence from a wide range of randomized controlled trials, observational studies, systematic reviews, and meta-analyses to evaluate the clinical efficacy and microbiological impact of probiotics in preterm populations. The evidence indicates that probiotic interventions, particularly specific multi-strain formulations containing well-defined Lactobacillus and Bifidobacterium strains, are associated with significant reductions in the incidence of severe NEC (risk ratio 0.38-0.57) and all-cause mortality (risk ratio 0.69). However, their effect on preventing LOS and reducing colonization by multidrug-resistant organisms is less consistent. Probiotics consistently enhance the intestinal abundance of beneficial bacteria, modulate metabolic profiles, and reduce markers of intestinal inflammation. However, effects are strain-specific and cannot be extrapolated across products; future trials should report full strain identity, dose, viability, and product composition. Emerging evidence also suggests a potential role for probiotics in influencing immune cell development and the gut-brain axis, with implications for neurodevelopment and seizure prevention, though these findings are preliminary. This review also addresses critical challenges, including the considerable heterogeneity in study designs, probiotic strains, and clinical protocols, safety concerns in extremely low-birth-weight infants, and the methodological issues related to product quality and taxonomic identification. We conclude that multi-strain probiotics reduce severe NEC and all-cause mortality, but evidence for LOS is inconsistent and neurodevelopmental benefit remains unproven. Routine universal use is therefore not justified; translation requires product-specific trials and regulatory-grade quality control.
Mycoplasma pneumoniae-triggered hemophagocytic lymphohistiocytosis complicated by type I plastic bronchitis in a child: a case report.浏览
PMID 43022305 · Frontiers in immunology · 2026 10 29
作者：Zhang M, Zheng J, Zhou C, Xu Y
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：病例报告
摘要：BACKGROUND: Hemophagocytic lymphohistiocytosis (HLH) triggered by Mycoplasma pneumoniae (MP) is exceptionally rare in children, with fewer than 25 pediatric cases reported. Its concurrence with plastic bronchitis (PB) has not been well characterized, and host factors predisposing to recurrent infection-triggered hyperinflammation remain poorly understood. CASE PRESENTATION: A 6-year-old previously healthy girl experienced two hyperinflammatory episodes separated by 7 months. The first episode (August 2023) featured fever, lymphadenopathy, hepatosplenomegaly, hyperferritinemia (4,184 ng/mL), elevated cytokines, and declining blood counts but did not fulfill complete HLH-2004 criteria; it resolved with hepatoprotective therapy and a short corticosteroid course. The second episode (March 2024), triggered by confirmed M. pneumoniae infection (targeted next-generation sequencing), fulfilled 7 of 8 HLH-2004 criteria: fever, splenomegaly, pancytopenia (hemoglobin nadir 72 g/L, platelets 32 × 109/L, ANC 0.86 × 109/L), hypertriglyceridemia/hypofibrinogenemia (0.748 g/L), hyperferritinemia (31,905 ng/mL), decreased NK-cell activity, and bone marrow hemophagocytosis, with elevated soluble CD25 (9,249 pg/mL, approximately 2,312 U/mL-marginally below the 2,400 U/mL threshold but indicative of significant T-cell activation) and an HScore of 231 (>99% probability). Complications included severe hepatic injury (AST 4,017 U/L), consumptive coagulopathy, toxic-metabolic encephalopathy with seizure, and histologically confirmed type I plastic bronchitis requiring two bronchoscopies for bilateral cast extraction. Immunological evaluation revealed borderline NK-cell degranulation (ΔCD107a 5.68%; severe impairment threshold ≤5%) with normal CTL degranulation and preserved perforin, MUNC13-4, SAP, and XIAP expression. Quantitative NK-cell deficiency had been documented during the first episode. Treatment with modified HLH-2004 protocol (dexamethasone and IVIG without etoposide), doxycycline, and supportive care achieved complete recovery. CONCLUSION: This case demonstrates that MP-HLH can present with exceptional severity including type I PB and multiorgan failure, particularly in children with prior hyperinflammatory episodes suggesting underlying immune vulnerability. Borderline NK-cell degranulation combined with quantitative NK-cell deficiency and recurrent hyperinflammation supports a threshold model of HLH susceptibility. Functional immune screening including CD107a degranulation assays should be performed in children with recurrent or unusually severe infection-triggered hyperinflammation, even without a definitive monogenic diagnosis.
Reirradiation for Recurrent Craniopharyngioma-Long-Term Follow-up.浏览
PMID 43022027 · Advances in radiation oncology · 2026 11 03
作者：Hassan SP, Moreno AS, Mansour I, Dama H et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
摘要：PURPOSE: To report long-term tumor control, toxicity, and functional outcomes after a second course of fractionated radiation therapy for recurrent craniopharyngioma previously treated with radiation therapy. METHODS AND MATERIALS: We identified all patients with craniopharyngioma with recurrent or progressive disease, unsuitable for further medical management or re-resection, who underwent 2 courses of fractionated photon radiation therapy at a single institution between 1998 and 2024. Reirradiation was delivered to 54 Gy in 30 fractions with full overlap of the initial target; dose constraints to optic nerves, chiasm, and brainstem were Dmax/D0.03cc (Maximum Dose or Dose to 0.03 cubic centimetres) ≤54 Gy. Outcomes were summarized descriptively. RESULTS: Six patients received reirradiation a mean of 9.8 years (range, 5.6-20.5) after initial radiation therapy (50-54 Gy in 25-30 fractions). Median follow-up after reirradiation was 4.1 years (range, 0.4-8.8). At the last follow-up, 4 patients were alive, all with local disease control (2 with radiological regression, 2 with stable disease). Two deaths occurred (1 due to progressive disease and 1 due to functional decline with stable imaging). Visual outcomes were heterogeneous: 1 patient maintained intact vision; others had intermittent diplopia, bitemporal hemianopsia, or unilateral blindness with contralateral impairment. Endocrinopathy was frequent: 3 of 4 survivors had pan-hypopituitarism; 1 had partial deficits (hypothyroidism/amenorrhea). Functional status was impaired in survivors, with the inability to work or study due to fatigue, visual loss, or cognitive issues. Additional toxicities included posttreatment seizures requiring hospitalization (n = 1) and transient dexamethasone for headache (n = 1). CONCLUSIONS: In carefully selected patients lacking other curative options, modern highly conformal reirradiation can achieve meaningful local control after prior high-dose radiation therapy, but carries an additive risk of visual, endocrine, and functional morbidity. Decisions should be individualized within a multidisciplinary team to identify appropriate cases for reirradiation and to ensure thorough long-term follow-up to manage delayed toxicity.
Recent advances in the pharmacological effects and mechanisms of borneol and borneol-containing formulations in central nervous system diseases.浏览
PMID 43021956 · Frontiers in pharmacology · 2026 10 29
作者：Zhang X, Wu Y, Han J, Zhao Z et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：综述
摘要：Borneol is an aromatic medicinal agent widely used in traditional Chinese medicine, both as a standalone component and as an ingredient in multi-herb formulations. Its pharmacological relevance to central nervous system (CNS) disorders has attracted increasing attention, particularly in Alzheimer's disease, Parkinson's disease, epilepsy, ischemic stroke, and glioma. Accumulating evidence indicates that borneol may influence multiple pathophysiological processes, including brain-barrier function and drug delivery, neuroinflammation and oxidative stress, neurotransmitter homeostasis and neuronal excitability, cellular injury and death, and the clearance of pathological substances and post-injury repair. Here, we integrate and compare these mechanisms across disease contexts. Notably, although several actions of borneol appear to converge across CNS disorders, others are strongly disease- and context-dependent; for example, its effects on brain-barrier permeability, oxidative stress, and cell death may differ in direction according to disease pathology and therapeutic intent. Pharmacokinetic studies support measurable brain exposure to borneol, yet direct comparative evidence of pharmacological activity, brain disposition, and human pharmacokinetics among borneol preparations of different botanical origins and stereochemical configurations remains scarce. Borneol-containing formulations also exhibit multi-level pharmacological effects; however, because most studies lack borneol-alone, borneol-depleted, or add-back controls, these effects cannot be unequivocally attributed to borneol itself. Overall, the current evidence base remains dominated by cellular and animal studies, with several proposed mechanisms inferred primarily from changes in molecular expression or computational predictions. Robust causal validation, high-quality clinical evidence, and long-term safety data are still limited. Future studies should therefore define the dose-exposure-response relationships and disease-specific therapeutic windows of different borneol preparations, establish the independent contribution of borneol within complex formulations, and determine its genuine therapeutic value and long-term safety through rigorous mechanistic and clinical investigation.
Case Report: Probable paroxysmal sympathetic hyperactivity during vascular and postoperative critical illness with bilateral white matter abnormalities.推荐

摘要：BACKGROUND: Recognition of paroxysmal sympathetic hyperactivity (PSH) during vascular and postoperative critical illness is complicated by overlapping infection and seizure features. CASE PRESENTATION: A 37-year-old man developed encephalopathy and rigidity after extensive arterial thrombosis, thrombectomy, and suspected postoperative sepsis. Brain magnetic resonance images dated February 20, 2025, showed symmetric bilateral periventricular and deep white matter abnormalities without definite diffusion restriction. Toward trustworthy diagnostic AI: debiasing and grounding large language models for seizure semiology interpretation.推荐
PMID 43021820 · Frontiers in neurology · 2026 10 29
作者：Yuan X, Zhang X, Xu H, Ye Q et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
摘要：Large language models (LLMs) have been proposed to map free-text seizure semiology to seizure-onset-zone (SOZ) likelihoods, but it remains unclear whether inference-time interventions can improve on the original SemioLLM benchmark without additional training. We evaluated two such interventions, candidate-order permutation within self-consistency and TF-IDF retrieval of case-specific labelled examples, individually and in combination (retrieval-augmented order-invariant self-consistency, RAOI-SC), across seven locally served open-weight LLMs on 1,268 Semio2Brain rows. Performance was assessed with weighted- and macro-F1, paired bootstrap confidence intervals, and exact McNemar tests against the self-consistency baseline, and a multi-dimensional reliability profile covering citation coverage and conditional verifiability, retrieval agreement, strict schema adherence, and confidence concentration; a TF-IDF nearest-neighbor classifier and grouped-split sensitivity analyses bounded how much of any gain could reflect retrieval leakage rather than the intervention itself. RAOI-SC had a higher weighted-F1 point estimate than self-consistency for six of seven models, but the improvement was supported by paired confidence intervals for four models and by McNemar testing for only one, Mistral-7B-Instruct-v0.3; the TF-IDF nearest-neighbor baseline alone achieved 55.72 weighted-F1 under row-level splitting, falling to 50.43 and 48.97 once identical-text and publication-reference duplicates were grouped together. These results support a cautious, model-specific reading of retrieval and order permutation as testable prompting interventions rather than a universally superior or clinically validated method, and define a row-level benchmark that does not by itself establish patient- or source-level generalization.
Bilateral cerebellar high-frequency rTMS for refractory epilepsy: a randomized, single-blind, sham-controlled study.推荐
PMID 43021808 · Frontiers in neurology · 2026 10 29
作者：Liu A, Li L, Han Y, Wan L et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
摘要：BACKGROUND: For patients with drug-resistant epilepsy who are not suitable for surgery, transcranial magnetic stimulation (TMS) is an important non-invasive neuromodulation therapy. The cerebellar cortex can inhibit cortical excitability via the cerebello-thalamo-cortical pathway. Here, we explore whether high-frequency (20 Hz) cerebellar repetitive TMS (rTMS) reduces seizure frequency and interictal epileptiform discharges (IEDs) in refractory epilepsy. METHODS: Eighty-seven patients with refractory epilepsy were included in the intention-to-treat (ITT) population (n = 87) and then randomized to active rTMS (n = 44) or sham stimulation (n = 43). One patient in treatment group had no post-baseline efficacy data; efficacy analyses were therefore performed in the modified intention-to-treat (mITT) population (n = 86). The active group received two cycles of 20 Hz rTMS targeting the cerebellum. Seizure frequency and IEDs were assessed before and after treatment. RESULTS: Poisson generalized linear mixed models revealed a significant group×time interaction for seizure frequency [F(2,236) = 162.65, p < 0.001]. The active group had significantly lower seizure frequencies at months 1, 2, and 3 (all p < 0.001), with the largest difference at month 2. Percentage reductions of seizure frequency in the active group were 64.6%, 73.2%, and 13.3%, respectively, compared with 3.9%, 0.6%, and -8.4% in the control group (all P < 0.001). Responder rates (≥50% reduction) in the active group were 90.7%, 95.3%, and 0% vs. 0% in controls (p < 0.001 for months 1-2). Within-group analysis showed significant IED reduction after active rTMS (p < 0.001) but no change in controls. Moreover, a significant group × baseline interaction (p < 0.001) indicated greater suppression in patients with higher baseline IEDs. Exploratory subgroup analysis further confirmed this. All adverse events were mild, with no significant difference in the incidence between the two groups. CONCLUSION: Active cerebellar rTMS was associated with reduced seizure frequency, improved responder rates, and suppressed IEDs in refractory epilepsy with no obvious discomfort. The optimal efficacy duration was approximately 2 months, with greater IED suppression in patients with higher baseline levels.
Risk factors for seizures in patients with brain metastases: integrating clinical features and molecular markers.推荐
PMID 43021697 · Journal of neurosurgical sciences · 2026 11 13
作者：Malanga OM, Pellerino A, Bertero L, Bruno F et al.

发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
摘要：BACKGROUND: Brain metastases (BM) affect nearly 30% of patients with solid tumors. Despite this prevalence, the mechanisms underlying brain tumor-related epilepsy (BTRE) in this population remain incompletely elucidated. This study aims to investigate risk factors associated with seizure occurrence in patients with BM, focusing on histology and molecular markers of the primary tumor. METHODS: We conducted a retrospective cohort study on 325 patients who underwent surgical resection of BM between 2014 and 2019. 摘要：OBJECTIVE: This study aims to characterize the clinical spectrum, outcomes, and factors associated with influenza-associated encephalopathy in hospitalized children with influenza-associated neurological complications. METHODS: This retrospective cohort study included children younger than 18 years who were hospitalized with laboratory-confirmed influenza at a tertiary university hospital between 2018 and 2023. 摘要：Farber disease (FD) is a progressive lysosomal storage disorder caused by deficient acid ceramidase (ACDase) activity. Spinal Muscular Atrophy with Progressive Myoclonic Epilepsy (SMA-PME) is also caused by biallelic variants of the ASAH1 gene, which encodes ACDase. Some FD patients experience progressive neurological deterioration, ataxia, seizures, and developmental delays. SMA-PME patients develop progressive muscle weakness and seizures. Treatment normalized spleen and liver sizes and pathology, as well as peripheral blood characteristics including plasma MCP-1 and IL-6 levels. Moreover, LV-mediated HSCT-GT significantly reduced histiocytic infiltration and ceramide accumulation in the livers and brains of treated mice.

PMID 43021547 · Molecular therapy : the journal of the American Society of Gene Therapy · 2026 11 13
作者：Cruite K, Moore M, Gallagher MD, Lin S et al.

命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
摘要：Approximately one-third of individuals with epilepsy are refractory to current anti-seizure medications, highlighting the need for new, mechanism-based therapeutic approaches. Fms-like tyrosine kinase 3 (FLT3), a receptor tyrosine kinase classically studied in hematopoiesis and leukemia, is also expressed in neurons, although its function in the brain remains largely unexplored. Our prior work identified small-molecule FLT3 inhibitors (FLT3i) as unexpected enhancers of potassium-chloride cotransporter 2 (KCC2), a neuron-specific chloride transporter essential for maintaining GABAergic inhibition. Here, we applied a functional genomics framework in mouse primary neurons and human stem cell-derived neurons to define FLT3i-induced transcriptional programs, revealing conserved gene networks governing synaptic function and ion channel regulation. We further demonstrate that KW-2449, an orally bioavailable FLT3 inhibitor, penetrates the blood-brain barrier, elevates KCC2 expression in vivo for up to 24 hours, and significantly reduces seizure susceptibility in a kainic acid-induced mouse model of epilepsy. Together, these findings reveal a previously unrecognized role for FLT3 in neuronal function and establish proof-of-principle for FLT3 inhibition as a mechanistically supported candidate strategy warranting further development as a next-generation anti-seizure therapy.
Correction of acid ceramidase deficiency by intravenous AAV-mediated gene therapy in symptomatic P361R-Farber mice.浏览

发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
摘要：Acid ceramidase (ACDase) is a ubiquitously expressed lysosomal enzyme that cleaves ceramide into sphingosine and a free fatty acid. Acid ceramidase deficiency caused by mutations in the ASAH1 gene leads to Farber disease (FD) and spinal muscular atrophy with progressive myoclonic epilepsy (SMA-PME), two fatal ultra-rare lysosomal storage disorders (LSD) in which ceramides accumulate in cells leading to inflammation and lesions in various tissues, including the nervous system. Feeding challenges at initiation and maintenance of the ketogenic diet in children with infantile epileptic spasms syndrome: 20 years single center experience.推荐
PMID 43021443 · Developmental neurorehabilitation · 2026 11 12
作者：Lowe H, Kaur P, Dickson J, Fraser E et al.

命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
摘要：RATIONALE: Infants with Infantile Epileptic Spasms Syndrome (IESS) are at high risk for feeding challenges such as aspiration. These vulnerabilities may be exacerbated by the ketogenic diet (KD), an accepted second-line therapy. Hormonal therapy (ACTH or oral corticosteroids), the first-line IESS treatment, may transiently enhance appetite and improve oral feeding performance, and hence mask feeding challenges. METHODS: A retrospective chart review was conducted for infants with IESS who initiated KD between January 2005 and January 2025 at our center. Collected data included ages at diagnosis and KD initiation, comorbidities, clinical and video fluoroscopic swallow study (VFSS) assessments, timing relative to hormonal therapy, feeding method before and during KD initiation, and incidence of feeding concerns during KD. Feeding challenges included aspiration, confirmed by VFSS or occupational therapy clinical assessment of feeding (OCTA), or inadequate oral intake. RESULTS: Forty infants were enrolled. Twelve (30%) had previous aspiration reported in the medical charts or VFSS at baseline; nine were tube-fed prior to KD. Of the 31 (31/40, 77.5%) orally fed infants at initiation of KD, 23 were referred for feeding safety evaluation. Twelve of these had a feeding concern found on OTCA and/or VFSS, of which 3 presented with aspiration without a protective cough confirmed by VFSS. Nineteen babies were deemed safe to initiate KD orally; however, 5/19 (26%, 95% CI 11.8%-48.7%) went on to experience aspiration or aspiration pneumonia during KD treatment. CONCLUSION: Feeding challenges are frequent, dynamic, and under-recognized in IESS. Comprehensive feeding evaluation at KD initiation and maintenance is essential for safe treatment.
Intrafamilial neurovascular heterogeneity in pediatric NF1-associated moyamoya syndrome: a three-generation pedigree study and pattern-oriented synthesis.推荐
PMID 43021292 · Italian journal of pediatrics · 2026 08 18
作者：Liu X, Li H, Lu X, Li D
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：病例报告
摘要：BACKGROUND: Neurofibromatosis type 1-associated moyamoya syndrome (NF1-MMS) is an important pediatric cerebrovascular phenotype. Early symptoms may be nonspecific, and vascular lesions can be detected before clear focal neurologic deficits. The value and timing of cerebrovascular screening in asymptomatic children with NF1 remain uncertain. CASE PRESENTATION: We report a 32-month-old girl, the older twin, admitted for progressive brain imaging abnormalities more than 14 months after a single focal-to-bilateral tonic-clonic seizure around 18 months of age. She had no recurrent seizures, hemiparesis, definite transient ischemic attacks, or other focal neurologic deficits during available follow-up. Serial MRI/MRA showed persistent ischemic white-matter changes, left-sided linear sulcal FLAIR hyperintensity suggestive of an ivy sign, and narrowing of the terminal left internal carotid artery and proximal left anterior/middle cerebral arteries with collateral formation. Whole-genome sequencing and familial Sanger validation identified a heterozygous NF1 NM_000267:c.6947T > G, p.(Leu2316Arg) variant. The variant was present in four relatives across three generations: the paternal grandmother, father, proband, and twin sister. The paternal grandmother, father, and twin sister had no overt neurovascular symptoms during one year of clinical follow-up, although cerebrovascular imaging was unavailable for the paternal grandmother and twin sister. LITERATURE SYNTHESIS: Representative pediatric NF1-MMS studies show recurrent patterns of clinically silent or atypical presentation, early unilateral involvement, and imaging signs of chronic hypoperfusion. CONCLUSIONS: This family illustrates possible intrafamilial neurovascular variability under the same familial NF1 variant background. These observations should be interpreted cautiously and warrant prospective multicenter studies to clarify the role of baseline cerebrovascular imaging in high-risk NF1 families.
Hypertensive posterior reversible encephalopathy syndrome in Lesch-Nyhan disease: possible contribution of chronic hyperuricemia and renin activation - a case report.推荐
PMID 43021181 · BMC pediatrics · 2026 08 18
作者：Nitta A, Oto Y, Koyama S, Takatani T
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：病例报告
摘要：BACKGROUND: Posterior reversible encephalopathy syndrome (PRES) is characterized by seizures, altered consciousness, and vasogenic edema predominantly affecting the parieto-occipital regions. Although acute hypertension is a major precipitating factor, impaired cerebral autoregulation and endothelial dysfunction are also considered to contribute to its pathophysiology. Lesch-Nyhan disease (LND) is a rare X-linked disorder caused by hypoxanthine-guanine phosphoribosyltransferase deficiency and is associated with chronic hyperuricemia and severe neurological manifestations. Hypertension has rarely been described in association with LND. CASE PRESENTATION: We report a 4-year-old boy with genetically confirmed LND who developed hypertensive PRES during status epilepticus. On admission, his blood pressure was markedly elevated (207/101 mmHg). Neurological symptoms improved promptly after antihypertensive therapy. Brain magnetic resonance imaging demonstrated bilateral occipital subcortical hyperintensities on T2-weighted and fluid-attenuated inversion recovery images. Echocardiography revealed left ventricular hypertrophy. Plasma renin activity was elevated prior to initiation of angiotensin-converting enzyme inhibitor therapy. No structural renal abnormalities were detected. CONCLUSIONS: This case raises the possibility that chronic hyperuricemia-associated vascular dysfunction, together with activation of the renin-angiotensin system, may have contributed to susceptibility to hypertensive PRES. These findings should be regarded as hypothesis-generating rather than evidence of causality and require confirmation in future studies.
A second seizure and the truth unveiled: adolescent neurocysticercosis acquired locally in a low-endemic region.推荐
PMID 43021120 · BMC pediatrics · 2026 08 18
作者：James V, Balbi H, Muntean C, Kamath V
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：病例报告
摘要：Neurocysticercosis (NCC), caused by the larval form of Taenia solium, is a common cause of seizures in endemic regions but may go unrecognized in low-endemic settings such as the United States. We report the case of a previously healthy 19-year-old Hispanic male in New York City who presented with a second generalized tonic-clonic seizure (GTCS). Brain imaging revealed scattered sub-5 mm extra-axial calcifications on CT, consistent with chronic NCC sequelae, and a 5 mm ring-enhancing lesion with central dot sign and surrounding edema in the left frontal lobe on MRI, most consistent with an active or degenerating stage of NCC: together representing mixed-stage disease. Notably, this was his second seizure; a similar event had occurred one year earlier and was not evaluated with neuroimaging, suggesting a delayed diagnosis. There was no history of travel to T. solium-endemic regions; his family had immigrated from an endemic area, raising concern for local or intrafamilial transmission. This case highlights the importance of early and thorough evaluation, including brain imaging, after a first unprovoked seizure in adolescents and young adults: especially those from immigrant backgrounds or with epidemiologic risk factors for parasitic infections. Timely recognition of NCC can prevent recurrence and long-term neurologic complications.
Delayed-onset seizure after dantrolene-treated malignant hyperthermia in a patient with traumatic brain injury and a pathogenic RYR1 variant: a multiple-hit model.推荐

摘要：BACKGROUND: Malignant hyperthermia (MH) is a life-threatening pharmacogenetic disorder primarily affecting skeletal muscle via ryanodine receptor 1 (RYR1) hyperactivation. Central nervous system (CNS) complications following MH are rarely reported, and the role of dantrolene in preventing or treating such sequelae remains unclear. Patients with concurrent traumatic brain injury (TBI) present a particularly complex scenario where multiple etiologies for neurological complications may converge. CASE PRESENTATION: We report a 53-year-old male with a confirmed pathogenic RYR1 variant (c.7108_7109delinsAAGCC, p.Gly2370delinsLysPro) who developed fulminant MH during emergency intracranial hematoma evacuation following traumatic brain injury with extensive cerebral contusions. CONCLUSION: In patients with MH and concomitant TBI, delayed seizures may arise primarily from the structural brain injury (cerebral contusions and/or the surgical manipulation of hematoma evacuation), although acute systemic insult (MH) and potential genetic susceptibility (RYR1 variant) could represent additional, unconfirmed contributing factors.Dantrolene should not be relied upon for CNS complications, as its limited CNS penetration and RyR1 selectivity render it ineffective for seizure control.This case suggests that CNS sequelae deserve consideration in MH patients with overlapping risk factors, while recognizing that traumatic brain injury remains the most likely primary cause of seizure in this setting.
Optimization and Characterization of a Guinea Pig Model of Diisopropylfluorophosphate-Induced Status Epilepticus, Nitrooxidative Stress, and Neuroinflammation for Therapeutic Evaluation.推荐
PMID 43020338 · Neurotoxicology · 2026 11 13
作者：Massey N, Luo D, Parameswaran S, Ekanayake MMB et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
摘要：Acute exposure to organophosphate (OP) nerve agents and pesticides induces status epilepticus (SE), nitrooxidative stress, neuroinflammation, and long-term neurological dysfunction. Although rodent models are widely used to study OP-induced neurotoxicity, the guinea pig offers important translational advantages due to its closer similarity to humans in cholinergic physiology and brain organization. The present study aimed to establish and characterize a guinea pig model of diisopropylfluorophosphate (DFP)-induced neurotoxicity suitable for therapeutic evaluation. Unlike the rat model, acute lower doses of DFP (3 or 4mg/kg, s.c.) did not induce behavioral toxicity signs or seizures, while 6mg/kg induced non-convulsive seizures with occasional convulsive seizures. However, 8mg/kg, reliably induced severe and sustained SE (>40min) without mortality. DFP-exposed animals (8mg/kg) transiently lost body weight (~10%) followed by recovery to control levels within three days post-exposure. During the 8 weeks of post-exposure, serum analysis revealed persistent nitro-oxidative stress characterized by elevated nitrite and reactive oxygen species levels, reduced glutathione concentrations, and a decreased GSH/GSSG ratio. DFP exposure (8mg/kg) significantly increased inflammatory cytokines and chemokines in both serum and cerebrospinal fluid, including TNF-α, IL-6, IL-1α, IL-1β, IL-17A, IL-18, and MCP-1 compared to control. Histological analyses revealed increased microgliosis, astrogliosis, and evidence of neurodegeneration in the amygdala, cortex, hippocampus, and thalamus at eight weeks post-DFP exposure (8mg/kg). These findings identify 8mg/kg DFP as the optimal dose for inducing robust status epilepticus (SE), neuroinflammation, oxidative stress, and neurodegeneration without mortality. Collectively, these results support the guinea pig as a clinically relevant higher-order preclinical model for evaluating novel medical countermeasures against organophosphate (OP)-induced neurotoxicity.
Targeting Seizures and Neurobehavioral Alterations: Efficacy of Brivaracetam-Sertraline Combination in PTZ-Induced Kindling Model.推荐
PMID 43020307 · European journal of pharmacology · 2026 11 12
作者：Farooq M, Javaid S, Younas S, Ashraf W et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
摘要：Epilepsy is a neurological disorder associated with various psychiatric comorbidities, among which anxiety and depressive disorders are most prevalent. These psychiatric comorbidities remain frequently untreated due to concerns about exacerbation of seizures and limited awareness about antidepressant selection. This study aimed to evaluate the efficacy of brivaracetam (BRV) and sertraline (SRT) administered alone and in combination on the progression of seizures and associated behavioral changes. Male BALB/c mice were subjected to the pentylenetetrazol (PTZ)-induced kindling process followed by behavioral tests to examine the impact of test treatments on kindling-associated anxiety, cognitive deficit, and depressive disorders. Subsequently, the brains were dissected, and oxidative stress was estimated in brain homogenates; histopathological studies were also carried out to monitor changes in the hippocampal regions. The combination of BRV and SRT attenuated seizure development and progression and markedly protected the mice from cognitive impairment, as their discrimination indices were increased in novel object recognition test together with longer step-through latencies in the passive avoidance test. The combination of BRV and SRT exerted enhanced therapeutic benefits in reducing seizures and improving behavioral changes as compared to BRV or SRT administered alone. Our findings suggest that combining sertraline with antiseizure medications may provide a promising strategy to manage seizures and psychiatric disorders in epileptic patients, warranting more preclinical and clinical investigations to enhance patients' quality of life with comorbid psychiatric disorders.

摘要：Advances in cancer immunotherapy have transformed survival outcomes and driven widespread adoption of these treatments. However, as the population receiving immunotherapy has grown, neurologic complications have increasingly emerged, with rates of 1 to 3% among patients receiving immune checkpoint inhibitors and 26.9% among chimeric antigen receptor (CAR) T cell therapy recipients across all FDA-approved products. This review focuses on the early recognition and acute management of neurologic toxicities associated with immunotherapy, with particular emphasis on immune effector cell-associated neurotoxicity syndrome (ICANS) related to CD19 and BCMA-directed CAR T cell therapies. Early-onset epilepsy and inborn errors of metabolism: A large retrospective pediatric cohort.推荐
PMID 43019943 · Epilepsy & behavior : E&B · 2026 11 12
作者：Cucuzza ME, Costanzo MC, Meli C, Greco F et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
摘要：BACKGROUND: Inborn errors of metabolism (IEM) are an under-recognised cause of childhood epilepsy. Early diagnosis is critical because specific treatments can substantially alter the disease course. METHODS: We performed a retrospective study on 21,072 children evaluated for epilepsy in two Child Neurology referral centres in Eastern Sicily (i.e. Messina, Catania, and Enna) between January 2001 and December 2025. Medical records were reviewed to identify patients with a confirmed IEM diagnosis. Clinical, electrophysiological, neuroimaging, and treatment data were collected and analysed. RESULTS: Fifty-seven children (0.27%) had a co-diagnosis of epilepsy and IEM (28 males, 29 females). Lysosomal storage disorders were the most common category (31.6%), followed by aminoacidopathies (22.8%), mitochondrial disorders (15.8%), congenital disorders of glycosylation (CDG, 12.3%), and organic acidaemias (7.0%). Seizure onset occurred before the age of three years in 83.3% of IEM cases, compared with 27.6% of children with non-metabolic epilepsy (OR 12.3; 95% CI 6.2-24.4; p < 0.001). Diagnostic delay exceeded 3.5 years from first seizure in half the cohort. Approximately 50% of patients showed drug-resistant epilepsy despite multi-drug anti-seizure medication therapy. CONCLUSIONS: IEM should be systematically considered in any child presenting with epilepsy, particularly when onset occurs in the first three years of life. A structured stepwise diagnostic approach can substantially reduce diagnostic delay and facilitate access to disease-specific therapies.
Defining feasible and realistic policy objectives for the decriminalization of personal possession of drugs.浏览
PMID 43019843 · The International journal on drug policy · 2026 11 12
作者：McAdam E, Eekhoudt CR, Reddon H, DeBeck K
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
摘要：BACKGROUND: In January 2023, British Columbia (BC), Canada implemented a three-year pilot project decriminalizing personal possession of selected illicit substances. The pilot ended in January 2026 with government officials stating that the policy did not deliver the results they had "hoped" for. While a range of theoretical rationales are cited to justify drug decriminalization, there is limited empirical research examining what policy objectives decriminalization can realistically achieve. METHODS: We conducted 16 semi-structured interviews between November-December 2021 pre-policy implementation, with experts/key interest-holders, including people with lived experience, law enforcement representatives, and policymakers, to identify feasible and realistic objectives of drug decriminalization. A thematic analysis was conducted using inductive and deductive approaches. RESULTS: The most feasible and realistic objectives of decriminalization were identified as: reducing contact with the criminal justice system for people who use drugs; reducing police seizures for personal possession and related harms; and reducing stigma. Other secondary objectives included: improving the health and well-being of people who use drugs; reducing racial disparities in enforcement of simple possession; and increasing efficiency within the criminal justice system. Notably, measurably reducing overdose deaths was articulated as an inappropriate and impractical objective of drug decriminalization. CONCLUSIONS: Findings underscore that realistic and feasible policy objectives of decriminalization are relatively narrow in scope and do not include measurable reductions in overdose fatalities. Policy objectives identified offer a practical and mechanistically grounded foundation for policymakers to refine decriminalization approaches and ensure evaluations focus on outcomes that accurately reflect the intended scope and function of the policy.
Ketogenic diet therapy in adults with status epilepticus: A systematic review.推荐
PMID 43019611 · Seizure · 2026 11 07
作者：Kromm J, Mo M, Howard A, Bencsik C et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：综述
摘要：OBJECTIVE: To systematically review evidence describing the use of ketogenic diet therapy in adults with status epilepticus. METHODS: A PRISMA-compliant systematic review was conducted and registered in PROSPERO. MEDLINE, EMBASE, CINAHL, and Cochrane CENTRAL were searched from inception to June 13, 2025. Eligible studies included original research involving adults 18 years and older with status epilepticus treated with enteral or parenteral ketogenic diet therapy. Outcomes included ketosis, seizure control, mortality, functional outcomes, adverse events and ICU/hospital outcomes. Study quality was assessed using design appropriate tools. RESULTS: Twenty-three studies comprising 123 adult patients were included, primarily of observational studies, case series, and case reports. Ketosis and seizure cessation were frequently reported, occurring in 79 % and 81 % of patients respectively. Reported median or individual times from ketogenic diet initiation and ketosis to seizure cessation ranged from two to 25 days and zero to 13.5 days respectively. Mortality among cohorts with deaths varied widely, overlapping with historically reported mortality for super-refractory status epilepticus. Neurological, cognitive, and patient-reported outcomes were infrequently assessed, and reporting of antiseizure medication or sedation-sparing effects were limited. Heterogeneity in study design, patient populations, diet protocols, ketosis monitoring, and outcome definitions precluded meta-analysis. CONCLUSION: Evidence suggests an association between KD therapy and seizure cessation in adults with refractory and super-refractory status epilepticus, although evidence remains limited. While KD therapy requires careful management and adverse events have been reported, it remains a promising therapeutic strategy for this patient population. Prospective studies incorporating standardized outcome measures, ketosis monitoring, adverse-event reporting, and protocolized ketogenic diet implementation are needed to better define its role in this population.
Prehospital recognition and differentiation of seizure presentations by paramedics and ambulance nurses: A scoping review.推荐
PMID 43019610 · Seizure · 2026 10 30
作者：Hill J, Williams B
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：综述
摘要：AIM: The aim of this scoping review was to identify and map the literature examining prehospital clinician recognition and differentiation of seizure presentations. DESIGN: Scoping Review. METHODS: A scoping review was conducted in accordance with the PRISMA Extension for Scoping Reviews. A literature search was conducted in January 2026 across five electronic databases. DATA SOURCES: Medline, Emcare, ProQuest, CINAHL, and SCOPUS. RESULTS: The search yielded 1264 citations, with 32 full-text articles assessed for eligibility and 18 studies included, six of them identified or re-assessed during peer review. Studies examined paramedics (n = 16) and ambulance nurses (n = 2), with the largest proportion of publications between 2019 and 2021. Across studies reporting diagnostic performance, measured against variable reference standards, sensitivity ranged from 54.0 to 58.5% and specificity from 95.9 to 96.6% using the principal diagnostic definitions reported. Recognition rates ranged from 56.8 to 89.7%, while non-recognition reached 55.3% in one study. Non-convulsive seizures were particularly challenging, with non-recognition rates of 53.0-81.4%. CONCLUSION: This scoping review indicates that prehospital seizure recognition and differentiation remain suboptimal, particularly for non-convulsive presentations. However, variation in emergency medical services (EMS) workforce configurations and scope of practice limits generalisability across settings. Future research should prioritise outcomes-focused evaluations of seizure recognition and differentiation from key mimics.
A translational platform for brain-computer interfaces and adaptive neuromodulation: Technical characterization, long-term validation, and implementation of the CorTec brain interchange-BCI2000 ecosystem.推荐
PMID 43019594 · Journal of neural engineering · 2026 11 12
作者：Lampert F, Baker MR, Mivalt F, Engelhardt W et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
摘要：Adaptive neuromodulation systems and implantable brain-computer interfaces (BCIs) are promising therapies for neurological and psychiatric disorders. However, their broader translation into research and clinical practice remains limited by technological complexity, restricted access to implantable research platforms, and the lack of standardized, reproducible experimental workflows. We therefore aimed to develop and validate an open, general-purpose translational ecosystem that enables rapid development, evaluation, and dissemination of novel neuromodulation and implantable BCI paradigms.&#xD;Approach: The CorTec Brain Interchange (BIC) implantable neural sensing and stimulation device was integrated with the open-source BCI2000 platform to create a modular, extensible neuromodulation ecosystem. We established a standardized battery of quantitative assessments to characterize implantable neuromodulation systems to comprehensively evaluate the CorTec BIC device through benchtop characterization, long-term preclinical in vivo validation, and a human feasibility demonstration using the benchtop BIC system connected to externalized leads.&#xD;Results: Comprehensive benchtop characterization of the BIC device independently validated previously reported performance while extending its evaluation through quantification of recording noise, acquisition and stimulation latencies, and validation of impedance measurements. Long-term in vivo validation in five canines, with the longest implantation exceeding three years, demonstrated stable chronic recordings while also capturing progressive deterioration of individual channels through longitudinal changes in signal quality and impedance. The ecosystem enabled active functional decoding more than two years after implantation, closed-loop stimulation using arbitrary spectral biomarkers, capturing brain stimulation evoked potentials, detection of epilepsy-associated biomarkers and observational assessment of their response to stimulation. In addition, we translated an established one-dimensional BCI cursor-control paradigm to the BIC benchtop system, demonstrating ecosystem portability and feasibility with a human participant. Finally, we share surgical protocols, imaging and analysis pipelines, datasets, and software to facilitate reproducible neuromodulation research.&#xD;Significance: We present a versatile, open-source translational ecosystem that supports a wide range of neuromodulation and implantable BCI applications with minimal modification. By combining comprehensive hardware characterization with standardized software tools and experimental workflows, this work provides a practical reference for researchers adopting the BIC platform while establishing a broadly applicable framework for systematic characterization of implantable neuromodulation systems. The ecosystem lowers technical barriers to implantable neurotechnology research, promotes reproducibility, and provides a foundation for accelerating the development and clinical translation of next-generation adaptive neuromodulation and implantable BCI therapies for patients with neurological and psychiatric disorders.

Functional characterization and pharmacochaperone rescue of a novel SLC6A1 variant associated with developmental epileptic encephalopathies.推荐
PMID 43019163 · Scientific reports · 2026 10 07
作者：Song ZD, DeLeeuw MB, Mandelenaki D, Shen W et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
摘要：UNLABELLED: Mutations in SLC6A1, encoding the γ-aminobutyric acid (GABA) transporter 1 (GAT-1), are associated with a spectrum of neurodevelopmental disorders, including epilepsy syndromes, intellectual disability (ID), and autism. An important question is whether the impact of these mutations can be predicted at scale using artificial intelligence (AI), and how such predictions correlate with experimental findings. In this study, AI was explored as a predictive tool, not as a replacement for data collected experimentally. We reported clinical presentations including seizures and EEG features, autism related phenotypes and motor skills in a novel variant c.1069G > A (p.Ala357Thr, A357T) in two siblings. We used both AI tools and experimental approaches to characterize this novel mutation in the SLC6A1 gene and compared it with another variant in the same amino acid, c.1070 C > T (p.Ala357Val, A357V). Both AI and experimental approaches indicate that the GAT-1(A357T) and (A357V) mutations destabilize the global protein conformation and have increased localization inside the endoplasmic reticulum (ER). Additionally, the presence of the mutant GAT-1 made the wildtype GAT-1 less mature. Radioactive 3H-labeled GABA uptake assay indicated the mutation drastically reduced the function of the GAT-1(A357T) compared with the wildtype across cell types and pharmacochaperones such as 4-phenylbutyrate rescued the trafficking and function of the mutant GAT-1. Common and differential clinical phenotypes were identified in the siblings carrying the same variant. Mutant GAT-1 caused increased ER retention and impaired the trafficking of wild type GAT-1. PBA treatment rescued the mutant GAT-1 protein expression and GABA uptake function. AI-assisted in silico protein structural modeling and experimental approaches consistently suggest that the A357T and A357V variants destabilize global GAT-1 protein conformation. AI-assisted protein stability predictions provide supportive structural insight but cannot replace experimental assessment of GAT-1 expression and function. Hand-Arm Bimanual Intensive Therapy Including Lower Extremities in Preschoolers With Bilateral Cerebral Palsy: A Randomized Clinical Trial.推荐
PMID 43018972 · JAMA network open · 2026 11 02
作者：Araneda R, Ebner-Karestinos D, Paradis J, Saussez G et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
摘要：IMPORTANCE: Intensive interventions for children with cerebral palsy (CP) have traditionally focused on the upper extremities, despite frequent impairments in gross motor function and the critical period of motor development occurring before age 5 years. Hand-Arm Bimanual Intensive Therapy Including Lower Extremities (HABIT-ILE) has demonstrated efficacy in improving manual dexterity and gross motor function in school-aged children, but evidence in preschool children with bilateral CP remains limited. OBJECTIVE: To determine whether early HABIT-ILE improves gross motor function more than usual unstructured motor activities in preschool-aged children with bilateral CP. DESIGN, SETTING, AND PARTICIPANTS: This multicenter randomized clinical trial was conducted between December 2019 and December 2021. Recruitment was from European university hospitals, CP-specialized centers, and spontaneous applications at 4 sites: Brussels, Belgium; Brest and Angers, France; and Pisa, Italy. Children aged 12 to 59 months with spastic or dyskinetic bilateral CP who were able to follow instructions were included. Exclusion criteria were uncontrolled seizures, scheduled botulinum toxin injections or orthopedic surgery during the 6 months before or during the study period, severe visual or cognitive impairments, and contraindications to magnetic resonance imaging. Data were analyzed in May 2024. INTERVENTIONS: Children were randomly assigned (1:1) to receive 2 weeks of usual motor activity, including usual rehabilitation (control group), or 2 weeks (50 hours) of HABIT-ILE (HABIT-ILE group). MAIN OUTCOMES AND MEASURES: The primary outcome was Gross Motor Function Measure-66 (GMFM-66; range, 0-100, with lower scores indicating lesser gross motor ability). Secondary outcomes included Both Hand Assessment (BoHA), Melbourne Assessment 2, Pediatric Evaluation of Disability Inventory-Computer Adaptive Test (PEDI-CAT), and Canadian Occupational Performance Measure (COPM). Assessments were performed at baseline (immediately before intervention) and 2 weeks and 3 months after baseline. RESULTS: Among 49 preschool children with CP (mean [SD] age, 40.18 [11.53] months; 18 girls [36.7%] and 31 boys [63.3%]), Gross Motor Function Classification levels were I in 5 children, II in 14 children, III in 17 children, and IV or V in 13 children and Mini-Manual Ability Classification levels were I in 10 children, II in 27 children, III in 7 children, and IV or V in 6 children. Change in mean (SD) GMFM-66 score from baseline to 3 months was significantly greater in the HABIT-ILE group (5.01 [2.52] logits) than the control group (0.70 [1.98] logits) (adjusted mean difference [aMD] in score, 4.33 logits; 95% CI, 3.03-5.62 logits; P < .001). Likewise, mean (SD) scores favored the HABIT-ILE group vs the control group for secondary outcomes of BoHA (5.32 [4.47] vs 0.13 [5.16]; aMD, 5.15; 95% CI, 2.23-8.08; P < .001), PEDI-CAT mobility domain (1.70 [2.18] vs -0.40 [4.38]; aMD, 2.27; 95% CI, 0.20-4.33; P = .03), and COPM performance (4.22 [1.61] vs 1.14 [1.35]; aMD, 3.55; 95% CI, 2.86-4.24; P < .001) and satisfaction (4.37 [1.38] vs 1.15 [1.63]; aMD, 3.11; 95% CI, 2.25-3.97; P < .001). CONCLUSIONS AND RELEVANCE: In this randomized clinical trial, early HABIT-ILE significantly improved upper and lower extremities motor performance in preschool-aged children with bilateral CP at 3 months after the intervention. These findings suggest that HABIT-ILE is effective in improving gross motor function in preschool-aged children with bilateral CP. TRIAL REGISTRATION: Clinicaltrial.gov Identifier: NCT04020354.
Sudden Unexpected Death in Epilepsy: Mechanisms, Biomarkers, and Unanswered Questions.推荐
PMID 43018833 · Current neurology and neuroscience reports · 2026 11 12
作者：Odom N, Fernandez LA, Bateman LM
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：综述
摘要：PURPOSE OF REVIEW: Sudden unexpected death in epilepsy (SUDEP) remains the leading cause of epilepsy-related mortality. While heterogeneous in its pathophysiology, a significant proportion of cases are likely attributable to cardiorespiratory collapse following a convulsive seizure. We synthesize recent mechanistic, biomarker, and prevention literature, moving from retrospective risk inference toward individualized action. RECENT FINDINGS: Human and animal data support a multiple hit model, in which seizure-induced cardiorespiratory dysfunction is necessary but not necessarily sufficient for death: the environment, genetic susceptibility, sleep and circadian state, and potentially as yet unidentified factors determine which seizures prove fatal. The Mortality in Epilepsy Monitoring Units study (MORTEMUS) provided key insights into SUDEP pathophysiology, while multicenter prospective studies continue to examine SUDEP risk factors and mechanisms, and elucidate candidate biomarkers for individual risk stratification. Prevention is slowly moving beyond mainstays of nocturnal supervision and antiseizure therapies, guided by data from animal models of seizure-associated death. Emerging technologies such as seizure detection devices are being widely deployed to enhance protection, though none has been shown in a randomized trial to reduce SUDEP. Efforts toward universal SUDEP education and counseling not only address legal and moral disclosure responsibilities, but encourage patient autonomy and informed decision-making for individual risk reduction. The decade following publication of the MORTEMUS study continues to provide new insights into SUDEP pathophysiology, propose novel candidate biomarkers and offer potential intervention targets. While obstacles remain in the replication of putative clinical biomarkers and the translation of promising findings from animal models, enhancing SUDEP knowledge and effective patient counseling are critical to improving outcomes and saving lives.
A case of infant-type hemispheric glioma, ALK-altered, with a 23-year long-term follow-up.浏览
PMID 43018828 · Brain tumor pathology · 2026 11 12
作者：Taomoto R, Aoki M, Tajiri T, Enomoto T et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
摘要：At 3 months of age, a tumorous lesion was detected extending from the right temporal lobe to the occipital lobe, and a craniotomy with tumor resection was performed. The pathological diagnosis was glioblastoma (WHO2000). At 1 year and 5 months of age, a second craniotomy with tumor resection was performed for tumor recurrence, and the diagnosis was recurrent glioblastoma (WHO2000). Chemotherapy was administered for 2 years, after which the patient remained tumor-free. At age 23, following a seizure, a hemorrhagic lesion was identified within the resection cavity; however, the hematoma resolved with conservative management. No tumor recurrence was detected on imaging. Re-examination of tissue from 23 years prior revealed findings suggestive of high-grade glioma, low-grade glioma, and ganglioglioma. Immunohistochemistry showed ALK positivity, and FISH demonstrated ALK split positivity, leading to a diagnosis of infant-type hemispheric glioma, ALK-altered. This is a novel tumor type first described in the WHO CNS 5th edition (2021). Compared to high-grade glioma in older children, survival is expected to be longer, and ALK-rearranged tumors have been reported to show a relatively favorable prognosis trend, although many aspects remain unclear. We report this valuable case of infant-type hemispheric glioma, which encompasses an exceptionally long follow-up period.
TARS-EEG: a tropical algebraic representation for EEG seizure detection.推荐
PMID 43018671 · Biomedizinische Technik. Biomedical engineering · 2026 11 13
作者：Rajeev A, Kumar S
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
摘要：OBJECTIVES: To propose an interpretable tropical algebraic signal representation, named TARS-EEG, for EEG-based seizure detection by modelling short EEG segments through max-plus and min-plus waveform-dominance profiles. METHODS: Short EEG segments from the Epileptic Seizure Recognition dataset were mapped into multiscale tropical dilation, erosion, gradient, and residue profiles. A Tropical Seizure Index was introduced to quantify normalized local waveform-dominance energy. The extracted tropical features were evaluated using standard classifiers with recording-group-aware train-test analysis, recording-group-aware five-fold cross-validation, and ablation experiments. RESULTS: The proposed TARS-EEG representation achieved stable seizure/non-seizure classification performance across standard classifiers. Under recording-group-aware five-fold cross-validation, MLP achieved the highest mean accuracy of 97.70 %, while XGBoost achieved the highest ROC-AUC of 0.9926. Ablation analysis showed that the complete multiscale tropical representation performed better than individual neighbourhood-scale representations. CONCLUSIONS: TARS-EEG provides a compact and interpretable algebraic representation for EEG seizure detection. The results suggest that multiscale max-plus and min-plus waveform descriptors can capture seizure-related local waveform dominance effectively.

Institutional Variation in Electroencephalography Utilization in Neonates With Complex Congenital Heart Disease Across the United States and Its Association With Outcomes.推荐
PMID 43018035 · Journal of the American Heart Association · 2026 11 12
作者：Aly S, Elsisy MF, Qattea I, Newburger JW et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
摘要：BACKGROUND: Neonates with complex congenital heart disease are at increased risk for perioperative brain injury and seizures. Electroencephalography (EEG) detects clinical and subclinical seizures, but national utilization patterns remain poorly characterized. METHODS: We conducted a retrospective cohort study using the Pediatric Health Information System (2016-2025) of neonates ≤28 days with dextro-transposition of the great arteries or single-ventricle physiology undergoing surgical repair or palliation. Mixed-effects logistic regression with hospital as a random effect identified predictors of EEG utilization. RESULTS: Among 13 202 neonates, 1654 (12.5%) underwent EEG monitoring. EEG monitoring was performed in only 10.8% of neonates with dextro-transposition of the great arteries, 16% of those with hypoplastic left heart syndrome, and 13% of those with non- hypoplastic left heart syndrome single-ventricle lesions. EEG utilization increased from ~6% in 2016 to >14% in 2025, while seizure detection among EEG-monitored patients increased from 13% to ~26% (both P-trend<0.01). Utilization varied substantially across hospitals (0% to >40%), persisting after adjustment for surgical volume. EEG-monitored neonates had higher seizure detection (21.8% versus 5.2%), longer length of stay (33 versus 24 days), and higher hospitalization costs ($344 719 versus $174 865; all P<0.01). In multivariable analysis, EEG utilization was independently associated with seizures (OR ~4), dextro-transposition of the great arteries, and hypoplastic left heart syndrome, but not with cardiac arrest, extracorporeal membrane oxygenation, or hypoxic-ischemic encephalopathy. CONCLUSIONS: EEG utilization in neonates with complex congenital heart disease is increasing but remains low and highly variable across US centers. The association of EEG use with higher mortality and costs may reflect confounding by indication or illness severity. Standardized protocols are needed to optimize neurologic monitoring and improve long-term outcomes.
