Efficacy of Repetitive Transcranial Magnetic Stimulation in Preventing Poststroke Spasticity: A Sham-Controlled Trial.推荐
PMID 43017772 · Stroke · 2026 11 12
作者：Mupparaju VD, Bhoi SK, Jha M, Samal P et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
摘要：BACKGROUND: Poststroke spasticity significantly impairs recovery, yet preventive strategies remain limited. This study evaluated the effectiveness of repetitive transcranial magnetic stimulation (rTMS) in reducing the development and severity of spasticity in patients with early subacute stroke, offering a possible early intervention. METHODS: In this double-blind, randomized, sham-controlled trial, 70 patients with early subacute stroke (7-30 days) were randomized to receive 3 sessions of low-frequency (1 Hz, 1000 pulses at 90% resting motor threshold) rTMS over contralesional primary motor cortex (n=35) or sham stimulation (n=35), alongside standard care. The primary outcome was upper-limb spasticity assessed using the modified Modified Ashworth Scale. Secondary outcomes included handgrip strength, muscle power assessed by the Medical Research Council scale, pain assessed by the Visual Analogue Scale, modified Rankin Scale, and Stroke Impact Scale 3.0. RESULTS: rTMS therapy significantly lowered spasticity scores at 1 and 3 months across all assessed upper-limb joints (P=0.001), improved handgrip strength (P=0.01), and prevented pain (P=0.001). Stroke Impact Scale scores at 1 month showed greater gains in activities of daily living, mobility, hand function, social participation, and recovery in the rTMS group (P=0.001). Functional independence (modified Rankin Scale) was significantly better in the rTMS group (P=0.006) at 3 months. On binary logistic regression, sham stimulation was associated with greater odds of developing spasticity at 1 month (shoulder odds ratio [OR], 8.29 [95% CI, 1.95-35.27], P=0.04; elbow OR, 7.62 [95% CI, 1.69-34.35], P=0.08; wrist OR, 5.18 [95% CI, 1.60-16.78], P=0.06). At 3 months, sham stimulation remained predictive, with higher odds of spasticity at the shoulder (OR, 15.3 [95% CI, 8.26-28.43]), elbow (OR, 13.43 [95% CI, 3.94-45.78]), wrist (OR, 19.9 [95% CI, 4.9-80.96]), and fingers (OR, 26.25 [95% CI, 5.19-132.87], all P=0.001). Minor adverse events like headache and light-headedness were observed in 5 patients. CONCLUSIONS: Low-frequency rTMS over contralesional primary motor cortex significantly prevented spasticity, improved motor recovery, reduced pain, improved functional outcomes and quality of life in early subacute stroke, supporting its role as a promising adjunctive therapy in early stroke rehabilitation. REGISTRATION: URL: https://ctri.nic.in/; Unique identifier: CTRI/2024/01/061225.
Fulminant pancreatic encephalopathy with brain edema and herniation in severe hypertriglyceridemia-induced pancreatitis: a case report.推荐
PMID 43017420 · Frontiers in medicine · 2026 10 28
作者：Aboudargham N, El Akach N, Hamieh N, Rammal A et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：病例报告
摘要：Pancreatic encephalopathy (PE) is a rare but potentially devastating neurological complication of severe acute pancreatitis. Its manifestations range from confusion and agitation to seizures, coma, and cerebral edema, often making the diagnosis challenging due to its overlap with other causes of encephalopathy. We report a 23-year-old woman with hypertriglyceridemia-induced acute pancreatitis who rapidly progressed to severe acute pancreatitis with multiorgan failure. Within 48 h of admission, she developed agitation, seizure-like activity, and coma. Neuroimaging demonstrated diffuse cerebral and cerebellar edema, features of elevated intracranial pressure, cerebellar tonsillar herniation, and extensive restricted diffusion. After evaluation of alternative etiologies, the neurological deterioration was attributed to PE. Despite aggressive medical management, including insulin therapy, renal replacement therapy, mechanical ventilation, and hyperosmolar therapy, she did not show any signs of neurological recovery. This case demonstrates that PE may rarely progress to malignant cerebral edema and tonsillar herniation. New neuropsychiatric manifestations in patients with severe acute pancreatitis should prompt urgent neurological assessment and early neuroimaging whenever clinically feasible.
Epilepsy genetics in Africa: a systematic review.推荐
PMID 43017388 · Frontiers in genetics · 2026 10 28
作者：Bamba S, Yalcouyé A, Cissé L, Kamuli S et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：综述
摘要：BACKGROUND: While our understanding of genetic epilepsies is growing, data from Africa remain scant. In this study, we reviewed the profile of epilepsy genetics in Africa. METHODS: We searched for studies in PubMed, Scopus, Google Scholar, and African Journals Online up to December 2025 using a combination of terms related to "epilepsy", "genetics", and "Africa", and extracted data including phenotype and genetic diagnostics. RESULTS: Seventy-eight studies from 12 countries met our criteria, encompassing 1,858 individuals (1,339 unaffected, 519 affected) from 384 families. The vast majority of families were reported from three countries, Egypt (144/384, 37.5%), South Africa (76/384, 19.8%), and Morocco (60/384, 15.6%). Generalized epilepsy was the most common presentation (30.5%), followed by focal (18.5%), and unspecified (10.4%). Exome sequencing was used in 71.8% of studies and functional validation was performed in 17.9% of studies (n = 14/78), including cell-based models (12.8%, n = 10/78) and animal models (5.1%, n = 4/78). A total of 165 unique diagnostic genes were reported. Genetic heterogeneity was high, with only 12 genes shared across three or more countries. Of the 384 families, 76.3% (n = 293/384) had pathogenic or likely pathogenic variants, with the remaining 23.7% (n = 91/384) having VUSs in genes consistent with the seizure phenotype. Six founder variants accounted for 10.7% of the families, 62.0% of variants were novel at the time of reporting, and 67.8% were classified as pathogenic or likely pathogenic. Consanguinity was 45.6% with autosomal recessive the most common inheritance pattern (49.5%). CONCLUSION: This study highlights substantial clinical and genetic heterogeneity of epilepsy in Africa with population-specific founder variants. Notably, only a limited number of African countries (12/54) have published on molecular diagnostics for epilepsy. The data suggest the potential for significant discoveries yet to be made but also point to ongoing disparities in the availability of genomic testing.
Pediatric psychotropic prescribing and polypharmacy, 2019-2022: a retrospective electronic health record study in Qatar.浏览
PMID 43017114 · Frontiers in psychiatry · 2026 10 28
作者：Abo El-Ella DM, Dhieb D, Kanan Y, Hamad S et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
摘要：BACKGROUND: Real-world evidence on pediatric psychotropic prescribing and polypharmacy in Gulf health systems remains limited. Characterizing medication-class patterns and multi-class prescribing is essential for medication safety surveillance in children and adolescents. This study examined psychotropic prescribing, potential polypharmacy, and recorded medication combinations among children and adolescents using public-sector electronic health records in Qatar. METHODS: A retrospective observational study used electronic health record data from Hamad Medical Corporation and the Primary Health Care Corporation, Qatar's principal public healthcare providers, from January 2019 to December 2022. Children and adolescents aged 0-17 years with at least one psychotropic prescription were included. Medications were classified as antidepressants, antipsychotics, anxiolytics, mood stabilizers, or attention-deficit/hyperactivity disorder (ADHD) medications. Potential cross-class polypharmacy was assessed as the recording of two or more medication classes per patient during the study period; available duration information did not permit confirmation of concurrent exposure. Logistic regression examined patient-level associations with this outcome. RESULTS: The dataset included 2,941 eligible prescription records corresponding to 2,221 unique patients. The proportion of prescriptions for ADHD medications decreased from 28.7% in 2019 to 13.7% in 2022, whereas the proportion assigned to anxiolytics increased from 26.0% to 37.5%. ADHD medications predominated among prescriptions for children aged 5-11 years, while antidepressants and antipsychotics predominated among those for adolescents aged 12-17 years. The potential-polypharmacy criterion was met by 322 patients (14.5%), reaching 21.6% among adolescents. In a gender-adjusted model restricted to patients aged 1-17 years, each additional year of age was associated with higher odds of meeting the criterion (adjusted OR 1.13, 95% CI 1.10-1.16). After excluding patients with prescription-linked seizure disorder or cerebral palsy, the corresponding proportion was 16.96% (259/1,527). Among patients meeting the criterion, antidepressant-antipsychotic and ADHD medication-antipsychotic pairs were most frequent; methylphenidate-risperidone was the most common recorded individual-drug pair. CONCLUSIONS: This study characterizes pediatric psychotropic prescribing and potential polypharmacy in Qatar's public healthcare records and helps address the limited regional evidence from Gulf health systems. The observed patterns identify potential medication-safety surveillance priorities, particularly among adolescents and patients with antipsychotic-containing medication histories. Further assessment incorporating exposure duration and clinical outcomes is needed to establish concurrency and clinical significance.
Admission patterns and clinical management of rare diseases in 46 pediatric intensive care units across China: a multicenter survey.浏览
PMID 43016923 · Frontiers in pediatrics · 2026 10 28
作者：Chen D, Wen G, Xu S, Zhang Q

摘要：OBJECTIVE: To investigate the admission status of pediatric patients with rare diseases in pediatric intensive care units (PICUs) across China, to provide an empirical basis for optimizing critical care management, resource allocation, and healthcare policy development for rare pediatric diseases. METHODS: An online questionnaire survey was administered to gather cross-sectional data on the admission, diagnosis, treatment, and resource requirements of pediatric patients with rare diseases. A total of 65 questionnaires were returned from all invited member institutions of the Pediatric Critical Care Branch of the Chinese Medical Doctor Association across 30 provinces/municipalities; after exclusion of invalid questionnaires, 46 PICUs across 20 Chinese provincial-level regions constituted the final analytic sample. CONCLUSION: Pediatric rare diseases frequently manifest during infancy and early childhood with acute, life-threatening presentations requiring PICU admission. Currently, clinical management faces substantial bottlenecks, including prolonged diagnostic delays and restricted accessibility to orphan drugs. 摘要：BACKGROUND: Significant controversy exists regarding the optimal pharmacologic approach for endotracheal intubation in patients suffering from status epilepticus (SE).Study Aims: To evaluate post-hospitalization neurologic sequelae and other patient-centered outcomes in SE patients receiving neuromuscular blockers (NMBs). (Exposure: Paralytic selection during rapid sequence intubation - intervention (D-NMB) vs. control (ND-NMB)). 摘要：BACKGROUND: Outcome data for hemispheric low-grade glioma from resource-limited settings remain limited. This study describes surgical, seizure, and survival outcomes among patients treated at two tertiary centers in Ethiopia. OBJECTIVE: To describe surgical, seizure, and survival outcomes and explore factors associated with overall survival among patients undergoing surgery for hemispheric low-grade glioma. METHODS: We conducted a multicenter retrospective cohort study of patients who underwent surgery for hemispheric low-grade glioma at two tertiary hospitals in Addis Ababa, Ethiopia, between January 2015 and December 2020. Demographic, clinical, radiologic, operative, histopathologic, treatment, and follow-up data were analyzed. Overall survival was estimated using the Kaplan-Meier method. Associations with survival were explored using univariable Cox proportional hazards analyses. Given the small number of events, survival-factor analyses were considered exploratory. RESULTS: Thirty-four patients constituted the clinical cohort, with a median age of 39 years. Gross-total resection was performed in 23 (67.6%) patients. Thirty-one patients had sufficient information for time-to-event analysis, among whom 13 deaths were observed. No patients remained under observation at 60 months; therefore, a 5-year survival estimate was not reported. CONCLUSION: This multicenter cohort provides real-world surgical and survival data for hemispheric low-grade glioma care in Ethiopia. Larger prospective studies incorporating contemporary molecular diagnostics and standardized longitudinal follow-up are needed.
Nogo-related frontal theta-band activity after magnetic seizure therapy in treatment-resistant depression: an exploratory task-based EEG study.推荐
PMID 43016734 · Frontiers in psychiatry · 2026 10 28
作者：Yuan L, Chang F, Yang C, Yuan C et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
摘要：INTRODUCTION: Magnetic seizure therapy (MST) is an emerging convulsive treatment for treatment-resistant depression (TRD) with efficacy comparable to electroconvulsive therapy and superior cognitive safety. However, its neurophysiological mechanisms-particularly those underlying conflict monitoring and response inhibition-remain unclear. Frontal midline theta-band activity indexes anterior cingulate-prefrontal cognitive-control activity and may reflect inefficient neural processing in depression. METHODS: In this open-label, single-arm trial, 19 patients with TRD (16 female; mean age 20.89 ± 8.16 years; 10 of the 19 participants were minors) received 6-12 MST sessions using 100-Hz double-cone coil stimulation targeting bilateral F3/F4 frontal regions. Clinical assessments, including the 24-item Hamilton Depression Rating Scale (HAMD-24), Hamilton Anxiety Rating Scale (HAMA), Montgomery-Åsberg Depression Rating Scale (MADRS), and 64-channel Go/Nogo task-based electroencephalography (EEG), were performed before and after treatment. EEG outcome measures included event-related potentials (N2/P3), time-frequency power (theta/beta), microstate dynamics, and theta-beta cross-frequency coupling. RESULTS: Following MST, patients showed significant reductions in depressive symptoms (HAMD: 40.53 ± 10.25 to 22.53 ± 11.21; MADRS: 35.68 ± 8.84 to 20.68 ± 11.12). Response rates ranged from 42.1% to 57.9%. Behavioral data could not be formally analyzed due to incomplete data storage. At an uncorrected threshold, total Nogo-related theta-band activity (4-7 Hz) at Fz and FCz was lower after treatment; however, these effects did not survive FDR correction. ERP amplitudes did not change significantly. Exploratory microstate analysis showed nominal changes in Map4 AUC during Go processing and Map2 duration during Nogo processing; none survived FDR correction. These parameters should not be interpreted as reflecting changes in neural activation or inhibitory preparation. No significant changes were observed in theta-beta cross-frequency coupling or Go-condition theta power. DISCUSSION: These preliminary findings suggest that MST was associated with short-term symptom improvement and lower total Nogo-related theta-band activity at an uncorrected threshold. Because no control group, complete behavioral data, normative comparison, Time × Condition interaction, or EEG-clinical association was available, these findings cannot establish causal mechanisms, condition specificity, improved neural efficiency, or biomarker utility. Replication in adequately powered controlled studies with complete behavioral and longitudinal clinical measures is required.
Progressive multifocal leukoencephalopathy, bispecific antibodies, and multiple myeloma: a case report and systematic review of the literature.推荐
PMID 43016661 · Frontiers in oncology · 2026 10 28
作者：Tzanni M, Polyzoidis P, Angelopoulos K, Skafida A et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：系统综述
摘要：BACKGROUND: Bispecific antibodies (BsAbs) have transformed outcomes in relapsed/refractory multiple myeloma (RRMM) but carry a substantial, sustained infectious burden, including rare cases of progressive multifocal leukoencephalopathy (PML). METHODS: We present an index case of a patient with RRMM treated with BsAbs diagnosed with PML complicated by immune reconstitution inflammatory syndrome (PML-IRIS) and perform a systematic review of the literature contextualizing this case among all published reports of PML and PML-IRIS following BsAb therapy in multiple myeloma (MM). CASE PRESENTATION: We report a 65-year-old woman with triple-class-refractory λ light-chain MM who was sequentially treated with the anti-B-cell membrane antigen (BCMA)×CD3 BsAb elranatamab and, subsequently, the anti-GPRC5D×CD3 BsAb talquetamab. During the second cycle of talquetamab, she developed rapidly progressive confusion, visual disturbance, and generalized tonic-clonic seizures. Cerebrospinal fluid JC virus (JCV) polymerase chain reaction (PCR) was negative on two occasions despite a clinical and radiographic course highly suggestive of PML; brain biopsy confirmed PML-IRIS. Despite talquetamab discontinuation, IVIG, mirtazapine, and compassionate-use nivolumab, the patient achieved only transient neurological stabilization and died 2 months after presentation. RESULTS: A systematic review of the literature identified 16 additional sources describing 29 patients with PML or PML-IRIS following BsAb therapy for MM, confirmed on full-text review to represent distinct individuals with no duplication between reports. BCMA-directed constructs accounted for most implicated agents, and talquetamab was implicated only in patients with prior or concurrent BCMA-directed exposure. PML-IRIS was documented in two of these patients and could not be excluded in a third. Despite uniformly supportive management, PML-attributable mortality was 89% (25/28) among patients with a definitive reported outcome, including the index case. CONCLUSION: PML, and PML-IRIS specifically, is an emerging, likely class-level complication of BsAb therapy in MM. Clinicians should maintain a low threshold for neuroimaging and CSF JCV testing. These findings should be interpreted in light of the substantial clinical benefit that BsAbs provide in this heavily pretreated population.

A case of massive gamma-hydroxybutyrate overdose mimicking brain death.推荐
PMID 43016365 · Anaesthesia and intensive care · 2026 11 12
作者：Chua V, Balakumaran N, Neal J, Azad R et al.

命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
摘要：A 36-year-old female was brought into hospital following a massive overdose of 100 ml of gamma-hydroxybutyrate. Unlike prior case reports where ethanol was a co-ingestant with gamma-hydroxybutyrate or its precursor, gamma-hydroxybutyrate was believed to have been the only substance ingested in this case. The patient developed a severe and persistent high anion gap metabolic acidosis and required continuous renal replacement therapy. The patient developed fixed and dilated pupils early on in her clinical course and was found to have a transient loss of most brainstem reflexes on clinical examination, thus mimicking brain death. She also developed generalised tonic-clonic seizures and received midazolam and levetiracetam for seizure control. She was successfully extubated after 5 days on mechanical ventilation and was discharged home on day 11 with no neurological deficits.

Anticholinergic Burden in People With Intellectual Disability.浏览
PMID 43016194 · Journal of intellectual disability research : JIDR · 2026 11 11
作者：Lally N, Duffy J, McTigue Ó
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
摘要：BACKGROUND: Individuals with intellectual disability (ID) experience high rates of multimorbidity and psychotropic prescribing, placing them at greater risk of anticholinergic burden (ACB), which has been associated with adverse cognitive and physical health outcomes. However, data on ACB among people with an ID attending specialist mental health services remain limited. METHODS: A retrospective chart review was conducted for all adults with an ID (N = 159) attending a specialist mental health service. Demographic, clinical and prescribing data were extracted from clinical records. ACB was determined using the online ACB Calculator. Rates of psychiatric and physical health comorbidity and prescribing were examined. RESULTS: Elevated ACB was common, with 65% of service users recording clinically significant scores. ACB was higher in females and those in residential services and positively correlated with age and total medication burden but did not differ according to ID level. Polypharmacy was present in 57% of service users, and antipsychotics were the most frequently prescribed psychotropic medication (82%). Individuals with Down syndrome demonstrated lower ACB scores than those without. In multivariate analyses, the number of prescribed medications and antipsychotics independently predicted clinically significant ACB. CONCLUSIONS: Adults with an ID attending specialist mental health services experience substantial exposure to anticholinergic medications within the context of multimorbidity and polypharmacy. Elevated ACB appears to be driven by overall medication burden and psychotropic prescribing, particularly antipsychotic use. Routine assessment of ACB alongside individual-reported experiences and clinician-rated outcomes should form part of regular medication review processes to identify potentially modifiable prescribing risks and support safer pharmacological management. Future research should examine whether interventions that reduce ACB improve subjective, clinician-rated and broader health outcomes in ID.
Epileptiform Discharges in Seizure-Free Children with Autism Spectrum Disorder and the Role of Antiseizure Medications.推荐

作者：Al-Mosawi VS, Gargary KH
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
摘要：OBJECTIVE: To determine the frequency of epileptiform discharges (EPDs) in children with autism spectrum disorder (ASD) without clinical seizures, assess autism severity using the Childhood Autism Rating Scale, Second Edition (CARS-2), and reassess the children after treatment with antiseizure medications (ASMs). STUDY DESIGN: A prospective cohort study. Place and Duration of the Study: Four autism centres in Duhok City, Kurdistan Region, Iraq, from August 2024 to August 2025. METHODOLOGY: Fifty children with seizure-free ASD (aged 2.5-18 years) underwent clinical evaluation, electroencephalography (EEG) recording, and severity assessment using CARS-2. Children with EPDs were offered ASMs according to EEG pattern and clinical profile. EEG and CARS-2 assessments were repeated after 6 months. Data were analysed using SPSS version 31.0. Descriptive statistics were presented as means ± standard deviations for continuous variables and frequencies with percentages for categorical variables. For group comparisons, independent t-tests or Mann-Whitney U tests for small samples were used. A p-value <0.05 was considered statistically significant. RESULTS: EPDs were detected in 24 participants (48%). EPDs were focal in 19 cases (79.16) and generalised in 5 cases (20.83). Adherence to ASMs after 6 months was observed in 9 cases (37.5 %). When ASMs were used consistently, EEG improvements were observed, and ASD severity scores on the CARS-2 were reduced.   Key Words: Anti-seizure medications, Autism spectrum disorder, Electroencephalography, Epileptiform discharges.
Clinical characterization and genotype-phenotype correlations in Chilton-Okur-Chung syndrome.浏览
PMID 43015773 · BMC medical genomics · 2026 08 15
作者：Duz MB, Lasa-Aranzasti A, Cazurro-Gutiérrez A, Hackett A et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
摘要：BACKGROUND: Chilton-Okur-Chung neurodevelopmental syndrome is a rare disorder characterized by global developmental delay, variable intellectual disability, behavioral challenges, and structural brain anomalies caused by variants in CDC42BPB. CDC42BPB encodes MRCKβ, a ubiquitously expressed serine/threonine kinase involved in cytoskeletal regulation. The phenotypic spectrum and genotype-phenotype relationships remain incompletely characterized. METHODS: We aggregated data from 20 newly identified individuals and 17 previously reported individuals. We analyzed the data for genotype-phenotype correlations according to variant type. RESULTS: Developmental delay was the most common phenotype, affecting 85% of individuals, with early delays notable in walking and speech. Intellectual disability was observed in 62%. Behavioral diagnoses included autism spectrum disorder (68%) and ADHD (60%). Structural brain abnormalities were present in 48% of cases, predominantly affecting the cerebellum and ventricles. Dysmorphic features were present in 83% of cases. Hypotonia was present in 68%, seizures in 41%, and macrocephaly in 38%. Genotype-phenotype analysis indicated that predicted loss-of-function (LoF) variants were generally associated with milder neurodevelopmental features than missense variants. A male predominance was observed despite this being an autosomal dominant condition, suggesting a possible protective effect in females. Some heterozygous predicted LoF variants were inherited from self-reported unaffected parents or with subtle neurodevelopmental or cardiac features, supporting incomplete penetrance and variable expressivity. CONCLUSIONS: Our findings highlight the differences between predicted loss-of-function and missense variants. Future studies assessing biochemical differences in kinase activity and substrate specificity may explain these clinical differences.
Fructose-1,6-bisphosphatase deficiency initially diagnosed as acute necrotizing encephalopathy in a child: a case report.推荐
PMID 43015689 · BMC pediatrics · 2026 08 17
作者：Ling W, Xu C, Meng X, Tang J et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：病例报告
摘要：BACKGROUND: Fructose-1,6-bisphosphatase (FBP1) deficiency is a rare inherited defect of gluconeogenesis that can cause fasting- or infection-triggered hypoglycemia, lactic acidosis, and multisystem biochemical disturbances. Acute metabolic crises may produce symmetric deep gray-matter or diffuse cortical abnormalities on neuroimaging, mimicking infection-triggered encephalopathy syndromes such as acute necrotizing encephalopathy (ANE). We report a school-aged patient whose presentation and early imaging suggested ANE but was ultimately explained by genetically confirmed fructose-1,6-bisphosphatase deficiency. CASE PRESENTATION: A previously healthy 7-year-old girl was found in a coma with ongoing generalized seizures and fever. Her fingerstick glucose was 1.8 mmol/L. Clinical tests showed metabolic acidosis (pH 7.22), significant hyperlactatemia, hyperuricemia, low sodium levels, and high transaminases and lactate dehydrogenase. Initial brain magnetic resonance imaging (MRI) showed mild bilateral thalamic hyperintensity, with high levels of Interleukin-6 (IL-6) and Interleukin-8 (IL-8) in the cerebrospinal fluid, prompting empiric treatment with high-dose corticosteroids, intravenous immunoglobulin, intrathecal corticosteroids, tocilizumab and intensive supportive care. The patient improved after metabolic correction. A follow-up MRI on day 7 revealed extensive bilateral hemispheric abnormalities atypical for necrotizing encephalopathy. Infectious causes were ruled out. Whole-exome sequencing identified a homozygous c.490G > A (p.Gly164Ser) mutation in the FBP1 gene; both parents were heterozygous, and a sibling was also homozygous. Treatment focused on prompt glucose administration, correcting acidosis, and dietary management. The patient was discharged on day 22 in stable condition, with complete resolution of acute abnormal signals, and remained asymptomatic at six months. CONCLUSIONS: Fructose-1,6-bisphosphatase deficiency can present beyond infancy and mimic necrotizing encephalopathy. Recognition of severe hypoglycemia, lactic acidosis, and multisystem abnormalities should prompt immediate metabolic stabilization and targeted genetic testing to avoid misdiagnosis and unnecessary immunomodulatory therapy.

摘要：BACKGROUND: Postmarketing data for adjunctive brivaracetam (BRV) suggest potential benefit in earlier antiseizure medication (ASM) regimens. OBJECTIVE: We aimed to evaluate real-world effectiveness of BRV in earlier ASM therapy lines (over ~12 months) in patients with focal seizures with/without focal to bilateral tonic-clonic seizures (primary objective) and assess patient characteristics, health outcomes, and tolerability. METHODS: Prospective, postmarketing, noninterventional study of adjunctive BRV in Europe/Canada (December 2020-January 2024; BRITOBA/EP0103). Selection criteria are adults (≥ 18 years) with history of focal seizures, no prior BRV treatment, and ≤ 3 lifetime (prior/at BRV initiation) ASMs when initiating adjunctive BRV. RESULTS: A total of 392 patients received ≥ 1 BRV dose (mean age 44.9 years; mean lifetime ASMs 2.0). Of 298 patients, 41.9% were seizure-free for ≥ 6 consecutive months during up to ~12 months' observation. Of 392 patients, 13.8% experienced ≥ 1 drug-related treatment-emergent adverse event (TEAE), 9.4% discontinued BRV due to TEAEs. CONCLUSIONS: In adults with focal seizures, BRV in earlier ASM regimens was effective and well tolerated. At 12 months, there were improvements in quality of life, clinical condition, work productivity, and treatment satisfaction.
SySCOM: epileptogenic focus localisation using synthetic interictal SPECT images in SISCOM.推荐
PMID 43015500 · EJNMMI physics · 2026 08 16
作者：Fard AS, Reutens DC, Ramsay SC, Phamnguyen J et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
摘要：INTRODUCTION: Interictal SPECT is a critical component of SISCOM analysis for presurgical localisation of epileptogenic regions. However, its clinical reliability can be affected by uncertainty regarding whether the interictal scan is a true representation of a seizure free baseline (i.e., the interictal state). In addition, most patients will also have an interictal FDG PET scan and MRI performed, and it would be useful if the number of scans required could be reduced. This study explores the use of machine learning synthesised interictal SPECT within the SISCOM framework (i.e., SySCOM) as an alternative baseline to the acquired interictal SPECT scan. METHODS: PET scans from 40 patients with refractory focal epilepsy were used to train a three-dimensional generative adversarial network to synthesise interictal SPECT images. Synthesis performance was evaluated on an independent validation cohort of nine unseen patients using standard image quality metrics (i.e., SSIM, PSNR, RMSE). Both real and synthetic interictal SPECT images were used to generate SISCOM outputs following standard clinical protocols. Five clinicians experienced in the interpretation of SPECT images in epilepsy performed blinded visual localisation of regions of hyperperfusion across 18 predefined anatomical regions. Observer based similarity, agreement, and reproducibility between SISCOM and SySCOM were assessed. RESULTS: Synthetic interictal SPECT images demonstrated high similarity to real images (mean SSIM = 0.94, mean PSNR = 27.9 dB, mean RMSE = 0.041). Visual localisation based comparison of SISCOM with SySCOM demonstrated good agreement between the methods (Dice score = 70%), with an overall 89% agreement across all subjects and regions. Individual reader agreement between sessions remained high, indicating stable interpretation across image types. CONCLUSION: Machine learning-generated interictal SPECT can be used to produce SySCOM, which is visually and quantitatively comparable to SISCOM, as confirmed by expert readers. The findings demonstrate consistent localisation patterns, high observer agreement, and stable reader reproducibility. This work addresses uncertainty associated with confirming a true seizure-free baseline during interictal SPECT acquisition. SySCOM can therefore be considered a feasible approach that requires further clinical validation before routine clinical implementation.

CASE PRESENTATION: A 31-year-old woman with a 16-year history of schizophrenia remained stable on clozapine (300 mg/day) after failing to respond to multiple antipsychotics. Recurrent hip dislocation in an adult patient with trisomy 21: a case report.浏览
PMID 43014786 · The journal of medical investigation : JMI · 2026
作者：Sugimine Y, Wada K, Shigekiyo S, Tamaki Y et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：病例报告
摘要：BACKGROUND: Hip instability in individuals with trisomy 21 commonly presents as habitual dislocation or progressive subluxation, whereas recurrent dislocation is extremely rare. Only a few reports have described recurrent hip dislocation in skeletally mature patients. Furthermore, the role of acetabular osteotomy remains unclear. CASE PRESENTATION: We report a 22-year-old man with trisomy 21 who developed recurrent posterior hip dislocation following a seizure-related fall. Radiographs and computed tomography revealed severe acetabular dysplasia (Tönnis angle, 28º;acetabular version, 0°) with femoral anteversion of 57°. Navigation-assisted eccentric rotational acetabular osteotomy was performed;however, a posterior column fracture occurred intraoperatively. Although postoperative radiographs demonstrated improved acetabular orientation with a Tönnis angle of 3º and acetabular version of 15°, posterior subluxation recurred 6 months after surgery. The posterior column fracture healed without displacement, but residual ligamentous laxity and femoral deformity likely contributed to persistent instability. Despite radiographic progression of osteoarthritis at 1 year, the patient remained asymptomatic. CONCLUSION: Recurrent hip dislocation in adults with trisomy 21 typically results from a multifactorial combination of acetabular dysplasia, femoral anteversion, and ligamentous laxity. When acetabular osteotomy is considered, careful assessment of posterior column thickness and meticulous osteotomy planning are essential to avoid intraoperative fracture and optimize postoperative stability. J. Med. Invest. 73 : 519-524, August, 2026.

[Epilepsy and Alcohol].推荐
PMID 43014721 · Brain and nerve = Shinkei kenkyu no shinpo · 2026 Nov
作者：Akamatsu N
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：综述
摘要：The association between alcohol consumption and epilepsy is both complex and clinically significant. Acute alcohol intake suppresses central nervous system activity, while abrupt cessation of chronic heavy drinking can trigger rebound neuronal hyperexcitability, resulting in alcohol withdrawal seizures. In patients with epilepsy, heavy or binge drinking may increase seizure risk, particularly when concurrent with sleep deprivation, poor antiseizure medication adherence, dehydration, nutritional disturbances, or metabolic abnormalities. Alcohol-related seizures must be distinguished from alcohol withdrawal seizures, seizures associated with underlying epilepsy, and acute symptomatic seizures attributable to other causes, such as head trauma, cerebrovascular disease, infection, and metabolic disturbances. Therefore, clinical assessments should consider the amount and pattern of alcohol consumption, interval between the last drink and seizure onset, withdrawal symptoms, seizure characteristics, epilepsy history, medication adherence, and other potential precipitants. Benzodiazepines are the mainstay of acute management for alcohol withdrawal seizures, with treatment aiming to control withdrawal syndrome and prevent severe complications, in addition to preventing recurrent seizures. Although heavy alcohol consumption is associated with an increased risk of seizures and epilepsy, evidence regarding the effects of low-level alcohol consumption remains limited. Individualized risk assessment and counseling are essential in clinical practice.
[Effects of Alcohol on the Central Nervous System].浏览
PMID 43014720 · Brain and nerve = Shinkei kenkyu no shinpo · 2026 Nov
作者：Matsushita S
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：综述
摘要：Alcohol affects the central nervous system through acute and chronic mechanisms. The acute effects include intoxication and blackouts, whereas abrupt cessation or reduction in alcohol intake after chronic excessive consumption may result in alcohol-withdrawal syndrome, seizures, and alcohol-induced psychotic symptoms. Chronic excessive alcohol consumption may cause cognitive impairment and structural changes in the brain through direct neurotoxicity, nutritional deficiency, neuroinflammation, and oxidative stress. Neuroimaging studies have shown that alcohol use disorder is associated with reduced brain volume, and it may accelerate age-related brain changes, particularly in the frontal regions. Alcohol use disorder is also associated with an increased risk of dementia, particularly early-onset dementia. In contrast to progressive neurodegenerative disorders, such as Alzheimer's disease, alcohol-related cognitive impairment can partially improve with sustained abstinence or reduced alcohol consumption. Alcohol-related dementia is a clinically important but incompletely defined condition, and its diagnostic criteria, underlying pathophysiology, relationship to Wernicke-Korsakoff syndrome, and nosological status, are subject to ongoing debate.
Individualized and stereotypical seizure semiology in a porcine model of post-traumatic epilepsy.推荐
PMID 43014692 · Experimental neurology · 2026 11 11
作者：Pretell M, Rodriguez MG, Chen W, Escobosa A et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
摘要：Large-animal models of traumatic brain injury may yield translatable data on epileptogenesis, given their similarities in anatomy, brain size, and immune systems to humans. Adult male and female swine received bilateral cortical impact (N = 16) or sham surgery (N = 6) and were screened for convulsions via video-EEG for up to one year. Post-traumatic epilepsy (PTE) was defined as 2 seizures after 1-week post-injury. Eight out of sixteen pigs (50%) receiving bilateral cortical impact developed PTE, with an average latent period of 5 months (± 3.7). Seizures (N = 190) began focally, sometimes with motor onset including automatisms, before becoming generalized, with tonic-clonic or tonic convulsions. We defined a library differentiating peri-ictal behaviors (N = 31) from rhythmic/odd behaviors typical in healthy pigs (N = 12). Seizures had an average of 6.95 behaviors per seizure (max 26) lasting an average of 1.8 min (max 7.9). For seizures comprised of multiple convulsive episodes, the first convulsion had a greater number of peri-ictal behaviors than subsequent convulsions (P < 0.001). The array of peri-ictal behaviors displayed was moderately pig-specific, with many behaviors consistently observed across seizures. The seizure frequency detected was 0.43/day. This large-brain model of PTE exhibits a variable period of epileptogenesis, a substantial rate of PTE, and an expansive repertoire of ictal behaviors. This first description of semiology in this species will serve as a guide for other porcine epilepsy models. Biofidelic models of PTE are expected to increase our understanding of the pathophysiology, enabling the identification and testing of therapeutics that translate into human patients.
Disease-modifying therapies in development for post-traumatic epilepsy.推荐
PMID 43014691 · Experimental neurology · 2026 11 11
作者：Reddy DS
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：综述
摘要：Post-traumatic epilepsy (PTE) is a chronic neurological disorder that develops following traumatic brain injury (TBI), a condition affecting nearly 69 million people worldwide. PTE accounts for 20% of acquired epilepsies and is characterized by spontaneous recurrent seizures and long-term neurological deficits. Current antiseizure medications primarily suppress early seizures but do not prevent epileptogenesis or improve neurological dysfunction. This review critically evaluates the current landscape of disease-modifying therapies for PTE with an emphasis on pathophysiological basis, therapeutic pipeline and challenges in developing antiepileptogenic drugs for preventing PTE and improving long-term neurological outcomes after TBI. Epileptogenesis following TBI is driven by dynamic interactions among neuronal, glial, vascular, immune, metabolic, and epigenetic pathways that evolve from the acute injury phase through the latent period to chronic epilepsy. Consequently, therapeutic interventions differ in optimal timing and target objectives of acute neuroprotection, prevention of epileptogenesis, or modification of established epilepsy and its comorbidities. Promising disease-modifying approaches include epigenetic modulators, anti-inflammatory & immunomodulatory agents, JAK-STAT inhibitors, mTOR inhibitors, antioxidant & mitochondrial-targeted therapies, tau-directed interventions, adenosine augmentation, neurosteroids, and regenerative cell-based therapies. Among these, epigenetic therapy targeting histone deacetylases has emerged as a particularly attractive strategy. Pharmacological inhibition of histone deacetylase has demonstrated robust disease-modifying effects in experimental PTE by normalizing aberrant histone deacetylase activity, suppressing neuroinflammation, reducing seizure burden, and attenuating biomarkers of epileptogenic progression. Despite multiple approaches in development, no therapy has yet demonstrated clinical efficacy for preventing human PTE. Major translation barriers include the heterogeneity of PTE, limitations of experimental models, inconsistent biomarker validation, and challenges in clinical studies. Future progress will require standardized paradigms, rigorous preclinical evaluation, biomarker-guided clinical trials, and optimization of disease-modifying therapies capable of preventing epilepsy and improving long-term neurological outcomes after brain injury.
Status Epilepticus in a Patient Prescribed Theophylline: A Case Report.推荐
PMID 43014359 · Clinical practice and cases in emergency medicine · 2026 Nov
作者：Coyle C, Samones EJ, Phan T, Guptill M
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：病例报告
摘要：INTRODUCTION: Seizures are a frequent reason for emergency department (ED) evaluation, and new-onset events carry a wide differential in which medication toxicity can be overlooked. Toxicity from seldomly prescribed drugs, such as theophylline, can be particularly challenging to recognize because they can cause nonspecific adverse effects, including palpitations, nausea, vomiting, and seizures. Although theophylline use was widespread in the 1980s, current prescribing patterns are unknown. Nevertheless, theophylline toxicity continues to result in ED visits and poison center calls. Because adverse effects may be acute or chronic, identifying theophylline toxicity remains difficult. CASE REPORT: A 74-year-old woman presented to the ED with status epilepticus. After thorough history and extensive medical work-up, medication toxicity was suspected as the cause of her seizures. Upon further investigation, the patient was found to have theophylline toxicity. Prompt treatment was initiated resulting in cessation of seizures, highlighting the importance of considering medication toxicity in geriatric patients with new-onset seizures. CONCLUSION: Theophylline toxicity can present with vague, multisystem symptoms, with status epilepticus being one of its most severe complications. Rapid identification of the underlying toxic etiology is essential to guide treatment and stop the seizures. Moreover, clinical suspicion should be elevated in the geriatric population related to frequent polypharmacy. In this case, confirming the medication history with a family member raised concerns for medication misuse, enabling timely intervention. Although rarely prescribed, physicians should counsel patients on the risks and adverse effects of theophylline and emphasize close monitoring.
Cauda Equina Syndrome Secondary to Seizure-Induced Spinal Fracture During Hyperbaric Oxygen Therapy: A Case Report.推荐
PMID 43014352 · Clinical practice and cases in emergency medicine · 2026 Nov
作者：Aloise D, Nash J, Alfred G, Iqbal R et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：病例报告
摘要：INTRODUCTION: Hyperbaric oxygen therapy carries a risk of central nervous system oxygen toxicity, which can result in seizures. Seizure-induced spinal fractures are uncommon complications that occur during generalized convulsive activity. While often benign, lumbar vertebral body burst fractures may result in cauda equina syndrome. Additionally, patients with beta-thalassemia may have decreased bone mineral density due to ineffective erythropoiesis and bone marrow expansion increasing the risk for pathologic and traumatic fractures. CASE REPORT: A 35-year-old man with beta-thalassemia major presented to the emergency department after a witnessed, first-time generalized tonic-clonic seizure during hyperbaric oxygen therapy for chronic venous ulcers. Initial evaluation revealed severe low back pain and midline lumbar tenderness. Computed tomography revealed an acute third lumbar vertebra (L3) burst fracture with retropulsion. The patient rapidly developed progressive right lower-extremity weakness and acute urinary retention. Magnetic resonance imaging revealed 70% loss of height of L3, severe thecal sac stenosis at L2-L3 and L3-L4, and a ventral dural hematoma resulting in stenosis at L4-L5. The patient was diagnosed with cauda equina syndrome and underwent emergent neurosurgical decompression. CONCLUSION: This case represents a rare convergence of multiple complications: central nervous system oxygen toxicity during hyperbaric oxygen therapy, seizure-induced lumbar burst fracture with retropulsion, and traumatic cauda equina syndrome in a patient with beta thalassemia. Physicians must maintain a high index of suspicion for vertebral fractures and monitor for evolving neurological deficits when managing the patient with acute back pain following seizure activity.
Is it possible to vaccinate AI against bias? An exploratory study in epilepsy.推荐
PMID 43014131 · Epilepsy & behavior : E&B · 2026 11 10
作者：Bhansali RM, Westover MB, Goldenholz DM
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
摘要：Large language models (LLMs) may respond differently to clinical vignettes containing different patient descriptions. This exploratory proof-of-concept study examined whether an instruction to reconsider irrelevant information changed such responses in epilepsy. One diagnostic and one treatment vignette each had an affluent and a disadvantaged composite profile, differing jointly in sex and social descriptors. The reported design comprised six models, two prompt conditions, and ten repetitions per cell (480 responses). We retained the original binary reference-answer rubric and recalculated descriptive results from the reported aggregate table. Diagnostic accuracy under this rubric increased from 43/120 (35.8%) to 66/120 (55.0%), while the signed pooled accuracy gap decreased from 45.0 to 26.7 percentage points. Treatment accuracy increased from 61/120 (50.8%) to 75/120 (62.5%), while the pooled gap decreased from 25.0 to 8.3 points. Mean absolute model-level gaps decreased from 45.0 to 30.0 points for diagnosis and from 35.0 to 11.7 points for treatment. Individual models showed improvement, deterioration, or unchanged performance. Retained response collections and scoring records do not permit complete verification of all reported runs or model versions. These findings illustrate a method for examining prompt sensitivity; they do not isolate socioeconomic bias or establish a clinically effective debiasing intervention. Simple prompt-based interventions may offer a practical strategy to mitigate AI bias in epilepsy care.

摘要：OBJECTIVES: This is a scale-up report on the SMART (Self-management for people with epilepsy and a history of negative health events) epilepsy self-management (ESM) program. METHODS: Engagement in SMART was assessed via session attendance (maximum of 8). DISCUSSION: This analysis from a real-world, non-research study sample suggests scaling up ESM in an epilepsy-care clinical setting appears feasible, acceptable to PWE, and can improve outcomes for PWE with depression, anxiety and suboptimal quality of life. Effect of Campi the Seahorse video interventions on improving knowledge about epilepsy and reducing stigmatizing beliefs in primary school pupils.推荐
PMID 43014129 · Epilepsy & behavior : E&B · 2026 11 11
作者：Brabcová DB, Mašková I, Kohout J, Lohrová K
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
摘要：Children with epilepsy continue to face stigma and peer exclusion in school settings, highlighting the need for effective age-appropriate educational interventions. This study evaluated the effects at 6-month follow-up of Episodes 3 + 4 of the Campi the Seahorse educational video series in improving epilepsy knowledge and reducing stigma among primary school pupils aged 9-12. A total of 94 pupils received the Campi 3 + 4 intervention and completed measures of epilepsy knowledge and stigma at baseline and at a 6-month follow-up. Their outcomes were compared with data from 197 pupils who had previously received the Campi 1 + 2 video (n = 111) or a Campi-based read story intervention (n = 86). In the Campi 3 + 4 group, epilepsy knowledge increased significantly from baseline to follow-up (M = 7.14, SD = 2.42 vs. M = 9.36, SD = 3.37, p < 0.001, d = 0.75), with substantial improvements in knowledge of brain function, first aid, and seizure manifestations. ANCOVA controlling for baseline scores showed no significant differences in knowledge at the 6-month follow-up across the three interventions (p = 0.243). After adjustment for baseline stigma scores, the overall difference in stigma scores among the three intervention groups was statistically significant (p = 0.040), with the Campi 3 + 4 group showing the lowest adjusted mean. However, the multiplicity-adjusted pairwise comparisons were not significant (p > 0.05). Findings of this non-randomized study suggest that Campi Episodes 3 + 4 provide a brief educational intervention associated with improvements in epilepsy knowledge and lower stigma. Their focus on first aid, common misconceptions, and social inclusion may make them a relevant option when implementation of the complete four-episode series is not feasible.
Design, synthesis, and biological evaluation of novel Nav1.2 inhibitors as antiepileptic agents.推荐
PMID 43014116 · Bioorganic chemistry · 2026 11 09
作者：Wu J, Li M, Qian M, He L et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
摘要：Voltage-gated sodium channel Nav1.2 is a validated target for anticonvulsant therapy, yet Nav1.2 inhibitors remain scarce. To explore the unexplored N-4 position of the [1,2,4]triazolo[1,5-a]pyrimidine scaffold, we designed and synthesized two series of novel derivatives bearing diverse substituents at this position. Their Nav1.2 inhibitory activities were evaluated using FLIPR membrane potential and whole-cell patch-clamp assays, while their anticonvulsant efficacy and neurotoxicity were assessed in MES/ sc-PTZ seizure models and rotarod test, respectively. Structure-activity relationship (SAR) analysis revealed that hydrophobic volume complementarity, rather than aromaticity, is the primary driver of Nav1.2 potency. Compound 3b, bearing a simple n-butyl group at N-4, emerged as the most promising lead, exhibiting moderate Nav1.2 inhibition (88.9% at 30 μM; FLIPR IC50 = 11.55 μM; patch-clamp IC50 = 7.62 μM for the inactivated state) and exceptional in vivo efficacy (ED50 = 9.17 mg/kg, TD50 > 813.40 mg/kg, PI >88.70) in the sc-PTZ model. Compound 3y also demonstrated moderate Nav1.2 inhibition (69.8%, IC50 = 12.19 μM) and potent anticonvulsant activity (ED50 = 13.7 mg/kg, PI >23.1). Patch-clamp electrophysiology confirmed that 3b and 3y inhibit the inactivated state of Nav1.2 (IC50 = 7.62 μM and 8.31 μM, respectively) with negligible effect on the resting state. Molecular docking and MD simulations revealed that 3b binds within the Nav1.2 pore via hydrogen bonds, π-π stacking, and hydrophobic contacts. Tissue distribution studies demonstrated efficient blood-brain barrier penetration (brain-to-plasma ratio of 1.65 at 1 h post-dose). Although 3b showed limited stability in human and rat liver microsomes (T1/2 = 12.20 and 10.65 min, respectively), its potent in vivo efficacy and exceptionally high protective index support its continued optimization and preclinical development as a promising candidate for epilepsy therapy.
Frequency of occurrence, signalment, clinicopathologic and imaging findings, and outcomes with nontraumatic spontaneous intracranial hemorrhagic stroke in dogs.推荐
PMID 43013978 · Journal of the American Veterinary Medical Association · 2026 11 11
作者：Thibodeaux LA, Kent M, Bennett M, Barber R
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
摘要：OBJECTIVE: To describe the frequency, clinicopathologic and imaging findings, and outcomes in dogs with spontaneous intra-parenchymal hemorrhagic stroke (sIHS). ANIMALS: Retrospective study involving 25 dogs. CLINICAL PRESENTATION: Medical records were reviewed for signalment; neurological, clinicopathologic, and imaging findings; anatomic localization; comorbidities; therapy; and outcome in dogs with sIHS (2010 to 2024). Lesion number, location, and volume were recorded. Outcome was defined as excellent, good, or poor. Frequency was defined out of a contemporaneous population of dogs ≥ 6 years old with an acute presentation of neurological signs. RESULTS: Affected dogs were older, small- to medium-breed dogs. Dogs often presented for neurological dysfunction reflective of a prosencephalic lesion (19 of 25 dogs) or new-onset seizures (13 of 25 dogs). A single, well-defined hemorrhage within the prosencephalon was commonly observed on MRI. Outcome was frequently excellent. Complete seizure remission was common. Larger lesion volumes were associated with poor outcomes. One dog experienced clinical signs suggestive of an additional sIHS 4 months after the initial stroke. Frequency of sIHS was 25 of 427 dogs (6%) ≥ 6 years old undergoing brain MRI. CLINICAL RELEVANCE: sIHS should be considered in older dogs with an acute onset of prosencephalic dysfunction and/or seizures. Following exclusion of secondary IHS, an excellent long-term outcome may be realized in dogs with sIHS, despite the presence of comorbidities. Short-term anticonvulsant therapy may be necessary. Development of poststroke epilepsy is unlikely. Dogs rarely experience another IHS.
Drug-resistant epilepsy in non-NF1 RASopathies: a two-centre retrospective paediatric cohort and scoping review.推荐
PMID 43013924 · Seizure · 2026 10 29
作者：Santi V, Pisanò G, Thirumalai S, Sutaria A et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
摘要：OBJECTIVE: To characterise drug-resistant epilepsy (DRE) in non-NF1 RASopathies through a scoping review and a two-centre paediatric cohort. METHODS: We searched PubMed (MEDLINE), Embase and Scopus from inception to September 2026 for terms covering non-NF1 RASopathies and epilepsy/seizures. Four reviewers independently screened records and extracted data in duplicate; discrepancies were resolved by consensus. Eligible records reported individuals with a non-NF1 RASopathy, seizure onset up to 17 years, and confirmed epilepsy. DRE was defined by ILAE (failure of ≥2 appropriate antiseizure medications [ASMs] at adequate dose). In parallel, we retrospectively identified paediatric patients with genetically confirmed Noonan syndrome (NS) or cardio-facio-cutaneous syndrome (CFC) and DRE from two London centres over a five-year period; clinical, EEG and MRI features, ASMs and outcomes were abstracted. RESULTS: Of 801 records identified across three databases, 435 were screened following deduplication; 48 met inclusion criteria. Across the literature, NS showed lower epilepsy frequency and severity; DRE was uncommon and often associated with structural aetiologies. CFC displayed a severe, polymorphic phenotype with frequent status epilepticus and high DRE rates. Recent evidence also identified subclinical epileptiform activity, including continuous spike-and-wave during sleep, in CFC patients without overt seizures. Our cohort comprised six patients: two NS (variants in BRAF, PTPN11) and four CFC (all MAP2K1). Seizure types included tonic-clonic, focal seizures and infantile spasms; most required multiple ASMs. EEG abnormalities were non-specific. MRI showed heterogeneous findings, including mesial temporal sclerosis, cerebral atrophy, delayed myelination, and hippocampal dysgyria. Neurodevelopmental impairment (including autism spectrum disorder) was common. CONCLUSIONS: In non-NF1 RASopathies, DRE reflects combined genetic and structural contributors; in CFC, clinical-imaging correlations support a spectrum towards developmental and epileptic encephalopathy. Management should consider non-pharmacological options and carefully selected targeted approaches; mechanism-informed choices may be considered where appropriate, acknowledging that the supporting evidence remains observational.
Clinical Risk Factors for Suicide in Persons With Epilepsy: A Nationwide Population-Based Case-Control Study.推荐
PMID 43013790 · Neurology · 2026 11 11
作者：Melin S, Tomson T, Sveinsson O, Carlsson S et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
摘要：BACKGROUND AND OBJECTIVES: Individuals with epilepsy face an elevated risk of suicide, but risk factors for suicide remain incompletely understood. We conducted a nationwide case-control study in Sweden to investigate clinical characteristics associated with suicide risk in persons with epilepsy. METHODS: Persons with epilepsy were identified through Swedish nationwide registers, with epilepsy diagnoses validated through medical record review. Cases were individuals who died by suicide between 2006 and 2011, validated through autopsy reports, and controls were derived from the source population using similar validation procedures. Characteristics included epilepsy type, etiology, tonic-clonic seizure (TCS) frequency, antiseizure medication (ASM) treatment, living conditions, and psychiatric comorbidity. Odds ratios (ORs) with 95% CIs were estimated using conditional logistic regression. RESULTS: The study included 111 suicide cases and 1,147 controls. Suicide cases had a mean age at epilepsy onset of 20 years, and 35% were female. Alcohol abuse (OR 11.81, 95% CI 6.25-22.30) and other substance abuse (OR 10.71, 95% CI 4.52-25.38) showed stronger associations than depression (OR 2.94, 95% CI 1.56-5.53) and anxiety disorders (OR 2.93, 95% CI 1.57-5.44). Living alone was associated with increased risk (OR 2.59, 95% CI 1.58-4.26). Recent TCS showed a dose-response relationship, with the highest risk among individuals experiencing 4-10 TCS in the preceding year (OR 5.01, 95% CI 2.2-11.3). Structural and unknown epilepsy etiologies were associated with elevated risk (OR 3.07, 95% CI 1.0-9.5 and OR 8.80, 95% CI 2.9-27.1, respectively). ASM treatment was associated with lower suicide risk (monotherapy OR 0.51, 95% CI 0.27-0.95; polytherapy OR 0.21, 95% CI 0.1-0.5), and nonadherence was associated with higher risk (OR 3.02, 95% CI 1.8-5.1). DISCUSSION: This study identified a distinct risk profile for suicide in persons with epilepsy, highlighting alcohol and substance abuse, living alone, seizure burden, nonadherence, and structural or unknown etiologies as key risk factors. The stronger associations for substance abuse may reflect characteristics specific to epilepsy populations and the focus on completed suicide. Screening for substance abuse and improved seizure control may contribute to suicide prevention. Major limitations include the modest number of cases and incomplete information on prior suicidal behavior.
Artificial Intelligence-Based Neonatal Seizure Detection: A Pragmatic Evaluation of Clinical Performance Tradeoffs in Clinical EEG.推荐
PMID 43013760 · Journal of clinical neurophysiology : official publication of the American Electroencephalographic Society · 2026 11 11
作者：Keene JC, Abend NS, Benedetti GM, Fulmer Z et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
摘要：PURPOSE: The aim of this study was to evaluate how three artificial intelligence (AI)-based neonatal seizure detectors (NSDs) perform in a large, clinically acquired data set and their potential to support timely seizure identification. METHODS: We conducted a secondary analysis of continuous EEG (cEEG) recordings from 169 neonates born at ≥35 weeks gestation with neonatal encephalopathy. Seizure presence was determined by independent review by two or three pediatric encephalographers, and 2,455 individual seizures were annotated by a single expert as a pragmatic, clinically representative reference standard. Three NSDs were evaluated: a feature-based support vector machine detector (NSD1) and two deep-learning detectors (NSD2 and Persyst-15). Using an any-overlap approach, outcomes included neonate-level seizure identification, seizure-level detection, false detections per day (FD/day), and detection latency. RESULTS: We analyzed 15,343 hours of cEEG (median 98 hours per neonate); 55 neonates (33%) had seizures. All NSDs identified ≥95% of neonates with seizures. Seizure-level detection varied: NSD2 detected 35% (IQR 17-53%), NSD1 63% (IQR 38-82%), and Persyst-15 60% (IQR 37-80%) of seizures. NSD1 generated significantly more FDs (46 FD/day, IQR 22-82) than NSD2 (3.2 IQR 1.2-9.3) and Persyst-15 (2.8 IQR 1.1-6.2; P < 0.0001). The median time to first true detection was <1 minute for NSD1 and Persyst-15, versus 26 minutes for NSD2. CONCLUSIONS: AI-based NSDs demonstrated high infant-level sensitivity but different tradeoffs between seizure-level detection and alarm burden. These findings highlight how AI tools may augment clinical EEG workflows and support earlier seizure recognition, while underscoring the need for evaluation under real-world conditions to guide safe and effective implementation.
International Survey of Stereo-EEG Practices: A Comparative Analysis of Epilepsy Surgery Centers in Europe and North America.推荐
PMID 43013759 · Journal of clinical neurophysiology : official publication of the American Electroencephalographic Society · 2026 11 11
作者：Singh J, Inoue R, Dimova PS, Krsek P et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
摘要：PURPOSE: Stereoelectroencephalography (SEEG) is widely used for presurgical evaluation in epilepsy, yet practice patterns vary substantially across centers, contributing to heterogeneity in the literature. We examined differences in SEEG experience, techniques, and complications across Europe and North America centers. METHODS: An electronic 39-item REDCap survey was distributed to centers participating in the American Stereo-EEG Consortium and EpiCARE Consortium. The survey assessed center characteristics, electrode planning and implantation, monitoring practices, and complications. RESULTS: Sixty-six centers responded, including 38 from North America and 28 from Europe. North American centers more frequently used nurse-led ictal and postictal assessments (16/38 vs. 5/28; P = 0.06) and robotic image-guided implantation systems (37/38 vs. 17/28; P < 0.001), with a trend toward higher case volumes. Compared with European centers, North American programs were less likely to have over 15 years of SEEG experience (6/38 vs. 12/28; P = 0.015), fewer dedicated SEEG readers (12/38 vs. 25/28; P < 0.001), and less frequent use of SEEG-guided radiofrequency ablation (13/38 vs. 25/28; P < 0.01). Symptomatic hemorrhage was more frequently reported among North American centers in the unadjusted analysis (9/38 vs. 1/28; p = 0.035); however, the regional difference was no longer statistically significant after adjustment for center SEEG experience and annual case volume. CONCLUSIONS: Significant differences exist between North American and European SEEG practices. North American centers more commonly use robotic implantation and manage higher volume, whereas European centers demonstrate greater experience and more frequent use of SEEG-guided ablation. These findings support the need for standardized protocols to improve safety and outcomes.
Persistent focal onchocerciasis transmission near the Maridi Dam, South Sudan, despite intensified elimination interventions: Repeated Ov16 serosurveys in young children, 2019-2026.推荐

摘要：BACKGROUND: Maridi County in South Sudan is an onchocerciasis hotspot with a high burden of onchocerciasis-associated epilepsy. Seroprevalence was 11.5% (10/87) among 3-year-olds and 24.1% (26/108) among 4-year-olds (p = 0.024). Across the same four villages and age range, overall prevalence was 14.3% (6/42) in 2019, 20.0% (14/70) in 2023, and 18.5% (36/195) in 2026 (p = 0.744). To account for unequal village representation across surveys, village-standardized prevalence estimates were also calculated by assigning equal weight to each village and closely paralleled the crude estimates. A generalized linear mixed model with village as a random intercept was fitted, adjusting for age and sex. Compared with 2019, there was no evidence of a temporal difference in seropositivity in 2023 (OR 1.32, 95% CI 0.40-4.40; p = 0.652) or 2026 (OR 1.24, 95% CI 0.43-3.60; p = 0.686). CONCLUSIONS/SIGNIFICANCE: Repeated Ov16 surveys identified continued substantial seropositivity among children aged 3-4 years, particularly in Kazana 2 near the Maridi Dam, consistent with recent O. volvulus exposure and ongoing local transmission. The findings support improving ivermectin coverage among eligible residents, sustained entomologically monitored vector control, and geographically targeted surveillance. Safe paediatric antifilarial options could help close the protection gap in children at risk of onchocerciasis-associated epilepsy.
A Dual-Branch Model based Federated Learning Framework for Seizure-Type Classification.推荐
PMID 43013434 · IEEE journal of biomedical and health informatics · 2026 11 11
作者：Wen X, Zou Y, Xu Y, Jiang L et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
摘要：Accurate seizure-type identification is of great significance for epilepsy patient treatment and management. Manual electroencephalogram (EEG) data analysis, the traditional method, is time-consuming, labor-intensive, and subjective. While deep learning has shown great potential in medical diagnosis, it faces privacy and data silo challenges in handling multi-center EEG data. To address these issues, this paper proposes a dual-branch model and federated learning-based automatic classification method for seizure types. A federated learning framework is introduced to facilitate multi-center data collaborative training while protecting data privacy. In the base model, the one-dimensional convolutional neural network (1DCNN) and the two-dimensional convolutional neural network (2DCNN) are integrated to extract the complexity features of EEG signals and the topological features of brain networks, respectively, and the dual-modal features are adaptively integrated through the dynamic attention fusion module. In local training of federated learning framework, the FedProx optimization strategy is adopted to effectively solve the model update divergence problem caused by non-independent and identically distributed (Non-IID) data and improve the model generalization ability. Experimental results show that the proposed federated learning framework performs well in core indicators of seizure-type classification such as accuracy and macro-average F1 score. Even in the case of extremely unbalanced data, the proposed method can still maintain an accuracy of 98.42% and a macro-average F1 score of 94.79%. This method provides an effective solution for distributed medical data collaborative training, which is of great significance for promoting the accurate diagnosis and treatment of epilepsy.
Avoidable Mortality in Adults With Down Syndrome or With Other Intellectual Disabilities.推荐
PMID 43013309 · JAMA network open · 2026 11 02
作者：Bokor L, Yu MKL, Carter B, Chauhan U et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
摘要：IMPORTANCE: Down syndrome is the most common genetic cause of Alzheimer disease. Adults with Down syndrome experience higher mortality than the general population. However, the avoidable causes and their associated factors remain unclear. OBJECTIVE: To compare the proportion and causes of avoidable mortality in adults with Down syndrome, adults with other intellectual disabilities, and the general population, and to identify factors associated with avoidable death. DESIGN, SETTING, AND PARTICIPANTS: This population-based retrospective cohort study used data from the Learning From Lives and Deaths mortality review and the Office for National Statistics in England from 2016 to 2023. It included adults with Down syndrome (n = 3381), other adults with intellectual disabilities (n = 15 347), and the general population (n = 4 163 160). EXPOSURES: Down syndrome status, ethnicity, socioeconomic deprivation, co-occurring health conditions, COVID-19 pandemic period. MAIN OUTCOMES AND MEASURES: Proportion and causes of avoidable, treatable, and preventable deaths according to Organisation for Economic Co-operation and Development/Eurostat criteria; odds of avoidable death estimated using logistic regression; and age at avoidable death using Cox regression. Factors associated with avoidable death and age at death were also evaluated. RESULTS: Among 3381 adults with Down syndrome (1892 male [56.0%]; median [IQR] age at death, 59.5 [54.3-64.2] years), 1589 (47.0%) died from avoidable causes. Among 15 347 adults with other intellectual disabilities (8675 male [56.5%]; median [IQR] age at death, 63.8 [51.7-73.4] years), 6885 (44.9%) died from avoidable causes. Both proportions were substantially higher than in the general population (22.0%) (both P < .001). Treatable deaths were more common (972 [28.7%] vs 3847 [25.1%] vs 317 968 [7.6%]), with epilepsy and pneumonia as the leading treatable causes. Down syndrome was independently associated with higher odds of avoidable death (aOR, 1.15; 95% CI, 1.06-1.25) and earlier age at avoidable death (aHR, 1.49; 95% CI, 1.40-1.58). Minoritized ethnic background, asthma, diabetes, and osteoporosis were each independently associated with higher odds of avoidable death, and minoritized ethnic background and greater socioeconomic deprivation with earlier age at avoidable death. CONCLUSIONS AND RELEVANCE: In this cohort study of adults with Down syndrome and other adults with intellectual disabilities in England, avoidable mortality exceeded that of the general population. For the adults with Down syndrome, epilepsy and pneumonia were the dominant treatable causes. These findings support proactive integrated neurological care, including epilepsy review with dementia monitoring, pneumonia prevention, and respiratory vaccination, alongside targeted interventions for minoritized ethnic groups and socioeconomically deprived groups.
Center-dependent "non-operability" in diffuse glioma: challenging assumptions through awake resection.浏览
PMID 43013157 · Journal of neuro-oncology · 2026 11 11
作者：Elia A, Roux A, Defrance G, Debacker C et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
摘要：PURPOSE: The concept of non-operability in diffuse glioma remains poorly standardized and may reflect institutional experience rather than absolute surgical limitations. We evaluated whether patients previously deemed "non-operable" by outside institutions could undergo awake craniotomy and achieve outcomes comparable to those of patients initially considered operable. METHODS: We retrospectively analyzed 448 adults who underwent awake function-based craniotomy for a diffuse glioma (2011-2024). Sixty-six patients (14.7%) had previously been considered "non-operable" and were compared with patients initially considered operable. Feasibility, safety, and were assessed using propensity score matching and voxel-based lesion-symptom mapping. RESULTS: Patients in the "non operable group" more frequently had prior oncological treatments, focal neurological deficits, larger tumor volumes, greater midline mass effect, and IDH-wildtype glioblastoma. Despite greater preoperative complexity, feasibility, safety, and efficacy outcomes did not significantly differ between groups, including intraoperative adverse events, surgery-related complications, 6-month postoperative Karnofsky Performance Status score and seizure control, extent of resection, and residual tumor volume. No awake procedure was aborted in the "non-operable" group. Propensity score-matched confirmed these findings. Voxel-based lesion-symptom mapping identified no independent anatomical predictor of prior non-operability designation. CONCLUSION: In selected patients referred for a second opinion, a prior "non-operable" designation did not preclude safe awake resection, suggesting that non-operability may partly reflect a center-related judgment. Patients initially deemed "non-operable" can achieve outcomes comparable to those of conventionally considered operable patients when managed in specialized high-volume centers with expertise in awake function-based craniotomy. These findings support systematic consideration of referral for a second opinion in such patients.
Drinking water hardness and nervous system mortality: a 15 year ecological study of calcium and magnesium content in the Slovak Republic.浏览
PMID 43013102 · Environmental geochemistry and health · 2026 11 11
作者：Rapant S, Beňová N, Cvečková V, Stehlíková B et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
摘要：The chemical composition of the natural physical environment significantly impacts human health, yet the specific influence of drinking water minerals on nervous system disorders remains largely unexamined. This study investigated the relationship between drinking water hardness, specifically calcium and magnesium concentrations, and nervous system disease mortality across populations in the Slovak Republic. Two size wise comparable groups of rural municipalities supplied exclusively by public water systems were monitored over a 15 year period (1994-2008), totaling 1.58 million person-years. The soft water cohort comprised 34 municipalities (Ca ≤ 25 mg L-1; Mg ≤ 10 mg L-1), and the hard water cohort comprised 21 municipalities (Ca ≥ 50 mg L-1; Mg ≥ 20 mg L-1). Relative mortality rates and indirectly age-standardized mortality ratios were calculated using national registry data for 99 specific nervous system diagnoses (ICD-10 codes G00-G99). Total nervous system mortality was approximately 2.5 times higher in the soft water regions than in the hard water regions. Highly significant statistical associations were discovered for epilepsy and cerebral palsy, with relative mortality rates expanding 4.25 and 16.41 times higher in the soft water group, respectively. No statistically significant differences were observed for neurodegenerative conditions like Alzheimer's or Parkinson's diseases. Low levels of calcium and magnesium in public drinking water are associated with increased population-level mortality from epilepsy and cerebral palsy. However, due to the ecological design of this study, these findings represent geographical correlations and cannot establish a direct causal relationship. Controlled individual-level epidemiological studies or clinical trials are required to determine whether targeted mineral interventions would successfully reduce mortality in high-risk demographics.
National incidence and predictors of in-hospital venous thromboembolism after inpatient lumbar fusion, 2016-2022.浏览
PMID 43013098 · European spine journal : official publication of the European Spine Society, the European Spinal Deformity Society, and the European Section of the Cervical Spine Research Society · 2026 11 11

命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）

摘要：PURPOSE: To estimate the national incidence of in-hospital VTE recorded during the index lumbar fusion admission and identify independent comorbidity predictors. METHODS: Adult (≥ 18 years) inpatient admissions undergoing lumbar fusion were identified in the National Inpatient Sample (NIS) from 2016 to 2022 using ICD-10-PCS procedure codes. The primary outcome was in-hospital VTE during the index admission. Characteristics were compared between VTE and non-VTE admissions, and multivariable survey-weighted logistic regression evaluated independent predictors. Significance was set at the P < 0.05 level. RESULTS: Across 631,165 weighted inpatient lumbar fusion admissions, in-hospital VTE incidence was 0.6%. CONCLUSIONS: In-hospital VTE recorded during the index lumbar fusion admission was uncommon but concentrated among patients with distinct high-risk comorbidity profiles. These findings support risk-stratified inpatient surveillance and prevention strategies during hospitalization.
Systemic sarcoidosis with neurosarcoidosis presenting with pulmonary embolism, epileptic seizures, and neuropsychiatric involvement in a patient with pre-existing schizophrenia: a diagnostic challenge.推荐

作者：Fulco E, Arganese C
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：来信
摘要：Neurosarcoidosis affects approximately 5-10% of patients with systemic sarcoidosis and may mimic primary psychiatric disease, delaying diagnosis. We report a 52-year-old man with pre-existing schizophrenia admitted with bilateral pulmonary embolism and mediastinal lymphadenopathy. Systemic sarcoidosis was confirmed by endobronchial ultrasound-guided lymph node biopsy and gastric histology. The patient subsequently developed epileptic seizures and a progressive neuropsychiatric syndrome initially attributed to his known psychiatric disorder. Electroencephalography demonstrated diffuse slowing with abundant epileptiform discharges and electro-clinical seizures of left temporal onset; brain MRI showed leptomeningeal involvement with cortical diffusion restriction; cerebrospinal fluid excluded infectious causes and showed an intrathecal immune response. A diagnosis of probable neurosarcoidosis was established, although a seizure-related contribution to the MRI findings could not be excluded. Treatment with high-dose corticosteroids and antiseizure medication was followed by marked clinical improvement and reported resolution of the MRI abnormalities. This case underscores the importance of considering neurosarcoidosis in patients with systemic granulomatous disease who develop new neuropsychiatric symptoms, even when a pre-existing psychiatric diagnosis offers an apparently sufficient explanation.
Electroconvulsive therapy for the treatment of depression: from convulsive treatment to precision neuromodulation.推荐

链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
