摘要：INTRODUCTION: ECT remains one of the most effective treatments for severe depression, including psychotic and treatment-resistant presentations and high-acuity cases requiring rapid response. This review examines ECT as a parameter-sensitive neuromodulation procedure rather than a fixed convulsive treatment. AREAS COVERED: We review historical development, clinical efficacy, safety, relapse prevention, and mechanistic models involving seizure induction, electric-field effects, inhibitory plasticity, neurochemical and neuroendocrine signaling, network change, and postictal brain states. The literature was identified through searches of PubMed/MEDLINE, Web of Science, Scopus, IEEE Xplore, and Google Scholar from database inception to June 2026, supplemented by reference-list screening, with evidence evaluated according to study design, clinical relevance, and technological maturity. Established components of contemporary ECT practice are distinguished from near-term optimization strategies and experimental technologies. EXPERT OPINION: Precision ECT should be regarded as an emerging framework rather than an established standard of care. Patient-specific electric-field modeling, biomarker-guided dosing, amplitude optimization, and adaptive stimulation are promising strategies for improving the therapeutic index, but require prospective validation against optimized standard ECT using clinical, cognitive, and patient-reported outcomes.
[System construction and clinical practice of genetic disease carrier screening in donor sperm-assisted reproduction].浏览
PMID 43012240 · Zhonghua nan ke xue = National journal of andrology · 2026 Mar
作者：Lin F, Wang J, Sun H, Li J et al.
链接：PubMed
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）

摘要：Objective The aim of this study is to explore the feasibility of genetic disease carrier screening in donor sperm-assisted reproduction, and to establish a standardized screening and matching system, in order to prevent the occurrence of severe monogenic genetic diseases in offspring at the source. Methods A retrospective analysis was conducted on couples who visited the reproductive medicine center of the First Affiliated Hospital of Nanjing Medical University between January 2017 and December 2025, meeting the criteria for donor sperm use and with the female partner being a known carrier (or patient) of a pathogenic mutation for a specific monogenic disease. A process of "on-demand application, targeted screening, double-blind management, and specimen locking" was implemented to perform genetic testing on sperm donors and select specimens free of the corresponding pathogenic mutations for clinical treatment. The matching success rate, pregnancy outcomes and offspring health status were recorded. All couples were successfully matched with donor sperm that did not carry the targeted pathogenic mutations, achieving a 100% matching success rate. All of the couples achieved clinical pregnancy and delivered healthy live-born infants at term. No related genetic diseases were found in offspring through genetic testing or phenotypic follow-up. Conclusion Targeted genetic carrier screening in donor sperm-assisted reproduction is feasible and effective, significantly reducing the risk of severe monogenic diseases in offspring. It is recommended to gradually promote the institutionalization and standardization of such screening to provide practical evidence for primary prevention of birth defects.
Efficacy and safety of sepiapterin versus sapropterin in people living with phenylketonuria: a plain language summary of the AMPLIPHY trial.推荐
PMID 43012090 · Journal of comparative effectiveness research · 2026 11 11
作者：Giżewska M, Guilder L, Gallagher S, Inwood A
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：综述
摘要：WHAT IS THIS SUMMARY ABOUT?: This summary is about a clinical trial called AMPLIPHY. AMPLIPHY was a trial that compared two treatments, sepiapterin and sapropterin, to see how well they worked in children and adults living with phenylketonuria (PKU). PKU is a rare genetic condition where the body cannot properly break down phenylalanine (Phe), which is an amino acid found in many foods. If people living with PKU are not treated, Phe levels build up in the body, which can affect brain development, thinking skills and behaviour. If left untreated for a long time, this may cause symptoms such as seizures, rashes and problems with movement. A key goal of PKU treatment is to keep blood Phe levels within a target range to avoid these effects. WHAT HAPPENED IN THE AMPLIPHY TRIAL?: In the AMPLIPHY trial, the researchers wanted to see how well sepiapterin worked in people living with PKU when compared with sapropterin, an alternative treatment for PKU, and to compare the safety of the treatments. The AMPLIPHY trial had two parts: During Part 1, the participants took sepiapterin for 2 weeks to find out whether it decreased their Phe levels. -Participants whose blood Phe levels decreased by at least 20% were classed as responsive to sepiapterin and could continue to Part 2. -Participants whose blood Phe levels decreased by less than 20% did not continue to Part 2. During Part 2: -The participants took either sepiapterin or sapropterin for the first 4 weeks followed by 4 weeks of the other treatment. WHAT WERE THE RESULTS?: During Part 1, the researchers found that most people benefited from sepiapterin. Specifically, 82% of participants (67 out of 82) had a reduction in blood Phe levels of at least 20%, and 76% of participants (62 out of 82) had a reduction of at least 30%. During Part 2, the researchers found that sepiapterin lowered blood Phe levels by 70% more than sapropterin did. There were no adverse events that were considered serious or that stopped anyone from taking treatment or participating in the trial. WHAT DO THE RESULTS OF THE TRIAL MEAN?: How well did the treatments work: these results showed that sepiapterin lowered blood Phe levels more than sapropterin in children and adults with PKU. What were the safety findings: sepiapterin was well tolerated, with no unexpected side effects or safety concerns identified. ClinicalTrials.gov, NCT number: NCT05099640.

摘要：UNLABELLED: Human herpesvirus 6 (HHV-6) encephalitis is an uncommon but severe post-transplant complication. A 64-year-old woman developed confusion, behavioural changes, and staring episodes 6 months after liver transplantation. Cerebrospinal fluid (CSF) multiplex testing was positive for HHV-6, and magnetic resonance imaging showed bilateral hippocampal and mesial temporal abnormalities, later involving the bitemporal and insular regions. Beware of Tall Stature: Growth Hormone Excess Associated with a Rare Parasellar Extra-Axial Tumour-Diagnostic and Management Challenges in a Resource-Limited Setting.推荐
PMID 43012026 · European journal of case reports in internal medicine · 2026 10 19
作者：Thiono MT, Perwitasari RK, Hisbiyah Y, Noviandi R et al.

命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
摘要：INTRODUCTION: Tall stature can be the sole indicator of growth hormone (GH) excess, which causes pituitary gigantism before epiphyseal fusion. In developing regions like Indonesia, a lack of systematic screening often delays diagnosis. While classic presentations involve accelerated linear growth, atypical cases may be dominated by neurological signs, such as seizures secondary to tumour mass effect. CASE DESCRIPTION: A 14-year-old boy presented with a 2-year history of rapidly accelerating growth velocity, increasing shoe size, and a 6-month history of severe morning headaches accompanied by drug-resistant focal seizures. Endocrine evaluations revealed significantly elevated basal GH (4.27 ng/ml) and insulin-like growth factor 1 (IGF-1) levels (536 ng/ml). Brain magnetic resonance imaging identified a large parasellar extra-axial tumour with suprasellar extension. The patient underwent a craniotomy and tumour excision; histopathology subsequently confirmed a rare, aggressive malignant mesenchymal tumour. Postoperatively, the patient achieved seizure resolution and normalization of GH and IGF-1 levels. However, he developed evolving pituitary dysfunction consistent with multiple pituitary hormone deficiency (MPHD), specifically central hypothyroidism, necessitating targeted hormonal replacement therapy and continuous clinical follow-up. CONCLUSION: GH excess and gigantism can arise secondary to parasellar pathology rather than intrinsic pituitary disease, with neurological features like refractory epilepsy occasionally preceding classic endocrine manifestations. Rapid adolescent growth paired with neurological signs warrants urgent structural neuroimaging. Meticulous perioperative pituitary monitoring and early multidisciplinary involvement are essential to optimize long-term patient outcomes. LEARNING POINTS: Pituitary gigantism can be driven by extra-axial parasellar masses rather than intrinsic pituitary adenomas, operating via mass-effect compression that disrupts hypothalamic somatostatin inhibition.Drug-resistant epilepsy and severe headaches caused by local tumour infiltration can dominate the clinical picture, meaning rapid adolescent growth paired with neurological symptoms mandates urgent magnetic resonance imaging of the brain.Postoperative multiple pituitary hormone deficiency often evolves silently over weeks or months, requiring lifelong, multidisciplinary surveillance to detect late-stage deficiencies like secondary adrenal insufficiency.
A Multiplex LC-MS/MS Approach for Quantifying Antiepileptic Drugs for Therapeutic Drug Monitoring: Validation of 15 Representative Tests.推荐
PMID 43011968 · Journal of mass spectrometry and advances in the clinical lab · 2026 08 26
作者：Tang X, Wei R, Chegwidden E, Giles R et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
摘要：INTRODUCTION: Therapeutic drug monitoring of antiepileptic drugs is critical for optimizing clinical outcomes, minimizing toxicity, assessing drug compliance, and managing overdoses and drug interactions. OBJECTIVES: We developed and validated an LC-MS/MS method for the quantification of 15 antiepileptic drugs (ethosuximide, primidone, pentobarbital, carbamazepine-10,11 epoxide, pregabalin, gabapentin, zonisamide, lacosamide, rufinamide, felbamate, lamotrigine, topiramate, 10,11-dihydro-10-hydroxycarbamazepine, perampanel, and brivaracetam). METHODS: Antiepileptic drugs were extracted from plasma and serum using methanol-based protein precipitation, followed by dilution. A commercial ClinCal® 3-point calibrator was used for all analytes except pentobarbital, which used a 6-point in-house calibrator. Chromatographic separation was achieved using a reverse-phase C18 column with a 7.31 min elution gradient of water and methanol, both containing 2 mM of ammonium acetate. RESULTS: All analytes required a 5 µL injection volume, except ethosuximide and pentobarbital, which required 20 µL and 10 µL, respectively, for optimal performance. The method demonstrated excellent linearity for all 15 antiepileptic drugs within their respective concentration ranges, with acceptable selectivity, accuracy (80.0-118.3%), and intraday and interassay precision (CV < 7.4%). Stability studies showed that antiepileptic drugs were stable in serum and plasma for up to 24 h at room temperature, 7 days at 4 °C, and 30 days at -20 °C, and after extraction for 7 days at 4 °C. CONCLUSION: This LC-MS/MS method supports routine quantification of 15 antiepileptic drugs in a clinical laboratory using a common sample preparation and chromatography workflow across all measurements. It was operationalized into three instrument methods to accommodate the different injection volume and calibrator requirements.
In-ear electroencephalography: a systematic review of technology, applications, challenges, and future directions.推荐
PMID 43011629 · Frontiers in neurology · 2026 10 27
作者：Haria C, Harris BHL, Mortimer L, Platek-Johnson K et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：系统综述
摘要：Electroencephalography (EEG) is widely used to monitor brain activity in both clinical and research settings. However, conventional scalp EEG systems require multiple electrodes, specialised equipment, and trained personnel, which limits their practicality for long-term monitoring in everyday environments. In-ear EEG has emerged as a promising wearable alternative that enables discreet and portable neural monitoring. This systematic review examines the current state of research on in-ear EEG technologies. Twenty-four studies met the inclusion criteria and were analysed thematically. The literature highlights rapid progress in device miniaturisation and wearable design, with reported applications including sleep monitoring, seizure detection, cognitive workload assessment, fatigue monitoring, and emotion recognition. Despite these advances, challenges remain related to signal quality, spatial coverage, and motion artefacts. Further research is required to validate in-ear EEG for broader clinical and real-world applications.
Aphasic status epilepticus presenting with prominent psychiatric symptoms: A case report.推荐
PMID 43011433 · PCN reports : psychiatry and clinical neurosciences · 2026 11 10
作者：Yamada Y, Takahashi S, Fujiwara M
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
摘要：BACKGROUND: Aphasic status epilepticus (ASE) is a subtype of nonconvulsive status epilepticus characterized by aphasia as its primary ictal manifestation. Although psychiatric symptoms are uncommon in ASE, they may obscure the diagnosis when present. We report an atypical case, presenting predominantly with agitation and irritability, which was rapidly diagnosed as ASE by electroencephalography (EEG) based on the abrupt symptom onset and neurological history. CASE PRESENTATION: A 49-year-old right-handed woman with alcohol use disorder was referred to our psychiatric hospital because of agitation, irritability, and disorganized thinking. Approximately 2 months before presentation to our hospital, she developed a generalized tonic-clonic seizure 3 days after sustaining a head injury. A left posterior fossa subdural hematoma was diagnosed, and burr-hole evacuation was performed at a general hospital. She continued rehabilitation; however, 6 days before admission to our hospital, she suddenly developed incoherent speech with agitation and irritability, prompting referral to our psychiatric hospital. Neurological examination revealed sensory aphasia. Given the history of brain injury and abrupt symptom onset, EEG was performed and demonstrated left-dominant periodic lateralized epileptiform discharges, confirming ASE. Antiseizure treatment resolved the EEG abnormalities and aphasia; however, aphasia, agitation, and irritability recurred with periodic lateralized epileptiform discharges. The symptoms improved with intravenous diazepam, and no recurrence was observed for >2 years following optimization of antiseizure medication. CONCLUSION: ASE may present predominantly with psychiatric symptoms and be misdiagnosed as a primary psychiatric disorder. Recognizing symptom chronology and epileptogenic background is essential for prompting EEG evaluation and early diagnosis.
Troponin I Surveillance to Risk-Stratify Early Events After Immune Checkpoint Inhibitor Initiation.浏览

命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
摘要：BACKGROUND: Troponin surveillance may facilitate early detection of immune checkpoint inhibitor (ICI)-associated myocarditis, but mild elevations and baseline abnormalities complicate interpretation. We assessed early post-ICI troponin I (TnI) dynamics and the performance characteristics of clinically applicable peak TnI thresholds within 3 months of ICI initiation. METHODS AND RESULTS: We analyzed 281 participants from a cardio-oncology surveillance program. TnI was measured at predefined intervals. Using peak TnI levels within 3 months, we evaluated the performance characteristics of candidate TnI thresholds for identifying adjudicated clinically significant TnI elevation associated with clinically meaningful conditions (e.g., ICI-associated myocarditis) at cut-offs of 50, 100, and 500 pg/mL. Eleven (3.9%) patients had elevated baseline TnI. Within 3 months, clinically significant TnI elevation occurred in 5 (1.8%) patients: ICI-associated myocarditis (n=2); immune-related myositis (n=1); pulmonary embolism (n=1); and seizures (n=1). A 50 pg/mL cut-off yielded 100% sensitivity, 96.0% specificity, 31.3% positive predictive value, and 100% negative predictive value. A 100 pg/mL cut-off demonstrated 100% sensitivity, 99.6% specificity, 83.3% positive predictive value, and 100% negative predictive value. A 500 pg/mL cut-off yielded 40.0% sensitivity and 100% specificity. CONCLUSIONS: In this exploratory study, a peak TnI threshold of 100 pg/mL within 3 months identified all clinically significant events with high specificity. This threshold may serve as a pragmatic trigger for expedited evaluation of myocarditis and alternative etiologies.
Antiseizure Medication Dispensing Before and After Initiation of Antidementia Medicines: A Population-Based Matched Cohort Study.推荐
PMID 43010636 · Pharmacoepidemiology and drug safety · 2026 Nov
作者：Almeida OP, Etherton-Beer C, Sanfilippo FM, Preen DB et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
摘要：PURPOSE: Seizures and behavioural and psychiatric features may prompt antiseizure medication (ASM) use in dementia. We examined whether increased ASM dispensing precedes or follows initiation of antidementia treatment to inform hypotheses about when these indications emerge. METHODS: Using the Australian Pharmaceutical Benefits Scheme (PBS) 10% sample, we matched people aged ≥ 50 years with incident antidementia dispensing in 2018-2021 1:1 to controls without antidementia dispensing (2013-2024) by age, sex, index year and diabetes and hypertension treatment. We compared annual ASM dispensing (ATC N03, excluding gabapentinoids) from 5 years before to 3 years after initiation using McNemar's test and interrupted time series analysis. Sensitivity analyses re-matched on Rx-Risk comorbidity and removed the survival requirement. RESULTS: Among 4385 matched pairs (59.8% women; mean age 78.3 years), ASM dispensing was higher in cases 5 years before initiation (2.7% vs. 1.8%; ratio 1.53, 95% CI = 1.15-2.03) and the difference widened after initiation (year 3: 9.1% vs. 2.4%; ratio 3.85, 95% CI = 3.13-4.75). Valproate accounted for most of the excess (year 3 ratio 6.97, 95% CI = 4.80-10.12). Levetiracetam dispensing rose gradually from year -3 and was higher after initiation (year 3 ratio 2.30, 95% CI = 1.51-3.50). Matching on comorbidity attenuated pre-initiation differences (year -5 ratio 1.42). The excess was greater at ages 50-79 years. CONCLUSIONS: ASM dispensing, most noticeably valproate, was higher in people with antidementia medicine dispensing than in controls, modestly before and markedly after initiation. Behavioural and psychiatric indications seem to predominate, with a smaller seizure-related contribution possibly beginning before treatment, although dispensing data cannot confirm indications. Valproate use warrants review.
Pituitary stalk interruption syndrome: a case report.推荐
PMID 43010074 · Journal of medical case reports · 2026 08 14
作者：Biswas S
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：病例报告
摘要：BACKGROUND: Pituitary stalk interruption syndrome (PSIS) is a rare congenital disorder characterized by an interrupted or absent pituitary stalk, ectopic posterior pituitary, and anterior pituitary hypoplasia, resulting in varying degrees of hypopituitarism. CASE PRESENTATION: An 18-year-old white American male with a history of panhypopituitarism, adrenal insufficiency, and Asperger's syndrome presented with seizures. Examination revealed short stature and absent secondary sexual characteristics. Endocrine evaluation demonstrated multiple pituitary hormone deficiencies. Magnetic resonance imaging showed a hypoplastic anterior pituitary gland, ectopic posterior pituitary, and absent pituitary stalk, confirming the diagnosis of PSIS. Hormone replacement therapy with hydrocortisone, levothyroxine, growth hormone, and testosterone enanthate was initiated. CONCLUSIONS: PSIS should be considered in patients with growth failure, delayed puberty, and multiple pituitary hormone deficiencies. Early recognition and timely hormone replacement are critical to optimize growth, pubertal development, and long-term outcomes.
Clinical, genetic, neuroimaging, and severity spectrum of peroxisomal disorders in Iran: a multicenter cohort study.浏览
PMID 43010042 · Orphanet journal of rare diseases · 2026 08 12
作者：Khan GS, Mashayekhi N, Bayat S, Toosi MB et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
摘要：BACKGROUND: Peroxisomal disorders comprise a heterogeneous group of inherited metabolic diseases including peroxisome biogenesis disorders (PBDs), single-enzyme defects, and peroxisomal dynamics disorders. Data from Middle Eastern populations remain limited despite high regional consanguinity rates. We aimed to characterize the clinical, genetic, and neuroimaging spectrum of peroxisomal disorders in a multicenter Iranian cohort. METHODS: We performed a retrospective multicenter study of genetically confirmed pediatric peroxisomal disorders diagnosed between 2016 and 2023 across five referral centers. Clinical, neuroimaging, and molecular data were systematically reviewed. Patients were classified as PBDs or non-PBD peroxisomal disorders, while non-classical peroxisome-associated phenotypes were analyzed separately. A pragmatic retrospective severity index was applied to assess disease burden. RESULTS: Forty-two patients from 42 families with peroxisomal gene-associated disorders were identified, including 40 patients in the primary analysis cohort. PBDs accounted for 33 patients and non-PBD peroxisomal disorders for seven. PEX1 was the most frequently affected gene. Novel variants accounted for 67% of disease-associated alleles, and parental consanguinity was present in 79% of families. Developmental delay or regression was universal, while visual impairment (62%), gait abnormalities (59%), hearing impairment (54%), pyramidal signs (46%), and seizures (41%) were common. Brain MRI data were available for 20 patients, including serial studies in five, demonstrating recurrent involvement of the parieto-occipital white matter, cerebellum, brainstem, and corpus callosum, with progressive neuroimaging abnormalities observed across genetically distinct disorders. Several recurrent variants raised the possibility of regional founder effects. Non-PBD peroxisomal disorders demonstrated higher median severity indices than PBDs, although the difference did not reach statistical significance. CONCLUSIONS: This study expands the clinical, genetic, and neuroimaging spectrum of peroxisomal disorders in the Middle East and highlights extensive allelic heterogeneity, a high burden of novel variants, many occurring in the homozygous state, and substantial genotype-phenotype variability. These findings support broad genomic testing and integrated neuroimaging assessment in highly consanguineous populations.
From opioids to methamphetamine: an eleven‑year analysis of changing drug treatment admissions in Türkiye, 2013-2023.推荐
PMID 43009974 · Substance abuse treatment, prevention, and policy · 2026 10 31
作者：Kumtepe AS, Özdemi̇r L, Sığındere S, Çağlayan D
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
摘要：BACKGROUND: Substance use disorders pose a major public health challenge globally, with marked shifts from plant-based to synthetic drugs over the past decade. Türkiye, situated on the Balkan Route, has become both a destination and a production hub for synthetic substances. This study analyzes eleven-year trends in drug treatment admissions, demographic characteristics, drug-related deaths, and infectious disease burden among people who inject drugs (PWID) to inform clinical practice and health policy. METHODS: We conducted a retrospective epidemiological analysis of data from Turkish National Drug Reports (2013-2023). Joinpoint regression was used to identify significant changes in trends. Descriptive statistics, Z-tests for proportions, and Pearson correlations were applied. Sensitivity analyses excluded 2016 and the pandemic years (2020-2021) to test robustness. RESULTS: Total treatment admissions increased by 60% (from 226,471 in 2013 to 362,561 in 2023). Joinpoint analysis demonstrated a continuous, statistically significant upward trend throughout the eleven years (APC = 4.12%, 95% CI: 1.03% to 7.41%, p = 0.011). The share of opioid-related admissions dropped from 76.3% (2013) to 32.2% (2023), while methamphetamine admissions surged from 0.25% (2014) to 37.1% (2023). Female admissions increased from 5.6% to 9.7% (p < 0.05), with a significant upward trend after 2017 (APC = 12.34%, p = 0.013). Mean age at treatment rose from 24.3 to 27.6 years (p < 0.05). Drug-related deaths peaked in 2017 (941), then declined by 73.9% by 2021, but increased slightly in 2023. HIV seroprevalence among PWID increased 6.7-fold (from < 0.21% to 1.3%, p < 0.05), while HCV remained high (41.7% in 2023). A strong correlation was found between methamphetamine seizures and treatment admissions (r = 0.812, p = 0.026). CONCLUSIONS: Türkiye has undergone a rapid transition from opioid-dominant to methamphetamine-dominant substance use patterns, accompanied by demographic shifts (aging population, more women) and escalating HIV risk among PWID. While reductions in drug-related deaths and adolescent admissions indicate some policy successes, the methamphetamine epidemic and emerging infectious disease syndemic require urgent adaptation of treatment systems, expansion of harm reduction services, and gender-responsive care. These findings carry early-warning value for other Balkan countries facing similar synthetic drug pressures. CLINICAL TRIAL NUMBER: Not applicable.
Comorbidities in individuals with intellectual disabilities with and without hearing loss: a prospective cohort study.浏览
PMID 43009901 · BMC public health · 2026 09 26
作者：Naghipour A, Gietmann C, Jankovic V, Scharpenberg M et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
摘要：BACKGROUND: Both intellectual disability (ID) and hearing loss (HL) are associated with a high prevalence of certain comorbidities. However, a combined analysis and overview classified according to the International Classification of Diseases, 11th Revision (ICD-11), is lacking. HL is disproportionately common among individuals with ID and often remains undetected. We evaluated: (1) which comorbidities are most common in ID; (2) how disease prevalence differs between those with and without HL; (3) whether the degree of ID or HL influences the number of additional diseases, surgeries, or medication intake. METHODS: This study is part of a prospective cohort study investigating effectiveness and feasibility of repeated universal hearing screenings and interventions for people with ID. We included 1,053 participants with ID (37% female, 63% male, and < 1% intersex; age range 1-90 years). Medical history assessment and on-site hearing testing were combined. Diseases were ICD-11-coded. Descriptive statistics and binary logistic regression models assessed associations between common diseases and age, sex, HL, and ID. RESULTS: Nearly all comorbidities observed in participants with HL (prevalence > 5% [= 14]) were more frequent in participants with HL vs. without (13/14), eight showing odds ratios [OR] and 95% confidence interval [CI] > 1: visual impairment; hypertension; trisomy 21; obesity; mental, behavioural, or neurodevelopmental disorders; spinal conditions; diabetes; hypothyroidism. Logistic regression underscored associations between: (1) HL and visual impairment, trisomy 21, spinal conditions, hypothyroidism; (2) age and hypertension, obesity, diabetes; (3) female sex and trisomy 21, hypothyroidism. Epilepsy occurred similarly (OR 1.01, 95% CI: 0.65-1.56) and autism spectrum disorder (ASD) less often in the HL-subcohort (OR 0.42, 95% CI: 0.24-0.73). Trisomy 21 was 3-4 times more frequent in the HL-subcohort, showed a female preponderance and high rates of cardiovascular, metabolic, and visual diseases. Associated comorbidities (mean 2.75 vs 1.65) and multimorbidity (≥ two diseases, 63% vs. 42%) were higher in persons with HL than without, as was medication intake (mean 2.73 vs. 0.84). CONCLUSION: In our study individuals with ID and HL showed increased comorbidity, multimorbidity, and higher medication intake than those without HL. Heightened awareness and individualised, multidisciplinary management are essential in order to ensure equitable care and prevent secondary complications.
Evaluation of factors affecting therapeutic levetiracetam drug levels in intensive care unit patients: a retrospective study.推荐
PMID 43009842 · BMC neurology · 2026 08 13
作者：Ayhan YE, Küçük RS, Ervatan Z, Bektay MY
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
摘要：BACKGROUND: Levetiracetam (LEV) is a widely used anti-seizure medication (ASM) with linear pharmacokinetics and a wide therapeutic range. Although therapeutic drug monitoring (TDM) for LEV is not routinely recommended, critically ill patients in the intensive care unit (ICU) may experience altered pharmacokinetics. This study aimed to evaluate the factors influencing LEV serum levels in ICU patients and to identify predictors of therapeutic drug concentrations. METHODS: This retrospective observational study included ICU patients aged ≥ 18 years who received LEV therapy and underwent TDM between January 2021 and July 2024 at a tertiary care hospital. LEV serum levels were categorized as subtherapeutic (< 12 mg/L), therapeutic (12-46 mg/L), or supratherapeutic (> 46 mg/L). Data on demographics, clinical parameters, renal function (estimated glomerular filtration rate [eGFR] calculated with the CKD-EPI 2009 equation and used as a surrogate for creatinine clearance), and concurrent ASM use were analyzed. Predictors of log-transformed LEV concentration were assessed using multiple linear regression. RESULTS: A total of 66 patients were included, with 59.1% achieving therapeutic LEV levels. Subtherapeutic and supratherapeutic levels were observed in 25.8% and 15.2% of patients, respectively. Advanced age and renal dysfunction (eGFR < 30 mL/min/1.73 m²) were significantly associated with supratherapeutic levels (p = 0.044 and p < 0.001, respectively). In multiple linear regression on log-transformed concentrations, renal dysfunction (+ 150%), in-hospital death, treatment rather than prophylactic indication, and daily LEV dose were independent predictors of serum LEV concentration (adjusted R² = 0.325, p < 0.001). LEV concentration did not discriminate seizure occurrence (area under the receiver operating characteristic curve 0.568, 95% CI 0.415-0.722). CONCLUSION: LEV concentrations showed substantial variability in ICU patients and were independently associated with renal function, LEV dose, indication, and discharge status. However, serum LEV concentration did not discriminate seizure occurrence. These findings support individualized interpretation of LEV TDM results, particularly at the extremes of renal function, but do not demonstrate that achieving the predefined reference range improves seizure control or clinical outcomes.
The sodium channel SCN2A regulates cortical excitatory and inhibitory neurogenesis.浏览
PMID 43009472 · Nature communications · 2026 07 27
作者：Uy JA, Dargaei Z, Geahchan S, Botler L et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
摘要：Voltage-gated sodium channels regulate neuronal excitability and synaptic transmission in the postnatal and adult brain. The gene SCN2A, encoding the sodium channel Nav1.2, regulates synaptic development and variants in SCN2A are associated with autism spectrum disorders (ASD) and a broad spectrum of epilepsy phenotypes, including early-onset developmental and epileptic encephalopathies. The expression pattern of SCN2A begins during prenatal cortical development, prior to the onset of synaptic transmission, but it is unknown whether SCN2A regulates early cortical development through mechanisms independent of synaptic transmission. Here we reveal that isogenic and ASD patient-derived human forebrain organoids modelling a loss of SCN2A function display impaired excitatory and inhibitory neurogenesis, leading to a developmental imbalance. Unexpectedly, we find precocious generation of cortical inhibitory neurons is driven by elevated Sonic hedgehog signaling and is reversible through pharmacological inhibition. Functionally, these developmental phenotypes arise due to Nav1.2-dependent sodium channel dysfunction and reduced action potential generation, leading to abnormal neuronal network activity. Our results identify a mechanism for cortical excitatory and inhibitory neurogenesis involving SCN2A, and reveal that early neurogenesis deficits precede postnatal neural circuit dysfunction in SCN2A-associated disorders.

Comb-Like EEG Pattern as an Early Diagnostic Clue in Maple Syrup Urine Disease (MSUD): A Systematic Review.推荐
PMID 43009336 · American journal of medical genetics. Part A · 2026 11 10
作者：Furuta Y, Li M, Zhang G, Murphey KE et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：综述
摘要：Maple syrup urine disease (MSUD) is a life-threatening inborn error of amino acid metabolism that often presents in the neonatal period with nonspecific symptoms such as poor feeding, lethargy, and seizures. Although newborn screening (NBS) enables early identification, false-negative results and delays in reporting may lead to missed or delayed diagnosis, resulting in severe neurologic injury. A characteristic EEG finding, the "comb-like rhythm," consisting of central- or frontocentral-predominant, sharply contoured 4-7 Hz theta bursts has been reported in MSUD. However, its diagnostic utility has not been systematically evaluated. Therefore, we aimed to investigate the comb-like rhythm as a diagnostic clue for MSUD. A systematic literature search was conducted in accordance with PRISMA guidelines using MEDLINE and Embase databases up to May 18, 2026. Studies evaluating EEG findings in individuals with MSUD were included. Data on clinical presentation, biochemical findings, brain imaging, and EEG features were extracted and synthesized qualitatively. In addition, two neonatal cases from our institution were included. Associations of the comb-like EEG rhythm with patient age at EEG (neonatal vs. non-neonatal) and plasma leucine concentrations were evaluated using Fisher's exact test and the Mann-Whitney U test. A total of 113 records were screened, and 18 studies were included, comprising 24 symptomatic cases including our two patients. Abnormal EEG findings were reported in 21 of 24 (88%). A comb-like pattern was exclusively observed in neonates with acute metabolic crisis (13/15 neonates [86.7%] vs. 0/9 non-neonates [0%], p < 0.0001). The comb-like rhythm was associated with higher plasma leucine concentrations (median, 3545 vs. 1782 μmol/L, p = 0.0033). The pattern often diminished or resolved following treatment or clinical stabilization. In our two patients, the comb-like rhythm contributed to raising diagnostic suspicion despite initially normal NBS results. The comb-like rhythm may serve as an adjunctive EEG marker that raises suspicion for acute MSUD, given its strong association with neonatal metabolic crisis and severe hyperleucinemia. Further studies are needed to clarify its pathophysiological basis and determine the clinical utility of EEG in MSUD crisis.
Interictal Electroencephalographic Characteristics of the Human Centromedian Thalamic Nucleus Recorded in Focal Epilepsy.推荐
PMID 43008527 · Clinical neurophysiology : official journal of the International Federation of Clinical Neurophysiology · 2026 11 03
作者：Xu X, Ye H, Hu L, Ye L et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
摘要：OBJECTIVES: The centromedian (CM) thalamic nucleus is critical in sleep-wake regulation and epileptic networks; yet its electroencephalography (EEG) characteristics remain incompletely understood. CM EEG across vigilance states and interictal epileptic discharges (IEDs) in focal epilepsy were investigated. METHODS: Interictal stereo-electroencephalography (SEEG) recordings from the CM and neighboring ventroposterior (VP) and ventrolateral (VL) nuclei were analyzed with synchronous cortical SEEG and scalp EEG during wakefulness, non-rapid eye movement sleep, rapid eye movement sleep, and spontaneous non-epileptic arousal. Spectral power was quantified across frequency bands. Sleep-related thalamic activities and thalamic IEDs were visually identified. RESULTS: SEEG data from 33 patients with drug-resistant focal epilepsy and 3 non-epileptic individuals were analyzed. CM exhibited continuous fast activity during wakefulness and delta/theta-dominant activity during sleep, confirmed by increased gamma-fast ripple power in wakefulness and enhanced delta/theta power in sleep (all p < 0.014). During spontaneous non-epileptic arousal, thalamic EEG changes preceded scalp EEG arousal (median 800 ms; interquartile range: 400-1500 ms). Four sleep-related thalamic EEG activities were reproducibly observed in non-epileptic individuals, supporting a potential physiological nature. Thalamic IEDs occurred in 36.4% of patients with focal epilepsy, invariably involved the CM. Thalamic IEDs were more frequent in extratemporal than temporal lobe epilepsy and were not associated with surgical outcome. CONCLUSIONS: CM SEEG demonstrates distinct, state-dependent electrophysiological dynamics across the sleep-wake cycle and preferential involvement in interictal epileptic activity compared with VL/VP nuclei. SIGNIFICANCE: These findings lay the foundation for a thalamic EEG atlas and refine understanding of differential engagement of thalamic nuclei in epileptic networks.
Optimizing antiseizure treatment in women of reproductive age and during pregnancy: Nationwide practice versus specialized epilepsy care in Poland.推荐
PMID 43008432 · Epilepsy & behavior : E&B · 2026 11 10
作者：Wójcik-Kawa K, Sienkiewicz-Jarosz H, Słowik A, Bosak K et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
摘要：PURPOSE: To compare antiseizure medication (ASM) treatment patterns among women with epilepsy (WWE) of reproductive age and pregnant WWE using nationwide administrative data and a university epilepsy referral center cohort in Poland. METHODS: This retrospective study analyzed two complementary datasets. Nationwide data were obtained from the Polish National Health Fund, including 44,951 WWE aged 20-49 years in 2024 and 594 pregnant WWE who delivered in 2022. Referral center data were collected from medical records of 277 WWE aged 20-49 years treated in 2024 and 112 pregnancies managed between 2019 and 2026. RESULTS: Levetiracetam and lamotrigine were the most frequently prescribed ASMs in both cohorts. Compared with the nationwide population, women of reproductive age treated at the epilepsy center were significantly less likely to receive valproate (18.4 % vs. 31.1 %, p < 0.001) or carbamazepine (6.5 % vs 16 %p < 0.001), while lacosamide, brivaracetam, and cenobamate were prescribed more frequently. The proportion of women receiving monotherapy was similar in both cohorts. Among pregnant WWE, non-valproate monotherapy predominated in both groups but was significantly more common at the referral center (77.7 % vs. 62.0 %, p < 0.001). Valproate use during pregnancy was markedly lower in the referral center than in the nationwide cohort (2.7 % vs. 16.0 %, p < 0.001), whereas levetiracetam and lamotrigine use was comparable. CONCLUSIONS: Women managed at an epilepsy referral center were substantially less likely to receive potentially teratogenic ASMs, particularly valproate, despite representing a more clinically complex population. These findings highlight the contribution of specialized epilepsy care to optimizing pharmacological management for women of reproductive age and during pregnancy while supporting adherence to contemporary treatment recommendations.
Seizure and psychiatric outcomes following epilepsy surgery in patients with preoperative interictal psychosis: A systematic review.推荐
PMID 43008431 · Epilepsy & behavior : E&B · 2026 11 10
作者：Andros Carol LC, Côrtes Centeno CP, Centeno RS
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：综述
摘要：BACKGROUND: Interictal psychosis (IIP) is a severe psychiatric comorbidity of epilepsy, but its implications for epilepsy surgery and postoperative outcomes remain uncertain. OBJECTIVE: To evaluate seizure and psychiatric outcomes following resective epilepsy surgery in patients with preoperative IIP. METHODS: A systematic review was conducted in accordance with the PRISMA 2020 guidelines and prospectively registered in PROSPERO (CRD420261362874). Observational studies reporting seizure and/or psychiatric outcomes in patients with preoperative IIP undergoing resective epilepsy surgery were included. The primary outcome was seizure freedom (Engel class I or equivalent), and the secondary outcome was sustained postoperative worsening of preexisting IIP. RESULTS: Seven independent studies met the inclusion criteria. Among 59 patients with available seizure-outcome data, 38 achieved seizure freedom at last follow-up. Two comparative cohorts reported no statistically significant association between preoperative IIP and seizure freedom. In the only study using structured positive- and negative-symptom rating scales, 4 of 12 patients experienced sustained worsening of psychotic symptoms. Diagnostic definitions and psychiatric outcome assessments varied substantially across studies. CONCLUSIONS: Available evidence is insufficient to determine whether preoperative interictal psychosis independently influences seizure or psychiatric outcomes following epilepsy surgery. Small samples, heterogeneous diagnostic definitions, inconsistent psychiatric assessments, and limited comparator data preclude firm conclusions. Future studies should use standardized operational definitions and longitudinal psychiatric assessments.
Clinical value of video monitoring in seizure classification and medication decision-making: A questionnaire-based study in outpatients with epilepsy.推荐
PMID 43008430 · Epilepsy & behavior : E&B · 2026 11 10
作者：Le Z, Lu L, Zhang E, Ou Z et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
摘要：OBJECTIVE: To evaluate the clinical value of ictal video monitoring in refining seizure classification and guiding antiseizure medication (ASM) decisions in outpatients with epilepsy, and to examine the concordance between eyewitness descriptions and objective video evidence. METHODS: In this questionnaire-based, single-center study, 525 patients with epilepsy who attended the neurology outpatient clinic of West China Hospital, Sichuan University, between July and September 2025 were enrolled. A self-designed questionnaire was used to collect demographic information, medication history, seizure types, eyewitness descriptions, ictal video recordings, and diagnostic classifications before and after video review. Patients with ictal videos were grouped by recording modality: mobile phone recording, surveillance footage, and video-EEG (VEEG) monitoring. Initial classification was performed without video; a second classification was then performed with video when available. Classifications were independently reviewed once by an experienced epilepsy-specialist neurologist, with final adjudication by a senior physician in cases of disagreement.Categorical variables were compared using the χ2 test or Fisher's exact test when expected frequencies were < 5, with two-sided P < 0.05 considered statistically significant. RESULTS: Ictal video recordings were available for 159 of 525 patients (30.3 %). Among the 159 patients with complete pre- and post-video diagnostic records, diagnostic revision occurred in 32 patients (20.1 %). The most common revision pathway was bilateral tonic-clonic seizure of unknown onset (BTC) to focal-to-bilateral tonic-clonic seizure (FBTC), accounting for 46.9 % (15/32) of all revisions. A determinate final seizure classification (focal or generalized) was more frequent in patients with ictal videos than in those without (96/159 [60.4 %] vs 121/366 [33.1 %], P < 0.001). ASM regimens were changed based on video evidence in 19/159 patients (11.9 %), and 16 patients required subsequent readjustment. Diagnostic revision rates were 15.2 % (19/125) for mobile phone recordings, 36.8 % (7/19) for surveillance footage, and 40.0 % (6/15) for VEEG monitoring. Diagnostic revision was significantly associated with ASM regimen changes (χ2 = 4.22, P = 0.040). An eyewitness was present at consultation for 512/525 patients (97.5 %), and the diagnostic concordance rate between eyewitness descriptions and video evidence was 89.9 %. CONCLUSION: Ictal video recording was associated with clinically meaningful revision of seizure classification and influenced ASM decisions in outpatients with epilepsy, with higher diagnostic yield observed for more professional recording modalities. Video evidence may help correct classification bias arising from reliance on verbal history, particularly in identifying focal onset.
Epilepsy Online Social Support: Characterizing topics and challenges shared in the r/Epilepsy community.推荐
PMID 43008429 · Epilepsy & behavior : E&B · 2026 11 10
作者：Medina JY, Young J, Min A, Shih PC et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
摘要：Epilepsy is one of the most common neurological conditions, and people living with epilepsy (PLWE) often use social media as a resource. However, a comprehensive understanding of the topics represented in epilepsy-specific communities where PLWE may be more honest is essential to designing better technologies to address epilepsy self-management. To understand the main topics and concerns of PLWE, we collected 23,945 r/Epilepsy subreddit posts and performed topic modeling, thematic analysis, and psycholinguistic analysis. We found five major themes among those topics: symptoms and triggers (e.g., mental health and memory, sleep/nocturnal, and photosensitivity), treatment and healthcare experience (e.g., medication, understanding epilepsy), daily functions (e.g., perceived level of independence and finances), seizure activity (e.g., auras and ictal symptoms), and support for PLWE (assisting PLWE and support for PLWE). We highlight the top psycho-linguistic characteristics of posts across different topics. Our contributions include providing an understanding of the challenges of an online epilepsy community and their social support needs, and implications for designing technologies.
A VPS4A Microtubule-Interacting and Trafficking Domain Variant in a Child With Hypomyelination and Developmental Regression: A Case Report With Features of CIMDAG Syndrome.浏览
PMID 43008389 · Pediatric neurology · 2026 10 22
作者：Zhang J, Uctepe E, Sargın H, Sonmez FM
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
摘要：BACKGROUND: Hypomyelinating leukodystrophy in infancy has a broad differential diagnosis. Heterozygous variants in VPS4A, an endosomal sorting complexes required for transport-associated AAA-ATPase, have been reported in CIMDAG (cerebellar hypoplasia-cataracts-impaired intellectual development-congenital microcephaly- dystonia-dyserythropoietic anemia-growth retardation) syndrome, a multisystem neurodevelopmental disorder with variable systemic involvement. METHODS: A 14-month-old girl presented with inability to crawl or walk and delayed milestones. Examination showed a head circumference of 43.5 cm, a depressed nasal bridge, bilateral 4th-5th toe clinodactyly, motor delay, and hypotonia. Metabolic screening and targeted genetic tests were nondiagnostic. Maternal testing was negative; paternal testing was unavailable. The laboratory classified the variant as a variant of uncertain significance. CONCLUSIONS: The clinical and imaging findings overlap with the VPS4A-associated CIMDAG spectrum. This case describes a candidate heterozygous microtubule-interacting and trafficking domain variant in a child with hypomyelination and regression; its contribution to the phenotype remains unconfirmed.
Comparative cytokine profiling in cerebrospinal fluid and serum of dogs with meningoencephalitis of unknown origin.浏览
PMID 43008071 · Journal of veterinary internal medicine · 2026 Nov 02
作者：Gasser A, Démoulins T, Stein VM
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
摘要：BACKGROUND: Meningoencephalitis of unknown origin (MUO) is a common inflammatory central nervous system (CNS) disease in dogs with unclear etiology, complicating diagnosis. HYPOTHESIS/OBJECTIVES: Dogs with MUO exhibit distinct cytokine profiles in cerebrospinal fluid (CSF) compared to other CNS diseases and controls. ANIMALS: The study included 23 dogs diagnosed with MUO based on clinical signs, supportive CSF, and imaging findings. For comparison, 110 dogs were included, comprising dogs with CNS disorders, neurological diseases not involving the CNS (non-CNS), and orthopedic diseases. METHODS: In this prospective cross-sectional observational study, 6 cytokines (interferon-γ [IFN-γ], TNF-α, IL-2, IL-6, IL-10, IL-15) were measured in paired CSF and serum samples using multiplex bead-based immunoassays. Primary outcome was the association between CSF cytokine concentrations and MUO, assessed by logistic regression. RESULTS: Meningoencephalitis of unknown origin showed significantly higher CSF concentrations compared to idiopathic epilepsy, intracranial neoplasia, intervertebral disc herniation, and non-CNS diseases in IFN-γ (MUO: median 108.4 pg/mL, IQR 25.8 vs comparison group: 10.5 pg/mL, IQR 0.0; β-coefficient 0.039, 95% CI, 0.021-0.057, P < .001) and interleukin (IL)-10 (MUO: 171.1 pg/mL, IQR 37.0 vs 8.5 pg/mL, IQR 0.0; β-coefficient 0.021, 95% CI, 0.011-0.030, P < .001). Moreover, TNF-α (β-coefficient 0.062, 95% CI, 0.023-0.102, P < .01), IL-15 (β-coefficient 0.080, 95% CI, 0.012-0.148, P < .05) and IL-2 (β-coefficient 0.055, 95% CI, 0.005-0.104, P < .05) concentrations were also elevated in MUO versus comparison group. CONCLUSIONS AND CLINICAL IMPORTANCE: Cerebrospinal fluid cytokine profiling identifies a characteristic intrathecal immune signature in MUO, advancing the understanding of its immunopathogenesis.
Side-to-side and other complex head movements as ictal signs in epilepsy: A video-EEG case series and review of literature.推荐
PMID 43007049 · Epileptic disorders : international epilepsy journal with videotape · 2026 11 10
作者：Pourlou C, Vassallo P, Torzillo E, Oliveira JFA et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
摘要：Side-to-side head shaking is often considered a distinctive clinical sign that differentiates functional seizures from epileptic seizures; however, it may also occur as an ictal phenomenon in focal epilepsy. We retrospectively reviewed all video-telemetry reports of patients with drug-resistant focal epilepsy undergoing presurgical evaluation between 2015 and 2025. All reports were electronically screened for mentioning side-to-side or other complex head movements and the video-EEG of these patients was reviewed and included if associated with ictal EEG changes. Seventeen patients were identified who experienced 631 seizures in total, 201 of which included such movements (median: 2 per patient, range: 1-160). One patient (No. 5) accounted for 79% of these events and represented an outlier. Head movements were predominantly horizontal (93.5%), followed by mixed (5%) and rotatory (1.5%). While small-amplitude movements predominated overall, larger amplitudes were more frequent after excluding the outlier case. In six patients, head movements were the initial observed feature of seizure semiology and typically represented a brief component of the seizure, although prolonged in the outlier case. Interrater agreement ranged from fair to good across semiological variables. Presumed epileptogenic zones were heterogenous, including frontal, temporal, anterior quadrant, and hemispheric regions. Ictal side-to-side and other complex head movements can occur in focal epilepsies but lack localizing or lateralizing value. They likely reflect seizure propagation and involvement of broader neuronal circuits. Further studies are needed to clarify their mechanisms and clinical significance.

Chemogenetic activation of parvalbumin interneurons in the piriform cortex alleviates hippocampal focal seizures.推荐
PMID 43007027 · Epilepsia · 2026 11 10
作者：Zhang H, Li N, Huang Z, Lau CG
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
摘要：OBJECTIVE: The anterior piriform cortex (APC) is a limbic area closely associated with temporal lobe epilepsy (TLE) but is understudied. Our previous study demonstrated that in the APC, parvalbumin-expressing (PV+) interneurons provide strong inhibition and help maintain excitation-inhibition balance. Here, we examined whether and how APCPV neurons can ameliorate seizure symptoms in TLE. METHODS: We used a chronic intrahippocampal kainic acid (IHKA) mouse model that develops hippocampal pathology and spontaneous recurrent seizures (SRSs). APCPV neurons were chemogenetically activated, and local field potentials were recorded longitudinally from multiple regions. Seizure metrics, spectral power, and functional connectivity were compared between APCPV-activated and control samples. RESULTS: Loss of PV+ synapses emerged in the APC during epileptogenesis. Selective activation of APCPV neurons reduced the frequency and duration of hippocampal SRSs and modified long-range dynamics, with region- and frequency-specific changes in interictal band power and functional connectivity. SIGNIFICANCE: APCPV chemogenetic activation exerts a robust anti-seizure effect and identifies the APC as a promising cortical node for seizure network control. The associated spectral and connectivity signatures suggest that targeted modulation of APC microcircuits can rebalance distributed seizure networks and lower seizure recurrence.
Current landscape of small molecule MGMT expression inhibitors as potential resistance reversers for glioblastoma.浏览
PMID 43006171 · Future medicinal chemistry · 2026 11 10
作者：Wang Q, Zhang Y, Zhao L, Zhong R et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：综述
摘要：Primary or acquired temozolomide (TMZ) resistance in glioblastoma (GBM) critically limits clinical efficacy, with O6‑methylguanine‑DNA methyltransferase (MGMT) serving as the key repair protein that directly reverses TMZ-induced O6-methylguanine (O6-MG) lesions. Classical MGMT inactivators such as O6‑benzylguanine (O6‑BG) and O6‑(4‑bromothenyl)guanine (O6‑4‑BTG, lomeguatrib) effectively deplete MGMT activity and sensitize tumor cells to alkylating agents. However, their lack of tumor selectivity depletes MGMT in normal hematopoietic tissues, inducing dose-limiting myelotoxicity and necessitating counterstrategies such as MGMT-tolerant hematopoietic stem cell protection. To overcome this bottleneck, MGMT intervention strategies have expanded from direct enzymatic inhibition toward regulation at the expression level. This review focuses on small molecules that downregulate MGMT expression through transcriptional and epigenetic regulation, CNS-active drug interventions, modulation of MGMT-related signaling pathways, and naturally derived bioactive compounds. It also summarizes emerging technological approaches including tumor-targeted conjugation, blood-brain barrier-penetrating delivery systems, MGMT gene silencing via siRNA, and induced protein degradation through ubiquitin-proteasome modulation. The expression-regulation strategy offers a broader therapeutic window for restoring GBM sensitivity to TMZ. Nevertheless, clinical translation still requires further optimization in tumor selectivity, effective brain exposure, rational combination timing, and robust pharmacodynamic validation to establish meaningful patient benefit.
Surgical management of meningioma in a resource-limited setting: a case report.推荐
PMID 43006120 · International journal of surgery case reports · 2026 09 29
作者：Edouard RK, Ndeni DM
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：病例报告
摘要：BACKGROUND: Meningiomas represent the most prevalent primary intracranial neoplasms, accounting for approximately 30% of all intracranial tumors, with 90% classified as benign (World Health Organization [WHO] Grade I). Surgical resection remains the primary therapeutic modality. However, in low-income and conflict-affected regions, timely diagnosis and surgical intervention are frequently constrained by limited access to neuroimaging and specialized neurosurgical care. CASE PRESENTATION: We present the case of a 37-year-old male who developed progressive neurological deterioration over 6 months. Clinical features included left-sided hemiparesis, aphasia, seizures, right-eye blindness, and sphincter incontinence. Cranial computed tomography (CT) revealed a right fronto-parietal extra-axial mass with substantial peritumoral edema, subfalcine herniation, and probable transtentorial involvement. The patient underwent gross total resection of a 5 cm encapsulated tumor via craniotomy. Histopathological examination confirmed a WHO Grade I meningioma composed of epithelioid cells arranged in clusters, interspersed with fibroblast-like cells. Postoperative recovery was favorable, with partial neurological improvement and persistent visual impairment. DISCUSSION: This case highlights the challenges associated with the diagnosis and management of intracranial meningiomas in resource-limited and conflict-affected settings, where delayed presentation and restricted access to neuroimaging frequently result in advanced neurological deterioration at the time of diagnosis. Despite the absence of several standard neurosurgical resources, including preoperative magnetic resonance imaging and neuronavigation systems, successful surgical management was achieved through careful CT-based anatomical planning and meticulous microsurgical technique. Histopathological examination confirmed a WHO Grade I meningioma with a low MIB-1 (Ki-67) labeling index (<3%), supporting a favorable long-term prognosis following Simpson Grade I gross-total resection. CONCLUSION: This study demonstrates how care can be provided in a constrained environment using a preoperative CT scan and a Simpson Grade I excision. There was an improvement in symptoms, but visual loss could not be recovered.
Molecular origami in neuroscience: noncanonical DNA structures and functional nucleic acids in neural regulation and disease.推荐
PMID 43005901 · Frontiers in neural circuits · 2026 10 26
作者：Roy S, Pradhan AK, Jainarayanan AK
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：综述
摘要：Gliomas are increasingly recognized as network diseases in which aberrant interactions between tumor cells, neurons, and immune populations drive hyperexcitability, seizures, cognitive dysfunction, and disease progression. Simultaneously, advances in structural biology and nucleic acid engineering have revealed DNA to be a dynamic molecular architecture capable of sensing, regulating, and modulating cellular states. In this review, we examine how noncanonical DNA structures and functional nucleic acids including Z-DNA, G-quadruplexes, cruciform structures, aptamers, and DNA origami systems, shape neural regulation and glioma associated circuit pathology. We discuss how activity dependent DNA conformational transitions influence transcriptional programs controlling neurotransmission, ion channel expression, synaptic signaling, metabolism, and inflammatory pathways in glioma cells and peritumoral neural networks. Z-DNA formation appears linked to rapid transcriptional activation and enhancer dynamics, whereas G-quadruplex rich regulatory regions modulate genes associated with excitatory signaling, cellular proliferation, and tumor progression. Cruciform DNA structures additionally interface with genome stability and DNA repair, creating mechanistic entry points for therapeutic intervention. Beyond endogenous DNA architectures, we highlight the emergence of aptamer based diagnostics and therapeutics capable of detecting neurotransmitters, cytokines, and metabolites associated with glioma induced network dysfunction. DNA origami platforms further enable programmable multivalent delivery systems for targeted transport of small molecules, oligonucleotides, CRISPR machinery, and ion-channel modulators across the neurovascular unit and tumor microenvironment. We also explore closed-loop neuromodulatory strategies in which electrophysiological signatures guide state dependent therapeutic actuation through nucleic acid based sensing and delivery systems. Finally, we discuss key translational considerations, including blood brain barrier delivery, nuclease stability, immunogenicity, biodistribution, manufacturing reproducibility, and integration with existing neuro-oncology and anti-seizure therapies. By bridging molecular origami, electrophysiology, and neurotechnology, this review proposes a framework for precision neuromedicine in glioma and broader neurological disease, where programmable nucleic acid architectures are coupled to the dynamic circuit states that drive pathology.
Surgical management of splenic artery aneurysms: a case series of three patients with diverse presentations.推荐
PMID 43005826 · International journal of surgery case reports · 2026 10 23
作者：Mahmoodi S, Shadidi Asil R, Sami K, Dodangeh M
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
摘要：BACKGROUND: Splenic artery aneurysm (SAA) is the most common visceral artery aneurysm and includes true aneurysms and pseudoaneurysms, which have different etiologies and clinical behavior. Presentation ranges from incidental detection to life-threatening rupture with hemoperitoneum. Management depends on aneurysm size, symptoms, morphology, anatomical relationships, comorbidities, and rupture status. METHODS: We retrospectively described three consecutive patients with SAA or pseudoaneurysm treated surgically at a tertiary surgical center, Loghman Hakim Hospital, Shahid Beheshti University of Medical Sciences, Tehran, Iran, between 2020 and 2025. Clinical records, imaging, operative findings, histopathology, postoperative course, and available follow-up were reviewed. The series was reported in accordance with the PROCESS 2025 reporting guideline. CASE PRESENTATIONS: Case 1 was a 66-year-old man with chronic liver disease and thrombocytopenia in whom a giant true SAA measuring approximately 72 × 68 × 76 mm was incidentally identified during trauma imaging. The aneurysm was adjacent to the pancreatic body without rupture. Elective open splenectomy with aneurysm resection and concurrent liver wedge biopsy was performed. Case 2 was a 63-year-old woman with a 7-month history of postprandial epigastric pain and a symptomatic true SAA of undocumented size. Elective open resection with splenectomy was performed; histopathology unexpectedly demonstrated necrotizing granulomatous splenitis. Case 3 was a 38-year-old woman with sickle cell disease who developed acute abdominal pain after a generalized seizure, with severe anemia and hemoperitoneum. A ruptured splenic artery pseudoaneurysm was identified at emergency laparotomy after a ruptured ectopic pregnancy had initially been suspected. RESULTS: All three patients underwent open splenectomy with aneurysm excision, which was elective in cases 1 and 2 and emergent in case 3. There were no intraoperative deaths or reoperations. All three patients had uneventful postoperative recoveries. 6-month follow-up was available for case 1; longer-term uniform follow-up was not available for all patients. CONCLUSION: SAAs encompass a broad clinical spectrum, from incidentally discovered giant aneurysms to symptomatic lesions and catastrophic rupture. These cases illustrate the importance of anatomy-directed treatment selection, early recognition of rupture, and consideration of vascular causes in patients with unexplained hemoperitoneum. Endovascular therapy is preferred for anatomically suitable elective lesions, whereas open surgery remains an important option for giant, complex, or ruptured aneurysms. The proposed algorithm is an educational synthesis of published evidence, current Society for Vascular Surgery guidelines recommendations, and the clinical experience represented by this institutional series rather than a prospectively validated decision tool.
Changes in Staff Knowledge and Clinical Confidence Following a Structured Teaching Programme on Functional Neurological Disorders: A Two-Cycle Quality Improvement Project.推荐
PMID 43005709 · Cureus · 2026 11 09
作者：Senthilkumar S, Chauhan D, Kondalkar K, Islam MT et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
摘要：Background Functional neurological disorder (FND) is a common neurological condition requiring accurate diagnosis, effective communication, and coordinated multidisciplinary management. Healthcare professionals' knowledge and confidence can influence the quality of care provided to patients with FND. This study evaluated baseline staff knowledge, awareness and confidence in managing FND and assessed the impact of a structured multidisciplinary teaching programme on a tertiary neurology ward. Methods A questionnaire-based study was conducted among healthcare professionals and support staff involved in patient care on a tertiary neurology ward. Participants included doctors, nurses, healthcare assistants, pharmacy technicians, physiotherapists, occupational therapists and ward clerks. The questionnaire explored knowledge of FND, recognition of positive clinical signs, attitudes towards FND, understanding of treatment options, confidence in clinical management and perception of services in place. Following the baseline assessment, a dedicated teaching week was delivered, comprising daily sessions on the clinical features of FND, underlying mechanisms, dissociative seizures, nursing management and available treatment resources. A second questionnaire was administered following the intervention to the same set of participants. Responses from the two cycles were compared. Results The pre-teaching survey included 45 respondents, and the post-teaching survey included 30. Despite frequent clinical exposure to FND, 73.3% of baseline respondents reported no previous formal FND teaching. Higher self-reported confidence ratings were observed in the post-teaching group across all five assessed domains. The proportion reporting higher confidence (scores of 4-5) in recognising FND was 46.7% before teaching and 86.7% afterwards, while confidence in explaining the diagnosis was 48.9% and 90.0%, respectively. The proportion identifying the complete set of positive clinical signs was 17.8% in the pre-teaching group and 63.3% in the post-teaching group. Recognition that FND can be diagnosed clinically using positive signs was reported by 37.8% and 76.7%, respectively. Recognition of multidisciplinary management was high in both groups (88.9% and 96.7%). These findings represent descriptive differences between anonymous, potentially overlapping respondent groups rather than measured individual improvements. Conclusion A dedicated multidisciplinary teaching week was associated with higher self-reported confidence and greater recognition of positive clinical signs of FND among post-teaching respondents. The findings highlight a potential educational gap among staff who frequently encounter FND in clinical practice and suggest that structured, recurring teaching may be beneficial. However, the small sample size, anonymous questionnaires, absence of a control group, and lack of longer-term follow-up or patient outcome measures limit causal interpretation and assessment of sustained benefits.
Clinical characterization and in silico structural analysis of microtubule-related gene variants in eleven patients.推荐
PMID 43005693 · Frontiers in genetics · 2026 10 27
作者：Niu Y, Xu Z, Zhou Z, Jiao X et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
摘要：OBJECTIVE: This study aimed to analyze associations of Microtubules (MTs) mutations with nervous system phenotypes, summarize mutation characteristics of genes including TUBA1A, TUBB2A, TUBB4A, and TUBGCP6, explore clinical phenotypic diversity and molecular mechanisms, and expand the MTs genotype-phenotype spectrum. METHODS: Eleven patients with MTs gene mutations were enrolled, and their clinical data were collected. Protein structure prediction was performed using AlphaFold3, and FoldX was employed to calculate mutation-induced energy changes, thereby evaluating protein stability and conformational impacts. RESULTS: While global developmental delay was present in all individuals (11/11, 100%), epileptic seizures and structural brain abnormalities were variable phenotypic features, observed in 81.8% (9/11; including 8 with unprovoked epilepsy and 1 with an isolated febrile seizure) and 63.6% (7/11) of patients, respectively, with normal cranial MRI findings documented in four patients (36.4%). Four patients with TUBA1A were associated with severe cortical developmental malformations (lissencephaly, pachygyria), with two exhibiting dynamic developmental regression; four patients with TUBB2A consistently presented with severe developmental impairment and intractable epilepsy (including infantile epileptic spasm syndrome and Lennox-Gastaut syndrome), some displaying sound-induced reflex seizures, while none manifested developmental regression during the follow-up period. Two patients with TUBB4A showed marked differences in age of onset, and one presented with an isolated co-occurrence of systemic lupus erythematosus; one patient with TUBGCP6 was mainly characterized by microcephaly and short stature without epilepsy. Across the 11 patients, a total of 12 variants were identified, comprising 10 missense variants, one non-canonical intronic/splice variant, and one copy number duplication. Prediction of protein structure and energy changes for the 10 missense variants showed varying degrees of increased free energy. Based on in silico structural predictions, we hypothesize that these mutations may potentially impair microtubule function by altering hydrogen bonds and conformation, with representative examples including TUBA1A Y172D (5.15 kcal/mol energy increase) and TUBB4A G10S (introduction of new hydrogen bond interactions). CONCLUSION: This study delineates the diverse clinical spectrum across patients harboring MTs variants: TUBA1A variants were predominantly observed in individuals with cortical malformations and potential developmental regression, TUBB2A with severe static neurodevelopmental delay and drug-resistant epilepsy, TUBB4A with motor impairment (with systemic lupus erythematosus noted in one individual as an unusual co-observation), and TUBGCP6 with primary growth and developmental delay. In silico structural modeling (AlphaFold3 and FoldX) indicates that missense substitutions may perturb protein stability and local conformation (with ΔΔG values varying across sites); however, because these variants are predominantly identified in single individuals, these computational insights represent theoretical biophysical hypotheses rather than validated variant-specific phenotypic signatures. Further functional assays and larger cohorts are required to establish definitive genotype-phenotype correlations.
CHST3 Skeletal Dysplasia and GRIA3 Neurodevelopmental Disorder: A Dual Diagnosis With a Recurrent Syrian CHST3 Variant.推荐
PMID 43005514 · Case reports in genetics · 2026 11 08
作者：Hashemi B, Nkrumah EAF
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
摘要：CHST3-related skeletal dysplasia is a rare autosomal recessive skeletal disorder characterized by disproportionate short stature, progressive skeletal abnormalities, and congenital joint dislocations, typically with preserved cognitive function. We report a Syrian boy presenting with severe skeletal dysplasia, epilepsy, profound developmental delay, and intellectual disability. Clinical evaluation and trio exome sequencing identified a homozygous recurrent pathogenic variant in CHST3 and a de novo likely pathogenic splice-site variant in GRIA3. The skeletal manifestations, including the pattern of dysplasia, were consistent with CHST3-related disease. However, the severity of the neurodevelopmental phenotype and epilepsy were not explained by CHST3 deficiency alone and were more consistent with a GRIA3-related neurodevelopmental disorder. This case illustrates the importance of considering dual molecular diagnoses when a patient's phenotype extends beyond the expected clinical spectrum of a known genetic condition. Furthermore, it provides additional evidence supporting the pathogenicity of a recurrent CHST3 variant observed in individuals of Syrian ancestry, suggesting a possible founder effect. Recognition of blended phenotypes resulting from multiple genetic diagnoses is essential to avoid inappropriate expansion of disease phenotypes and to ensure accurate genetic counseling and clinical management.
In Silico Assessment of SYNGAP1 Missense Variant Pathogenicity.推荐
PMID 43005510 · Human mutation · 2026 11 08
作者：Ali AE, Otranen HM, Lehtonen JV, Courtney MJ et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
摘要：SynGAP, a critical regulator at the postsynaptic density, is associated with Alzheimer's disease, tumorigenesis, and most notably, SYNGAP1-related disorders (SRD), which manifests as autism, epilepsy, and cognitive impairment. SRD is likely primarily caused by SynGAP missense (SGM) variants, whose pathogenicity is difficult to confirm experimentally. To address this issue, a wide range of structure- and/or sequence-based as well as machine learning- and deep learning-based in silico predictors were benchmarked against clinically annotated SRD data. Folding stability calculations (e.g., Foldetta) and deep learning methods ESM1b and AlphaMissense showed the highest prediction accuracy under default settings. Applying optimized nondefault thresholds, especially with AlphaMissense, could further improve predictor performance. The predictions exhibited a strong structural pattern: Pathogenic variants were highly enriched within defined structural domains, particularly the GAP and PH domains, whereas variants located in nondomain and nonmotif regions were predominantly predicted to be benign. The verified phosphosites and predicted phase separation impacting variants occupy matching sections, including the protein's N-terminal region and the vicinity of putative SH3-binding motif in the disordered regions. Thus, these regions collectively could act as phase separation-promoting segments, complementing the protein's trimerization-coupled coiled-coil domain. Finally, the SynGAP Missense (SGM) server was presented to support SynGAP variant assessment. The SGM server integrates a Data Table with top-performing predictors, a 3D Viewer for structure-level assessment and AI-generated lay summaries for all variants arising from single-base substitutions, including those not yet observed in public databases.
Gastrodia elata in neurological disorders: a systematic review of anti-inflammatory mechanisms and cross-disease therapeutic potential.浏览
PMID 43005312 · Frontiers in aging neuroscience · 2026 10 26
作者：Chu YY, Wang BY, Qu CY, Lan Y et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：系统综述
摘要：BACKGROUND: Neuroinflammation serves as a common pathological substrate across various neurological disorders, including neurodegenerative diseases, cerebrovascular diseases, and neuroimmune conditions. Key pathogenic mechanisms underlying these disorders include aberrant microglial activation, sustained release of pro-inflammatory cytokines, and dysregulation of critical signaling cascades, particularly the NLRP3 inflammasome, NF-κB, and Nrf2/HO-1 pathways, all of which collectively contribute to progressive neuronal damage and functional decline. OBJECTIVES: This review aims to systematically evaluate the preclinical evidence for the neuroprotective effects of Gastrodia elata Blume and its major bioactive constituents-gastrodin, parishin, p-hydroxybenzyl alcohol, and G. elata polysaccharides-in the treatment of Parkinson's disease, Alzheimer's disease, acute ischemic stroke, vascular dementia, and epilepsy, with a particular focus on their modulation of inflammation-related signaling pathways. METHODS: A comprehensive literature search was performed in PubMed, Web of Science, ScienceDirect, China National Knowledge Infrastructure (CNKI), and Wanfang databases from inception to May 2026. Original studies investigating G. elata or its components in animal or cellular models of the aforementioned diseases and reporting inflammatory mechanisms were included. RESULTS: G. elata and its active constituents regulate multiple neuroinflammation-associated signaling pathways, including Nrf2/HO-1, NF-κB/NLRP3, JAK2/STAT3, and MAPK, thereby suppressing inflammatory responses. Mechanistically, these effects are primarily manifested as: (1) inhibition of microglial M1 polarization and consequent reduction of pro-inflammatory cytokine release; (2) blockade of inflammation-induced neuronal death through suppression of ferroptosis, pyroptosis, and apoptosis; (3) enhancement of Nrf2 nuclear translocation, leading to upregulation of antioxidant enzymes and attenuation of oxidative stress-mediated inflammation (e.g., ROS-driven cascades); and (4) modulation of the gut microbiota-brain axis to reduce both peripheral and central low-grade chronic inflammation. CONCLUSION: Despite certain limitations in the existing evidence, this review clearly identifies future research priorities, including investigation of non-gastrodin active components, causal validation using gene-knockout models, and development of brain-targeted delivery strategies. These findings provide a valuable reference for the mechanism-guided development of neuroprotective agents derived from G. elata.
Clinical features of ROHHAD syndrome and treatment outcomes following rituximab therapy: a descriptive case series of Chinese children.推荐
PMID 43005169 · Frontiers in pediatrics · 2026 10 26
作者：Song D, Chang X, Zhang Y, Wu Y et al.
