命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
摘要：OBJECTIVE: We aimed to delineate the clinical phenotypes and symptom evolution of Rapid-onset Obesity with Hypothalamic dysfunction, Hypoventilation, and Autonomic Dysregulation (ROHHAD) syndrome in Chinese children and to evaluate the real-world efficacy of rituximab therapy. METHODS: We retrospectively reviewed the medical records of seven children diagnosed with ROHHAD syndrome between 2010 and 2025. Clinical data on baseline characteristics, core symptoms, tumor status, and therapeutic responses were extracted and analyzed using descriptive statistics. RESULTS: Among the 7 patients, there were 3 males and 4 females. The median age of onset was 2.8 years (2.0-7.9 years), and the median age at diagnosis was 3.8 years (3.5-8.5 years). The median diagnostic delay was 1.2 years (range, 0.5-3.6 years). All patients exhibited the triad of rapid-onset obesity, hypothalamic dysfunction, and autonomic dysregulation. The median ages of onset for obesity, hypothalamic dysfunction, and autonomic dysregulation were 3.2, 3.1, and 3.4 years, respectively. Neuropsychiatric abnormalities were universal (7/7, 100%), emerging concurrently with or shortly after weight gain. Central hypoventilation was observed in 5 patients (71.4%). Neural crest-derived tumors were found in 4 patients (57.1%). Cardiac involvement was present in 4 patients (57.1%), with 3 cases developing severe heart failure. All patients had neurological abnormalities, with 2 cases accompanied by epileptic seizures. Interleukin-6 (IL-6) levels were tested in 4 patients, with elevation in 2 cases. Six patients received first-line immunotherapy (corticosteroids/intravenous immunoglobulin) with poor response. Three patients received rituximab; all showed transient initial improvement but ultimately experienced relentless disease progression, and one patient died. The overall prognosis was poor: one death, one vegetative state, and progressive disease in the remaining 5 patients. CONCLUSION: ROHHAD syndrome presents with highly heterogeneous clinical manifestations. Neuropsychiatric abnormalities may occur in parallel with or closely following rapid-onset obesity. Unexplained obesity and neuropsychiatric abnormalities in young children should raise high suspicion for this syndrome. Such patients warrant routine multidisciplinary monitoring. In the three patients who received rituximab, clinical responses were variable and transient, with no sustained long-term improvement observed. However, due to the small sample size and heterogeneous treatment protocols, no definitive conclusion regarding rituximab efficacy can be drawn.
Visualization of super-selective Wada with computed tomography perfusion: toward a novel option in preoperative functional mapping.推荐
PMID 43005065 · Frontiers in neuroimaging · 2026 10 26
作者：Nakae S, Hayakawa M, Kumon M, Kawase N et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
摘要：INTRODUCTION: The Wada test has long been a well-established method for evaluating hemispheric dominance and functional localization; however, it has not been established as a visualizable functional imaging modality. Recently, the super-selective Wada test (ssWada) has emerged as a technique enabling more localized functional evaluation. By selectively catheterizing distal cerebral arterial branches, ssWada enables territory-based functional evaluation with higher spatial resolution than conventional approaches. In this study, we combined ssWada with computed tomography perfusion (CTP) to visualize pharmacologically inactivated brain regions preoperatively. These data were further fused with magnetic resonance imaging (MRI) and CT to propose a novel functional mapping approach delineating the relationship between perfusion-defined functional territories, lesions, and seizure onset zone (SOZ)-related stereoelectroencephalography (SEEG) contacts. METHODS: Patients who underwent ssWada for preoperative evaluation of brain tumors or drug-resistant epilepsy at our institution were included. During ssWada, propofol was selectively injected via a microcatheter into the ICA and distal branches of the middle cerebral artery (MCA), as well as the anterior or posterior cerebral artery when necessary. Perfusion territories were simultaneously visualized using CTP. These datasets were subsequently fused with MRI and post-SEEG CT to evaluate the spatial relationship between perfusion-defined functional territories, lesions, and SOZ-related SEEG contacts. RESULTS: Among the 23 patients, hemispheric dominance was left in 18 patients (78.3%), bilateral in 3 (13.0%), and right in 2 (8.7%). No complications were observed. In the MCA superior trunk territory, repetition, naming, and reading tasks showed positive rates of approximately 70%. In contrast, in the inferior trunk territory, repetition and reading demonstrated high positive rates (93.8%), whereas naming positivity was lower (56.3%). Fusion with MRI and post-SEEG CT enabled spatial visualization of lesions and SOZ-related SEEG contacts within perfusion-defined functional territories, facilitating individualized functional-anatomical assessment for surgical planning. DISCUSSION: ssWada combined with CTP enables territory-based visualization of pharmacologically inactivated brain regions and provides a novel preoperative functional mapping approach with high spatial resolution. Fusion with MRI and post-SEEG CT further facilitates individualized functional-anatomical assessment of lesions and SOZ-related networks. These findings support the clinical utility of ssWada-CTP fusion imaging as a novel strategy for individualized preoperative functional brain mapping.
Post-Traumatic Large Pneumoventricle With CSF Rhinorrhea: A Case Report.推荐
PMID 43005011 · Korean journal of neurotrauma · 2026 09 10
作者：Ankit A, Kumari R, Kumar A, Anwer M et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：病例报告
摘要：Pneumoencephalus results from the ingress of air into intracranial compartments due to a dural defect and is commonly encountered following head trauma or neurosurgical procedures. When air accumulates within the ventricular system, it is termed pneumoventricle, a rare entity that is usually self-limiting. However, progressive intraventricular air accumulation may lead to tension pneumoventricles, characterized by ventricular distension, mass effect, and features of raised intracranial pressure, necessitating urgent intervention. Post-traumatic large pneumoventricle is exceptionally uncommon, with only a few cases reported in the literature. A 23-year-old male who sustained a traumatic brain injury following a road traffic accident and initially presented with mild head injury was managed conservatively. He re-presented ten days later with generalized tonic-clonic seizure activity and cerebrospinal fluid rhinorrhea. Neuroimaging revealed pneumocephalus with large pneumoventricle. Despite radiological evidence of large ventricular air, the patient remained neurologically stable. A conservative management strategy comprising high-flow oxygen therapy, head elevation, avoidance of Valsalva maneuvers, carbonic anhydrase inhibition, and close neurological monitoring was instituted. Serial imaging demonstrated progressive resolution of intraventricular air, and the patient achieved complete clinical and radiological recovery without surgical intervention. This case highlights that, although large pneumoventricles are traditionally regarded as a neurosurgical emergency requiring ventricular decompression, selected neurologically stable patients may be successfully managed conservatively under strict monitoring. Early diagnosis, careful patient selection, and understanding of the underlying pathophysiology are crucial for optimal outcomes in this rare but potentially life-threatening condition.
Sequential Anti-HER2 (Human Epidermal Growth Factor Receptor 2) Therapy and Late-Line Tucatinib in Patients With HER2-Positive Breast Cancer and Brain Metastases: A Real-World Case Report.推荐
PMID 43004999 · Cureus · 2026 10 09
作者：Thakur P, Gupta M, Kant LC, Mehta P et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：病例报告
摘要：Human epidermal growth factor receptor 2 (HER2)-positive breast cancer is associated with a high incidence of brain metastases (BMs), particularly among young and heavily pretreated patients, with intracranial disease remaining a major determinant of survival and quality of life (QoL). Tucatinib, a HER2-selective tyrosine kinase inhibitor (TKI), combined with trastuzumab and capecitabine, has demonstrated significant progression-free survival (PFS) and overall survival (OS) benefit, including in patients with active BMs. We present the case reports of two patients, both younger than 50 years, with HER2-positive metastatic breast cancer and biologically aggressive disease who progressed on prior anti-HER2 therapy (trastuzumab, lapatinib, and ado-trastuzumab emtansine (Ujvira™) in Case 1 and trastuzumab in Case 2) with worsening central nervous system involvement. Both were subsequently treated with tucatinib-based combination therapy (Tukavo™) later in the treatment sequence, once it became available and affordable, and showed clinical and radiological improvement. Case 1 had improvement in right-sided limb weakness and speech with no further seizures and remained clinically stable for seven months, and Case 2 had complete resolution of headache and vomiting, with Eastern Cooperative Oncology Group (ECOG) performance status improving from 2 to 1. QoL was assessed clinically from symptoms and performance status rather than with a validated instrument. This real-world experience illustrates the potential benefit of tucatinib-based regimens in young patients with aggressive HER2-positive breast cancer and BMs, even when used later in the treatment sequence. These findings are consistent with pivotal trial data in a resource-limited, real-world setting.
Traumatic brain injury from a developing country in the CT-scan era: a 13-year prospective in-hospital cohort study.浏览
PMID 43004998 · Frontiers in neurology · 2026 10 26
作者：Adeleye AO, Agboola OO, Oyemolade TA, Malomo TA et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
摘要：INTRODUCTION: Most previous reports from Africa and other low middle-income countries (LMIC) on cranial neurotrauma address a more loosely defined subject of head injury (HI), rather than traumatic brain injury (TBI), and lack deployment of standardized scoring systems. We present a prospective TBI cohort from an academic neurosurgery practice at Ibadan, Nigeria, the nation's foremost university teaching hospital where TBI constitutes a core component of our clinical activity. MATERIALS AND METHODS: This was a prospective cohort study of patients enrolled in a TBI registry over 13 years. We analyzed the clinical epidemiology of TBI, defined as cranial trauma with definite perturbation of brain function, qualified/quantified using relevant clinical features, the Glasgow Coma Scale (GCS), the Abbreviated Injury Scale-Head (AIS-Head), and Injury Severity Score (ISS). Brain imaging was graded using the Rotterdam CT scoring system. In-hospital outcome was assessed with Glasgow Outcome Scale (GOS), dichotomized for analysis. RESULTS: There were 1,440 TBI cases, 1,110 (77.1%) males, mean age [32.2 Standard deviation (SD), 19] years. Most (76%) were referrals, with delayed presentation from outlying health facilities, median duration 13 [interquartile range (IQR) 5, 48] hours. Road trauma accounted for 1,173 (82%), even in the elderly, predominantly by two- and three-wheelers (57%); fall for 136 (9.4%), this mainly in childhood. Clinically, loss of consciousness (LOC) occurred in 89%, median duration 11.0 (IQR 2, 48) hours, vomiting 25%, seizures 16%, and anisocoria in 27%. In 86% the AIS-head was >2; the TBI was moderate-severe, GCS 3-12, in 47.0%. Brain CT was obtained in 1,046/1,440 (72.6%), while 13% received acute operative treatment. In-hospital mortality was 21%, mostly among the young and the severely injured. Good outcome (GOS: good recovery/moderate deficit) was 75%. The outcomes were significantly associated with the TBI severity per the GCS scores and brain CT severity. CONCLUSIONS: The clinical epidemiology of TBI from this single-institution LMIC series differs substantially from that of high-income countries. A 21% mortality predominantly affecting young economically productive adults represents a serious public health burden. Substantial gaps persist across many aspects of the TBI-management continuum, presenting clear opportunities to improve the current sub-optimal outcome-metrics in sub-Saharan Africa.
CD44 Predisposes the Epileptogenesis of Cortical Tubers by Regulating CASK and Acts as a Potential Biomarker to Identify Epileptogenic Tuber in Tuberous Sclerosis Complex.推荐
PMID 43004698 · CNS neuroscience & therapeutics · 2026 Nov
作者：Wang Z, Huang K, Liu X, Li Y et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
摘要：BACKGROUND: Tuberous sclerosis complex (TSC) is characterized by developmental delay and intractable epilepsy, with multiple cortical tubers in the brain. However, the mechanism underlying epileptogenesis within these tubers remains unclear. METHODS: RNA sequencing (RNA-seq) analysis of resected human cortical tubers identified differential CD44 expression as a potential contributor to TSC epilepsy. By analyzing clinical characteristics, resected brain tissues, 18F-fludeoxyglucose PET images, cultured cortical neurons, and neocortical seizure animal models, the effect of CD44 on the epileptogenesis of cortical tubers was investigated. RESULTS: Increased CD44 expression was found in human Epi tubers compared with non-Epi tubers and closely correlated with the clinical characteristics of Epi tubers. CD44 activation increased neocortical seizure susceptibility and aggravated cortical pyramidal neuronal excitatory/inhibitory (E/I) imbalance. CASK was selected as the downstream target of CD44 using RNA-seq. Anti-CD44 monoclonal antibodies rescued the E/I imbalance in resected Epi tubers, and CD44 activation rescued the decreased firing frequency and increased action potential threshold induced by CASK inhibition. The CD44-CASK pathway exhibited diagnostic potential for identifying Epi tubers and predicting surgical outcomes via ROC analysis. CONCLUSION: CD44 activation predisposes cortical tubers to epileptogenesis, and could be a potential biomarker for identifying Epi tubers and predicting surgical outcomes in TSC patients.

Delayed diagnosis of tuberous sclerosis complex in a young adult with long-term refractory epilepsy: a case report.推荐
PMID 43004460 · Journal of medical case reports · 2026 08 14
作者：Zhao D, Yu A, Chang Y, Xu L et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：病例报告
摘要：BACKGROUND: Tuberous sclerosis complex (TSC) is a rare autosomal dominant neurocutaneous hamartomatous disorder characterized by widespread multi-system hamartomatous lesions, with epilepsy occurring in the majority of affected patients. When refractory epilepsy serves as the sole long-standing clinical manifestation, subtle cutaneous and radiological hallmarks are easily overlooked, inevitably leading to diagnostic delay. CASE PRESENTATION: We herein report a 23-year-old female patient presenting with a decade-long history of drug-refractory seizures. Physical examination revealed mild cognitive impairment with a Mini-Mental State Examination (MMSE) score of 22 (technical secondary school education) and no focal neurological deficits. A typical lumbosacral shagreen patch and a surgical scar from prior great toe periungual fibroma resection were identified on cutaneous inspection. Brain MRI demonstrated white matter migration lines on fluid-attenuated inversion recovery (FLAIR) sequences, while cranial CT detected subependymal calcified nodules. Sanger genetic sequencing confirmed a heterozygous pathogenic splice-site variant NM_000368.5:c.107-2A > G in the TSC1. Given her childbearing potential, valproate was gradually tapered and substituted with lamotrigine to minimize teratogenic risks. Sirolimus-mediated mTOR-targeted therapy was initiated alongside renoprotective agents and regular serial imaging surveillance. Optimized combination therapy yielded a remarkable reduction in seizure frequency during follow-up. CONCLUSIONS: TSC may manifest insidiously for years with refractory epilepsy as its only prominent clinical feature. Comprehensive screening consisting of thorough cutaneous examination, standardized cranial imaging and molecular genetic testing is mandatory for early identification of this disorder. Individualized antiseizure regimens combined with mechanistic target of rapamycin (mTOR) inhibitors can significantly improve seizure control and long-term clinical outcomes in TSC patients.
A novel homozygous missense variant in the GAMT gene causing cerebral creatine deficiency syndrome 2 in an Iranian patient: molecular dynamics insights and literature review.浏览
PMID 43004291 · BMC medical genomics · 2026 08 10
作者：Al-Karim A, Khosravi T, Hajmohammadi M, Mohsenipour M et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：综述
摘要：Cerebral creatine deficiency syndrome 2 (CCDS2; OMIM #612,736) is a rare autosomal recessive neurometabolic disorder caused by variants in the GAMT gene, leading to deficient creatine synthesis in the brain. Here, we report a 17-year-old Iranian Fars female patient from a consanguineous marriage presenting with severe intellectual disability, inability to walk, muscle atrophy, and epileptiform EEG activity. Whole-exome sequencing identified a previously unreported homozygous missense variant in GAMT (NM_000156.6: c.326A > G, p.Lys109Arg), classified as likely pathogenic according to ACMG guidelines. The affected lysine residue at position 109 is highly conserved across species. Molecular dynamics (MD) simulations over 100 ns revealed that the p.Lys109Arg substitution induces a more compact global conformation (lower RMSD and Rg) but drastically alters the dynamic coupling network. Compared to wild-type, the mutant exhibited a substantially higher standard deviation in the cross-correlation matrix (0.323 vs. 0.236) and a markedly increased number of both strongly positive (4,006 vs. 1,574) and strongly negative (3,326 vs. 576) correlated residue pairs, indicating the emergence of extensive long-range allosteric perturbations that may disrupt functional dynamics and stability. Our report highlights the value of MD simulations as a tool to evaluate the structural consequences of genetic variants.
Epilepsy in women with progestogen-associated intracranial meningiomas.推荐
PMID 43004079 · Journal of neuro-oncology · 2026 11 09
作者：Hudelist B, Roux A, Elia A, Defrance G et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
摘要：PURPOSE: Progestogen-associated intracranial meningiomas are increasingly recognized as a distinct clinical entity, often characterized by multiplicity and preferential skull-base location. However, epilepsy in this population remains poorly characterized. We aimed to assess the prevalence, clinical features, and factors associated with epilepsy in women with progestogen-associated meningiomas. METHOD: We retrospectively analyzed consecutive adult women managed for intracranial meningioma at a tertiary neuro-oncology center between 2000 and 2024, with documented exposure to at least one progestogen associated with increased meningioma risk and available clinical and imaging data. Epilepsy was analyzed at the patient level using one index meningioma per patient. Given the low number of epilepsy events, analyses were descriptive. RESULTS: A total of 209 women were included (mean age 51.9 ± 10.1 years), including 69 (33.0%) with multiple meningiomas. Epilepsy at diagnosis occurred in 4.8% of patients and in 13.3% of symptomatic patients. Patients with epilepsy had larger index meningiomas than those without (24.6±24.3 vs 8.8 ± 16.9 cm3), more frequent meningioma-related brain edema (70.0% vs 19.1%), and more frequent midline mass effect (60.0% vs 13.6%). No association was detected with progestogen type or exposure pattern. Following antiseizure medication initiation, seizures were controlled in all patients with epilepsy at diagnosis. At last follow-up, seizures were controlled in 7/9 (77.8%) evaluable patients. During follow-up, de novo epilepsy remained uncommon, particularly among patients initially managed conservatively. CONCLUSION: Epilepsy is uncommon in women with progestogen-associated intracranial meningiomas. When present, it appears to be primarily related to tumor burden and meningioma-brain interface involvement, whereas no association with specific progestogen exposure characteristics was detected.
From phenytoin to pragmatism: evolution of second-line antiseizure medications for established convulsive status epilepticus.推荐
PMID 43004026 · Neurological sciences : official journal of the Italian Neurological Society and of the Italian Society of Clinical Neurophysiology · 2026 11 10
作者：Ngadimon IW, Lim KS, Yow HY, Fong SL
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：综述
摘要：The choice of intravenous (IV) second‑line antiseizure medication (ASM) in established convulsive status epilepticus (CSE) remains a key determinant of outcome yet has evolved largely in response to pragmatic constraints rather than definitive comparative superiority. Over the past seven decades, management has shifted from barbiturates and paraldehyde to phenytoin, and subsequently to fosphenytoin, valproate, levetiracetam, and, more recently, lacosamide. Early International League Against Epilepsy (ILAE) task force reports highlighted marked global variability in practice and emphasised rapid treatment escalation but did not endorse a preferred second-line agent. Contemporary evidence, including the American Epilepsy Society (AES) guideline and the Established Status Epilepticus Treatment Trial (ESETT), has reshaped second-line treatment, with ESETT demonstrating similar observed treatment success with fosphenytoin, valproate, and levetiracetam. Lacosamide has also emerged as a potential alternative, although randomised evidence is derived from nonconvulsive seizures and is therefore indirect for established CSE. This review synthesises the evolution of second‑line ASMs in established CSE following adequate benzodiazepine treatment, integrating pharmacological rationale, clinical trial evidence, and guideline development, while highlighting ongoing uncertainties in optimal agent selection and the positioning of newer therapies.
Episodic Ataxia Type 1推荐
PMID 20301785 · GeneReviews® · 2010-2-9（更新 2026-11-10）
作者：Hasan SM, Pessia M, D'Adamo MC
链接：PubMed
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：图书章节/更新
摘要：CLINICAL CHARACTERISTICS: Episodic ataxia type 1 (EA1) is a potassium channelopathy characterized by constant myokymia and dramatic episodes of spastic contractions of the skeletal muscles of the head, arms, and legs with loss of both motor coordination and balance. During attacks individuals may experience a number of variable symptoms including dysarthria, stiffening of the body, blurred vision, diplopia, nausea, headache, vertigo, diaphoresis, clumsiness, and difficulty in breathing, among others. EA1 may be associated with epilepsy. Other possible associations include delayed motor development, cognitive disability, choreoathetosis, and carpal spasm. Usually, onset is in childhood or early adolescence. DIAGNOSIS/TESTING: The diagnosis of EA1 is established in a proband with characteristic clinical findings, electrophysiologic evidence of axonal hyperexcitability, and a heterozygous pathogenic variant in KCNA1 identified by molecular genetic testing. MANAGEMENT: Treatment of manifestations: Acetazolamide, a carbonic-anhydrase inhibitor, may reduce the frequency and severity of attacks in some but not all affected individuals. Anti-seizure medication may significantly reduce the frequency of attacks in some individuals. Supportive therapies, such as physical therapy, may reduce the risk of later-onset orthopedic complications. Routine treatment of seizure disorders, scoliosis, and developmental disabilities. Surveillance: Annual neurologic examination; EEG as needed; at each visit assess developmental and educational progress, mobility, self-help skills, and family needs. Agents/circumstances to avoid: Triggers of attacks, including physical exertion, sudden movements, loud noises, emotional stress, and changes in environmental temperature. Marked generalized myokymia has been reported during induction of anesthesia. Evaluation of relatives at risk: Offer molecular genetic testing for the familial KCNA1 pathogenic variant to apparently asymptomatic older and younger at-risk relatives of an affected individual in order to identify affected individuals as early as possible and facilitate avoidance of known disease triggers. Pregnancy management: Affected women should be made aware that pregnancy may trigger attacks; possible loss of balance and falls could endanger the fetus. Several stressors that trigger attacks may cause breathing difficulties; thus, delivery by cesarean section should be considered. GENETIC COUNSELING: EA1 is inherited in an autosomal dominant manner. Most individuals diagnosed with EA1 have an affected parent; however, de novo pathogenic variants have been reported. Each child of an individual with EA1 has a 50% chance of inheriting the KCNA1 pathogenic variant. Significant clinical variability may be observed among affected family members, including differences in the severity and frequency of EA1 attacks. Once the KCNA1 pathogenic variant has been identified in an affected family member, prenatal and preimplantation genetic testing are possible.
Evaluation of serum isatin as a potential biomarker in patients with neuropsychiatric disorders: a prospective observational study.推荐
PMID 43003771 · Biomarkers in medicine · 2026 11 09
作者：Hota D, Mishra A, Shaju A, Mishra BR et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
摘要：AIMS: To evaluate serum isatin, an endogenous stress-responsive indole, as a diagnostic, pharmacodynamic and prognostic biomarker in major depressive disorder (MDD), seizures and schizophrenia. PATIENTS AND METHODS: In this prospective observational study, 120 participants (30 each with MDD, seizures or schizophrenia, and 30 healthy controls) were enrolled. Serum isatin was measured by HPLC at baseline and, in patients, after 12 weeks of standard pharmacotherapy. RESULTS: Baseline isatin was significantly higher in all disease groups than in controls and discriminated each disease with near-perfect accuracy (AUC 0.996-1.000). Isatin decreased significantly in all three disease groups after 12 weeks, most in schizophrenia (-23.91 ± 15.43 nmol/L), and responders showed greater reductions than non-responders in every disease. In seizures, baseline isatin predicted non-response (AUC 0.814; 95% CI 0.637-0.991). Mediation analysis identified competing pathways: higher baseline isatin drove greater isatin reduction while independently impairing response. Baseline isatin was the strongest predictor of follow-up isatin (β = 0.814, p < 0.001). CONCLUSION: Serum isatin shows promise as a transdiagnostic pharmacodynamic biomarker and as a prognostic marker in seizures, with its change being more informative than absolute levels. Validation in larger, multicentre longitudinal studies is needed before clinical use.
Efficacy and tolerability of brivaracetam as adjunctive therapy in children with drug-resistant epilepsy: a single-center prospective cohort study.推荐
PMID 43003710 · Journal of the Formosan Medical Association = Taiwan yi zhi · 2026 11 09
作者：Zhao F, Zhang W, Zhang Q, Jiang W et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
摘要：PURPOSE: To evaluate the clinical efficacy and tolerability of adjunctive brivaracetam (BRV) in children with drug-resistant epilepsy (DRE). METHODS: This single-center prospective cohort study enrolled children with DRE who received adjunctive BRV and were followed for 6 months. The primary outcome was the 50% seizure response rate at 3 months. Secondary outcomes included the 50% response rate and seizure-free rate at 6 months, adverse reactions, and retention rates assessed by survival analysis. RESULTS: A total of 104 patients were included (50 males and 54 females; median age at BRV initiation, 8.00 years). The most common seizure type was focal seizures (67.3%), and etiologies included genetic (13.5%), structural (21.1%), immune (5.8%), and unknown (59.6%) causes. The 50% seizure response rates were 54.8% (57/104) at 3 months and 47.1% (49/104) at 6 months. Corresponding seizure-free rates were 25.0% (26/104) and 20.2% (21/104), respectively. Survival analysis indicated a gradual decline in retention over time; however, the overall retention rate remained above 80%. Patients who responded to BRV had significantly higher retention rates than non-responders over the 6-month period (HR = 0.30, 95% CI: 0.11-0.77; p = 0.014). Adverse reactions were reported in 7.7% of patients, with irritability, dizziness, and somnolence as the most common. CONCLUSION: Adjunctive BRV therapy demonstrated favorable efficacy and good tolerability in children with DRE during short-term follow-up. These findings provide practical guidance for clinical decision-making and contribute to the real-world evidence from China on the use of adjunctive BRV therapy in pediatric DRE.

Sex representation and assessment of sex-related differences in central nervous system medicine development: a retrospective cross-sectional study of European Medicines Agency marketing authorisation dossiers (2012-2022).浏览
PMID 43003451 · BMJ open · 2026 11 09
作者：Schoenmakers S, Bakker C, Ganev A, Butlen-Ducuing F et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
摘要：OBJECTIVES: To assess representation of females and males in phase 2 and phase 3 clinical trials supporting centrally authorised medicines for central nervous system (CNS) disorders, and to evaluate how sex-related differences in effect were addressed during drug development and regulatory review. DESIGN: Marketing authorisation application (MAA) dossiers submitted to the European Medicines Agency for products authorised for marketing between 2012 and 2022 were evaluated. Participant sex distribution in phase 2 and phase 3 trials was analysed and compared with prevalence by disorder. Regulatory consideration of sex-related differences in non-clinical and clinical data, and their impact on the final Summary of Product Characteristics (SmPCs), was assessed. SETTING: Centralised regulatory procedure at the European Medicines Agency. PARTICIPANTS: 35 MAA dossiers covering 13 CNS disorders, including 28 677 participants in phase 2 and 53 075 in phase 3 studies. MAIN OUTCOMES: Female to male ratios in phase 2 and phase 3 trials, concordance with epidemiology of the licensed indication and the impact of sex-related regulatory considerations on product information. RESULTS: Females comprised 16 973 (59.2%) phase 2 and 32 873 (61.9%) phase 3 participants, respectively. Representation was highest in migraine, multiple sclerosis, depression and epilepsy and lowest in alcohol-related disorders and schizophrenia, broadly reflecting disease epidemiology. Initial regulatory assessments identified 25 sex-related non-clinical and clinical considerations across 19 products, concerning safety, efficacy and pharmacokinetics. However, in most cases, the clinical significance was considered negligible, and hence, sex-related differences were reflected in the final SmPC text only for two products. CONCLUSIONS: Females constituted the majority of participants in phase 2 and phase 3 CNS trials. Sex-related aspects were consistently considered during regulatory assessment. Usually, they were found to have no clinical relevance, and they rarely resulted in different recommendations for females and males in the SmPC text.
Suspected SCALP syndrome (sebaceous nevus, CNS malformations, aplasia cutis congenita, limbal dermoid, and pigmented nevus) and the spectrum of mosaic RASopathies: ophthalmic and systemic manifestations in a paediatric cohort.推荐
PMID 43003392 · Ophthalmic genetics · 2026 11 09
作者：Sasi A, Sharma B, Lingappa L, Edward DP et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
摘要：PURPOSE: To describe the clinical spectrum of the ophthalmic and systemic manifestations in children suspected as having SCALP (Sebaceous Nevus, CNS Malformations, Aplasia Cutis Congenita, Limbal Dermoid, and Pigmented Nevus) syndrome. METHODS: We reviewed the medical records of 13 children suspected to have SCALP syndrome between 1 January 2016 and 31 March 2025. Their demographic details and clinical findings were analyzed. RESULTS: Median age at presentation was 1.2 years (range: 5 days to 10 years). Of the 13 patients, 6 (46.2%) were aged ≤ 1 year. The cohort comprised 9 males and 4 females. One patient was born to consanguineous parents. Ocular involvement was bilateral in 6 patients and unilateral in 7. Among the cohort, two children had the complete SCALP phenotype, three presented with partial features, and eight had features of probable mosaic RASopathy. The most consistent clinical findings were limbal dermoids, which were present in all cases except one. Twelve children had scalp findings, with four having aplasia cutis and eight having nevus psiloliparus. Systemic manifestations observed included sebaceous nevi, developmental delay, epilepsy with cortical dysplasia. CONCLUSION: SCALP syndrome is a rare neurocutaneous disorder with consistent oculocutaneous features and variable neurological involvement. Early recognition and multidisciplinary care are essential. Developing structured diagnostic criteria and exploring genotypic-phenotypic links would be critical to improving outcomes. SCALP syndrome is best regarded as lying within the spectrum of mosaic RASopathies; in the absence of lesional molecular testing, the diagnosis in individual cases should be considered provisional, with aplasia cutis congenita serving as the key clinical discriminator.
Differential structural profiles of the hippocampus and thalamus and their cognitive relevance in unilateral mesial temporal lobe epilepsy.推荐
PMID 43003198 · Progress in neuro-psychopharmacology & biological psychiatry · 2026 11 09
作者：Qin X, Peng J, Yao C, Wang Y et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
摘要：BACKGROUND: Cognitive dysfunction is common in mesial temporal lobe epilepsy (MTLE), yet how much the hippocampus and the thalamus each contribute remains unclear. We compared fine-grained hippocampal and thalamic structure and their cognitive relevance in unilateral MTLE. METHODS: We studied 71 patients with unilateral MTLE (39 with hippocampal sclerosis [HS], 32 without) and 17 controls. FreeSurfer-derived cortical, hippocampal-subfield, and thalamic-nucleus volumes were compared using permutation-based general linear models. In patients, 19 cognitive measures were related to volume using covariate-adjusted partial correlations under seizure-referenced (ipsilateral/contralateral) and anatomical left/right codings. Nested additive models tested the incremental cognitive contributions of hippocampal and thalamic volume. RESULTS: Relative to controls, patients showed widespread thalamic atrophy (34 of 44 nuclei in the full cohort, 32 of 44 in each subgroup), whereas hippocampal subfield atrophy was largely confined to HS and no cortical region survived correction. All 48 corrected volume-cognition associations involved hippocampal subfields; none involved thalamic nuclei. Additive models showed that ipsilateral hippocampal volume explained 7.4%-14.6% of cognitive variance alone and explained an additional 6.8%-18.9% of cognitive variance beyond thalamic volume, whereas global thalamic volume did not add significant corrected incremental variance. Preserving anatomical hemisphere linked verbal comprehension and naming to left hippocampal subfields and working memory predominantly to right subfields. CONCLUSIONS: Hippocampal and thalamic abnormalities showed distinct structural and cognitive profiles in unilateral MTLE. Thalamic atrophy was widespread, whereas hippocampal volume was more closely related to cognition and showed additional variation by HS status and anatomical hemisphere.
Clinical features and outcomes in children with late-onset febrile seizures.推荐
PMID 43002912 · Brain & development · 2026 11 09
作者：Menderes D, Özbudak P
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
摘要：OBJECTIVE: Late-onset febrile seizures (LFS), occurring at or after five years of age, are uncommon and may represent a heterogeneous clinical group. We aimed to characterize their clinical, EEG, neuroimaging, and short-term outcome features and to compare de novo and persistent LFS subgroups. METHODS: This retrospective, single-center study included 119 children with fever-associated seizures at or after 60 months of age. De novo LFS was defined by no febrile seizure history before five years, whereas persistent LFS was defined by one or more earlier febrile seizures. Clinical characteristics, EEG and brain MRI findings, antiseizure medication (ASM) use, and incident epilepsy after the index event were evaluated. RESULTS: Seventy children (58.9%) presented at 60-83 months, and 93 (78.2%) had seizures lasting <5 min. Forty-four children had de novo LFS and 75 had persistent LFS. EEG was performed in 104 children: 82 (78.8%) were normal, 20 showed background slowing, and 2 showed focal epileptiform discharges. Brain MRI was performed in 66 children; 63 were normal and 3 showed incidental findings, with no potentially epileptogenic lesion identified. During 12-36 months of follow-up, 10 children (8.4%) were diagnosed with incident epilepsy, all in the persistent LFS subgroup (10/75 vs. 0/44); eight of these cases fulfilled the ILAE practical definition of epilepsy. CONCLUSION: Most LFS were brief and had generally favorable short-term outcomes. All incident epilepsy cases occurred in children with febrile seizures before five years of age; however, given the small number of outcomes and overlapping confidence intervals, this observation is hypothesis-generating and does not permit reliable subgroup-specific risk estimation. Larger prospective studies with standardized assessments and longer follow-up are needed.
Automated intracranial spike analysis across sleep-wake states localizes the seizure onset zone in drug-resistant epilepsy.推荐
PMID 43002879 · Clinical neurophysiology : official journal of the International Federation of Clinical Neurophysiology · 2026 10 26
作者：Ricci L, Lachner-Piza D, Groppe D, Scheuer M et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
摘要：OBJECTIVE: Interictal epileptiform discharges (IEDs) are an established biomarker of epilepsy, but their utility for seizure-onset zone (SOZ) localization has been limited by reliance on short recording samples, research-grade detectors, and a primary focus on spike frequency. We assessed multi-day intracranial IED activity across sleep-wake states and its ability to localize the SOZ in adults and children. METHODS: We analyzed simultaneous scalp and intracranial EEG data from 17 patients in the EPILEPSIAE database (predominantly adult) and 10 pediatric patients at the Alberta Children's Hospital (ACH), with 48 to 72 h of data per patient. FDA-cleared clinical software (Persyst P15a) performed automated sleep staging and IED detection. We introduced a novel IED activity metric reflecting IED amplitude and the relative consistency of channel participation across detected events and used logistic regression with leave-one-patient-out cross-validation to predict SOZ channels for each sleep-wake state. RESULTS: IED activity was significantly higher in SOZ than in non-SOZ channels across every sleep-wake state in both cohorts. N2 sleep yielded the highest AUROC for SOZ localization (0.69 EPILEPSIAE; 0.77 ACH), but differences across states were not statistically significant. The presence of clinical seizures during the recording did not significantly affect localization performance. CONCLUSIONS: Automated intracranial IED analysis localizes the SOZ in adults and children, with only modest variation across sleep-wake states. SIGNIFICANCE: Amplitude-aware IED analysis using clinically deployed software is a practical adjunct to presurgical SOZ localization. The modest effect of sleep-wake states suggests that clinical value can be obtained without state-specific analysis. This approach may strengthen presurgical evaluation in adult and pediatric drug-resistant epilepsy.
Patient characteristics and treatment patterns in Dravet syndrome and Lennox-Gastaut syndrome: real-world evidence from physician- and caregiver-reported data in Japan.推荐
PMID 43002840 · Epilepsy & behavior : E&B · 2026 11 09
作者：Yamanouchi H, Shiraishi H, Gillespie A, Grant N et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
摘要：BACKGROUND: Dravet syndrome (DS) and Lennox-Gastaut syndrome (LGS) are rare, severe childhood-onset developmental and epileptic encephalopathies associated with treatment-resistant seizures and significant cognitive, behavioral, and motor impairments, all of which require extensive support from caregivers. OBJECTIVES: To describe real-world patient characteristics and treatment patterns in those with DS or LGS, and assess caregiver burden in Japan. METHODS: Data for patients in Japan were analyzed descriptively from the Adelphi DS and LGS Disease Specific Programme™, a cross-sectional linked survey of physicians, patients, and caregivers (November 2022-August 2023). RESULTS: Thirty-four physicians provided data for 81 patients (DS = 31; LGS = 50). Within the 4 weeks prior to data collection, the most common seizure types reported for patients with DS were generalized tonic-clonic (35%) and tonic (32%), and for patients with LGS were tonic (68%) and atypical absence (22%). In both DS and LGS, cognitive and language impairments were reported to be prevalent, and valproate and carbamazepine were reported to be the most common first-line treatments before and after diagnosis. Current treatment satisfaction among responding physicians was reported to be moderate (somewhat satisfied: DS 56%; LGS 45%). Caregivers (n = 25) reported that managing seizures was the most difficult aspect of care for patients with DS or LGS. Moreover, many reported an impact of caregiving on their own health. CONCLUSIONS: Patients with DS or LGS in Japan are reported to experience substantial seizure and non-seizure burden, and perceived caregiver burden is high. These data underscore the importance of optimized therapies and comprehensive support systems to address both patient and caregiver needs.
Epigenetic age acceleration in the brain and blood of people with epilepsy and in a mouse model of focal cortical dysplasia: a retrospective cross-sectional study.推荐
PMID 43002795 · EBioMedicine · 2026 11 09
作者：Chasani P, Katoch M, Lemke JR, Jamra RA et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
摘要：BACKGROUND: People with epilepsy have reduced life expectancy and more age-related comorbidities, suggesting a link to faster biological ageing. Direct molecular evidence has been scarce. METHODS: We applied DNA-methylation-based epigenetic clocks to brain and blood samples from 324 unique individuals with structural or genetic epilepsy and 65 non-epilepsy controls, and to an in utero electroporation mouse model of somatic mTOR pathway activation (etiocopy of human FCD type 2) sampled before and after seizure onset. Epigenetic age acceleration was defined as the residual from a robust regression on chronological age, sex, cell-type composition, and array type. Relative telomere length was measured in the mouse model to independently validate biological ageing. FINDINGS: In brain, epigenetic age acceleration was consistent across structural lesion types (median +7.3 years, p < 0.0001), most pronounced in GG (+10 years, adj. p < 0.0001), followed by MOGHE (+8.1 years, adj. p < 0.0001), TLE (+6.6 years, adj. p < 0.001), and FCD (+5.6 years, adj. p < 0.001). In blood, age acceleration was not significant after adjustment for cell composition, age, and sex (median +0.7 years, p = 0.20), except in FCD (+2.3 years, adj. p < 0.05). Across structural epilepsies, acceleration was greater in brain than in blood (median +7.3 vs. + 1.4 years, p < 0.0001). Available VPA-exposure data (n = 175) was not significantly associated with age acceleration. In a drug-naïve mTOR mouse model, mutant epileptic brains, but not blood, showed epigenetic age acceleration (adj. p < 0.05). Ageing signatures were already detectable in brains of animals in the pre-seizure latency phase and were independently supported by shortened relative telomere length (adj. p = 0.05), indicating that biological ageing can precede overt epilepsy. INTERPRETATION: Accelerated epigenetic ageing is a consistent feature of the epileptic brain in drug-resistant structural epilepsies and a candidate pathway for disease modification. The contribution of systemic ageing remains unresolved, and generalisability to other aetiologies will need to be further tested. FUNDING: Else Kröner Fresenius Stiftung, German Science Foundation, L'Institut pour la Recherche en Santé Publique, Interdisziplinäres Zentrum für Klinische Forschung, Czech Science Foundation, Ministry of Health of the Czech Republic, Ministry of Education, Youth and Sports of the Czech Republic, Research Foundation-Flanders FWO.
Patient-level EEG seizure classification with AHCNet: Hybrid CNN and handcrafted feature fusion.推荐
PMID 43002782 · Biomolecules & biomedicine · 2026 11 09
作者：Ben Younes S, Chabchoub S, Abed Mohammed M, Ben Salah R
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
摘要：Automated seizure classification from electroencephalography (EEG) necessitates rigorous validation, particularly concerning patient independence and potential dataset biases. This study evaluated hybrid feature fusion and confidence-based deferral for patient-level EEG classification. We assembled a class-balanced cohort of 198 patients from the Temple University Hospital seizure and epilepsy corpora, including individuals with focal non-specific seizures, generalized non-specific seizures, or no epilepsy. Our approach combined a multiscale one-dimensional convolutional neural network with handcrafted spectro-temporal and inter-channel spatial features. We assessed its performance using nested patient-level cross-validation and metadata-only baselines. Stacking and feature-fusion models achieved accuracies of 78.8% (bootstrap 95% confidence interval, 73.2-84.3%) and 78.3% (72.2-83.8%), respectively, showing no statistically significant difference. Notably, segment-level validation inflated accuracy by 20-23 percentage points. Metadata alone yielded 72.2% accuracy, and complete confounding between corpus origin and control-versus-seizure status precluded attributing this distinction solely to EEG content. Among 132 seizure patients from the same corpus, a two-class focal-versus-generalized classification achieved 81.1% accuracy, significantly outperforming metadata alone (at most 59.8%). Spatial features enhanced accuracy by 2.0-8.1 percentage points across models. While confidence weighting did not improve accuracy, deferring the 20% least-confident patients increased accuracy among the retained patients from 78.8% to 84.8%. These findings underscore the critical importance of patient-level validation and thorough confounding assessment when evaluating hybrid EEG classifiers. This framework is still investigational. For clinical application, it requires controls drawn from the same corpus as seizure cases and external validation.
Pearls & Oy-sters: Anti-GABAA Receptor Encephalitis Presenting as Fulminant Hemicerebellitis in a 5-Year-Old Girl.浏览
PMID 43002719 · Neurology · 2026 11 09
作者：Krohn KE, Luke R, Lacy A, Santoro JD et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：病例报告
摘要：A previously healthy 5-year-old girl presented with several days of fever/malaise followed by acute neurologic decline. Head CT demonstrated fulminant right cerebellitis with edema and fourth-ventricle effacement concerning for impending herniation. She underwent emergent posterior fossa decompression. CSF showed mild pleocytosis, and MRI revealed right cerebellar nonenhancing T2/fluid-attenuated inversion recovery hyperintense lesions with evolving supratentorial cortical involvement. Despite empiric antimicrobials and initial immunotherapy (high-dose IV methylprednisolone and tocilizumab followed by IV immunoglobulin/plasma exchange), she developed progressive neuroinflammation with new seizures and markedly elevated CSF IL-6, prompting escalation to additional IL-6-directed therapy with tocilizumab and rescue intrathecal dexamethasone. She subsequently stabilized with radiographic improvement and meaningful neurologic recovery. Expanded antibody testing later confirmed anti-GABAA receptor (anti-GABAAR) encephalitis. This case highlights fulminant cerebellitis as a life-threatening presentation of anti-GABAAR encephalitis and underscores the importance of early posterior fossa decompression, parallel diagnostic evaluation (including expanded antibody panels), and coordinated, time-sensitive immunotherapy escalation in select critically ill presentations.
Azetukalner in Adults With Focal Seizures: The X-TOLE2 Phase 3 Randomized Clinical Trial.推荐
PMID 43002431 · JAMA neurology · 2026 11 09
作者：French JA, LaFrance WC, Ryvlin P, Trinka E et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
摘要：IMPORTANCE: Despite more than 30 approved antiseizure medications, many individuals with focal epilepsy continue to experience seizures, indicating a persistent need for novel therapies. OBJECTIVE: To determine efficacy and safety of the novel, potent KV7 channel opener azetukalner for treatment of focal seizures. DESIGN, SETTING, AND PARTICIPANTS: This phase 3, placebo-controlled randomized clinical trial of azetukalner was conducted over a 12-week double-blind period (November 18, 2022, to February 3, 2026) at 138 sites in North America, South America, Europe, and Asia Pacific. Adults with focal epilepsy experiencing 4 or more focal seizures per month despite stable treatment with 1 to 3 antiseizure medications were enrolled. Data were analyzed using a dataset derived from the database lock dated February 26, 2026. INTERVENTIONS: Participants were randomized 1:1:1 to receive placebo or 15 or 25 mg of azetukalner once daily with the evening meal for 12 weeks with no titration period. Participants completing the double-blind period could enter an open-label extension. MAIN OUTCOMES AND MEASURES: The primary efficacy end point was median percentage change from baseline in monthly (28-day) focal seizure frequency during the double-blind period for azetukalner vs placebo. Safety and tolerability were assessed by recording treatment-emergent adverse events, changes in clinical laboratory findings, vital signs, and electrocardiogram. RESULTS: Of 607 adults screened, 374 participants (190 [50.8%] female; mean [SD] age, 40.0 [12.9] years) were enrolled, randomized and treated with placebo (125 participants), 15-mg azetukalner (125 participants), or 25-mg azetukalner (124 participants). Participants had a baseline median (IQR) of 12.75 (7.56 to 32.97) seizures per month and had previously tried and stopped a median (IQR) of 5 (3 to 8) antiseizure medications. Most (336 participants [89.8%]) were receiving 2 to 3 concomitant antiseizure medications. The median (IQR) change from baseline in monthly seizure frequency was -10.4% (-39.1% to 8.3%) for the placebo group, -34.5% (-61.6% to -12.3%) for the 15-mg azetukalner group (P < .001 vs placebo), and -53.2% (-77.2% to -28.8%) for the 25-mg azetukalner group (P < .001 vs placebo). Azetukalner was generally well tolerated, and treatment-emergent adverse events were comparable to those observed in the phase 2b X-TOLE trial. CONCLUSIONS AND RELEVANCE: This randomized clinical trial found that azetukalner demonstrated robust, rapid, and durable reductions in seizure frequency with a favorable benefit-risk profile. Treatment with azetukalner resulted in one of the largest median percentage reductions in seizure frequency vs placebo reported to date in patients with drug-resistant epilepsy, supporting azetukalner as a promising novel antiseizure medication with significant opportunity to improve clinical outcomes. TRIAL REGISTRATION: ClinicalTrials.gov Identifier: NCT05614063.

Neurological and Medical Complications in Brain Tumor Patients. 2026 Update.推荐
PMID 43002329 · Current neurology and neuroscience reports · 2026 11 09
作者：Sarai G, Schiff D
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：综述
摘要：PURPOSE OF REVIEW: Patients with brain tumors have a plethora of potential complications that arise from both the tumor and its therapeutic management. These complicate clinical management and produce morbidity and occasionally mortality. In this review we discuss the most common neurological and medical complications that include tumor-related epilepsy, vasogenic edema, infection, thromboembolism, endocrine abnormalities and cognitive impairment and its management. This review is an update to the 2020 article by Schiff and Alyahya. RECENT FINDINGS: IDH mutant gliomas cause neuronal hyperexcitability via D-2 -hydroxyglutarate production; use of IDH inhibitors may lower the rate of seizures in this patient population. Pneumocystis prophylaxis with antibiotics has been traditionally recommended with radiation and concurrent temozolomide chemotherapy but newer data suggest no difference in hospitalization or survival. Podoplanin produced by tumor cell releases into the circulation, often via extracellular vesicles, activates platelets and may contribute to venous thrombus formation. Direct oral Anticoagulants (DOACs) are the treatment of choice for venous thromboembolism, with high quality of evidence and a strong recommendation in cancer-associated thrombosis. Though brain tumor patients were underrepresented in clinical trials, retrospective studies support its safety in this population. Brain tumor patients are prone to complications arising from both the tumor and the treatment strategies. Management requires a multidisciplinary strategy with supportive medications including antiseizure medications, corticosteroids, anticoagulants for thromboembolism, and neurocognitive rehabilitation. Early recognition and timely intervention can reduce the morbidity associated with these complications leading to better patient outcomes.
Anxiety and depression in adult low-grade glioma patients: risk factors and prognostic value.浏览
PMID 43002297 · Irish journal of medical science · 2026 11 09
作者：Liao C, Zhao K
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
摘要：BACKGROUND: Anxiety and depression are common in patients with glioma, but limited studies focus on these psychological discomforts in patients with adult low-grade gliomas (LGG). AIMS: This study was aimed to assess the prevalence, risk factors as well as prognostic value of anxiety and depression in adult LGG patients. METHODS: A prospective study of 192 patients used mixed methods to measure anxiety and depression before surgery, at 6 months after surgery, and at 12 months after surgery. Analysis was performed using the Hospital Anxiety and Depression Scale (HADS) and Zung self-rating anxiety/depression scale (SAS/SDS) respectively. Progression-free survival (PFS) was calculated by the survival data of patients. RESULTS: Both the prevalences of anxiety and depression before surgery were higher than those at 6 months or 12 months after surgery in adult LGG patients. In addition, we investigated risk factors and prognostic value of anxiety and depression in patients before surgery. We found that female, epilepsy, hydrocephalus, and larger tumor diameter were independent risk factors of HADS and SAS anxiety before surgery; female gender, epilepsy, hydrocephalus were independent risk factors of HADS and SDS depression. According to the log-rank test, HADS anxiety, SAS anxiety, HADS depression and SDS depression were associated with shorter PFS. CONCLUSIONS: Our study indicated that the prevalences of anxiety and depression were higher before surgery than after surgery in adult LGG patients, and the risk factors and prognostic value of anxiety and depression might be helpful to the early prevention and prognosis improvement in adult LGG patients.
Comprehensive Outcomes From Real-World Evidence for Vagus Nerve Stimulation Therapy: Seizure and Nonseizure 36-Month Outcomes in People With Drug-Resistant Epilepsy.推荐

发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
摘要：OBJECTIVES: Drug-resistant epilepsy (DRE) affects approximately one-third of all people with epilepsy and carries a disproportionate comorbidity burden. Many people with DRE are not candidates for, or do not want, resective surgery or disconnection procedures; therefore, vagus nerve stimulation (VNS) can be used for treatment. Despite its long-standing use, there is a notable lack of data regarding the newer generations of VNS Therapy devices. This study provides long-term, real-world data on the outcomes of contemporary responsive VNS Therapy devices. MATERIALS AND METHODS: Comprehensive Outcomes From Real-World Evidence for VNS Therapy (CORE-VNS; NCT03529045) is a large, international, observational, prospective study designed to systematically and comprehensively evaluate the clinical and safety outcomes of people with DRE who receive a contemporary VNS system as part of their treatment plan. CONCLUSIONS: Results of the CORE-VNS study indicate that contemporary VNS Therapy was associated with an early and sustained benefit on seizures in participants with DRE. This reinforces adjunctive VNS Therapy as an important therapeutic option for those who cannot undergo resective surgery or disconnection procedures.

Clinical features of the postictal state in adults with epilepsy: A cross-sectional survey.推荐
PMID 43002093 · Epilepsia · 2026 11 09
作者：Subota A, Khan S, Lukmanji S, Lee JYY et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
摘要：OBJECTIVE: Persons with epilepsy may experience many postictal symptoms after a seizure, which can significantly impact quality of life. We aimed to determine the frequency, duration, and severity of postictal symptoms to better characterize the postictal period. METHODS: Our cross-sectional questionnaire was adapted from a prior pediatric postictal survey. Face and content validity were evaluated by epilepsy experts and patients from the Calgary Epilepsy Program (CEP). The survey was pilot tested and modified, consisting of 14 physical, cognitive, and psychiatric postictal signs and symptoms. Consecutive CEP adults who had agreed to participate in research and experienced a seizure in the prior 2 years were approached. CEP clinical data were used to supplement survey data. Frequency of factors alleviating and exacerbating postictal symptoms were examined. Spearman's rho was used to explore associations between survey and CEP registry data with statistical significance at ≤ 0.05. RESULTS: Of 351 patients approached, 313 agreed to participate (89%). Fourteen were lost after consent was obtained, and 15 were excluded (incomplete surveys, missing registry data, not having epilepsy). Of the 298 final participants, 54% were female, mean age was 40 years (SD = 15.6), and 95% (282/298) experienced at least one postictal symptom. The three most frequent postictal symptoms were fatigue, sleepiness, or tiredness (84%), confusion or difficulty thinking clearly (75%), and memory problems (69%). For eight of 14 captured postictal symptoms, the most frequently reported symptom duration was <5 min. Mean postictal symptom burden score was correlated with patient reported quality of life and epilepsy-related disability measures. Sleep or rest, medication, and food or drinks were the most common alleviating factors. Lack of rest or sleep, and stress most often exacerbated postictal symptoms. SIGNIFICANCE: This work contributes toward a better understanding of the postictal period and informs the development of future interventions to minimize the impact of the postictal period in epilepsy.
Clinical Spectrum of epilepsy in children at a tertiary pediatric neurology center: Unaddressed methodological limitations.推荐

The Clinical Profile and Bedside Clues of Anti-LGI1 Encephalitis Presenting With Isolated Cognitive Impairment: A Multicenter Retrospective Cohort Study.推荐
PMID 43001616 · CNS neuroscience & therapeutics · 2026 Nov
作者：Jia Y, Wang H, Zhang J, Wang Q et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
摘要：AIMS: A significant subset of anti-LGI1 encephalitis manifests initially with isolated cognitive impairment. Characterizing the clinical profile and bedside clues is critical for timely diagnosis and treatment. METHODS: This multicenter retrospective cohort study enrolled 43 patients with isolated cognitive impairment during the first 2 weeks of illness. Patients were classified according to their entire disease course into sustained isolated impairment versus evolving neuropsychiatric syndrome. Domain-specific cognitive deficits (assessed using the Montreal Cognitive Assessment [MoCA]), paraclinical findings, and treatment response were systematically evaluated. RESULTS: Median age was 58 years and 67.4% were male. The MoCA revealed delayed recall impairment in 93.0%, with relatively preserved naming. Additional neuropsychiatric symptoms developed in 69.8% of patients, with median latencies as follows: psychiatric symptoms (21 days), FBDS (30 days), and focal seizures (47.5 days). The remaining 30.2% suffered from a sustained isolated cognitive impairment. The sustained-isolation subgroup was younger, had fewer naming deficits, and a similar immunotherapy response. EEG abnormalities (53.9%) and mesial temporal MRI hyperintensities (61.5%) were common in this subgroup. CONCLUSION: Isolated cognitive impairment frequently evolves into a broader neuropsychiatric syndrome, but a subset remains isolated. Delayed recall impairment with relative naming preservation, together with EEG and MRI findings, constitutes a practical bedside triad prompting early antibody screening and timely immunotherapy.
Continuous ketone monitoring in inborn errors of metabolism.浏览
PMID 43001310 · Metabolism open · 2026 10 28
作者：Sikonja J, Janez A, Gregoric N, Battelino T et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：综述
摘要：Disruptions in ketone metabolism can markedly impair energy homeostasis and clinical outcomes in individuals with certain inborn errors of metabolism (IEMs), making ketone monitoring an important component of disease management. Evidence for ketone monitoring has largely emerged from diabetes care, where it reduces the risk of diabetic ketoacidosis (DKA), hospitalizations, and healthcare costs. However, conventional ketone self-monitoring methods are limited by impracticality and lack of longitudinal data. Continuous ketone monitoring (CKM), which measures subcutaneous beta-hydroxybutyrate (BHB), overcomes these limitations by enabling real-time assessment of ketone dynamics. In this narrative review, we discuss emerging CKM technologies for the first time and outline their potential clinical utility in IEMs. However, direct clinical evidence supporting the use of CKM in IEMs is currently lacking, and further studies are needed to validate these proposed applications. In theory, CKM could improve metabolic control, seizure management, and prevent complications in disorders of ketolysis and in individuals with IEMs on ketogenic dietary therapies; help in early detection of catabolic states and metabolic decompensation; and enhance surveillance for DKA in IEM-associated insulin-deficient diabetes. Given the predictable metabolic vulnerability of patients with IEMs and the need for timely intervention, these disorders may represent a clinically meaningful early application of CKM beyond diabetes.
Twenty-five-year imaging follow-up of an untreated giant Spetzler-Martin grade V Brain arteriovenous malformation associated with cerebral infarction and epilepsy.推荐
PMID 43001301 · Radiology case reports · 2026 10 31
作者：Jiang F, Zhang M, Yang C
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：病例报告
摘要：Brain arteriovenous malformations (BAVMs) are typically associated with intracranial hemorrhage, whereas ischemic stroke associated with a high-grade BAVM is an exceptionally rare complication. We present the 25-year long-term imaging follow-up of a 39-year-old male with a giant Spetzler-Martin grade V BAVM. Initially diagnosed at age 14 via magnetic resonance imaging (MRI) and magnetic resonance angiography (MRA) due to a visual deficit, the complex lesion was managed conservatively owing to prohibitive procedural risks. Twenty-five years later, the patient presented with acute left limb weakness and recurrent adult-onset epilepsy. Current diffusion-weighted imaging (DWI) and apparent diffusion coefficient (ADC) mapping demonstrated chronic ischemic changes in the bilateral frontal lobes and a new acute-to-subacute infarction in the left parietal lobe. Concurrent MRA and MRI demonstrated a massive 13.5 × 6.3 cm nidus with prominent vascular flow voids and deep venous involvement. The patient received supportive medical management and antiseizure therapy. Motor function showed near-complete recovery at 3 months, although recurrent minor seizures persisted during longer-term follow-up. This 25-year imaging follow-up documents the long-term imaging follow-up of a giant, untreated Spetzler-Martin grade V BAVM and documents the occurrence of delayed ischemic complications during long-term follow-up of a giant BAVM. In extreme-risk, unruptured cases, conservative management with imaging surveillance is a carefully considered option, though it carries ongoing long-term risks.
Sustained Seizure Freedom and Associated Factors Among Children and Adolescents Attending a Pediatric Neurology Clinic in Uganda: A Retrospective Cross-Sectional Study.推荐
PMID 43001210 · Pediatric health, medicine and therapeutics · 2026 11 04
作者：Abraham Nyok A, Augustine Okejomoe O, Namisi PC, Sebunya R
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
摘要：PURPOSE: Sustained seizure freedom is a central aim of childhood epilepsy care, yet long-term outcome data from specialist pediatric services in Uganda remain limited. We estimated the prevalence of documented sustained seizure freedom and examined associated factors among children and adolescents attending a specialist pediatric neurology clinic in Uganda. PATIENTS AND METHODS: We reviewed records of children and adolescents attending the Pediatric Neurology Clinic at St. Francis Hospital Nsambya. The outcome was documented freedom from all epileptic seizures for at least 24 consecutive months. Under the investigator-confirmed recording rule, blank outcome fields were classified as not achieving sustained seizure freedom. Exploratory modified Poisson models with robust standard errors examined associations among records with usable covariates. RESULTS: Of 520 records screened, 496 were included; 385 met the documented endpoint (77.6%; 95% CI 73.7-81.1). Structured medicine selections were available for 395 records. Levetiracetam, sodium valproate, carbamazepine, and lamotrigine were selected in both monotherapy and polytherapy records; clobazam and diazepam were additionally selected in polytherapy in these fields. The available-case adjusted analysis included 389 records, of which 379 met the endpoint. Onset at 1-4 years versus <1 year had an adjusted prevalence ratio (aPR) of 1.08 (95% CI 1.00-1.17); polytherapy versus monotherapy had an aPR of 0.93 (95% CI 0.86-1.00). Point estimates were similar across age-variable specifications, but confidence intervals were close to or crossed the null. CONCLUSION: More than three-quarters of reviewed records met the documented 24-month seizure-free endpoint. Estimated associations with onset age and polytherapy were small, with uncertainty close to the null across model specifications. They should be interpreted cautiously because the adjusted subset differed from the full cohort and contained few records not meeting the endpoint.
Avermectin poisoning with panic-like symptoms: a case series.推荐
PMID 43001206 · Frontiers in toxicology · 2026 10 26
作者：Lv S, Zhang Z, Yu G, Kan B et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：病例报告
摘要：BACKGROUND: High doses of avermectin, a macrolide derived from Streptomyces avermitilis, can cause coma, delirium, respiratory failure, hypotension, and acidosis. However, certain adverse toxicological effects and psychological implications remain unrecognized in patients undergoing parasuicide. CASE PRESENTATION: Case 1: A 54-year-old man ingested approximately 500 mL avermectin benzylate. He was found to be somnolent 45 min later. Subsequently, the patient became unconscious with respiratory failure, hypotension, and acidosis (lactate, 16.2 mmol/L). Gastric lavage, mechanical ventilation, hemoperfusion, hemofiltration, and symptomatic treatments were administered. Three days after ingestion, the patient's primary symptoms were delirium and myoclonus, with a blood avermectin level of 110.06 μg/L. Thereafter, intermittent shaking, limb flexion, and muscle rigidity persisted. Thirteen days after ingestion, brain magnetic resonance imaging was unremarkable. Electroencephalography revealed no epileptiform discharges. After psychiatric evaluation, the patient was assessed as having panic-like symptoms, and his symptoms improved significantly with specialized treatment. Case 2: A 33-year-old man was injected with approximately 5 mL of 1% avermectin in his left forearm. Two days later, he reported significant pain and swelling along with intermittent palpitations, dizziness, and limb tremors consistent with panic-like symptoms. His blood avermectin level was 0.02 μg/mL. After 7 days of treatment, the swelling subsided, and the panic-like symptoms resolved. CONCLUSION: Avermectin poisoning causes significant toxic effects, including respiratory failure, hypotension, acidosis, coma, and delirium. Intramuscular injections may result in localized swelling. Parasuicide patients may also present with panic-like symptoms requiring careful differential diagnosis.
