BiXCA-DualFormer: A time-frequency fusion model for epileptic seizure prediction.推荐
PMID 43000882 · Proceedings of the Institution of Mechanical Engineers. Part H, Journal of engineering in medicine · 2026 11 08
作者：Wang S, Zhang J, Chen W, Xiang F et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
摘要：Epileptic seizure prediction based on electroencephalography (EEG) is important for developing early-warning technologies and improving patient safety. However, reliable prediction remains challenging because preictal EEG changes are subtle and non-stationary, with discriminative information distributed across both temporal and spectral domains. The purpose of this study is to develop a seizure prediction framework that can jointly model complementary temporal and spectral EEG information and capture their interactions. To this end, we propose BiXCA-DualFormer, a dual-branch time-frequency fusion model. Raw EEG signals and short-time Fourier transform representations are processed through separate temporal and spectral branches to extract complementary waveform and frequency-domain features. The main methodological contribution of the proposed model is the introduction of bidirectional cross-attention to explicitly exchange information between the two branches, followed by a Transformer encoder and learnable query pooling for global dependency modeling and feature aggregation. Experiments on the CHB-MIT dataset showed that the proposed model achieved 95.29% accuracy, 96.40% sensitivity, 95.05% specificity, and a false positive rate of 0.0495. Ablation analysis indicated that time-frequency interaction and global contextual modeling improved sensitivity and discriminative performance. These results suggest that BiXCA-DualFormer provides an effective end-to-end biomedical signal processing framework for EEG-based seizure prediction.
The lived experiences of caregivers of people with epilepsy in Botswana.推荐
PMID 43000760 · African journal of primary health care & family medicine · 2026 09 04
作者：Toe JM, Mamalelala TT, Ntsayagae EI
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
摘要：BACKGROUND: Epilepsy is a common neurological disorder affecting about 50 million people worldwide. Caregivers of people with epilepsy face significant emotional, physical and psychological distress due to the demands of caregiving. Limited resources, stigma and the lack of a proper support system often worsen these challenges. AIM: This study aimed to explore the lived experiences of caregivers of individuals with epilepsy in Botswana. SETTING: This study was conducted in Gaborone, the capital city of Botswana, located in Botswana's South-East District. METHODS: An interpretive phenomenological approach was used to capture the lived experiences of 13 caregivers, recruited through purposive sampling. In-depth interviews were conducted to gather qualitative data, which were analysed using the General Inductive Approach. RESULTS: Caregivers reported significant emotional distress, including anxiety, stress and social isolation due to the unpredictable nature of seizures. Financial difficulties were dominant concern, with many caregivers losing employment status due to caregiving responsibility. High cost of medication and access to healthcare services was another burden while stigma surrounding epilepsy further exacerbated emotional strain. Additionally, caregivers reported limited coping strategies, which further contributed to their psychological distress. CONCLUSION: The study highlights the urgent need for culturally sensitive interventions to address the psychological and economic burden faced by caregivers. Strengthening support system, ensuring reliable access to medication and providing financial aid could significantly improve caregivers' well-being and by extension, the quality of life for individuals living with epilepsy in Botswana.Contribution: This study offers important insights into caregiver experiences in epilepsy, emphasising the need for family inclusive, caregiver centred nursing practices, educational enhancement and improved community policies in Botswana. It highlights the importance of structured psychosocial support, integrating epilepsy care into home services, and culturally relevant coping strategies. These findings aim to guide targeted interventions and create more responsive health systems in similar resource-limited settings.
Anesthetic management for coarctation of aorta repair in a very low birth weight preterm infant: a case report.推荐
PMID 43000331 · BMC pediatrics · 2026 08 11
作者：Sun Z, Ying D, Ye S, Ye Q et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：病例报告
摘要：BACKGROUND: Anesthetic management for cardiac surgery in very low birth weight (VLBW) preterm infants is highly challenging due to organ immaturity and metabolic vulnerability. Coarctation of the aorta (CoA) requiring early intervention adds further complexity. Reports on anesthesia for CoA repair in VLBW infants remain limited. CASE PRESENTATION: A male infant born at 32⁺² weeks gestation with a VLBW of 1360 g underwent CoA repair at 39 days of age (weight 2600 g). Anesthesia was induced with etomidate (0.3 mg/kg) and maintained with sufentanil (2.5 µg/kg/h), propofol (6 mg/kg/h), and low-dose sevoflurane (0.5%). Mechanical ventilation was initiated with a tidal volume of approximately 9.6 mL/kg. Dopamine (5 µg/kg/min) was infused for hemodynamic support. INTERVENTION: Intraoperative blood glucose was monitored at induction (4.6 mmol/L), 1 h after incision (9.8 mmol/L), and at the end of surgery (significant transient intraoperative hyperglycemia of 15.6 mmol/L). Upon detection of hyperglycemia, which was likely multifactorial and potentially related to exogenous glucose administration, surgical stress, and dopamine infusion, although definitive causality cannot be established due to the absence of endocrine or metabolic assessments, glucose-containing infusions were immediately reduced. Blood glucose normalized spontaneously within 12 h without insulin therapy. OUTCOME: The infant remained hemodynamically stable with no seizures, hyperosmolality, or neurological complications. He was extubated on postoperative day 3, discharged on day 26, and showed good catch-up growth at one-month follow-up. CONCLUSION: This case highlights the importance of individualized anesthetic regimens and routine, serial perioperative glucose monitoring in VLBW infants undergoing cardiac surgery. Even modest glucose infusion rates may lead to significant hyperglycemia under surgical stress, emphasizing the need for proactive glucose stewardship.
Individualized hybrid electroconvulsive therapy (i-HECT) shows rapid anti-depressant effect in young patients with depression.推荐
PMID 43000283 · BMC psychiatry · 2026 08 11
作者：Zeng L, Zhang JY, Li J, Chen MM et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
摘要：BACKGROUND: For young patients with depression, electroconvulsive therapy (ECT) is highly effective but causes acute cognitive side effects. We designed a new individualized hybrid electroconvulsive therapy (i-HECT) combines ECT with low-charge electrotherapy (LCE) and individual symptom monitoring to reduce cognitive impairments. METHODS: i-HECT comprised two treatments: ECT and LCE. ECT utilized an energy set of 1.5 times the seizure threshold (ST), while LCE was set at 0.5 ST. The initial session employed standard bilateral ECT. Subsequent sessions involved ECT or LCE, depending on whether meeting the ECT-LCE transition criteria (Montgomery-Åsberg Depression Rating Scale (MADRS) total score < 22 or ≥ 50% reduction), assessed after each session. This trial employed a Simon's optimal two-stage design with a 3-month follow-up period in 46 young patients (age 16-25) with depression (MADRS ≥ 22). RESULTS: The intention-to-treat analysis revealed an 80.4% response rate and a 58.7% remission rate, The reduction in MADRS score was 19.1 ± 7.0, with Hedges' g = 3.29 (95% CI = 2.66 to 3.91). The orientation recovery tests (ORTs) took 22.7 ± 11.3 min after LCEs and 26.0 ± 10.3 min after ECTs. Improvements in cognitive functions were observed after i-HECT treatments and during the 3-month follow-up periods. CONCLUSION: In conclusion, our i-HECT protocol shows promise as a simple and rapid treatment option for young patients with depression, with observed improvements in cognitive function. TRIAL REGISTRATION: This trial was registered in the Chinese Clinical Trial Registry (http://www.chictr.org.cn), registration number: ChiCTR2100045682. Date of registration on April 23, 2021.
[The novelties of adult advanced life support and post-resuscitation therapy according to the 2025 European Resuscitation Council guidelines].浏览
PMID 42999961 · Orvosi hetilap · 2026 11 08
作者：Válint A, Bárszki L, Bukor B, Pávó M et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：综述
摘要：The European Resuscitation Council published its updated Guidelines for Resuscitation in 2025. The revised guidelines highlight advanced life support and post-resuscitation care, as well as post-resuscitation rehabilitation, as the third and fourth links in the updated Chain of Survival. In this review, we summarize the key updates and principal messages of the new guideline on advanced life support and post-resuscitation care. Although the Advanced Life Support guidelines do not represent a paradigm shift, they place a stronger emphasis on delivering high-quality, timely resuscitation. High-quality chest compressions remain the cornerstone of resuscitation, complemented by early, safe, and correctly performed defibrillation with minimal interruptions, appropriate drug administration, and prompt identification and treatment of the underlying cause of cardiac arrest. The updated guidelines also address emerging topics, including cardiopulmonary resuscitation-induced consciousness, the specific considerations for cardiac arrest occurring in closely monitored patients, and the introduction of physiology-guided resuscitation. Similarly, the post-resuscitation care guidelines introduce no major changes compared with previous recommendations. Instead, they reinforce the importance of delivering high-quality post-resuscitation care, with particular emphasis on neuroprotection. The key components of the stabilization, diagnostic, and optimization phases include an ABCDE approach to patient assessment, optimization of oxygenation, ventilation, and hemodynamic status, seizure management, prevention of fever during the first 72 hours after return of spontaneous circulation, and identification and treatment of the underlying cause of cardiac arrest. The indications for immediate coronary angiography following successful cardiopulmonary resuscitation have been further refined, and the guidelines also clarify the diagnostic approach when a non-cardiac cause of cardiac arrest is suspected. Greater emphasis is placed on multimodal neuroprognosis and post-resuscitation rehabilitation. Orv Hetil. 2026; 167(45): 1810-1824.
Pregnancy-Induced Hypertension Complicated by Posterior Reversible Encephalopathy Syndrome: A Case Series of Two Patients.推荐
PMID 42999784 · Clinical case reports · 2026 11 06
作者：Zhao X, Yan M, Long F, Mumdzjans A et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
摘要：Posterior reversible encephalopathy syndrome (PRES) is a rare but potentially life-threatening neurological complication associated with hypertensive disorders of pregnancy. This report presents two cases of pregnancy-induced hypertension complicated by PRES, both clinically characterized by headache and visual disturbances, with characteristic neuroimaging findings. The clinical manifestations, laboratory findings, and magnetic resonance imaging (MRI) characteristics of two patients are described. Following prompt multidisciplinary consultation and immediate interventions, including strict blood pressure control, seizure prophylaxis, infection prevention, and appropriate obstetric management, both patients achieved favorable clinical outcomes. No significant neurological abnormalities were observed at the 42-day postpartum follow-up. In patients with severe pregnancy-induced hypertension, PRES warrants recognition as a potential complication; management should prioritize rigorous blood pressure control, seizure prophylaxis, alleviation of pregnancy-related physiological stress, and timely neuroimaging to guide treatment, with strategies balancing maternal and fetal safety-early recognition and intervention underpinned by standardized protocols and multidisciplinary collaboration being critical to improving prognosis and reducing complications.

A Human iPSC-Derived Neuron and Glioma Culture Model Reveals Increased Neuronal Excitability and Loss of GABAergic Inhibition.浏览
PMID 42999486 · Research square · 2026 10 12
作者：Haile H, Pereira B, Niu J, Conboy K et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
摘要：Gliomas engage in bidirectional interactions with neural circuits, driving hyperexcitability, disruption of excitatory-inhibitory balance, and tumor-associated seizures. Human in vitro models that enable functional interrogation of tumor-induced changes in glutamatergic-GABAergic neuronal crosstalk remain limited. We established a defined human induced pluripotent stem cell (iPSC)-derived culture comprising of glutamatergic neurons, GABAergic neurons, and astrocytes with and without glioma cells. Network maturation and function were characterized using immunocytochemistry, multi-electrode array recordings, and whole-cell patch-clamp electrophysiology. Neuronal cultures developed mature synaptic architecture, spontaneous coordinated network activity, and suppression of neuronal firing following treatment with the GABA-A agonist muscimol, demonstrating functional GABAergic signaling. Glioma incorporation increased spike rate, increased coordinated network activity, and reversed the response to muscimol from suppression to elevation of neuronal firing, indicating disrupted GABAergic signaling. Whole-cell patch-clamp recordings demonstrated increased intrinsic excitability of glutamatergic neurons following glioma co-culture, characterized by increased action potential firing frequency and increased action potential amplitude. This human iPSC-derived glutamatergic-GABAergic neuron model recapitulates key features of tumor-induced neuronal dysfunction while enabling complementary network- and single-cell analyses. The model provides a relevant, scalable, and accessible system for mechanistic investigation of bidirectional neuron-glioma interactions and preclinical evaluation of therapies targeting tumor-induced neuronal dysfunction.
Intraoperative Very High-Frequency Activity for Localization of the Epileptogenic Zone: An International Multicenter Study.推荐
PMID 42999370 · medRxiv : the preprint server for health sciences · 2026 10 15
作者：Uda H, Kuroda N, Usui N, Ogawa H et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
摘要：BACKGROUND AND OBJECTIVES: Interictal spikes on intraoperative electrocorticography have been used to localize the epileptogenic zone but have limited specificity. We hypothesized that identifying spatially distributed, spike-associated very high-frequency activity (vHFA; 500-2,000 Hz) would improve the ability of spikes to localize the epileptogenic zone. We aimed to characterize spike-associated vHFA and to determine whether a seizure outcome prediction model incorporating vHFA could be externally validated across independent centers. METHODS: This retrospective two-center cohort study included patients with drug-resistant focal epilepsy who underwent electrocorticography and subsequent resection between January 2016 and May 2025. Spikes occurring across contacts within 50 ms were classified as co-occurring; all others were classified as isolated. We determined whether vHFA was associated with co-occurring spikes. We then assessed whether contacts enriched in vHFA-positive co-occurring spikes were more likely to be located within the resected region among seizure-free patients. Finally, we determined whether the rate of such spikes remaining outside the resection predicted seizure outcomes across the two centers. RESULTS: Among 56 patients (mean age: 21.2 years; 26 females), we identified 132,359 spikes, of which 1,925 were vHFA-positive. The vHFA peak preceded the associated spike peak by 8.0 ms (95%CI: 5.0-16.0). vHFA-positive spikes were more likely to be classified as co-occurring, even after adjustment for anatomy and inter-electrode distance (OR: 3.43; 95%CI: 2.82-4.17). Among the 35 seizure-free patients, contacts with higher rates of vHFA-positive co-occurring spikes were more likely to be resected (OR: 11.92 per occurrence/min; 95%CI: 5.62-25.32). This association was stronger than that observed for all spikes (OR: 1.09 per occurrence/min; 95%CI: 1.06-1.11). Twenty patients experienced postoperative seizure recurrence; a higher proportion of vHFA-positive co-occurring spikes in preserved areas was associated with recurrence (OR: 3.22 per 0.01 increase in proportion; 95%CI: 1.14-9.12). In bidirectional cross-center validation, models incorporating vHFA-positive co-occurring spikes yielded areas under the receiver operating characteristic curves of 0.80 and 0.69, with better performance when the model was developed using the larger sample. DISCUSSION: Spike-associated vHFA may identify a biologically meaningful subset of interictal spikes involved in propagation across the epileptogenic zone. vHFA-positive co-occurring spikes may represent a generalizable intraoperative biomarker of the epileptogenic zone.
The role of the NLRP3 inflammasome in a CLN2 mouse model of neuroinflammation.推荐
PMID 42999310 · bioRxiv : the preprint server for biology · 2026 10 14
作者：Sánchez KE, Elizalde S, Schwartz NB, Domowicz MS
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
摘要：Neuronal ceroid lipofuscinoses, or Batten disease, comprises a group of inherited pediatric neurodegenerative disorders, and is characterized by the lysosomal accumulation of the fluorescent storage material lipofuscin. The human classical late-infantile form of the disorder (CLN2) is caused by mutations in the CLN2 / Tripeptidyl peptidase 1 ( TPP1 ) gene. Comparable to human CLN2, a knockout of the Tpp1 gene ( Tpp1 -/- ) in the mouse leads to progressive vision loss, ataxia, motor atrophy, seizures, and premature death. While we previously showed using bulk RNA-seq analysis that reactive gliosis develops between 3 and 4 months in this animal model, the role of the inflammasome in CLN2 remains unexplored. The inflammasome is a multiprotein complex formed through pattern recognition receptor engagement, which culminate in the cleavage and release of interleukin-1 beta. Here we report that while some inflammasome-associated genes are upregulated as early as 1-month in the Tpp1 -/- cerebellum, it is not until 3-months-old that all gene transcripts necessary for inflammasome assembly and function are upregulated, leading to IL1β release. In Tpp1 -/- slice cultures, we demonstrated that MCC950, an inhibitor of inflammasome assembly, downregulates transcriptional expression of genes associated with reactive microglia and inflammasome function. Furthermore, prophylactic administration of MCC950 in drinking water of Tpp1 -/- animals for two months is sufficient to lower the expression of these same genes in the forebrain and cerebellum, suggesting that inflammasome inhibitors are promising therapeutics for CLN2 and neurodegeneration broadly.
Somnus, a tunable, open-source platform for automated EEG sleep scoring in physiological and disease mouse models.推荐
PMID 42999260 · bioRxiv : the preprint server for biology · 2026 10 12
作者：Brenner J, Caudill MS, Zoghbi HY
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
摘要：Many studies using mouse models of human brain disorders rely on extended recordings of brain electrical activity through electroencephalogram, or EEG. The features that researchers measure in these recordings, such as seizures and epileptiform activity, are deeply influenced by whether the animal is awake, in non-rapid eye movement (NREM) sleep, or in rapid eye movement (REM) sleep. Because total sleep duration varies naturally across animals, rigorous experimental comparisons require classifying the sleep state of every few seconds of a recording. Manual annotation is highly labor-intensive, often requiring hours per subject. While automated scoring tools accelerate this process, they are typically trained exclusively on healthy cohorts and frequently fail when applied to disease models that exhibit atypical or fragmented sleep architecture. Somnus is an open-source Python package designed to automate rodent sleep staging using EEG, muscle activity (EMG), and optional video-tracking data. It includes a robust base model trained on diverse recordings from six independent laboratories, enabling out-of-the-box classification across various equipment setups without requiring prior manual labeling. A central feature of Somnus is its capacity for rapid adaptation to pathological sleep structure. The software provides a graphical interface that directs researchers to the specific epochs where the base model is least certain. Users can manually correct these targeted epochs in a built-in viewer by comparing the model's classification to the raw EEG signal and video, and then use their corrections to fine-tune the model. This workflow empowers researchers to generate accurate custom scorers for complex disease models or novel treatments with just a few minutes of manual correction, bypassing the need to hand-score entire experimental cohorts or develop their own models from scratch.
GAT1508, a selective neuronal GIRK1/2 activator, exhibits superior antiepileptic efficacy and synergizes with cannabidiol.推荐
PMID 42999102 · bioRxiv : the preprint server for biology · 2026 10 16
作者：Simhadri J, Zorn A, Wang L, Kawano T et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
摘要：G protein-gated inwardly rectifying potassium (GIRK; Kir3) channels are key regulators of neuronal excitability and represent promising therapeutic targets for epilepsy. Here, we characterize GAT1508, a urea-based small-molecule activator selectively targeting neuronal GIRK1/2 heterotetramers, and compare its efficacy with previously described urea compounds ML297 and GiGA1. In a pentylenetetrazole (PTZ)-induced seizure model, GAT1508 demonstrated robust antiseizure activity, outperforming both ML297 and GiGA1 and achieving efficacy comparable to valproic acid. Electrophysiological studies using two-electrode voltage clamp in Xenopus oocytes revealed that GAT1508, unlike ML297 and GiGA1, selectively activates GIRK1/2 channels without significant activation of cardiac GIRK1/4 channels, suggesting reduced risk of cardiovascular side effects. Structural and computational analyses indicate that enhanced stabilization of GAT1508 compared to ML297 within the GIRK1/2 binding pocket promotes both ion permeation and channel gating through coordinated interactions with key residues. Furthermore, GAT1508, like the two other urea drugs, exhibited synergistic activation of GIRK channels when combined with Gβγ-mediated pathways and significantly potentiated the antiseizure effects of cannabidiol (CBD) in both hippocampal slice recordings and in vivo seizure models. These findings establish GAT1508 as a potent and selective GIRK1/2 activator and support a novel combination therapeutic strategy for epilepsy based on convergent GIRK channel modulation.
Drug consumption pattern of drivers causing traffic accidents with a motor vehicle in Hungary (2014-2023).浏览
PMID 42998650 · Forensic science international · 2026 11 03
作者：Kovács K, Hidvégi E, Rárosi F, Berkecz R et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
摘要：Driving under the influence of drugs (DUID) is a recognized risk factor for traffic accidents, but data on accident-involved drivers in Hungary are scarce. We characterized drug use among 1563 drivers classified by the police as at fault for a traffic accident and tested on suspicion of drug use (DCA) between 2014 and 2023, and compared their toxicological profile with a previously reported cohort of drivers suspected of DUID at roadside checks (sDUID) from the same region and period. Blood and urine samples were screened for illicit drugs, psychoactive medicines and new psychoactive substances by gas and liquid chromatography-mass spectrometry. Temporal trends were assessed using annual proportions of tested drivers and negative binomial regression with the annual number of tested drivers as an offset. Differences in the distribution of substance categories between DCA and sDUID drivers were assessed using Pearson's chi-square test, followed by post-hoc binomial tests with Bonferroni correction. Overall, 1308 drivers (83.7%) tested positive: classical illicit drugs in 48.6%, psychoactive medicines in 44.2%, synthetic cannabinoid receptor agonists (SCRA) in 15.4% and stimulant new psychoactive substances (sNPS) in 9.94%. Benzodiazepines accounted for 84.1% of medicine-positive cases and exceeded the upper therapeutic limit in 55.6% of these. Benzodiazepines and SCRA were more frequent among DCA than sDUID drivers, whereas active tetrahydrocannabinol was more frequent among sDUID drivers. National seizure trends broadly mirrored these prevalence. Because both cohorts were selected by police suspicion, these differences express relative enrichment rather than accident risk. They nevertheless indicate that benzodiazepine-related impairment is less conspicuous at roadside checks and may be systematically under-detected.
Variables associated with epilepsy self-management competency among people with epilepsy from an epilepsy self-management clinical trial.推荐
PMID 42998590 · Clinical neurology and neurosurgery · 2026 10 06
作者：Branning R, Tan L, Yu H, Tyrrell M et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
摘要：INTRODUCTION: Epilepsy is a chronic illness that requires ongoing self-management. Our study aimed to identify clinical and demographic variables associated with self-management competency as measured by the epilepsy self-management scale (ESMS). We hypothesized that female gender, higher level of education, and higher income were independently correlated with greater self-management competency. METHODS: This cross-sectional analysis of baseline data in PWE (N = 160) experiencing negative health events enrolled in the 6-month clinical trial SMART examined categories of self-management including seizure, lifestyle, medication, safety, and information management. The least absolute shrinkage and selection operator (LASSO), regression was used for variable selection. To exclude false positives, the Benjamini-Hochberg procedure was employed, to control the False discovery rate (FDR) q-values < 0.05. RESULTS: Variables correlated with greater total self-management competency were having a seizure within the past 30 days (p = 0.008), greater self-efficacy (p < 0.001), lower comorbidity (p = 0.008), and lower functional status (p < 0.001). Higher self-efficacy was associated with improved self-management competency across each self-management subdomain. CONCLUSION: Our analysis found that self-efficacy was associated with total self-management competency as well as competency in lifestyle, medication, information, safety, and seizure management. Future research may focus on methods to increase self-efficacy.
Early recurrence of febrile seizures in the pediatric emergency department: development and internal validation of FSERS, a practical bedside risk score.推荐
PMID 42998584 · Brain & development · 2026 11 07
作者：Bedir F, Canan A
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
摘要：BACKGROUND: Febrile seizures (FS) are common and generally benign. Early recurrence within 24 h does not itself mandate admission or treatment but may prompt reassessment before discharge, particularly when atypical or evolving features raise concern for an alternative acute neurologic diagnosis. We aimed to develop and internally validate a bedside score for early FS recurrence. METHODS: We included 400 children with confirmed FS presenting to a tertiary pediatric emergency department (PED) between January 2020 and December 2024. Complex-phenotype FS was defined by focal onset or prolonged duration only. Backward stepwise multivariable logistic regression derived a parsimonious model, followed by 1000-iteration bootstrap internal validation. RESULTS: Early recurrence occurred in 45/400 children (11.2%). Independent predictors were neutrophil-to-lymphocyte ratio (NLR; adjusted odds ratio [aOR] 2.345), seizure duration (aOR 1.154), C-reactive protein (CRP; aOR 1.187), serum sodium (aOR 0.606), and fever-to-seizure interval (aOR 0.821). The continuous model achieved an apparent area under the curve (AUC) of 0.924 and an optimism-corrected AUC of 0.897. The derived integer FS Early Recurrence Score (FSERS) had an apparent AUC of 0.905 and an optimism-corrected AUC of 0.887. CONCLUSIONS: FSERS integrates five readily available variables into an internally validated estimate of early FS recurrence risk. It is not an admission rule, treatment trigger, or diagnostic model for central nervous system infection or acute encephalopathy, and should complement clinical reassessment and red-flag evaluation. Prospective multicenter external validation is required before score-based changes in observation or disposition are considered.
Abnormal SEP recovery amongst multimodal electrophysiological studies to indicate cortical dysfunction in PME.推荐
PMID 42998417 · Epilepsy research · 2026 11 06
作者：Aydoğan A, Şirin NG, Elmalı AD, Öge AE et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
摘要：BACKGROUND: Progressive myoclonic epilepsies (PMEs) are rare neurodegenerative disorders characterized by cortical hyperexcitability and progressive neurological deterioration. We aimed to identify electrophysiological markers of cortical dysfunction using multimodal neurophysiological assessment. METHODS: Seventeen adult PME patients underwent clinical evaluation and video-EEG recording. Somatosensory evoked potentials (SEP), SEP recovery function and surround inhibition, blink reflex, and motor evoked potentials (MEP) were assessed in 11 patients and compared with 11 matched healthy controls. RESULTS: Generalized epileptiform discharges were observed in all patients. SEP recordings demonstrated increased N20/P25 amplitudes and prolonged P14, N18, and P25 latencies. SEP recovery showed marked attenuation at the shortest interstimulus interval followed by rapid amplitude recovery. Surround inhibition was preserved. Blink reflex studies revealed prolonged R2 latencies and increased amplitudes. MEP parameters did not differ significantly between groups. CONCLUSIONS: PME is associated with abnormal SEP recovery dynamics and giant SEPs, consistent with impaired somatosensory inhibitory processing. Blink reflex abnormalities suggest additional subcortical involvement, whereas corticospinal excitability appears relatively preserved.
Anesthetic Care of Children With Epilepsy Receiving Ketogenic Diet Therapy: An Educational Review.推荐
PMID 42998075 · Journal of perianesthesia nursing : official journal of the American Society of PeriAnesthesia Nurses · 2026 11 07
作者：Parks LG, Benzon HA, Blackford R, Rosenblatt A
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：综述
摘要：PURPOSE: Ketogenic diet therapy (KDT) is a nonpharmacologic treatment for pediatric drug-resistant epilepsy that induces ketosis through high-fat, low-carbohydrate intake. These children frequently present for anesthesia, and their baseline metabolic alterations, compounded by fasting and surgical stress, place them at higher risk of perioperative complications than other pediatric patients. This educational review organizes anesthetic considerations into preoperative, intraoperative, and postoperative phases and synthesizes them into best-practice recommendations for perianesthesia clinicians. DESIGN: Educational review. METHODS: A research librarian-assisted search of PubMed and Ovid MEDLINE was conducted in June 2025, limited to English-language publications from 2015 forward; of 202 unique records screened, 83 underwent full-text review, and 14 met inclusion criteria, and an unrestricted hand-search of included studies' reference lists added seven more, for 21 included studies (seven case reports, one case series, five retrospective record reviews, two prospective cohorts, two animal studies, three narrative reviews, and one summary of expert opinion). FINDINGS: Across this literature, the central requirement is maintenance of ketosis throughout all phases of perioperative care, with evaluation of every medication and intravenous fluid for carbohydrate and gluconeogenic potential, metabolic monitoring with prompt recognition and treatment of acidosis and hypoglycemia, anesthetic technique tailored to the patient's comorbidities and the planned procedure, and interdisciplinary coordination with the ketogenic diet team. CONCLUSIONS: The evidence base remains dominated by case reports and retrospective record reviews, with no randomized trials in this population, limiting generalizability; nonetheless, reviews and guidelines published since 2020 provide a workable foundation for safe perioperative care.

Roseola infantum and subsequent epilepsy: A nationwide 20-year retrospective cohort study.推荐
PMID 42998009 · Epilepsia · 2026 11 07
作者：Koh S, Pyo SI, Park JH, Kwon SS et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
摘要：OBJECTIVE: This study was undertaken to evaluate whether roseola infantum is associated with an increased risk of subsequent epilepsy. METHODS: We conducted a nationwide retrospective cohort study using the Korean National Health Insurance Service database from January 2002 to December 2021. Children with roseola, identified using International Classification of Diseases, 10th Revision (ICD-10) code B08.2, were matched 1:1 by age and sex with children without roseola. Participants were followed from the date of roseola diagnosis, or the corresponding index date for controls, until incident epilepsy, death, or the end of the study period. The primary epilepsy definition required at least two medical encounters with ICD-10 code G40.x listed among the top five discharge diagnoses and cumulative antiseizure medication prescriptions for 180 days or longer. Cox proportional hazards models were used to estimate hazard ratios (HRs) and 95% confidence intervals (CIs), with adjustment for prespecified baseline and time-varying covariates. Sensitivity analyses used a more inclusive epilepsy definition. RESULTS: The cohort included 1 595 494 children: 797 747 with roseola and 797 747 matched controls. The mean age at cohort entry was 1.15 years, and 51.0% were male. During a mean follow-up of 10.5 years, roseola was associated with an increased risk of subsequent epilepsy (adjusted HR = 2.00, 95% CI = 1.91-2.09). The association was consistent across prespecified subgroups, with adjusted HRs ranging from 1.66 to 3.60. Postindex febrile convulsion showed the strongest independent association with epilepsy (adjusted HR = 12.31, 95% CI = 11.82-12.83). In sensitivity analyses using the inclusive epilepsy definition, roseola remained associated with increased epilepsy risk (adjusted HR = 1.52, 95% CI = 1.48-1.57). SIGNIFICANCE: In this nationwide cohort study, roseola infantum was associated with an increased risk of subsequent epilepsy. These findings demonstrate a population-level association between roseola infantum and epilepsy and warrant prospective studies with virologic confirmation and detailed epilepsy phenotyping.

Seizure quantification using wearable devices.推荐
PMID 42998007 · Epilepsia · 2026 11 07
作者：Jeppesen J, Loddenkemper T, Thijs RD, Rubboli G et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：综述
摘要：Accurate assessment of seizure occurrence is central to treatment optimization, risk stratification, and management of epilepsy, yet remains largely dependent on patient-reported seizure diaries, which are frequently incomplete and unreliable. Wearable technologies have emerged as promising tools to enable objective and continuous seizure monitoring by capturing seizure-induced physiological signals. Over the past decade, substantial progress has been made in the development and clinical validation of wearable seizure detection systems, particularly for generalized convulsive seizures, where high sensitivity can be achieved, whereas detection of nonconvulsive seizures remains more limited. However, translating detection into clinically meaningful seizure quantification presents important challenges, including variability in performance across seizure types, patient populations, and individual physiological responses, as well as differences between controlled and real-world settings. This narrative review critically examines wearable technologies for seizure quantification, integrating recent clinical evidence and expert perspectives. We discuss key sensor modalities, including accelerometry, electrocardiography-derived features such as heart rate variability, multimodal biosignal integration, and emerging ultra-long-term wearable and subcutaneous electroencephalographic systems. Each modality offers specific advantages and limitations depending on seizure type and clinical context. Attention is also given to the importance of user trust, adherence, and data quality as key determinants of clinical utility. We further examine the role of wearable seizure quantification in clinical practice, including treatment monitoring, tele-epileptology, and clinical trials, as well as potential future contributions to sudden unexpected death in epilepsy risk assessment. With continued technological advances and robust real-world clinical validation, wearable systems have the potential to transform seizure monitoring and support more precise, data-driven epilepsy care.
Two-stage spatiotemporal graph neural network framework for seizure onset zone localization.推荐
PMID 42997993 · Epilepsia · 2026 11 07
作者：Le Tallec J, Saber R, Jahani A, Theaud G et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
摘要：OBJECTIVE: Accurate localization of the seizure onset zone (SOZ) is critical for the presurgical evaluation of drug-resistant epilepsy. Intracranial electroencephalography captures high-resolution seizure dynamics, but interpreting complex spatiotemporal patterns across implanted electrodes is clinically challenging. We propose a two-stage, uncertainty-aware framework for SOZ localization that integrates patient-specific seizure modeling with cross-patient probabilistic ranking to estimate the likelihood of each electrode belonging to the SOZ. METHODS: The framework was evaluated on a bi-institutional cohort of 36 patients (20 with good surgical outcome, 16 with poor surgical outcome), comprising 184 seizures. In the first stage, a patient-specific spatiotemporal graph neural network modeled seizure onset as a network-level transition using directed functional connectivity graphs derived from burst-integrated electrophysiological features. Trained solely to detect preictal-to-ictal transitions, this model generated electrode probability trajectories without SOZ supervision. In the second stage, these trajectories were summarized into interpretable descriptors and used to train a patient-agnostic classifier assigning SOZ likelihood scores to electrodes. Evaluation and hyperparameter optimization were performed using nested leave-one-patient-out cross-validation restricted to good outcome patients, ensuring strict patient-level separation between training, validation, and test data. RESULTS: Seizure onset detection was stable across patients, with comparable binary cross-entropy values between outcome groups (median = .495 vs. .438, p = .108). In good outcome patients, SOZ electrodes were prioritized among top-ranked contacts (area under the curve = .86 ± .06), with all patients exhibiting at least one SOZ electrode within the top positions. In poor outcome patients, predictions exhibited reduced spatial separability, lower likelihood magnitudes, and near-zero confidence gaps, reflecting attenuated model confidence. SIGNIFICANCE: This two-stage framework integrates spatial interactions, temporal dynamics, and probabilistic ranking within a pipeline. By providing graded SOZ likelihood estimates and uncertainty-aware prioritization, the approach is a decision-support tool to assist hypothesis refinement during presurgical evaluation rather than as a standalone diagnostic system.

Spatial correspondence between spontaneous brain activity alterations and normative molecular and neurochemical architecture in pediatric epilepsy.推荐
PMID 42997930 · Quantitative imaging in medicine and surgery · 2026 09 30
作者：Yu YH, Zhang XL, Hu ZX, Huang ZD et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
摘要：BACKGROUND: Pediatric epilepsy is increasingly recognized as a disorder of distributed brain networks, but the molecular context of spontaneous brain activity alterations remains incompletely understood. This study investigated regional spontaneous brain activity alterations in pediatric epilepsy and their spatial correspondence with normative molecular reference maps. METHODS: In this observational case-control neuroimaging study, resting-state functional magnetic resonance imaging (rs-fMRI) data from 44 children with epilepsy and 44 healthy controls selected using group-level frequency matching for age and sex were analyzed. Amplitude of low-frequency fluctuation (ALFF) and fractional ALFF (fALFF) were compared between groups. The unthresholded signed ALFF case-control t-map was subsequently parcellated using the Schaefer 2018 400-parcel atlas. The 200 left-hemisphere cortical parcels were spatially related to normative adult Allen Human Brain Atlas gene-expression data using partial least squares (PLS) regression, and the whole-cortex ALFF map was compared with 39 normative adult neurotransmitter maps. RESULTS: First partial least squares component (PLS1) explained 36.42% of the variance in the left-hemisphere cortical ALFF map and was significant under 10,000 spherical spin permutations (P_spin=0.0004); regional PLS1 scores were positively associated with ALFF t-values (ρ=0.5650, P_spin=0.0004). PLS1-associated genes were enriched for synaptic, ion-transport, membrane-regulation, and neuronal-projection annotations, and inhibitory-neuron gene sets showed significant enrichment (P=0.0014). The signed ALFF map showed inverse spatial associations with the MU_CARFENTANIL_c11 μ-opioid receptor map (median Spearman ρ=-0.3317, mean Fisher z=-0.3448, P_spin=0.0003, q_FDR =0.0044) and the mGluR5_abp_hc73_smart map (median Spearman ρ=-0.2610, mean Fisher z=-0.2672, P_spin=0.0001, q_FDR =0.0022). CONCLUSIONS: These findings provide indirect atlas-based spatial molecular context for the pediatric imaging phenotype. Because the molecular maps were derived from independent adult reference datasets, the associations should not be interpreted as patient-specific gene-expression or receptor measurements, causal mechanisms, or developmentally matched molecular abnormalities.
Joubert syndrome with hippocampal malrotation in a 4-month-old male: A case report.推荐
PMID 42997830 · Radiology case reports · 2026 10 30
作者：Rutta CA, Kamuzora MCK
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：病例报告
摘要：Joubert syndrome is a rare autosomal recessive ciliopathy characterized by a distinctive midbrain-hindbrain malformation, with the "molar tooth sign" as its characteristic neuroimaging hallmark, resulting from cerebellar vermian hypoplasia, thickening and horizontalization of the superior cerebellar peduncles, and a deepened interpeduncular fossa. We report a 4-month-old male infant presenting with developmental delay and progressive weight loss. Brain Magnetic Resonance Imaging revealed the characteristic molar tooth sign, vermian hypoplasia, and an associated hippocampal malrotation, a less frequently recognized but increasingly documented supratentorial finding in Joubert syndrome and related disorders. The patient also had retinal degeneration and elevated transaminases, findings that raise concern for multisystem involvement within the Joubert ciliopathy spectrum. Hippocampal malrotation has been described as an associated supratentorial finding in JS, but its clinical significance, including any relationship with epilepsy, remains uncertain. This case emphasizes careful evaluation of both infratentorial and supratentorial structures and systemic assessment in patients with JS.
Effectiveness, safety and therapeutic potential of medical cannabis across treatment-resistant neuropsychiatric disorders: systematic review and meta-analysis.推荐
PMID 42997457 · EClinicalMedicine · 2026 10 29
作者：Guo D, Zong R, Ma Z, Lu F et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
摘要：BACKGROUND: Medical cannabis has been suggested to have a therapeutic role in selected treatment-resistant neuropsychiatric disorders. We analyzed the available evidence to assess the effectiveness and safety of these interventions in treatment-resistant neuropsychiatric disorders. METHODS: For this systematic review and meta-analysis, we searched Web of Science, Cochrane, Embase, EBSCO, and PubMed for studies published between January 1, 1970, and April 25, 2026. We considered studies examining any type or formulation of medical cannabis in treatment-resistant neuropsychiatric disorders. Primary outcomes were disorder-specific clinical improvement measures. Safety outcomes were adverse events, serious adverse events, and withdrawals due to adverse events. Evidence from studies was synthesized as equivalent odds ratios (eORs) for comparative outcomes and as pooled proportions for single-arm safety outcomes, via random-effects meta-analyses. Risk of bias was assessed using RoB 2 for randomized trials, ROBINS-I for non-randomized studies, and structured quality assessment for case series and case reports, and certainty of evidence was assessed with GRADE. This study is registered with PROSPERO, CRD420251148868. FINDINGS: 127 eligible studies were included, of which 32 studies involving 3645 participants contributed to meta-analysis. Meta-analysis showed improvement in drug-resistant epilepsy (eOR 0.50, 95% confidence interval (CI) 0.34-0.72), refractory pain (eOR = 0.48, 95% CI = 0.30-0.78), and refractory multiple sclerosis spasticity (eOR = 0.22, 95% CI = 0.07-0.75). Evidence for psychiatric disorders was scarce. Small randomized trials reported improvement in PTSD-related nightmares and cannabis dependence after failed cessation. Uncontrolled studies reported improvements in hyperarousal, residual obsessive-compulsive symptoms, and refractory Tourette syndrome. Findings for treatment-resistant anxiety were inconsistent, and schizophrenia did not show a reliable therapeutic effect. Controlled analyses showed higher odds of any adverse events in drug-resistant epilepsy (eOR = 2.22, 95% CI = 1.52-3.23) and refractory multiple sclerosis (eOR = 1.89, 95% CI = 1.01-3.52), with higher odds of serious adverse events and withdrawals due to adverse events also observed in drug-resistant epilepsy. In single-arm and real-world studies, pooled proportions of any adverse events ranged from 15.5% in multiple sclerosis to 66.0% in Tourette syndrome, with substantial heterogeneity. INTERPRETATION: Adjunctive pharmaceutical cannabidiol has the most consistent comparative evidence for reducing seizure frequency in selected drug-resistant epilepsy. Evidence for refractory pain and multiple sclerosis is heterogeneous and varies by clinical indication, cannabinoid formulation, and outcome definition. Evidence in treatment-resistant psychiatric disorders remains limited and is insufficient to support routine clinical recommendations. Further high-quality studies directly examining diagnosis-specific and formulation-specific effects, safety, and tolerability are needed. FUNDING: STI2030-Major Projects and the National Natural Science Foundation of China.
Self-Guided Beta-Responsive Visual Neurofeedback in a Public Exhibition: Feasibility and Usability Randomized Controlled rial.推荐

作者：Baumgartner M, Weibel R, Hoesli T, von Wangenheim F et al.

命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
摘要：BACKGROUND: Neurofeedback is studied for stress and anxiety regulation, but most systems require supervision or research-grade equipment. Consumer electroencephalography (EEG) could broaden access, yet autonomous first-contact feasibility, usability, and baseline neural-state effects remain unclear. OBJECTIVE: We assessed autonomous completion and usability, tested whether a fixed breathing pacer improved outcomes over visual neurofeedback alone, and explored whether pretask resting beta was associated with usability. METHODS: We conducted a single-session, 2-arm randomized feasibility and usability trial at a public science exhibition in Bern, Switzerland, between March and June 2025. Eligible adults were aged 18 years or older, could read German or French, and reported no epilepsy or implanted electronic device. A tablet app guided participants without experimenter assistance through consent, setup, a 30-second eyes-closed baseline, 1:1 randomization, a 120-second beta-responsive task, and questionnaires. The final analytic sample included participants assigned to visual neurofeedback only (VN; n=27) or visual neurofeedback plus a 6 breaths/minute breathing pacer (VN+BP; n=25). Outcomes were usability, relaxation, and state mindfulness. Data were collected prospectively; no secondary data were used. Analyses used Mann-Whitney U, chi-square, Spearman, and permutation tests (2-sided, α=.05); EEG outcome and item analyses were exploratory. RESULTS: Of 110 visitors initiating a session, 79 completed the task with a valid EEG recording (71.8%, 95% CI 62.8-79.4) and 52 met analytic criteria (47.3%, 95% CI 38.2%-56.5%). Participants (35 women) had a mean age of 39.0 (SD 11.3) years. Usability was good (System Usability Scale [SUS] mean 72.3, SD 14.7; 95% CI 68.3-76.2), and 31/52 participants scored at least 68 (59.6%, 95% CI 46.1%-71.8%). Usability did not differ between VN and VN+BP (median 75.0, IQR 62.5-81.3 vs median 72.5, IQR 65.0-90.0; Mann-Whitney U=362.5, P=.65; rank-biserial r=-0.07, 95% CI -0.39 to 0.25). Relaxation (median 63.0, IQR 50.0-80.0 vs median 61.0, IQR 50.0-67.0; Mann-Whitney U=276.5, P=.27; r=0.18, 95% CI -0.14 to 0.49) and mindfulness (median 2.4, IQR 2.1-2.8 vs median 2.5, IQR 2.0-3.2; Mann-Whitney U=346.5, P=.68; r=-0.07, 95% CI -0.39 to 0.26) did not differ. Higher resting beta was associated with lower SUS before correction (Spearman ρ=-0.31, 95% CI -0.54 to -0.04; raw P=.03), but not after Benjamini-Hochberg correction (adjusted P=.30). SUS item analyses showed a global pattern (permutation P=.02), strongest for functional integration (ρ=-0.43, 95% CI -0.63 to -0.18; Holm-adjusted P=.01). CONCLUSIONS: This trial evaluated an autonomous consumer-EEG neurofeedback system integrating self-fitting, signal-quality checking, baseline calibration, adaptive feedback, randomization, and self-report without operator support. Many first-time users completed the autonomous workflow with acceptable-to-good usability; a fixed, open-loop breathing pacer did not improve usability, relaxation, or state mindfulness; and higher resting beta showed a preliminary association with lower perceived usability. These findings support autonomous consumer-EEG neurofeedback and motivate work on adaptive onboarding, validated EEG pipelines, and objective self-regulation outcomes.
