Attitudes toward treating functional neurological disorders: psychological service provider perspectives.浏览
PMID 42997130 · Frontiers in psychiatry · 2026 10 23
作者：Garrett R, Hoti L, Roberts NA, Burleson MH et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
摘要：INTRODUCTION: Functional neurological disorders (FND) are characterized by involuntary sensory, motor, and/or other seemingly neurological symptoms lacking expected medical explanation. Once diagnosed, patients often are referred to mental health providers for psychotherapy, which is effective in treating many FND symptom presentations either alone or as part of an integrative approach. Mental health providers may feel reluctant or unprepared to accept these referrals, however. METHODS: We conducted a U.S.-based online survey of 286 practicing psychological service providers (psychologists, psychiatrists, counselors, social workers in private practice, neuropsychologists) to examine knowledge of, attitudes toward, and preparedness to treat FND. RESULTS: Findings revealed that most of our sample had limited exposure to FND in routine practice. Confidence in treating FND was modest, with neuropsychologists reporting more knowledge of and more confidence in treating FND than other psychologists/mental health providers. Greater perceived adequacy of psychotherapy training for, and more experience working with, FND each were associated with more positive FND attitudes, including greater confidence and enjoyment in treating FND and greater belief that symptoms would improve. DISCUSSION: This study is novel in its examination of knowledge and attitudes among psychological service providers, who are pivotal in furnishing effective treatment for FND patients but may not be well-versed in their needs. In contrast to existing studies of medical providers that revealed stigma as a major barrier to providing adequate care for FND, our findings suggest lack of knowledge and lack of access to FND patients may limit the ability to gain more training and expertise. Targeted training and consultation for mental health providers may improve confidence and motivation and expand the workforce capable of delivering effective treatment for FND.
Comprehensive 3-Year Pediatric Resident Resuscitation Simulation Curriculum on the Care of the Severely Ill Pediatric Patient.推荐
PMID 42997119 · MedEdPORTAL : the journal of teaching and learning resources · 2026 11 06
作者：Kent J, Rogers A
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
摘要：INTRODUCTION: Pediatric residents must be able to manage severely ill patients by the end of training. Simulation provides training in low-frequency, high-risk clinical situations. A longitudinal simulation curriculum could provide consistent training for residents to develop these essential skills. METHODS: We utilized Kern's 6 steps of curriculum development. A needs assessment identified content gaps. We implemented a 3-year, 9-case resuscitation simulation curriculum for pediatric residents targeting shock (septic, hypovolemic, obstructive), arrhythmias (supraventricular tachycardia, ventricular fibrillation, asystole), and respiratory failure (with bradycardia, seizures, hypoventilation). Each year, participants completed 3 simulation cases over 3 hours utilizing high-technology manikins. Patients ranged from 10 days to 10 years. We assessed the curriculum using Kirkpatrick's model, including reaction data assessing for change in self-reported confidence recognizing and managing targeted clinical events from pre/postsurveys, free-text responses noting areas of improved knowledge on postsurveys, and chart review of code events assessing for behavior change if residents led actual codes. RESULTS: Ninety-two pediatric residents completed the curriculum. Participation was associated with improved confidence recognizing and managing targeted clinical events (pre: median 3-4, interquartile range [IQR] 3-5; post: 4-5, IQR 4-5; P < .05). Themes of lessons learned included increased knowledge in clinical management, crisis resource management skills, and local resources. Residents were more likely to assume the code leader role in actual patient codes postcurriculum (10% pre vs 40% post). DISCUSSION: Participation in the curriculum was associated with increased resident confidence recognizing and managing targeted content, improved knowledge, and greater resident participation as code leaders in actual codes.
Bilateral, Asynchronous, Inferior Shoulder Dislocation in a Multidisabled Patient: A Case Report and Review of the Literature.浏览
PMID 42997072 · Journal of orthopaedic case reports · 2026 Nov
作者：Ali FE, Ali YF
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：病例报告
摘要：INTRODUCTION: Inferior glenohumeral dislocation, or luxatio erecta humeri (LEH), is an exceptionally rare injury accounting for approximately 0.5% of all shoulder dislocations. It typically results from high-energy trauma through hyperabduction or direct axial loading, forcing the humeral head inferiorly and locking the arm in abduction. While unilateral presentation is rare, bilateral occurrence is extraordinary. Diagnostic or therapeutic delays can cause severe, permanent neurovascular and musculoskeletal sequelae. CASE REPORT: A 42-year-old male with profound neurodevelopmental disabilities - including cerebral palsy, intellectual disability, total blindness, and epilepsy - residing in a long-term care facility presented with an acute right LEH. Attempted closed reduction at an orthopedic center was unsuccessful, and the patient was discharged against medical advice without definitive intervention. Four years later, at age 46, the patient presented with a neglected, chronic left LEH. Due to the chronic, fixed nature of the bilateral injuries, the dislocations were deemed un-reducible, resulting in a profound and permanent accumulation of orthopedic disability. CONCLUSION: Managing LEH in patients with severe developmental disabilities presents unique diagnostic and therapeutic challenges. Cognitive and communication barriers often cause diagnostic overshadowing, leading to missed or severely delayed presentations. Furthermore, management is frequently complicated by patient uncooperativeness, high-risk anesthesia profiles, and increased risks of operative failure.
Artificial intelligence-generated and algorithmically personalized music interventions and their effects on brain function, behavior, and health: a critical review.推荐
PMID 42996984 · Frontiers in human neuroscience · 2026 10 23
作者：Jin J
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：综述
摘要：This critical review synthesizes empirical studies of algorithmically personalized and artificial-intelligence-generated music interventions reporting neurophysiological, neuroimaging, or objective behavioral outcomes. Literature was identified through structured searches of PubMed, Scopus, Google Scholar, IEEE Xplore, and the ACM Digital Library through 12 August 2026, with CNKI and Wanfang searched on 22 September 2026, supplemented by citation tracking. Twenty-one eligible studies (at least 1,005 participants) were identified. Five things the term artificial intelligence covers in this literature are separated: algorithmic selection from a corpus, rule-based sonification, parametric modulation of pre-existing material, machine-learning generative composition, and closed-loop delivery contingent on the listener's own signal. Only three studies used a machine-learning generative model, so most of the evidence concerns algorithmic personalization of existing material. Studies of closed-loop neural systems reported the most consistent neurophysiological signal, and music-based alpha neurofeedback produced higher normalized alpha power than yoked random feedback. In an open-loop design, personalized EEG-derived music was reported to attenuate anxiety induction where self-selected preferred music and silence did not, with enhanced prefrontal theta oscillations as the associated correlate. Eight studies carried a comparator capable of isolating an algorithmic component from music exposure, and all eight favored the algorithmic condition. These eight address different causal questions, use different designs, and point to no single mechanism, so their results are best read as preliminary and hypothesis-generating. Evidence quality was very low throughout. The median reported sample size was 22, no study blinded participants or measured expectancy, and no specific finding has been independently replicated. GRADE certainty was very low for every outcome domain, covering EEG oscillatory power, sleep architecture, autonomic regulation, epileptiform activity, and cognitive, behavioral and affective outcomes. Neurophysiological change is reported more often than behavioral change, and behavioral change more often than clinical benefit. The evidence supports continued investigation and does not support clinical deployment. Eight reporting and design requirements for future trials are proposed.
Cognitive Test Performance in CLN3 Disease Is Associated With the CLN3 Staging System (CLN3SS).推荐
PMID 42996795 · JIMD reports · 2026 11 05
作者：Moran SD, Augustine EF, Mink JW, Pereira-Freitas M et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
关联文献（UpdateOf）：PMID 42238422
摘要：CLN3 disease is an inherited neurodegenerative disease, typically with childhood onset, and characterized by vision loss, seizures, cognitive decline, and difficulties. The CLN3 Staging System (CLN3SS) characterizes disease progression. Our aim was to assess differences in cognitive test scores in relation to CLN3SS among individuals with CLN3 disease. We evaluated the relationship between cognitive test performance and the CLN3SS in individuals with genetically confirmed CLN3 disease. Participants completed tasks of verbal reasoning, vocabulary knowledge, attention, fund of information, and ability to recite the alphabet. One-way ANOVA testing assessed differences in mean cognitive test score among CLN3SS score groups, and chi-square testing was used to compare the proportion in each CLN3SS group that could recite the alphabet. Data were evaluated from a sample of 85 individuals with a total 245 CLN3SS assessments conducted within 6 months of their cognitive testing, A significant decrease in test scores was found between CLN3SS Stages 1 (vision loss present) and 2 (vision loss and seizures present) for each of the cognitive tests. The proportion of participants able to recite the alphabet also decreased from Stage 1 to Stage 2 (χ 2 = 12.1, p < 0.01). Cognitive ability declines with advanced disease severity in CLN3 disease, though motor disability in Stage 3 likely contributes to difficulty participating in cognitive assessment at this later disease stage. Understanding the relationship between cognition and CLN3 disease stage may help guide decision making, that is, determining who could or should undergo cognitive assessment for clinical care or for group stratification in disease modifying clinical trials.
Benchmarking freely accessible large language models for pediatric epilepsy care: a prospective mixed-methods study.推荐
PMID 42996738 · Translational pediatrics · 2026 09 25
作者：Muthaffar O, Alyazidi A
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
摘要：BACKGROUND: Large language models (LLMs) may support patient education and clinical documentation, but their accuracy, safety, and usability in pediatric epilepsy remain insufficiently characterized. This study aimed to compare four freely accessible LLMs using standardized pediatric epilepsy scenarios and to evaluate the top-performing model that met the prespecified safety criterion in a supervised clinician workflow assessment. METHODS: We conducted a prospective, two-phase mixed-methods study at the University Hospital at King Abdulaziz University in Jeddah. In phase 1, four freely accessible LLMs (ChatGPT, Gemini, DeepSeek, and Perplexity) were evaluated using 10 standardized pediatric epilepsy scenarios. Outputs were assessed by pediatric neurologists across factual accuracy, comprehensiveness and relevance, safety, clinical appropriateness, readability and caregiver tailoring, and transparency and sources. Critical safety flags were recorded separately. In phase 2, the model with the highest observed composite score among systems meeting the prespecified safety criterion underwent a supervised clinician workflow assessment using the System Usability Scale (SUS), a caregiver-facing educational-material assessment, and semi-structured interviews. RESULTS: Forty LLM responses were evaluated. Observed composite scores differed significantly across the four model-specific rater groups (P<0.001); however, because each model was evaluated exclusively by a single rater, the observed differences cannot be attributed to model effects independently of evaluator-specific scoring tendencies. DeepSeek and Gemini generated no critical safety flags, whereas ChatGPT had flags in 3/10 responses (30%) and Perplexity in 8/10 (80%). DeepSeek had the highest observed composite score (4.94/5), compared with 4.42/5 for Gemini, 3.59/5 for ChatGPT, and 2.05/5 for Perplexity, and was therefore selected for phase 2. Clinician approval for caregiver use was higher for responses without safety flags than for flagged responses (82.8% vs. 9.1%). CONCLUSIONS: In this specialty-specific benchmark, the evaluated LLMs showed substantial differences in observed accuracy and safety, with DeepSeek achieving the highest observed composite score and no critical safety flags. However, the single-rater-per-model design, small sample, model updates, and limited workflow assessment preclude definitive conclusions about comparative model superiority. These findings support specialty-specific evaluation of LLMs that prioritizes safety and clinical accuracy alongside usability. Larger, independently rated, multi-center studies with repeated model sampling and objective workflow and patient-centered outcomes are needed before routine clinical implementation can be considered.
Persistent cerebrospinal fluid tumor cells during transient radiographic response in bilateral retinoblastoma with central nervous system involvement: a pediatric case report.推荐
PMID 42996412 · Translational pediatrics · 2026 09 29
作者：Zhu J, Zhanghuang C, Ji F, Li Y et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：病例报告
摘要：BACKGROUND: Central nervous system (CNS) involvement in retinoblastoma carries a poor prognosis, and the frequency and clinical meaning of discordance between structural magnetic resonance imaging (MRI) and cerebrospinal fluid (CSF) cytology are not well defined. This report describes a compartment-specific mixed response and emphasizes the complementary roles of MRI and serial CSF assessment. CASE DESCRIPTION: A 6-year-11-month-old boy presented after 6 months of progressive left proptosis, tearing, and conjunctival injection, without seizures or somnolence. Examination showed marked left proptosis and corneal opacity. MRI demonstrated bilateral ocular abnormalities, direct extension along the left optic nerve and chiasm, and a 29.3 mm × 25.8 mm intracranial lesion; malignant cells were present in CSF. Four cycles of vincristine, carboplatin, and etoposide reduced the intracranial lesion to 8.7 mm × 7.5 mm, but the left intraocular lesion did not clearly regress, optic nerve thickness increased from 4.9 mm after two cycles to 5.4 mm after four cycles, and CSF remained cytologically positive. Treatment was intensified for cycles 5-6 with cyclophosphamide-containing systemic chemotherapy and intrathecal methotrexate plus cytarabine. After six cycles, the intracranial lesion enlarged to 11.4 mm × 8.6 mm, optic nerve thickness increased to 6.7 mm, and CSF tumor cells persisted. Severe myelosuppression, infection, transfusion requirements, and family-related barriers prevented further treatment. The child died in March 2026, approximately 10 months after diagnosis. Germline testing prompted by bilateral disease identified a pathogenic RB1 c.772_776del (p.Asn258GlufsTer11) variant that was absent from parental blood. CONCLUSIONS: The early reduction in measurable intracranial tumor burden did not represent CSF clearance and was followed by radiographic progression. In selected patients with CNS-involved retinoblastoma, MRI and CSF cytology provide complementary, non-interchangeable information; this single case supports integrated reassessment but does not establish a new response protocol.
Mild malformation of cortical development with oligodendroglial hyperplasia in epilepsy (MOGHE)-like spectrum with a unique NRAS c.182A>G (p.Q61R) variant: a case report.推荐
PMID 42996408 · Translational pediatrics · 2026 09 30
作者：Fang Y, Wang TS, Ma WJ, Zhou YF et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：病例报告
摘要：BACKGROUND: Mild malformation of cortical development with oligodendroglial hyperplasia in epilepsy (MOGHE) is a recently recognized cortical malformation linked to epilepsy, characterized by subtle cortical and white matter (WM) abnormalities. Typical MOGHE is predominantly associated with somatic SLC35A2 variants, while the genetic and phenotypic spectrum of MOGHE-like cortical malformations remains incompletely understood, with diverse undetermined molecular mechanisms contributing to disease heterogeneity and epileptogenesis. CASE DESCRIPTION: We report an 8-year-old girl with early-onset, drug-resistant epilepsy localized to the right temporal lobe. Brain magnetic resonance imaging (MRI) revealed atypical enlargement and irregular morphological changes of the right temporal lobe with a disrupted gray-white matter (GM-WM) interface, presenting cyst-like malformative changes that deviate from classic MOGHE radiological features. Neuropathological examination demonstrated cortical architectural disturbance, dysoriented neuronal clusters, and mild oligodendroglial hyperplasia consistent with a MOGHE-like spectrum lesion, while no histopathological evidence of low-grade epilepsy-associated tumor or other neoplastic lesions was identified. Whole exome sequencing (WES) identified a heterozygous NRAS c.182A>G (p.Q61R) variant, which is predicted to be functionally deleterious and interpreted as a potentially pathogenic variant based on standard clinical and genetic criteria. The patient achieved favorable post-surgical seizure control, and the pathogenic contribution of this rare NRAS variant requires further validation. CONCLUSIONS: This case characterizes a rare epileptogenic MOGHE-like cortical malformation driven by a unique NRAS c.182A>G (p.Q61R) variant, expanding the molecular genetic spectrum of MOGHE-spectrum lesions. The findings indicate that alternative RAS-pathway molecular alterations may participate in the development of atypical MOGHE-like malformations and related epileptogenesis. This study underscores the necessity of comprehensive integration of radiological, histopathological, and genetic evidence for accurate diagnosis of atypical cortical developmental lesions, and provides novel insights for future mechanistic exploration and precision therapeutic research in refractory epilepsy associated with cortical malformations.
Istradefylline prevents central nervous system oxygen toxicity.推荐
PMID 42996364 · Frontiers in molecular neuroscience · 2026 10 23
作者：Du Z, Li R, Jiang Y, Zhang Y et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
摘要：Central nervous system oxygen toxicity (CNS-OT) is a toxic reaction induced by inhalation of gases with excessively high partial pressure of oxygen, for which effective proactive preventive and therapeutic measures are currently lacking. Building on our previous work implicating adenosine A2A receptor (A2AR) signaling, we examined the prophylactic effects of istradefylline (KW-6002), an approved A2AR antagonist, in male mice. We found that pretreatment oral administration of istradefylline (0.5-20 mg/kg) in mice significantly prolonged the latency to seizure onset and markedly reduced both the intensity and frequency of seizures during a 30-min exposure to 100% oxygen at 5 ATA. Cortical targeted metabolomics showed that HBO exposure decreased GABA and increased glutamate; istradefylline increased cortical GABA without significantly reducing cortical glutamate. Separate ELISA measurements showed lower hippocampal glutamate in istradefylline-treated mice than in HBO-exposed vehicle controls, whereas reductions in the other regions examined were not statistically significant. In the striatum, istradefylline was associated with lower cAMP, immunoreactive PKA protein concentration, and phosphorylated CREB, together with reduced GluA1 Ser845 phosphorylation and increased GABA_A receptor β3 Ser408/409 phosphorylation. In the cortex, malondialdehyde levels decreased and catalase activity increased, whereas glutathione peroxidase activity decreased further. Striatal IL-1β, IL-6, and TNF-α mRNA levels were also reduced. These findings support a prophylactic effect of istradefylline against HBO-induced seizures in male mice and identify associated changes in neurotransmitter levels, receptor phosphorylation, signaling markers, oxidative-stress measures, and inflammatory-gene expression. These results provide a theoretical basis for the development of proactive preventive and therapeutic strategies for CNS-OT with potential clinical application.
Conserved Thalamic Astrocytic Pathology in Mice and Patients Links TBI to Epilepsy.推荐
PMID 42996341 · Research square · 2026 10 23
作者：Moro F, Lisi I, Needham E, O'Loughlin E et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
摘要：Post-traumatic epilepsy (PTE) can emerge months to years after traumatic brain injury (TBI) and is a major cause of morbidity, yet the pathological processes that distinguish TBI progressing to epilepsy remain poorly defined. Here, we combined longitudinal multimodal imaging, electrophysiology, neuropathology, and human imaging and postmortem analyses to identify cross-species features associated with PTE. In a mouse model of PTE-prone TBI, injury produced widespread brain pathology, whereas animals developing PTE showed disproportionate thalamic atrophy, white-matter abnormalities with reduced optic tract fractional anisotropy, and persistent metabolic alterations. Histological analyses revealed pronounced thalamic astrocytic reactivity, reduced cytoskeletal complexity, and impaired expression of proteins involved in glutamate homeostasis. Using an early ECoG signature to prospectively stratify PTE risk, we found that astrocytic simplification and accumulation of insoluble GFAP were already present during the latent period before spontaneous seizure onset. In patients with PTE, thalamic volume loss and reduced posterior thalamic radiation fractional anisotropy independently improved PTE classification beyond clinical variables. PTE patients also showed reduced pulvinar-centered thalamocortical functional connectivity involving visual and default mode networks. Postmortem tissue further demonstrated pronounced astrocytic reactivity and reduced astrocytic complexity in cortex and thalamus. In contrast, IBA1-defined microglial reactivity did not distinguish PTE from non-PTE at the chronic time points examined. Together, these findings identify a conserved thalamocortical phenotype associated with PTE and show that astrocytic remodeling emerges before spontaneous seizures. The convergence of cellular pathology with structural, metabolic, and functional abnormalities identifies the thalamus as a candidate hub of post-traumatic epileptogenesis and provides translational markers for tracking disease progression after TBI.
Transcriptomics-based drug repositioning in a neurocysticercosis rat model identifies putative therapeutic candidates reported to mitigate neuroinflammation and neurodegeneration.浏览
PMID 42996327 · Research square · 2026 10 21
作者：Melo O, Huerta O, Villalobos K, Chile N et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
摘要：Neurocysticercosis (NCC) is a major cause of epilepsy and neurological symptoms in low- and middle-income countries. Although antiparasitic treatment targets the parasite, it often fails to fully resolve symptoms and can exacerbate brain tissue damage. Using a rat model of NCC and transcriptomic profiling, we assessed the effects of infection and treatment on brain gene expression to uncover disease molecular pathways and identify putative therapeutic candidates through in silico drug repurposing. We analyzed transcriptomic profiles at two stages of infection (4- and 6-month post-infection (MPI)), and after treatment at 6 MPI (6 MPI + T). At 6 MPI, fewer immune-related genes and pathways were enriched compared to 4 MPI, likely due to MAPK pathway repression, but immune activation increased significantly after treatment, possibly due to cyst degeneration. Drug repositioning identified 30 candidate drugs. Among these, vorinostat, ciclosporin, papaverine, valproic acid, and chlorpromazine have been reported to mitigate neuroinflammation and neurodegeneration. Taken together, these results provide a molecular rationale for drug repositioning in neurocysticercosis; however, further experimental validation in larger cohorts is required to confirm the therapeutic potential of the identified candidates.
Altered interictal alpha-band organization in childhood absence epilepsy.推荐
PMID 42996304 · Frontiers in neuroinformatics · 2026 10 23
作者：Latka M, Glaba P, Kuryło M, Jędrzejczyk-Góral B et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
摘要：OBJECTIVE: Interictal background EEG in childhood absence epilepsy (CAE) may appear normal. We asked whether drug-naive children with CAE differ from controls in alpha frequency and the regional distribution of phase-lagged synchronization, and how these differences depend on participant-specific surrogate baselines and analysis frequency. METHODS: We analyzed eyes-closed resting EEG (19 channels, 26 s) from 49 drug-naive children with CAE and 83 controls. Individual alpha frequency (IAF) was estimated by spectral parameterization of posterior channels. Weighted phase-lag index (wPLI) at 12 Hz was summarized globally and as the frontal-posterior log-ratio λFP = ln(wPLIF/wPLIP), where F and P denote frontal and posterior within-region means. Participant-specific baselines were obtained from 100 circular-shift surrogates, preserving each channel's wavelet-coefficient sequence up to circular translation while altering inter-channel alignment. Group comparisons used studentized permutation tests. The regional contrast was also evaluated at each participant's IAF. RESULTS: IAF was lower in patients (8.80 versus 9.42 Hz; p = 0.002). At 12 Hz, controls showed posterior predominance of synchronization (λFP = -0.352), which was largely removed by circular shifting (-0.036), whereas patients' mean regional balance was near zero. After baseline subtraction, the patient-minus-control difference in λFP was 0.332 (95 % CI [0.181, 0.483]; p < 0.001; Hedges' g = 0.79). In the 131 participants with identifiable IAF, the contrast was 0.316 at 12 Hz and 0.124 at IAF (p = 0.139). A direct comparison using within-participant changes supported a larger contrast at 12 Hz (difference 0.192, 95 % CI [0.017, 0.367]; exploratory, unadjusted p = 0.032). The global wPLI difference after baseline subtraction remained uncertain (-0.021, 95 % CI [-0.044, 0.001]; p = 0.069). CONCLUSIONS: Drug-naive CAE was associated with slower alpha activity and reduced posterior predominance of phase-lagged synchronization at 12 Hz. Circular shifting largely removed the controls' posterior predominance, supporting a difference in regional organization sensitive to inter-channel alignment. The smaller contrast at IAF indicates that analysis frequency materially affects this finding, without establishing that alpha slowing alone explains it.
The impact of comorbid headache on surgical outcomes in drug-resistant focal epilepsy.推荐
PMID 42996172 · Frontiers in neurology · 2026 10 23
作者：Cheng W, Shi A, Du M, Ma Y et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
摘要：BACKGROUND: Headache is a common comorbidity of epilepsy and is closely associated with the poor prognosis of epilepsy. Resection surgery of the epileptogenic focus is an effective treatment method for drug-resistant focal epilepsy (DRFE). However, the relationship between headache and surgical outcomes remains unclear. This study aimed to investigate the impact of comorbid headache and its characteristics on short-term surgical outcomes in DRFE. METHODS: DRFE patients undergoing resection surgery were consecutively enrolled and followed up for at least one year after surgery. Seizure-free rate within 1 year after surgery was compared between the headache and non-headache groups. Subgroup analysis was performed within the headache group to compare headache characteristics between patients who were seizure-free and those who experienced seizures within 1 year after surgery. RESULTS: A total of 103 DRFE patients were enrolled, among whom 46 (44.7%) had comorbid headache. Of the 46 patients, 23 experienced unilateral headache, and in 22 of these 23 patients (22/23, 95.7%), the headache side was ipsilateral to the epileptogenic focus. The difference in the seizure-free rate within 1 year after surgery between the headache and non-headache groups was not statistically significant (78.3% vs. 73.7%, p = 0.590). Among DRFE patients with comorbid headache, compared with the seizure within 1 year after surgery group, unilateral headache was significantly more frequent (58.3% vs. 20.0%, p = 0.032), and the mean preoperative visual analogue scale (VAS) score was significantly lower (5.50 ± 2.171 vs. 7.60 ± 1.578, p = 0.007) in the seizure-free within 1 year after surgery group. Multivariate binary logistic regression analysis demonstrated that unilateral headache was independently associated with higher odds of seizure-free within 1 year after surgery (OR = 1.854, 95% CI 1.112-3.092, p = 0.018), whereas the preoperative VAS score was independently associated with lower odds of seizure-free within 1 year after surgery (OR = 0.136, 95% CI 0.020-0.923, p = 0.041). CONCLUSION: Comorbid headache does not significantly affect short-term surgical outcomes in DRFE patients. However, for DRFE patients with comorbid headache, unilateral headache or a lower preoperative VAS score may serve as potential clinical indicators for favorable short-term surgical outcomes.
Cortical physiological arousal following termination of absence seizures.推荐
PMID 42996074 · medRxiv : the preprint server for health sciences · 2026 10 22
作者：Sharma A, Khalaf A, Beniczky S, Glauser TA et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
摘要：Although the brain state preceding and during absence seizures has been well-characterized, the postictal brain state remains largely unexplored due to the prevailing assumption that absence seizures have minimal or no postictal changes. To address this gap, scalp EEG recordings from 55 patients with 3-4 Hz spike-wave discharges across four multi-site cohorts were analyzed. Spectral power was measured relative to each seizure's preictal baseline, and the postictal period was compared to a time-matched preictal window. Rather than an immediate return to baseline after seizure offset, a significant broadband increase in EEG power was observed in the gamma (>25 Hz) and delta (<4 Hz) frequency bands, followed by a gradual decline over the next 15 s to 30 s. In contrast, no comparable change preceded the seizures. This suggests that recovery from an absence seizure is an active process with a characteristic electrophysiological signature consistent with cortical activation and arousal. Thus, postictal arousal may facilitate exit from the seizure state, and with further work could serve as an electrophysiological marker and therapeutic target for recovery in absence epilepsy.
Repetitive clustered low-voltage fast activity on intracranial electroencephalogram in mild malformation of cortical development with oligodendroglial hyperplasia and epilepsy: A candidate electroencephalographic biomarker study.推荐
PMID 42995729 · Epilepsia · 2026 11 07
作者：Gnatkovsky V, Poguzhelskaya E, Borger V, Baumgartner T et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
摘要：OBJECTIVE: Mild malformation of cortical development with oligodendroglial hyperplasia and epilepsy (MOGHE) is a recently recognized cause of drug-resistant focal epilepsy. It is often magnetic resonance imaging-negative or radiologically resembles focal cortical dysplasias, which makes recognition difficult and limits presurgical counseling. We aimed to identify an intracranial electroencephalographic (iEEG) biomarker that distinguishes MOGHE from other developmental brain lesions encountered in epilepsy surgery. METHODS: We analyzed long-term stereo-EEG and subdural recordings in a retrospective multicenter test cohort (18 MOGHE, 20 non-MOGHE) to identify an iEEG pattern distinguishing MOGHE from other developmental brain lesions. We then evaluated its diagnostic performance by blinded visual assessment in an independent single-center validation cohort (11 MOGHE, 11 non-MOGHE). Firth-penalized multivariable logistic regression tested whether MOGHE histology independently predicted the presence of this pattern after adjustment for spasms, frontal localization, age at seizure onset, and age at implantation. Finally, within MOGHE, we assessed whether resection of the territory exhibiting this pattern was associated with postsurgical seizure freedom. RESULTS: We identified highly stereotyped clusters of very brief low-voltage fast activity (LVFA), recurring in statuslike episodes lasting several minutes and sometimes lacking clear clinical symptoms. LVFA clusters occurred in 16 of 18 MOGHE and in no non-MOGHE patients in the test cohort. In the validation cohort, visual identification of LVFA clusters correctly classified 10 of 11 MOGHE and 10 of 11 non-MOGHE cases (overall agreement = 90.9%, 95% Wilson confidence interval = 72.2%-97.5%). MOGHE histology was the dominant independent predictor of LVFA clusters. Within MOGHE, resection of LVFA cluster territory was associated with postsurgical seizure freedom (Mann-Whitney U-test, p = .0021). SIGNIFICANCE: Because LVFA clusters can be recognized visually on routine iEEG without specialized software, they may provide a clinically applicable candidate biomarker for presurgical identification of MOGHE and support surgical planning and outcome prediction. Prospective studies should validate their diagnostic and prognostic utility.
Relapsing Anti-NMDAR encephalitis with later detected MOG-IgG-Positive cortical encephalitis: a case report.推荐
PMID 42995546 · BMC neurology · 2026 08 11
作者：Lu Y, Zhao Z, Song Z, Xin Z et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：病例报告
摘要：BACKGROUND: Anti-N-methyl-D-aspartate receptor (NMDAR) encephalitis is the most common autoimmune encephalitis. Neuroimaging findings are frequently normal or nonspecific, and predominant cortical involvement at disease onset is uncommon in anti-NMDAR encephalitis. Overlap with myelin oligodendrocyte glycoprotein antibody-associated disease (MOGAD) has been increasingly recognized; therefore, the value of individual reports lies in defining their longitudinal phenotypic evolution and diagnostic limitations. CASE PRESENTATION: We report a 28-year-old woman with relapsing anti-NMDAR encephalitis followed for nearly seven years across three episodes (the index attack and two relapses). The initial presentation in 2018 was typical anti-NMDAR encephalitis, with positive cerebrospinal fluid (CSF) anti-NMDAR antibodies and complete clinical remission after the first episode. The 2022 relapse manifested as focal cortical encephalitis with progressive unilateral cortical swelling, sulcal enhancement, and seizures; MOG-IgG was not tested. In 2025, she developed recurrent headache, intracranial hypertension, and extensive unilateral cortical involvement, with later detected serum MOG-IgG at 1:32. CSF anti-NMDAR and MOG-IgG were not tested at this admission. The episode was therefore adjudicated as MOG-IgG-positive cortical encephalitis within a longitudinal anti-NMDAR/MOGAD overlap course rather than a proven simultaneous dual-antibody relapse. Oral methylprednisolone was followed by clinical and radiological improvement. CONCLUSIONS: Across nearly seven years, this case documents phenotypic evolution from CSF-confirmed anti-NMDAR encephalitis to recurrent unilateral cortical encephalitis and later serum MOG-IgG detection. A changing cortical phenotype should prompt renewed antibody assessment at each recurrence rather than reliance on the index antibody profile. Because MOG-IgG was not tested in earlier episodes and CSF anti-NMDAR was not tested in 2025, the timing and mechanistic contribution of each antibody cannot be determined.
Multimorbidity patterns and disease co-occurrence networks in older adults with diabetes: A national population-based network analysis.浏览
PMID 42995399 · Diabetic medicine : a journal of the British Diabetic Association · 2026 11 06
作者：Sun C, Mao L, Ji D, Si L et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
摘要：AIMS: This study aimed to characterize multimorbidity patterns and disease co-occurrence networks among older adults with diabetes in China. MATERIALS: This cross-sectional study analysed data from older adults aged ≥65 years with diabetes from the 2018 Chinese Longitudinal Healthy Longevity Survey. Network and hierarchical clustering analyses were used to examine co-occurrence patterns among 17 chronic physical and mental conditions. RESULTS: A total of 1408 participants with diabetes were included, comprising 593 male participants (42.1%) and 815 female participants (57.9%). Overall, 91.6% of participants had ≥1 additional chronic condition, and 71.3% had ≥2 additional chronic conditions. Hypertension (75.5%), heart disease (36.7%) and affective disorder (33.7%) were the three most common conditions; the hypertension-heart disease combination was the leading comorbidity pair (31.75%). Arthritis ranked highest for strength, closeness and betweenness in the overall network; epilepsy also ranked highly for strength and betweenness, particularly among participants aged ≥75 years and in the central and western regions. Five comorbidity clusters emerged from hierarchical clustering, with centrality profiles differing by age, sex and region. CONCLUSIONS: Our study indicated that multimorbidity burden was high among people with diabetes aged ≥65 years in China and was characterized by distinct network structures and clustering patterns. Arthritis and epilepsy showed high centrality in the comorbidity network, and network characteristics differed across age, sex and regional groups. These findings suggest that health management for older adults with diabetes should address not only common cardiometabolic conditions but also musculoskeletal, neurological and mental health problems.
Expanding mutational landscape and clinical phenotype of the CASK-related neurological disorders.推荐
PMID 42995192 · Journal of human genetics · 2026 11 06
作者：Pekerbas M, Bulut K, Yigit A, Sahin B et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
摘要：CASK-related disorders comprise two main phenotypes: severe microcephaly with pontocerebellar hypoplasia (MICPCH), typically caused by truncating variants, and milder X-linked intellectual disability (XLID), usually associated with missense variants. We aimed to characterize this molecular spectrum within an underrepresented national cohort. We evaluated 18 affected individuals from 16 unrelated families with CASK variants identified through exome or genome sequencing. Clinical, neurodevelopmental and neuroimaging findings were systematically reviewed. Selected missense variants were evaluated by in silico prediction tools and structural modeling. We identified 16 distinct CASK variants, 11 of which are novel, including frameshift, stop-gain, splice-site, and missense variants, as well as one large deletion. Consistent with known genotype-phenotype correlations, null alleles resulted in the severe MICPCH phenotype, while presumed hypomorphic alleles were associated with XLID. However, unlike previous reports, our MICPCH patients exhibited a higher frequency of dysmorphic facial features (86%), skeletal anomalies (57%) and sensorineural hearing loss (43%). Furthermore, contrary to the literature suggesting a milder epilepsy in females due to X-inactivation, 63% of our female patients with MICPCH presented with recurrent seizures. This cohort broadens both the molecular and phenotypic spectrum of CASK-related disorders. Findings reinforce that truncating variants predominantly cause MICPCH, while missense variants can lead to various outcomes depending on domain-level structural impact. We demonstrate that the CASK phenotypic spectrum is broader than previously recognized, frequently involving distinctive facial dysmorphism, skeletal defects and hearing impairment, suggesting that CASK plays a critical role not only in synaptic function but also in wider developmental processes.
Molecular Diagnosis in Children With Febrile Seizures: Beyond the Conventional Clinical Classification.推荐
PMID 42995169 · Annals of clinical and translational neurology · 2026 11 06
作者：Wang J, Zhang M, Wang Y, Shi X et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
摘要：OBJECTIVE: Febrile seizures (FS) are currently classified as simple or complex primarily to describe seizure characteristics, with limited ability to identify the underlying etiology. We evaluated whether molecular diagnosis could complement conventional classification and improve the diagnosis and management of FS. METHODS: This nationwide, multi-center, prospective cohort study, conducted from January 2019 to February 2023, enrolled patients who were clinically diagnosed with FS. The panel targeted 189 genes associated with fever-induced seizures. Initially, children were categorized into simple febrile seizures (SFS) and complex febrile seizures (CFS) groups based on medical history and clinical evaluation. Variant classification followed the ACMG guidelines. We analyzed the cohort demographic characteristics, potential genetic etiologies across different clinical phenotypes, and clinical utility of genetic diagnosis. RESULTS: Our study included 3300 children with FS, and ultimately analyzed sequencing data from 2552 children, of whom 273 received a positive diagnosis. The molecular diagnostic yield was 11% (273 out of 2552 patients), with the top 10 genes accounting for 70.4% of all confirmed genetic diagnoses. No significant difference in molecular diagnostic yield was observed between the SFS (9.5%, 111/1163) and CFS (11.7%, 162/1389) groups (p = 0.085). Molecular diagnostics had immediate clinical utility, influencing management (76.2%) and informing prognosis (72.6%). The cost of a single panel sequencing for a three-person family was $240, with a turnaround time of 14 days. INTERPRETATION: Molecular diagnosis may improve the diagnosis of FS by identifying genetic fever-sensitive epilepsies that may be missed by phenotype-based classification, with implications for risk stratification and individualized management.
